# Bored Chat vs every mainstream social & dating app — the honest audit (2026-10-18)

Benchmarks: WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook (incl. Messenger), Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, and the events trio Meetup/Partiful/Luma counted as one — 17 in all. Thirteen per-area sections follow this front matter; every number below is taken from their verdict paragraphs and tables.

## 1. The verdict in ten lines

1. **What is genuinely good.** The message pipeline and sync core (gapless per-conversation `seq`, `GET /api/sync` reconcile on every reconnect, an IndexedDB outbox with idempotent flush; reply, forward, edit, unsend-with-undo, reactions, pins, voice notes with scrub and speed, link previews, scheduled sends, disappearing timers); the beacon loop ("I'm bored" → atomic spark into a group with an icebreaker, venue poll, named roles, afterglow recap, private kudos and guilt-free rituals), which no benchmark app matches; the v0.9 auth pack (passkeys, QR login where the token never transits the phone, TOTP, recovery codes, revocation that closes live sockets); block enforcement that is 404-shaped at every surface; calls with ring-push retraction and watch-together; and the psychology rules enforced in code rather than copy — named faces instead of counts, receipts without timestamps, feeds with a hard end, no streaks.
2. **Where it is behind a 2015 messenger.** No Copy on a message, no drafts, no mute anyone can set, no day dividers, no sender clustering, no delete-for-me, no way to delete or archive a DM, no pin, search or filter on the chat list, a "Photo" tile that is a bare file input, a fixed 40-emoji picker, group rename / kick / promote that exist as server routes with no button, and 15 native `window.confirm`/`alert` boxes inside an otherwise custom sheet system.
3. **The account has no lifecycle.** A member cannot change their password, email or username, cannot delete or pause the account, cannot sign out everywhere, cannot export their data, and has no Terms or Privacy page to read; a suspended user is told to "check your connection".
4. **The safety control layer every benchmark ships is missing in one block.** Anyone who can prefix-search your name can open a DM with you; reports are one free-text box with no reasons; members cannot report a post, comment or story while logged-out strangers on share pages can; the only lever softer than nothing is a hard block.
5. **The feed is a third of the basic bar (22 of 68).** Grid is the default and strips authors from a friends feed, others' posts have no options menu, images open the raw proxy URL in a new tab, there is no edit, bookmark, permalink or threaded reply, and an author is never told that anyone replied.
6. **The shell is right and the skin is unfinished.** The light theme — the one most phones and every screenshot in this audit show — is, by `globals.css`'s own comment, "not designed": incoming bubbles sit at 1.04:1 against the chat background, tab labels at 2.85:1, and the tab bar disappears on profiles, settings, new chat and the feature directory.
7. **"The operator cannot read chats" is policy, not cryptography.** No admin route returns message bodies (ADR-013), which is a real and unusual boundary; but there is no E2EE, bodies are plaintext in Postgres, the operator has `psql`, and no page a user can read says so.
8. **Shippable today?** To a closed circle of friends on a private origin: yes, provided they accept that nobody can delete their account, mute a group, or change a password without burning a recovery code. To strangers on a public origin: no — registration has no off switch, there is no age attestation, no terms, no who-can-message gate, no report taxonomy and no appeal path, and that is operator liability, not polish.
9. **By the numbers.** Across the 13 areas, 46% of basic-tier expectations and 21% of standard-tier expectations are fully met (unweighted average; pooled it is 277/614 and 205/960). The strongest areas are Calls & media (30/41 basic) and Discovery (32/49); the weakest are Feed (22/68) and Stories (14/42).
10. **How much work.** The 30 gaps in §3 and the broken basics in §4 are mostly small, but they are not an afternoon: at the sections' own day estimates the basic block in §8 is about four engineer-weeks and the standard block about seven more. Most of it is wiring server halves that already exist to controls that do not.

## 2. Scorecard

Numerators and denominators are the sections' own (has ÷ has + half-built + missing + planned, banned rows excluded, half-built earns nothing). The grade band is set by basic coverage — B ≥70%, C 50–69%, D 35–49%, F <35%; nothing qualifies for an A — with one adjustment: Privacy & safety drops a step because it owns the zero legal and consent surface for the whole product, and that hole is charged once rather than to every area that touches it.

| Area | Basic coverage | Standard coverage | Grade | One-line reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Accounts & sign-in | 13/29 (45%) | 18/65 (28%) | D | Passkeys, QR login, TOTP and recovery codes are well built; change-password, change-email, delete-account, terms and an age line do not exist. |
| Profile & identity | 14/29 (48%) | 17/73 (23%) | D | Polished hero over a core with no private mode, no who-can-message, no posts tab, a bio the owner cannot see on their own tab, and HEIC avatars that 415. |
| Privacy & safety | 17/43 (40%) | 27/86 (31%) | F | Enforcement is solid and invisible; the control layer (who-can-message, mute, restrict, hidden words, report reasons) and the legal surface (terms, privacy, guidelines, consent, age) are absent. |
| Notifications | 18/43 (42%) | 10/70 (14%) | D | A complete delivery engine behind one switch: no mute, no categories, no preview hiding, no badge, no activity surface; logout leaves the device's push subscription live. |
| Chat & DMs | 34/68 (50%) | 14/79 (18%) | C | The pipeline covers every standard verb; the surface lacks Copy, drafts, mute, day dividers, clustering, delete-for-me and any chat-list management. |
| Groups & channels | 17/45 (38%) | 13/67 (19%) | D | Four visibility modes and plan groups are real; rename, kick, promote, poll creation, invite revoke and join-from-inside-the-app exist as routes with no UI. |
| Feed & posts | 22/68 (32%) | 16/73 (22%) | F | Finite lanes and faces-not-counts are right; no options menu on others' posts, no report, raw-URL images, grid-by-default, no edit, bookmark, permalink or threads. |
| Stories | 14/42 (33%) | 8/59 (14%) | F | A 2017 photo ring with careful privacy plumbing: a viewer has zero actions, video fails on the first post, the composer cannot draw or write text, and every in-app "Stories" pointer leads to a tab with no tray. |
| Discovery & strangers | 32/49 (65%) | 13/66 (20%) | C | The calm stranger-meeting loop is complete and tested; every safety basic a stranger surface needs (requests gating, age gate, report reasons, unmatch, safety copy) is missing, and the card you wave on was never captured in a browser. |
| Local: plans & board | 24/50 (48%) | 18/65 (28%) | D | Beacons beat every benchmark on group formation; the board is four narrow kinds with no sell/free, no body search, no renew, no outcome, and areas that split on spelling. |
| Calls & media | 30/41 (73%) | 13/78 (17%) | B | Competent signaling, mesh calls, PiP and watch-together; answered calls leave no trace, no camera shutter, no carousel, originals served at full size, no screen share or device routing. |
| UI & navigation | 27/70 (39%) | 13/76 (17%) | D | Tab bar, sheets, toasts and reduced motion are above the bar; the light theme is derived, contrast fails on primary nav, the tab bar vanishes on lateral routes, and 15 native dialogs ship. |
| Platform & ops | 15/37 (41%) | 25/103 (24%) | D | Sync, export/import and the update checker are clean; no theme toggle, stale shell after every deploy, open registration with no gate, content leaves the app by clipboard only. |
| **Unweighted average** | **46%** | **21%** | — | 13 areas, equal weight; pooled ratios are 277/614 = 45% and 205/960 = 21%. |

## 3. The 30 things every app has that this one doesn't

Ranked. P0 means a user hits it in the first hour, nearly every benchmark has it, and its absence breaks the core loop, a safety expectation or a legal expectation; P1 is expected within the first week. Only items the sections mark missing or half-built are listed — planned items (call history #102, profile share link and QR #110, posts tab #107, story audience and replies #98/#99, global search #108, email verification and forgot-password, OS share sheet per the SPECS share audit, self-serve data export) are left to §8, and banned items to §7.

**P0**

1. **Delete my account** — Accounts, Profile, Privacy, Platform. All 17. No `DELETE /api/users/me`, no row under Settings, no admin purge either; an erasure request is hand-written SQL.
2. **Change password while signed in** — Accounts, Profile, Platform. Every password app. The only way to set a new password is burning a recovery code from the login page, which signs out every device; `cli.ts:36` tells users to do something the product cannot do.
3. **Terms of Service and Privacy pages with a consent line at signup** — Accounts, Privacy, UI, Platform. All 17. Four docs cite an "instance privacy note" that was never written; the app stores email, IPs for 90 days, photos and a home area and tells nobody.
4. **Minimum-age attestation at signup** — Accounts, Privacy, Discovery. Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Threads, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup. A stranger pool and a drunk mode with no 16+/18+ checkbox; the no-DOB decision does not forbid a non-stored attestation.
5. **Who can message me, with a message-requests inbox** — Privacy, Discovery, Chat, Profile. Instagram, Messenger, X, Threads, Reddit, Snapchat, Discord, Telegram, TikTok, Signal; Tinder, Bumble and Hinge gate by match. `POST /api/conversations {dm}` checks blocks only.
6. **Report reasons (spam, harassment, impersonation, underage, self-harm, scam, off-platform, IP, other)** — Privacy, Discovery, Chat, Platform. 15 of 17 (all but WhatsApp and Signal). One optional free-text box; the queue cannot be triaged and a self-harm report looks like spam.
7. **Options menu on other people's posts (Report · Hide · Block) and in-app report of a post, comment or story** — Feed, Privacy, Stories. Instagram, Threads, X, Facebook, Reddit, TikTok, Nextdoor, Snapchat, BeReal. The ⋯ renders only on own posts and its one item is Delete; `ReportKindSchema` has no post, comment or story kind.
8. **Mute a chat (8 h / 1 week / always)** — Notifications, Chat, Groups, Privacy, UI, Platform. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, Discord, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, TikTok. The column has existed since migration 0000 and the push worker honours it; nothing writes it and nothing shows it.
9. **Copy message text** — Chat, Groups. 15 of 17. The long-press menu is Reply / Forward / Pin / Edit / Delete / Info / Report; there is no clipboard call in `ChatView.tsx`.
10. **Drafts persisted per chat with a "Draft" label** — Chat, UI, Platform. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Tinder, Bumble, Partiful. Composer text is React state only; switching chats mid-sentence loses it.
11. **Day dividers and same-sender clustering in a conversation** — Chat, Groups, UI. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, Snapchat, Hinge. Every bubble carries its own clock; a week-old chat cannot tell Tuesday from Saturday.
12. **Group admin verbs in the UI: rename, remove a member, promote/demote** — Groups, Chat, Privacy. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, Snapchat. `PATCH title`, `DELETE participants/:userId` and `PUT role` exist and are tested; the only buttons live in the channel members sheet.
13. **Search or filter the chat list by name** — UI, Chat. Every mainstream chat app. The Chats magnifier is `<Link href="/people">`; the planned global search (#108) is a different thing.
14. **A theme toggle (Dark / Light / System) and a designed light theme** — UI, Platform. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, Tinder, Luma. `data-theme` appears nowhere; light is nine derived CSS blocks against roughly 16,000 lines.
15. **Full-screen viewer with swipe between a post's images** — Feed, Calls, UI. Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit, Telegram, TikTok, Nextdoor, Threads, Discord. `MediaLightbox` exists for chat, board and profile and takes a single `src`; feed images are `<a target="_blank">` to the raw file.
16. **Comment or reply notification on my post** — Notifications, Feed. Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Meetup/Partiful/Luma. Comments fan out nothing; PSYCHOLOGY §6 allows a named-faces digest and none was built.
17. **A sign-out that signs out: clear cached messages and revoke this device's push** — Accounts, Notifications, Platform. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, and every native app by construction. `logout()` clears only the outbox store; a signed-out shared browser keeps showing message previews with text bodies.
18. **A suspended or banned user told why, with an appeal path** — Privacy, Accounts, Platform. WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Snapchat, Discord, Reddit, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge. `login/page.tsx:148-154` branches only on `bad_credentials` and 429; Help requires a session.

**P1**

19. **Pin, archive, mark-unread and swipe or long-press actions on chat rows** — Chat, Groups, UI. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, Discord, X. Rows are plain links sorted by last message.
20. **Delete for me, clear chat, remove a DM from the inbox** — Privacy, Chat, Groups. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, BeReal, Nextdoor. Nothing unwanted ever leaves the screen except by blocking.
21. **Per-category notification toggles, hide-message-preview and an app-icon badge** — Notifications, Chat, Privacy, Platform. All 17 for categories; WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Facebook, Instagram, Snapchat for preview. One Enable/Disable switch; the body is always the first 140 characters; no `setAppBadge`.
22. **Unread divider and jump-to-bottom pill** — Chat, Groups, Notifications, UI. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Messenger, Instagram. `last_read_seq` is on the client and never drawn; after a jump-to-reply there is no way back.
23. **Private account / connections-only profile** — Profile, Privacy. Instagram, TikTok, X, Threads, Snapchat, BeReal, Facebook, Luma. Bio, pictures, work/skills and trust reviews are visible to every logged-in member with no way to lock them.
24. **Mute or restrict a person without blocking** — Privacy, Profile, Notifications, Stories, Feed. Instagram, X, Threads, Snapchat, Nextdoor, TikTok, Facebook, Discord, Meetup. Only the story-canvas ban proves the soft-block pattern; feeds, stories and DMs have no mute.
25. **Edit a post with an "Edited" label** — Feed. Instagram, Facebook, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, BeReal, Threads, X. Fixing a typo means delete and repost, losing every face and comment.
26. **Save or bookmark a post or listing** — Feed, Local. Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads, Snapchat, Telegram, Meetup/Luma. Private, no psychology conflict, no table.
27. **Clickable links, @mentions and threaded replies on posts** — Feed. Facebook, X, Threads, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, Discord, Instagram. The post body is inert pre-wrap text; `URL_RE` and mention infra already exist for chat; `post_comments` has no `parent_id`.
28. **Sign out of all other devices** — Accounts, Privacy, Platform. WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Discord, X. `revokeAllSessions` exists and is reachable only by burning a recovery code, admin action or CLI.
29. **Per-chat media, links and files gallery** — Chat, Groups, Calls, UI. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, Snapchat. The chat-settings sheet is theme, wallpaper, sound, nicknames and disappearing; attachments by conversation are already stored.
30. **In-app camera capture from the composer, stories and feed** — Calls, Chat, Stories, Feed. Telegram, Threads, Hinge, Snapchat, BeReal, WhatsApp, Instagram, TikTok. No `capture` attribute anywhere; desktop has no camera path and mobile gets whatever the OS file chooser offers.

## 4. Basics that are broken or half-built

Everything here is met in the first hour. A. is behaviour that is wrong; B. is a server half or component with no control attached; C. is copy, pointers and docs that promise something the code does not do.

### A. Bugs and wrong behaviour

| What a user hits | Where | Fix size |
|---|---|---|

| First video story fails with "Could not post the story." | `StoriesStrip.tsx:176-187` posts before the worker marks the attachment ready → 400 `bad_attachment`; no processing state, no retry | afternoon |
| Author cannot draw on their own default story: 403 `canvas_closed` | `stories/routes.ts:366` refuses the author when `canvas_mode === 'off'` while `StoryCanvas.tsx:78/351` shows the tools | one branch |
| Story video autoplays unmuted; where the browser blocks it the poster sits still and the progress bar never completes | `StoryViewer.tsx:212-219`, `play()` rejection swallowed at :91 | afternoon |
| Feed images open the raw proxy URL in a new tab; an installed PWA is thrown out to the browser | `FeedView.tsx:1013` and `:1674` `<a target="_blank">`; `MediaLightbox` not imported | half day with a pager |
| A video post renders as a broken image while it transcodes | `FeedView.tsx:1013-1015` sends non-ready video through the `<img>` branch; the button still says "Add images" | afternoon |

| Settings → Chats and Settings → Calls pages | Any setting on either; Chats is two cross-reference rows | `settings/chats/page.tsx:41-66` |
| "Public profile pages" (FEATURES #15) | Anything public: `/u/` is `'use client'` inside `AppShell`, `GET /api/users/:username` requires auth, no OG | `u/[username]/page.tsx` |
| A "Posts" segment on the own Profile tab | A per-user posts endpoint; the segment filters the first page of the friends feed client-side, tiles not tappable | `settings/profile/page.tsx:85-87` |

### C. Pointers, copy and docs that lie

- **Feature directory ("Things you can do"):** 15 of 25 "Try it" links are not deep links — 12 go to `/`, 3 to `/settings` (`constants.ts:1037-1065`); Beacons and Rituals go to `/plans`, a bare redirect; "Stories" goes to `/feed`, which has no tray (`constants.ts:1049`, `gallery/page.tsx:111`, tour copy `:1029`). The tour promises "voice notes, doodles and polls" (`constants.ts:1011`); there is no poll tile.
- **The Chats magnifier** is `<Link href="/people">` with `aria-label="Search"` (`ConversationListView.tsx:81-84`).
- **FEATURES.md overclaims:** #9 "muting", #48 "group admins", #41 room "looking for" direction, #42 place reviews feeding venue polls, #54 "warn" as a moderation outcome, #15 "public profile pages"; and underclaims: #97 multi-story and #105 call layouts are shipped but listed planned, #110 video on share pages already works.
- **API.md and ADRs:** §1.13 forbids an all-off notification switch citing a digest that does not exist, while Settings lets you go fully silent; `API.md:1649`, `ADR-013:118`, FEATURES #54 and `admin/users/page.tsx:108` cite an "instance privacy note" that was never written; §1.68 and `ADR-020:67-69` say `member_count` stays off every in-app surface while the group header renders "N members"; `API.md:2622` promises the client completes share deep links back to the object and it cannot; `SPECS §79d` says public groups appear in discovery and nothing implements it; PSYCHOLOGY §5 "Build (v0.3)" items read as shipped and are not.
- **In-product copy:** `cli.ts:36` tells a reset user to "log in and change it" (no change-password); the group role chip says "Mod" where the API and every benchmark say "Admin" (`ChatInfoSheet.tsx:858`); the story composer says "Friends see it" when the audience is connections plus everyone you ever DMed (`StoriesStrip.tsx:206`); the DM composer placeholder is "Message… try @" with nobody to mention; the feed end card says "You're all caught up — nothing new from your people" to an account with zero connections (`FeedView.tsx:337`); Connections says "No more requests" to a user who never had one (`connections/page.tsx:205`); Help & about has no about — "Bored Chat · self-hosted" (`help/page.tsx:222`), no version, operator, terms or retention line; Devices & security says "Passkeys are bound to this server's domain (localhost)" and "generated 3:58" with no date; the login page says "Settings → Security" for a page titled "Devices & security" (`login/page.tsx:464`); the register heading is "Join the fun".

## 5. Feed & posts, honestly

**What a post card is today.** From `phone-feed-list.png` and the CSS that produces it: a 38px avatar; a 14px bold name that is plain text (only the avatar links to the profile); a 12px muted meta line "@handle · 3:47 · public" where the clock is absolute, has no am/pm and turns into "Oct 18" tomorrow; a 15px body rendered as inert pre-wrap text with no links, mentions or hashtags; media as a fixed 2×2 collage, every image `max-height: 340px; object-fit: cover`, so a single portrait is cropped; a poll as a 17px serif question over 13.5px option rows that already show counts before you vote; a reaction row whose only control when nobody has reacted is an 18px ghost smiley in a 36px button, with the 6-emoji quick row while comments get the full stamp picker; up to three 13.5px comment bubbles; and then a complete comment form — image button, "Say something back…" pill, Reply — under every single card. For a one-line text post with no comments the card sums to about 190px of which about 90px is chrome. Above the first post sit 124px of header: a 24px serif wordmark where a title should be, the grid/list pill, two boxed Friends/Discover lane buttons and a 40px outlined refresh square. The grid is the default for the Friends lane: three columns of 118px tiles where the poll tile has no author and the text tile shows the author at 10px, which puts a friends feed in direct conflict with the product's own faces-not-counters rule; with two posts the third cell is empty and the end card floats with roughly 47% of the frame blank beneath it. Instagram grids only a profile or Explore; Threads, X, Facebook and BeReal never grid a friends feed, all of them use one meta line and one action row, and all of them give every post a URL.

**The composer and the templates.** The kind picker (Post / Poll / Challenge / Join my…) sits above the textarea so the screen reads as a form before you can see where to type; "Join my…" truncates at 390px; a dashed 86×86 add tile is always visible; with the place row, the audience row, a policy sentence that repeats on every open at 12px in `--faint` (2.13:1 on white) and the Post button, the empty composer runs about 820 of the 844px frame; the disabled Post is the ink gradient at 55% opacity and reads as the darkest, most button-like thing on the card. Of the four templates, text and photo are the basics done plainly (≤2000 characters, up to four images, crop and rotate, HEIC conversion, a 10-per-hour rate limit); poll is shipped but renders its question twice and leaks tallies before the vote; the open game challenge (first claim wins, one winner under concurrency) and the join card (a live beacon or listing cross-posted with a Join button and server-enforced audience nesting) are the app's own and have no benchmark equivalent. What the composer cannot make: a link card (the chat unfurler exists; the feed never imports it — on the roadmap), a video post that works end to end (pending renders as a broken image, GIFs do not animate, the button says "Add images"), a carousel (static grid, single-`src` viewer), an event (the join card is a proxy for a beacon you must light first; there is no `event` kind), an edit (no `PATCH /api/posts`, no `edited_at`), a bookmark (no `saved_posts`), a threaded reply (`post_comments` has no `parent_id`), a permalink (no `/feed/[id]`; friends-only posts have no URL of any kind), a draft, an @mention, a hashtag, a schedule, an audience beyond Connections/Public, or a post with comments turned off.

**Comments and safety.** The inline box is a single-line input where Enter submits (the server allows newlines); comments carry no timestamp although `created_at` is on the DTO; commenter names are plain text; under your own post every friend's reply carries a bare "×" with a ~21px hit box that hard-deletes on one tap with no confirm or undo; tapping the reactor face chip toggles your own reaction instead of listing who reacted; the inline preview is the latest three with no "view replies" line, no count and no faces. Authors are never told anyone reacted or replied — the doctrine bans approval pings, but PSYCHOLOGY §6 explicitly sanctions a 2–3-per-day named-faces digest and none exists, so the rule is applied more strictly than its own brief. Safety is inverted: a logged-out stranger can report a public post from the share page, a logged-in member cannot report a post, a comment or a story from the feed, a stranger's post in Discover has no menu at all, and admin takedown cannot target a comment. Only `postcreate:` is rate-limited; comments, reactions and comment reactions are not. Delete and takedown publish no `post.deleted`, so other viewers keep a stale card and hit 404 on interaction.

**What to change first.** In order: (1) the safety pass — add `post`, `comment` and `story` to `ReportKindSchema`, `reports/routes.ts` and `ReportSheet.tsx`; render ⋯ on every card (own: Edit · Copy link · Revoke link · Delete; others: Report · Hide · Block); move comment delete behind long-press with a confirm; put `fixedWindow` on comments and reactions (1.5 days). (2) Wire plumbing the app already owns — `MediaLightbox` with an `items[]` pager for post and comment images, a pending-video slate, `BlurhashCanvas` under feed images, `URL_RE` plus `PreviewCard` on bodies, one relative time formatter wrapped in `<time>`, comment timestamps, avatar+name+handle as one profile link, a `capture` attribute, a character counter (1.5 days, no migration). (3) `/feed/[postId]` rendering the card plus the full thread, a `post.deleted` WS event from DELETE and takedown, and `GET /api/users/:username/posts` so the planned profile posts tab (#107) stops deriving itself from the first page of the friends feed (1 day). (4) Rebuild the card and composer — a 40px action row (React · Reply · Send · ⋯) that opens the existing grid-tile sheet as the thread view, Friends defaulting to list, poll dedupe and tally gating on `voted`, a two-option audience sheet, a Cancel button and local drafts, the add tile collapsed into a toolbar, close-and-toast on post (1.5 days). (5) `PATCH /api/posts/:id` with an "Edited" marker and the daily "Maya, Arjun and 2 others were glad you posted this" digest — named faces, no counts, no pings (1 day). Then send-into-chat via `ForwardSheet`, which needs a new message kind and audience-nesting enforcement. Two positions the whole audit takes once: a quiet count-free dot on the Feed tab when `post.new` or `story.new` arrives is user-pulled information, not a nudge, and should be built — one dot, not two; and `StoriesStrip` belongs at the top of Feed as well as Chats, because that is where every in-app pointer already sends people.

## 6. UI, honestly

Cross-cutting numbers, all from the UI section with their sources: the light theme is "not designed in the Royal pass — equivalents derived" (`globals.css:83`), 9 light-scheme blocks across 5 CSS files against roughly 16,000 lines of CSS; 0 `rem` units, so OS text scaling changes nothing; 27 distinct font sizes, 214 half-pixel declarations, 38 declarations at or under 11px; 23 distinct border radii; SVG stroke widths from 1.1 to 2.6 across 10 values with no shared icon module; 297 emoji characters in 74 `.tsx` files, many as chrome; 24 hard-coded Back links and 0 `router.back()`; 51 `aria-modal` sheets with 0 focus traps, no `inert`, no `popstate`; 15 native `window.confirm`/`alert` sites in 12 files; 0 `error.tsx`, `not-found.tsx` or `loading.tsx`; about 95 "Loading…" text states and no skeleton; 436 `aria-label`s, 24 `prefers-reduced-motion` blocks, 43 `env(safe-area-inset-*)` and 14 `100dvh` — the accessibility and phone-first plumbing is genuinely above the bar. Contrast: incoming bubble `#e8ecf5` on chat background `#eef0f6` = 1.04:1 light and 1.18:1 dark; tab labels 10.5px in `--inactive` = 2.85:1 light (`#8a93a6` on `#f4f6fb`) and 3.83:1 dark; `--faint` helper text 2.13:1 on white (10 usages); the tic-tac-toe "Your turn" cue is `--accent` on the outgoing bubble at 1.15:1; "0 votes" is `--muted` on the own bubble at 2.94:1; `--panel-2` chips on a white card 1.13:1; `--border` 1.24:1 on the page background and 1.34:1 on a white card; profile segment labels 2.86:1. The tab bar is mounted per page, not in the layout: only `/`, `/feed`, `/local`, `/people`, `/connections`, `/gallery` and `/settings/profile` carry it, so it vanishes on `/u/[username]`, `/new`, `/anon`, `/things`, `/puzzle`, `/welcome`, the `/settings` hub and every `/settings/*` sub-page. Status bar and manifest colour are pre-Royal `#0b0e14` against a `#12141c` body.

**Auth (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`, `phone-register.png`).** The best-executed screens in the app, and the ones with zero legal or trust signals: no consent line, no Terms or Privacy route, no age line, no "hosted by", under the heading "Join the fun". The only recovery door is a muted "Lost everything?" that accepts a code and never mentions the operator; the wordmark rotates Bored / Happy / Sad / Drunk every 2.6 s beside the password field; "Log in with phone · QR" renders at phone width; nothing says what the app is or that it is self-hosted with no ads; username rules, the 8-character minimum and availability surface only as post-submit errors; email is required, never verified, never used. From code, a new account meets up to five interruptions before a first message (recovery codes → `/welcome` → tour → push explainer → What's new). Fix: a consent sentence linking operator-editable `/terms` and `/privacy`, a 16+ attestation, "Hosted by <operator>", inline "Forgot password?" with the operator-contact fallback, a static wordmark, three lines of value proposition, helper text and a debounced availability check, hide the QR door on touch-only devices, one linear onboarding flow.

**Chats list (`phone-chats.png`, `desk-chats.png`, `phone-chats-more.png`, `phone-new.png`).** The magnifier is a link to Find people; there is no mute, no swipe, long-press or right-click, no pin, archive, mark-unread or filter chips; every letter avatar is the same tinted circle so "F", "A" and "M" are one person; group rows show the body with no "Arjun:" prefix; the stories strip reserves about 109–110px for a lone dashed "Your story" ring when nobody has a story; the unread badge pulses forever; the time column is "3:47" with no am/pm and no reserved column for empty rows; Rescue and the anonymous inbox live in the ⋯ overflow; the people picker shows "offline" under every contact where `@username` belongs. Fix: an inline field that filters loaded rows by title and snippet; a long-press/right-click sheet (Mute · Pin · Mark unread · Archive) and swipe actions; `hash(user_id) % 6` avatar tints; sender prefix in group snippets; a single-row chip when there are zero friend stories; one formatter; animate the badge once on change; one labelled Rescue pill on every tab root.

**Conversation (`phone-conversation.png`, `desk-conversation.png`, `chat-msg-longpress.png`, `phone-chat-plus-menu.png`, `desk-chat-plus-menu.png`).** Incoming messages are not bubbles in light mode (1.04:1); five consecutive bubbles each carry "03:47 PM" at 10.5px, a tail and ticks, with no day pill, no clustering, no unread divider and no jump-to-bottom; delivered and read ticks differ only by 55% versus 100% opacity while the list paints read in green; group senders share one colour and no avatar; the "+" grid is Photo, Doodle, Vibe check, Game, Puzzle, Location, Schedule — no Poll, Camera, File or Contact — and opens as a full-width bottom sheet across a 1280px desktop; long-press is touch-only; the menu has no Copy, Select or Star; delete is a native confirm with no "Delete for me"; the review nudge is wedged between scroll and composer; the typing indicator is its own row that shifts the layout; the placeholder says "Message… try @" in a 1:1 DM; Enter sends on phones; `navigator.vibrate` fires on page load. Fix: `--bubble-in #ffffff` with a 1px shadow (dark `#2a3042`) and an `--on-bubble-out` token pair; day pills, 5-minute same-sender clustering with stamp and tail on the last bubble, a 28px avatar on the first incoming bubble of a run; an "N unread messages" divider from `last_read_seq` and a floating ↓ pill; `onContextMenu` plus pointer-event long-press with Copy / Select / Save; a `ConfirmSheet` with for-me / for-everyone; Poll and Camera tiles and a 2-column desktop popover with paste and drop; typing in the header subline; newline on coarse pointers.

**Feed (`phone-feed.png`, `phone-feed-list.png`, `phone-feed-compose.png`, `desk-feed.png`, `feed-post-menu.png`, `feed-audience.png`).** Covered in §5; in one line each: no ⋯ on others' posts; a full comment form under every card; grid default for Friends; images open raw URLs; no permalink; no Send to a chat; the Audience chevron flips instead of opening a sheet; the poll question renders twice; reacting is an 18px ghost smiley; the end card has nothing to tap and is false for a zero-connection account; 124px of header chrome; an empty composer taller than the phone with a disabled Post that reads enabled; on desktop the header aligns to a 680px column and the feed to 560px so nothing lines up, inside a 1280px window with about 45% of it empty.

**Local (`phone-local.png`, `desk-local.png`, `phone-beacon-create.png`, `phone-beacon-detail.png`, `phone-listing-create.png`, `desk-beacon-create.png`).** The hierarchy is inverted: 22px section headings over a 17px page title, and the product's primary verb "+ I'm bored" is a 1.5px dashed outline in muted text — the same treatment as every empty slot. The SOS control is a 13px emoji inside a ~29px ghost icon button with no label, duplicated in the Chats overflow, absent from Feed, and rendered as a red block on Linux/Android. Four products stack vertically with no sub-navigation; the board starts below the fold at 844px and the seventh chip clips to "Pla…" with no edge fade; "Make me discoverable" is the most prominent CTA with no first-time explainer of what strangers see or that it lapses in an hour. The sentences that state the privacy promise ("Expires on its own. No one is notified when it fizzles.") are 12.5px in `--faint` at 2.13:1. Board rows have no container, thumbnail slot, posted time or excerpt; the "Plans" chip filters to nothing. The beacon sheet has no grabber, no detent and a disabled "Light it" that reads enabled, clipped at 1280×800; the listing sheet opens as a stub with Post in the header and loses six photos and 2,000 characters on an accidental swipe; the card kebab is 28×28 and "+ LIST SOMETHING" is 11.5px text with ~2px vertical padding. Fix: "I'm bored" as a full-width primary first; one labelled 44px Rescue pill on every tab root; a sticky segmented control (Plans · Bored now · Board) with search above the chips; a one-time consent sheet before the first opt-in; `--muted` at ≥13px for helper copy; listing cards with a placeholder tile, title, price chip and one meta line; sheet detents with a sticky footer and local drafts; 44px hit boxes.

**Profile, own and public (`phone-settings-profile.png`, `desk-settings-profile.png`, `phone-profile-arjun.png`, `desk-profile-arjun.png`, `phone-profile-options.png`, `phone-connections.png`, `phone-people.png`, `phone-gallery.png`).** Own tab and public page are two components with different content; the bio never renders on the own tab while the same two activities appear twice on one screen; the public header is a 128px gradient band with a beige blotch that reads as a rendering artifact, a hard edge mid-avatar, 36×36 grey orbs that look disabled, a permanent double ring that reads as a stuck focus ring, and an emoji orb beside two equal-weight pills where nothing is primary; tapping "✓ Friends" opens `window.confirm("Remove … as a friend?")`; no call buttons; "last seen 43m ago"; Back goes to Chats from everywhere; no tab bar. People management has four homes and three words (Friends / Connections / Your people); the Profile tab's chain-link icon reads as copy-link and "Edit profile" lands on a page titled "Account"; segment labels are 12px in `--inactive`; Gallery has three entry points on one screen; the initial-letter avatar has no "Add photo" affordance and there is no completion nudge; RECENTLY rows show a bare "3:47"; on desktop the profile is a phone layout centred in 1280px with the 72px rail absent. Fix: one `ProfileView` with `isMe` swapping only the action row; bio always under the name; drop the cover or build it from the user's own blurred avatar; translucent 44px nav circles; ring only on a live story; Message as the filled primary, "Friends ✓" as a status chip opening a sheet (Remove · Mute · Block); voice/video icons; `router.back()`; one People page (Requests · Your people · Suggestions) reachable from Profile and the Chats overflow; a labelled "Share profile" and gear; a dismissible "Finish your profile" checklist; tinted avatars; a horizontal profile header on desktop.

**Settings (`phone-settings.png`, `settings-account.png`, `settings-privacy.png`, `desk-settings-privacy.png`, `settings-notifications.png`, `settings-chats.png`, `settings-calls.png`, `settings-security.png`, `settings-help.png`).** No Change password, Change email, Delete account, Download my data or Sign out all devices anywhere. Booleans are "On"/"Off" word pills on Privacy, Enable/Disable buttons plus green "ON" words on Notifications, and Default/On/Off segments in the chat sheet — three vocabularies, and the grey "Off" pill is pixel-identical to action buttons like "Open" and "Regenerate"; multi-choice settings are eight native `<select>`s styled like text inputs, four of them identical; Privacy is a ~2,100px scroll of seven card sections. Five sub-pages use four layouts and three eyebrow systems (serif uppercase Marcellus, 12px/700 sans, chips), sometimes on one page. Two of seven categories are hollow (Chats is two cross-reference rows; Calls is a self-test plus "Call history is coming soon."). Settings are filed by where they were built: mood effects and default beacon audience under Privacy, name style and avatar motion between Bio and Save on Account, which is titled "Account" but is a long profile editor with three save models and no dirty guard. Notifications is one switch and three sound rows. Help & about has no about. Devices & security leaks "(localhost)", "generated 3:58", an always-visible passkey name field with zero passkeys, and "Authenticator codes OFF" + "Turn on"; the Devices page (not captured) prints a raw user-agent and IP. The root has no Appearance, Storage or Language row and Sign out is bare red text that logs out on tap and silently discards queued messages; the spotlight tour fires on top of the security page. Fix: an Account group with the four lifecycle rows in danger red at the bottom; one `<Switch>` and one `PickerRow` → radio sheet; `SettingsGroup` + `SettingsRow` with one `.eyebrow` token; fill Chats (theme, text size, Enter-to-send, media auto-download, archived) and Calls (history, ringtone, vibrate) or hide them; a new Appearance page (System/Dark/Light, text size, motion, name style); rename the profile editor "Edit profile" with one Done and "Discard changes?"; an About block fed from admin settings; a confirm sheet on Sign out.

**Sheets and menus (`chat-info.png`, `phone-beacon-create.png`, `phone-story-add.png`, `phone-listing-create.png`, `phone-chats-more.png`, `phone-profile-options.png`).** The DM "Chat settings" sheet opens on nine unlabelled theme tiles and seven wallpapers that fill two-thirds of the first viewport, repeats "ONLY YOU SEE THIS" four times, never shows who the chat is with, and has no Mute, Block, Report, Media, Search or Clear chat; disappearing messages sits below the wallpapers. Fifteen native dialogs guard leaving a chat, blocking, deleting a post, closing a listing, taking down a beacon, removing a friend and revealing an anonymous identity. Tall sheets have no grabber, no detents and no sticky footer, so the primary CTA sits at the bottom of an internal scroll; disabled primaries ("Light it", "Post story", "Send to the operator", "Continue") are the ink gradient at 55% and look enabled; 51 `aria-modal` sheets have no focus trap, no return focus and no history integration, so Tab escapes behind every backdrop and Android Back leaves the page; destructive items in ⋯ menus are not red; all 25 `*Sheet.tsx` components hand-roll `.sheet`. Fix: one `Sheet` primitive with grabber, detents, sticky footer and `useFocusTrap` plus `pushState`/`popstate`; one `ConfirmSheet` replacing all 15 sites; the chat sheet reordered to identity hero → Mute · Search · Media · Disappearing · Pinned → Appearance collapsed → Clear · Block · Report · Leave; disabled primaries at 40% with a helper line.

**Desktop (`desk-chats.png`, `desk-conversation.png`, `desk-feed.png`, `desk-local.png`, `desk-people.png`, `desk-settings-profile.png`, `desk-settings-privacy.png`, `desk-profile-arjun.png`, `desk-things.png`).** Only Chats uses the width; Feed, Local, People and Profile are a 680px column centred in 1280px with no second pane; the rail disappears on `/settings/*`, `/u/[username]` and `/things` and has no Settings entry; settings sub-pages are full-page navigations in a 640px column; there are zero keyboard shortcuts, no right-click, no paste or drop, and the chat "+" opens a full-width bottom sheet; the idle pane and the empty list share one heading; the beacon modal clips its CTA at 800px tall. Fix: mount the rail in the authed layout with avatar and gear at the bottom; a second pane at ≥1100px for Feed (opened post) and Local (beacon/listing detail); `.page` max-width 760–840px; two-pane Settings at ≥900px; `onContextMenu`, `onPaste`, `onDrop`, a 2-column popover for "+", and a handful of global shortcuts.

## 7. What NOT to copy

One position per pattern, stated once for the whole audit.

- **Public numeric counts (likes, followers, views, poll voters, channel subscribers).** Banned by PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; honoured end to end — no `*_count` for likes, followers or reactions in any DTO, faces instead. Right, and the product's clearest differentiator. Two leaks violate it today: `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` ride `UserPublic` to every viewer under a caption that says only the owner sees them, and the group header renders a member count that the docs say is off every surface. Emoji reaction chips on chat bubbles also show a number, not faces.
- **Infinite scroll, auto-advancing stories, prefetch-next feeds.** Banned; lanes are finite with an end card and stories end on "That's all from X". Right. The end card needs a next step (light a beacon, see Discover, find people), not more feed. Pull-to-refresh is swept into the same ban; it is a gesture, not a reward loop, and on a finite chronological feed it buys nothing either way — leave it, make the manual refresh a "New posts" pill, and do not spend time arguing.
- **Seen-at timestamps, view order, "active now" pings, minute-precise last-seen.** Banned; `story_views` has no timestamp column and no receipt payload carries one. Right, and structurally enforced where it was built. The app breaks its own rule on the profile hero ("last seen 43m ago", default Everyone) and with an online dot that cannot be turned off. Coarsen to "active today / this week" and ship appear-offline.
- **Streaks, loss-framed warnings, match-expiry countdowns, "your turn" reply-owed labels.** Banned; rituals pause instead of nagging. Right without reservation. The one hook still shipping is the unread badge that pulses forever — still it.
- **Leaderboards, best-friends rankings, top-contributor and achievement badges.** Banned. Right. Non-ranked role and tenure badges (operator, moderator, "new here", joined date) are not ranking and should not be swept up; they are also the only impersonation defence a small server has.
- **Algorithmic ranking, For You feeds, trending, boosts, paid placement, compatibility scores.** Banned; every list is `created_at DESC` or evidence-ordered. Right. One consequence to plan for: the 50-card newest-first discovery deck needs a non-algorithmic shuffle once more than 50 people are opted in at once, or the oldest opt-ins are never seen.
- **Re-engagement pushes: teasers, "what you missed", "someone liked you", "is typing", story pushes, marketing.** Banned; there is no re-engagement job. Right — this is the single biggest thing separating the product from Tinder, Hinge and X. The cost is that the sanctioned alternative, a 2–3-per-day named-faces digest, was never built, so reactions and comments currently reach nobody who is off-device, and `API.md §1.13`'s "no all-off switch" protects a digest that does not exist. Build the digest or delete the sentence.
- **Lead-photo ordering (Smart Photos, Best Photo, Top Photo).** Not written down anywhere; strip order is owner-chosen. It reorders by a hidden approval score — ban it, and add it to PSYCHOLOGY's never-build list so a later contributor does not add it as a helpful default.
- **Memories / On this day.** The app-fired nostalgia resurfacing is banned under rule 4 and that is right. A user-pulled past recap (PSYCHOLOGY §8 asks for one) is allowed, unbuilt, and blocked on a real story archive — the sweep keeps the pixels and throws away caption, date, overlays and viewers.
- **Also right, with a note each.** Repost/quote-post: the virality engine; the real need is "send this into a chat", which is missing. Swipe-to-reject with stamps: right; a plain swipe to advance a card would be harmless. Payments, ads, paid verification, social login, third-party GIF search, licensed music: right, and the no-ads/no-sale/self-hosted story is unused as a trust claim on login, register and Help. Map embeds: "no tile keys" is a no-SaaS rule, not a product rule — self-hosted OSM tiles are compatible and rides and rooms are spatial objects; "no map at all" reads rule 7 too broadly. Operator private-chat monitor: right, as long as the legal pages say it is a route-level promise and not encryption. Age and gender profile fields: right for profiles and filters; wrong if read as a ban on a non-stored age attestation at signup.

## 8. Roadmap: basic → standard → differentiating

Sizes are the sections' own estimates (afternoon ≈ half a day). Files are named where the sections name them. Everything in the basic block should land before any new novelty feature.

### Basic — the floor (about four engineer-weeks)

1. **Legal and consent floor** — `/terms`, `/privacy`, `/guidelines` rendered from operator-editable markdown with a default that states what is stored (email, IPs for 90 days, messages, media), that no admin route reads chats but chats are not encrypted and the operator holds the database; an operator contact field in `InstanceSettingsSchema` (`packages/shared/src/api.ts:2893-2897`); a consent sentence and a 16+ checkbox in `RegisterRequestSchema` (`api.ts:783-795`) and `apps/web/app/register/page.tsx`; "Hosted by <operator>" on auth pages; an About block in `settings/help/page.tsx`. **1 day.**
2. **Account lifecycle** — `POST /api/auth/password`, `PATCH /api/users/me/email` (password re-proof, `email_verified_at = NULL`), `DELETE /api/users/me` (re-auth, 7–14-day grace, cancel on login, then tombstone and cascade), `POST /api/auth/logout-all` reusing `revokeAllSessions`; an admin delete-user action; migration `0013`; rename `/settings/account` to "Edit profile" and build a real Account page; fix `cli.ts:36`. Files: `apps/server/src/modules/auth/routes.ts`, `users/routes.ts`, `apps/web/app/settings/account/page.tsx`. **2 days.**
3. **Login that tells the truth** — branch on 403 `account_suspended`/`account_banned` with the operator contact as the appeal path; `?reason=` banners on every bounce; surface `Retry-After` in `ApiError`; inline "Forgot password?"; hide the QR door on touch-only devices; static wordmark. Files: `login/page.tsx:148-154, :685`, `lib/api.ts:5-13`, `AuthScene.tsx`. **Afternoon.**
4. **A sign-out that signs out** — clear `messages`, `conv_meta` and `outbox` stores, call `disablePush()`, add `DELETE /api/push/subscriptions` and a `session_id` on `push_subscriptions` so remote revoke kills that browser's push; confirm sheet that mentions unsent messages. Files: `lib/connection.ts:117-125`, `lib/db.ts:33-37`, `push/routes.ts`, `schema.ts:675-678`. **1 day.**
5. **Mute end to end** — `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/mute { until }` writing the existing `muted_until`; Mute row at the top of `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`; muted bell and greyed badge in `ConversationListView.tsx`; endpoint test beside `test/v05.integration.test.ts`; `API.md §1.4`; then correct FEATURES #9. **1 day.**
6. **Chat rendering pass** — day pills, 5-minute same-sender clustering, "N unread messages" divider from `last_read_seq`, floating ↓ pill, one time formatter in `lib/format.ts`, `--bubble-in`/`--on-bubble-out` tokens, group snippet prefix, typing in the header subline, review nudge inside the scroll. Files: `components/ChatView.tsx:780`, `chat.module.css`, `globals.css`. **2 days.**
7. **Long-press menu and `ConfirmSheet`** — Copy, Delete for me (a `message_hides` table keyed `(conversation_id, seq, user_id)`, filtered in list and sync), Share via `navigator.share`, `onContextMenu`, pointer-event long-press; one `ConfirmSheet` replacing all 15 native sites (`ChatView.tsx:1334, :435` and the twelve files listed in the UI section) plus Sign out; then "Delete / clear a DM" as a per-user hide of the row. **1 day.**
8. **Wire the server halves plus drafts** — Poll tile and Camera tile (`capture`) in `Composer.tsx:519-528`; group rename from the hero via the existing `PATCH title`; mount `ChannelMembersSheet` for groups so admins can remove and promote; sole-admin guard server-side; `title_changed`/`role_changed` system messages; "Mod" → "Admin"; drafts per conversation in `lib/db.ts` with a "Draft" label on the row; Enter = newline on coarse pointers. **1 day.**
9. **Chat-list management** — migration `0013` adding `pinned_at`/`archived_at` to participants; `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/participant`; an inline filter field replacing the `/people` link at `ConversationListView.tsx:81-84`; long-press/right-click row sheet (Mute · Pin · Mark unread · Archive); pinned section. **1 day.**
10. **Feed safety and plumbing** — items (1) and (2) of §5: report kinds, ⋯ on every card, comment delete behind long-press, comment/reaction rate limits; `MediaLightbox` pager, pending-video slate, blurhash, linkify + unfurl, relative time, comment timestamps, name links, counter. Files: `api.ts:1288`, `reports/routes.ts`, `ReportSheet.tsx`, `FeedView.tsx:975, :1013, :1674`, `MediaLightbox.tsx:16-27`, `feed/routes.ts:566, :497`. **3 days.**
11. **Theme and token pass** — design the light palette (bubble-in `#ffffff` + shadow, panel-2 ≥ `#e6eaf3`, border `#cfd6e4`); delete the hard-coded `#3a4052` and `.warn-text`; `data-theme` on `<html>` with Dark/Light/System under a new `settings/appearance`; `html { font-size: 100% }` and a rem ramp with a 12px floor; inactive labels to `--muted` and `.tab` to 11–12px; disabled primaries at 40%; `themeColor` and `manifest.json` to `#12141c`; re-shoot every screen in light. Files: `globals.css:83`, `app/layout.tsx:17-20`, `social.module.css:29-30`. **2 days.**
12. **Shell** — mount `Tabs` in the authed layout and hide it only inside `/c/[id]`, viewers and calls; `router.back()` with a logical-parent fallback for the 24 hard Back links; `::before { inset: -8px }` on `.iconBtn`, `.orbBtn`, `.cardKebab`, `.layoutBtn` and 36–40px pills for the three text CTAs; rewrite `TOUR_STEPS` (`constants.ts:1012-1030`) to the four real tabs and start the tour only on the Chats root; one labelled Rescue pill on every tab root; a shared `<Icon>` module as the first step of retiring emoji chrome. Files: `AppShell.tsx`, `GlobalOverlays.tsx`. **1 day.**
13. **Platform honesty** — versioned SW cache name from `GIT_COMMIT` plus a "Reload to update" toast on `controllerchange` (`sw.js:13`, `ServiceWorkerRegister.tsx`); `/feed/[postId]`, `/local/[listingId]`, `/local/b/[beaconId]` and a fixed `SharePageView.deepLink()` (`:123`); the 15 dead `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` hrefs and the Stories pointers; `error.tsx`, `not-found.tsx`, `loading.tsx` with a shimmer row reused by Chats, Feed and Board; `Wordmark` on the `AppShell.tsx:32` boot gate; Caddy `handle /api/healthz`; Referrer-Policy, Permissions-Policy, nosniff; short commit hash in Help and on tickets; seed release notes for v1.0a, #103 and v1.1 Royal; `robots.txt`. **1.5 days.**
14. **Stories shipping bugs** — let the author through at `stories/routes.ts:366`; mount `StoriesStrip` in `FeedView`; gate Post on `attachment.ready`; `<video muted>` with tap-to-unmute; route delete through the undo pill; `story.deleted` WS frame from `hardDeleteStory`; the three one-line block and ban filters; "Friends see it" → honest copy; mark #97 shipped. **1 day.**
15. **Local and profile quick fixes** — a `listing` report target (`ReportSheet.tsx:22-26`, `reports.target_listing_id`, admin page); `LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE` on `SharePageView.tsx:245-265`; strip `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` for non-owners in `userPublicDto`; import `maybeConvertImage` in `settings/account/page.tsx:171`; delete the dead "Plans" chip; board search over body, tags and legs. **Half day.**
16. **Make push trustworthy** — `pushsubscriptionchange` in `sw.js`, re-POST on boot, `GET /api/push/subscriptions`, `POST /api/push/test` with a button, TTL on beacon and reminder pushes, `renotify` on message notifications. Files: `public/sw.js`, `jobs/push.ts:107-127`, `settings/notifications/page.tsx`. **1 day.**

### Standard — parity with the benchmarks (about seven engineer-weeks)

1. **Who-can-message plus a requests inbox** — `users.dm_policy` (everyone / connections / nobody), enforced at `POST /api/conversations {dm}` (`conversations/routes.ts:153-163`) and call creation, 404-shaped like blocks; `conversation_participants.accepted_at` so a stranger's first DM parks in Requests with Accept / Delete / Block / Report, no receipts, ring or typing before acceptance; a stranger banner in the chat; match and roulette DMs exempt; `MESSAGE_RATE_PER_MINUTE` on the message POST. **2 days.**
2. **Reporting that can be triaged** — a `reason` enum (spam, harassment, hate, nudity, self-harm, scam, impersonation, underage, off_platform, ip, other) on reports; "Also block @user" on `ReportSheet`; `GET /api/reports/mine` with received/reviewed states; admin lanes by reason; a `legal` support category; a formal "warn" outcome delivered through `sendSupportMessage`; takedown of messages, comments and stories by reported id, never by browsing. **1 day.**
3. **Age gate enforcement and stranger safety copy** — `users.age_attested_at`; `PUT /api/discovery/optin` and roulette join refuse accounts without it; a one-time consent sheet before the first "Make me discoverable"; `LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE` reused on the opt-in sheet, match overlay, roulette banner and beacon detail; "End chat" for DMs in the chat sheet; the discovery card's block through the house sheet. **1 day.**
4. **Private account, mute-a-person, discoverability** — `users.profile_visibility` and a hidden-authors list enforced in feed, stories and tray queries; opt-out of name search and suggestions; who-can-send-requests; `PickerRow`s on Privacy. **1.5 days.**
5. **One `ProfileView` and real profile content** — `GET /api/users/:username/posts` with the feed's audience filter; one component for `/settings/profile` and `/u/[username]` with `isMe` swapping the action row; bio always rendered; Posts/Pics segments; `created_at` → "Joined Oct 2026"; pronouns, links, verified chip from an operator-granted flag, mutual faces from the suggestions query; `/u/` links in `StoriesStrip`, `StoryViewer`, `SeenBySheet`, `ChannelMembersSheet`, `PollView`, `UserSearch`; a dirty flag and "Discard changes?" on the editor; a reason picker on `ReportSheet`. **3 days.**
6. **Feed parity** — items (3), (4) and (5) of §5: permalink and `post.deleted`, card and composer rebuild, `PATCH /api/posts/:id` with an Edited marker, the named-faces daily digest; then bookmarks (`saved_posts`), threaded replies (`parent_id`), @mentions on posts and comments, "See more" truncation, per-post comments-off, an author-set sensitive flag with blur, and send-into-chat via `ForwardSheet` with audience nesting. **4 days.**
7. **Notification controls** — a `notify` map in `UserSettings` keyed by user-visible trigger (messages, calls, mentions, plans, requests, waves, anon, support, comments) checked at enqueue; `message_preview: full | name | none` respected in `jobs/push.ts:419-427`; Wave nudges moved out of Privacy; one `<Switch>`; `navigator.setAppBadge` and a `document.title` prefix; the Feed-tab dot on `post.new`/`story.new`; comment-on-my-post as a push trigger writing an `activity` row; a bell sheet off the Chats topbar so kudos, matches and plan changes stop evaporating; timed pause (DND); mentions-only mode for groups; a new-device login notice through the existing Support channel. **2 days.**
8. **Close the group join loop and give admins tools** — auth-aware `SharePageView` and join page (Join / Request to join calling `joinGroup()`); a rendered "Requested" state with withdraw; a `group.join_request` frame and push to admins with a `pending_requests` count on the DTO; group avatar, description and size on the invite page; invite list and revoke via the existing routes; invite QR from `QrCode.tsx`; a ban list checked on invite accept; admin delete-any-message in groups; transfer ownership and delete group; description visible to members; sender name in group push bodies. **2 days.**
9. **Calls that leave a trace** — `call_ended` (with `duration_s`) and `call_failed` system messages beside the existing `call_missed`, rendered next to the Call back pill; `GET /api/calls` and a `/calls` page with All/Missed (the planned #102), deleting the "coming soon" line; faces on the Join chip from `participant_ids`; a live-call chip on list rows; a `call.media {mic, cam}` frame for the remote mute indicator; a busy reject reason; a Silence button on the incoming ring; a service-worker notification on `call.invite` when `document.hidden`; Screen Wake Lock; who-can-call following `dm_policy`. Files: `calls/service.ts`, `calls/routes.ts:271`, `CallOverlay.tsx`, `webrtc.ts`. **2 days.**
10. **Stop shipping full-size media** — canvas downscale to a 2048px long edge in `imageUpload.ts` behind a per-send "Send original"; a max-dimension cap in `pipeline.ts` next to the thumb resize; one ffmpeg 1080p H.264 leg in `jobs/media.ts` for anything above 1080p; XHR uploads with progress, cancel and retry; Upload quality and Data saver on the empty Settings → Chats page; a per-chat Media / Links / Files tab; 2–4 chat attachments as an album grid; paste and drop on desktop; voice-note lock and listen-back. **2.5 days.**
11. **Stories that can be interacted with** — a viewer footer for other people's stories (header link to `/u/`, Reply opening the DM as the #99 seam, ⋯ with Report, Mute stories, Block); text-only stories rendered client-side to a 1080×1920 PNG; `capture` on the input; a full-frame preview with a 9:16 preset; accept `video/quicktime` and transcode HEVC; a 60 s story duration cap; swipe gestures; posted-ago in the header; pause on `visibilitychange`; blurhash and next-segment warm-up; an archive that keeps caption, date, overlays and viewers; view-receipts honouring `read_receipts`. **3 days.**
12. **A board people will use** — `sell` (numeric price, currency, Free flag, condition, close reason) and `lost_found` kinds in `LISTING_KINDS`, the Zod details schemas, the create/edit sheets and the chips; `POST /:id/renew`; a close reason for every kind so contacters see "Sold" instead of a 410; a `direction` on rooms; posted time, excerpt and location on `ListingCard` with a placeholder tile; sort and filters by price, date and departure; bookmark; drafts in localStorage per kind; reorder photos; reply/edit/report on reviews; a "who asked" view for all kinds; "Pick a time" on the beacon create sheet exposing the datetime pickers the edit sheet has; `BEACON_MAX_HORIZON_S` to 45 days; an optional title; an independent `max_joiners` (410 `beacon_full`); `GET /api/beacons/:id/calendar.ics` with an "Add to calendar" row; invite specific people to a plan; an `areas` table seeded from existing values with autocomplete and case folding; then fix FEATURES #41, #42, SPECS §79d and PSYCHOLOGY §5. **5 days.**
13. **Operator controls** — `registration_mode: open | invite_only | closed` in `InstanceSettingsSchema` enforced in `POST /api/auth/register` and surfaced on the admin settings page; an admin release-notes textarea; api/web/worker container healthchecks backed by `/healthz`; `./bored backup` either includes MinIO or prints "DATABASE ONLY" in red; `plans_completed` and `dm_started` counters in admin metrics so the north-star is a number on a screen; a durable moderation log table instead of the Redis ring; a rate-limited `POST /api/client-log` from the error boundary; null `reports.reporter_ip` in the retention sweep; a report-to-suspend shortcut in the queue. **1.5 days.**
14. **Quiet security gaps in the good half** — step-up re-auth for passkey add/delete and TOTP enable; per-account lockout; a dummy argon2 verify when the user is not found; a reserved-username list (`admin`, `support`, `boredchat`, `moderator`, `help`, `security`); require UV on passkey assertion for credentials that reported it; `needsRehash` on login; credential-change notices; a real Deny on the login-approval sheet; app lock (local PIN or WebAuthn re-auth with an idle timer); parsed device names on the Devices page. **1.5 days.**
15. **Growth plumbing the ethos allows** — `navigator.share` with clipboard fallback at the five `copyShareLink` callers (the SPECS #110 share audit); `generateMetadata` on `/u/[username]` with a logged-out hero and "Join to see more"; profile share link plus QR and "Copy @username" (planned #110, `QrCode.tsx` exists); an install prompt (`beforeinstallprompt` on Chromium, an iOS-aware "add to Home Screen" card after the first conversation); `sms:` and `mailto:` buttons in `InviteSheet`; always render the invite card on `/welcome` plus an interests chip-grid step; branch the feed end card on connection count; and **contacts import** — this audit's single position: a P1 growth gap, not a privacy virtue, implementable with no third party as client-side salted hashes matched against `users.email` (and a phone column if one ever exists), both sides opted in, shipped with a "Delete uploaded contacts" control; the UI section's "keep it missing" line is superseded here. **3 days.**
16. **Settings and desktop structure** — `SettingsGroup`/`SettingsRow`, one eyebrow token, the Appearance page, Account as identity + credentials only, two-pane Settings at ≥900px, the rail on every desktop route with a Settings entry, a second pane for Feed and Local at ≥1100px, `.page` max-width 760–840px, a `Sheet` primitive with grabber, detents, sticky footer, focus trap and history integration, migrate the 25 sheets, a small-phone (360px) pass. **3 days.**

### Differentiating — where the product is already ahead, finished properly

1. **A beacon inside an existing group** — `conversation_id` on `BeaconCreateSheet` so a plan can be raised in "HSR Layout runners" instead of only spawning a new group; co-hosts with edit rights; host approval of joiners; pre-spark questions; guest-list visibility (everyone / joiners / host); a ritual paused state with a resume control; monthly rituals; a ride card linked to a sparked plan. The one feature that makes "local community board" true.
2. **The digest and the activity surface** — the 2–3-per-day named-faces digest PSYCHOLOGY §6 promised (reactions, comments, kudos, plan changes, requests) on the `activity` table from the standard block, with a durable kudos record; this is also what makes `API.md §1.13` true.
3. **Story audiences and replies** (#98, #99) — Everyone / Connections / Close friends / Hide-from; reply into the DM quoting the story; quick reactions surfaced to the author as faces; then the canvas before publish (drag, resize, rotate, eraser, undo) and a Highlights section fed by the real archive.
4. **Places and recommendations** — a `places` entity so two reviews of the same cafe become one page; "Priya and 3 friends recommend" with named recommenders and no stars; an area-scoped feed lane and an "ask for recommendations" post kind so "good plumber?" has a home; a pull-only notices kind; self-hosted OSM tiles for rides and rooms.
5. **Discovery card worth waving on** — `media` on `DiscoveryCardSchema`, a profile link from the card (FEATURES #106), a server-side remembered Pass with undo, an interests picker at onboarding with case-folded matching and a canonical tag list, mutual faces and "member since" on profiles, share-my-plan with a trusted contact, a post-meet check-in after beacons.
6. **Calls, media and co-watch** — audio-to-video upgrade with consent (#104), screen share via `getDisplayMedia` + `replaceTrack`, a pre-join lobby, co-watch of self-hosted uploaded MP4 so the YouTube iframe stops being the one third-party runtime script in a "no third-party SaaS" product (or amend rule 7 to name the exception), listen-together, and the LiveKit SFU when mesh ≤8 becomes the limit.
7. **Search** (#108) — people, public groups, listings and messages, with in-chat search widened beyond "my own messages"; public group directory by area and interest with a `conversations.area` tag.
8. **Email, then the rest of auth** — a mailer unlocks verification, forgot-password, magic link and email OTP that are already designed (SPECS 175-180) and whose admin toggles already persist; then E2EE for DMs (backlog) so "the operator cannot read chats" stops being a promise and becomes a property; i18n before the component count grows further.

## 9. Method

Seventeen benchmark checklists (about 4,700 features across basic, standard and advanced tiers, the events trio as one) were drawn up, then thirteen code inventories of this repository were written against them, one per area, followed by a per-area gap analysis (has / half-built / missing / planned / deliberately not built) and two adversarial verifiers per area that re-grepped every claim and reclassified rows; docs claims were treated as unverified until code was found. A seven-lens screenshot review (hierarchy, affordance, contrast, copy, consistency, touch, platform) ran over Playwright captures at 390×844 and 1280×800 in the light scheme, after which a completeness critic read the whole document and every section was revised against its notes (duplicates removed, denominators restated, priorities re-set, one position per cross-section topic). Not captured, so judged from code only: the story viewer, Seen-by sheet and collaborative canvas, the call overlay, incoming ring, pill and end card, the discovery card stack, opt-in sheet, match overlay and roulette banner, the admin console, the group chat-settings sheet, `/welcome`, the recovery-codes wall and the Devices page; 360×740 was not captured. The app was exercised locally against Postgres and Redis without MinIO, TURN or VAPID keys, so media upload, relayed calls and real push delivery were read from code and tests rather than observed.


---

## Accounts, sign-in and the account lifecycle

The headline is the missing lifecycle, not the polished sign-in. A member of this app cannot change their password, change or verify their email, change their username, delete or pause their account, sign out everywhere, export their data, or read a Terms or Privacy page, and the Account page advertises this with a disabled username field and an unlabelled read-only email. The sign-in half (the v0.9 auth pack: passkeys with conditional UI, QR + typed-code cross-device login, hand-rolled TOTP, recovery codes issued to every account, revocation that closes live sockets) is well designed, but it is design spent on a door with no house behind it. Counting the tables below (has ÷ has + half-built + missing + planned, banned rows excluded): **13 of 29 basic-tier features are fully present (45%)** and **18 of 65 standard-tier features (28%)**, because every mainstream app's basic expectations are dominated by lifecycle operations the app has never specced. Three concrete bugs compound it: logout leaves cached message bodies in IndexedDB on shared devices, suspended users get a misleading "check your connection" error with no appeal path, and the CLI tells users to change a password the product cannot change. The P0 list is short and needs no mailer: change password, delete account, clear IndexedDB on logout, a Terms/Privacy page and an age checkbox. Email verification and forgot-password should land the moment an SMTP sender ships; the admin page already stores the config for it.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Email + password signup with username and display name | basic | Nextdoor, Reddit, Discord, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Meetup | 201 + cookie + invite auto-accept + recovery codes in one call (`auth/routes.ts:27-94`). Registration is rate-limited 3/hour/IP (`routes.ts:28`). |
| Unique username chosen at signup with format rules and taken-check | basic | Instagram, Discord, BeReal, Telegram, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Reddit, Signal | `USERNAME_REGEX` 3–24 `[a-z0-9_]` (`constants.ts:73`); 23505 unique-violation race fallback → 409 `username_taken`; citext columns make it case-insensitive. |
| Display name separate from username, editable any time | basic | Discord, Snapchat, Reddit, TikTok, Signal, Instagram, Nextdoor | `UpdateMeRequestSchema.display_name`; no cooldown, which matches Snapchat. |
| Sign up through an invite link and land in the inviter's DM/group | standard | Nextdoor, Partiful, Discord, WhatsApp group links, Telegram | A bad invite never fails registration (`routes.ts:76-88`). |
| Signup / login rate limiting per IP | standard | WhatsApp, BeReal, Signal, Telegram | Register 3/h, login 10/min, recovery 5/h, passkey 10/min, TOTP 5/min, all keyed by IP (`routes.ts:28,99`; `constants.ts:1282-1320`). Per-IP only; see the per-account lockout item under Half-built. |
| Permission priming / onboarding explainer screens (push explainer, welcome step, spotlight tour) | basic | BeReal, Hinge, Instagram | The components exist, but a new account meets up to five interruptions before a first message: register → `RecoveryCodes` (`register/page.tsx:151`) → `/welcome` → `SpotlightTour` + `PushExplainer` (`GlobalOverlays.tsx:40-43`, mounted by AppShell on every route) → `WhatsNewCard` (`ConversationListView.tsx:119`). Code reading; only the tour-on-security-page part is screenshot-verified (see How it looks). |
| Password login with username OR email as the identifier | basic | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Meetup | `routes.ts:98-123`. Phone is absent (no phone identity exists). |
| Passkey (WebAuthn) passwordless login: platform authenticators, security keys, discoverable credentials | standard | WhatsApp, Snapchat, TikTok, Facebook, Luma, Bumble, Instagram, X, Reddit, Discord, Threads | ADR-021. Tinder, Hinge, Nextdoor and Telegram have none. UV is not required (see Half-built). |
| Passkey conditional UI (browser autofill from the username field) | advanced | TikTok, Facebook, Instagram | `autocomplete="username webauthn"` on the login field. |

One copy bug: `login/page.tsx:464` says "Settings → Security" while the page is titled "Devices & security". |
| Typeable short code fallback when QR cannot be scanned | standard | WhatsApp (phone-number code), TikTok (TV code) | `settings/security/page.tsx:832` "Approve a laptop"; approve-only capability split. |
| Saved login / stay signed in (persistent session, OS keychain autofill) | basic | Instagram, Threads, TikTok, Snapchat, Facebook | 30-day sliding session (`service.ts:20 SESSION_TTL_MS`). No opt-out (see Missing). |
| `?next` deep-link return after login/register (same-site only) | basic | all web apps | `safeNext` (`lib/api.ts:69`). |
| Password minimum length | basic | all password apps | `PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH` 8 (`constants.ts:74`). The only password rule; see the strength item under Half-built. |
| Show/hide password toggle | basic | all | The mascot covers its eyes. |
| Two-factor via authenticator app (TOTP) with QR + manual key setup | standard | Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, WhatsApp (PIN) | Hand-rolled RFC 6238 in `lib/totp.ts`, tested against published vectors; `totp.ts:65-117`. |
| Backup / recovery codes: generated once, count shown, copy + download, regenerate with password re-proof | standard | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Telegram | Six codes (`RECOVERY_CODES_COUNT`, `constants.ts:1304`) issued at signup to every account; regeneration re-proves the password (`recovery.ts:114-131`). |
| Low-remaining-codes warning and "no codes yet → Generate" for older accounts | standard | Snapchat, Facebook | `settings/security/page.tsx:791-809`. |
