Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge and BeReal have no list at all. The list itself is raw (see Half-built). |
| Device count surfaced on the Settings index | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram | `settings/page.tsx:108-139`. |
| Same account on web, phone and desktop at once, with server-side history sync and no primary-phone dependency | basic | Tinder, Telegram, Instagram, Discord, Signal (history sync 2025); WhatsApp/Signal are phone-primary with caps | Every session is a peer (ADR-001) and `GET /api/sync` reconciles on reconnect (golden rule 3). No device cap (see Missing). |
| Account recovery with single-use recovery codes from the login page: forced new password, revokes every session, bypasses lost 2FA, notifies the owner in the Support chat | standard | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, X, Discord, Reddit, Telegram | `recovery.ts:135-172`. The revoke and the notice are forced, not offered. This is also, today, the only way to set a new password at all. |
| Forgot-username recovery | basic | Reddit, Facebook | Login accepts the email as identifier (`routes.ts:103-108`), so the username is never required. Covered by design. |
| Lost-phone remote deactivation (revoke the lost device from another session) | standard | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram | Covered by the Devices page, or by burning a recovery code if no other session exists. |
| Change home area / neighborhood | standard | Nextdoor | `UpdateMeRequestSchema.area`; freeform and unverified by design. |
| Email address private by default (never serialized to other users, not searchable) | basic | all 17 (table stakes) | `email`/`email_verified` exist only on `userPrivateDto` (`lib/dto.ts:223-224`); search ILIKEs username/display_name only (`users/routes.ts:127`). No action. |
| Guest (logged-out) access to public share pages and invite join pages | standard | Telegram, Discord (preview-before-signup funnel) | `/join/[token]` SSRs the inviter card; `/s/[token]` renders public posts/beacons/listings/stories unauthenticated. No action. |
| Sign out of the current device (Settings index and Devices page) | basic | all | `POST /api/auth/logout` revokes the row, clears the cookie, closes that session's sockets (`routes.ts:126-131`). The "Logout clears local state" gap sits on top of it. |

Plumbing, not counted as features: opaque sha256-stored httpOnly/Secure/SameSite session token with the CSRF header and revocation that closes live sockets; argon2id m=64MiB t=3 (`service.ts:12-15`) with no `needsRehash` on login (grep = 0 hits, so raising the cost later leaves old hashes behind); TOTP secrets encrypted at rest and replay-protected (`lib/secretbox.ts`); WebAuthn one-shot challenges and signature-counter cloned-authenticator detection (`passkeys.ts:279-290`); citext case-insensitive uniqueness on username and email; registration/session IPs nulled nightly after `IP_RETENTION_DAYS` 90 (`jobs/gc.ts:49`), which users are never told about because the privacy note it is supposed to live in does not exist (see Missing).

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Logout clears local state (cached messages) on the device | basic | `connection.ts:117-125 logout()` clears only the `outbox` IndexedDB store | The `messages` and `conv_meta` stores (`lib/db.ts:33-37`) survive sign-out, so the next person on a shared browser can read cached message bodies. Day-one bug class, trivial fix. | P0 |
| Live username availability check while typing with suggestions | basic | Client regex only; the 409 surfaces at submit | A debounced availability route (must not leak more than the 409 already does). Users learn the handle is gone after filling the whole form. | P1 |
| Closed / invite-required registration mode (operator switch) | standard | `invites_enabled` (API.md §1.41) only disables invite links; `register` is always open; invite tokens already ride the register call | A "require an invite to sign up" switch. For a self-hosted community instance this is the single most useful spam control and the plumbing exists. | P1 |
| Suspended / banned account refused at login with a clear reason screen | standard | Server side is complete: 403 `account_suspended` / `account_banned` after the password check (`auth/routes.ts:117-119`), admin suspend/ban revokes every session and closes sockets (`admin/routes.ts:133-135`), state re-checked on every request (`service.ts:79`) | `login/page.tsx:148-153` branches only on `bad_credentials` and 429 (grep `account_suspended` under `apps/web` = 0 hits), so a suspended user sees "Could not sign in. Check your connection and try again." | P1 |
| Per-account lockout / progressive delay after repeated failed password attempts | standard | Login limiter keyed by IP only (`routes.ts:99`, 10/min); TOTP is per-token + per-IP; recovery 5/h/IP | Per-account counter. The primary password factor is guarded less than TOTP, passkeys and recovery; a distributed guesser against one account is slowed only by argon2 cost. | P1 |
| Password strength meter / common- or breached-password rejection / max length / not-equal-to-username | standard | `min(8)` in `RegisterRequestSchema` (`api.ts:786`); Fastify `bodyLimit` is the only length cap | Meter, local common-password list (keeps it SaaS-free), explicit max length, not-equal-to-username. "password" and "12345678" are accepted today. | P1 |
| Trusted devices / remembered browsers (skip 2FA on a known device, revocable list) | standard | 2FA asked once per 30-day sliding session (API.md §1.73) | No trusted-device table in `schema.ts`, no "forget devices" UI. Effectively "once per session" with no user control or visibility. | P1 |
| Step-up re-authentication ("sudo mode") before sensitive changes | standard | Recovery-code regeneration re-proves the password (`recovery.ts:114-131`); TOTP disable needs a code | `passkeys.ts:92,180` (add/delete passkey) and `totp.ts:84` (enable 2FA) need nothing. A borrowed unlocked phone can enroll a persistent passkey or arm 2FA with the attacker's authenticator, but cannot regenerate recovery codes. Inconsistent. | P1 |
| Session entries show a parsed device name, device icon, approximate location and last active | standard | `sessions/page.tsx:78` prints `user_agent.slice(0,80)` plus the raw IP (`:82`) and a "this device" chip (`:79`); `lib/auth.ts describeUserAgent()` exists but is used only by the approve sheet (`link/[code]/page.tsx:119`) | Parsing, icon, coarse location. Reads as an engineer's list, not a Devices screen. | P1 |
| Log out of all other devices / sign out everywhere (one tap) | standard | `service.ts:104-107 revokeAllSessions` (callers: admin suspend/ban, recovery, CLI) | No user route in `auth/routes.ts`, no button on the Devices page. The only user-triggerable path is burning a recovery code. | P1 |
| New-device / unrecognized-login alert (push, email or in-app) | standard | `sendSupportMessage` fires only on recovery-code use (`recovery.ts:155-159`) | Password, passkey and QR logins from a new device send nothing. The Support chat + push already exist, so this needs no mailer. | P1 |
| New-device login challenge delivered to an existing signed-in session before SMS/email fallback | standard | Only the inverse: a new device starts a QR link and the old session approves (`links.ts`) | A password login on a new device is never pushed to existing sessions for approval. Would reuse the approve sheet; useful because the app has no mailer for email challenges. | P2 |
| "Ways you can log in" overview that nudges users to add a backup method | standard | `settings/security/page.tsx` lists Sessions / Passkeys / 2FA / Recovery sections | No completeness summary or "add a backup method" nudge. Polish. | P2 |
| Last-resort recovery when codes and authenticator are both lost (support request / verified alternate contact) | standard | Operator CLI only: `cli.ts:16-37 reset-password` prints a temp password and revokes all sessions | Any user-reachable way to ask for it: Help requires an authenticated session and there is no unauthenticated contact form, so the user must already know the operator; no mailer for an alternate contact. And `cli.ts:36` tells the user to "log in and change it", which the product cannot do. | P2 |
| Profile link / QR code to share your handle | standard | `/u/[username]` exists behind AppShell auth; `lib/qr.ts` already draws QR codes | No profile QR / copy-link affordance on the Account page. Small win for in-person "add me". | P2 |
| Security features discoverable in the in-app feature directory / what's-new cards | standard | `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` (`constants.ts:1037-1065`) ends at v1.0 "gallery" | No passkey / 2FA / QR / recovery entry. The best-built part of the area is invisible outside Settings → Devices & security. | P2 |
| Signed-out-elsewhere / session-expired handling with an explanation ("You were signed out on another device") | basic | On WS close 4401 the client probes `/api/users/me` (`connection.ts:163-166`), sets `authed=false`, AppShell redirects to `/login?next=…` | Nothing tells the user why: no toast, no `?reason`, no copy (grep "signed out / session expired" in `AppShell`, `connection.ts`, `store.ts` = 0 hits; the only hits are recovery copy on the login page). A remote revoke, an admin suspend and a 30-day expiry all look like a random logout. WhatsApp, Telegram and Signal all say so. | P2 |
| Passkey login requires user verification (biometric/PIN), not merely presence | advanced | `userVerification: 'preferred'` on registration and login options (`passkeys.ts:84,222`) | `requireUserVerification: false` on both verify calls (`passkeys.ts:107,270`); `passkey_credentials` records `backed_up` and `counter` but not UV capability. A touch-only roaming key with no PIN is accepted as a single-factor passwordless login. Either require UV at assertion or record it at registration and require it for keys that support it. | P2 |
| Generic error on bad credentials / recovery / link (no user enumeration) | standard | Error bodies are byte-identical on every path (401 `bad_credentials`, one `fail()` in passkeys, one 401 `bad_recovery`, one 410 `link_gone`); login copy never disambiguates | `routes.ts:110` throws before `argon2.verify` runs, so the argon2id verify (m=64MiB, t=3) executes only for existing accounts: a timing oracle on username/email existence. `recovery.ts:146` short-circuits the same way. No dummy-hash branch anywhere under `modules/auth`. Registration also answers distinct 409 `username_taken` / `email_taken` (`routes.ts:38`), an explicit email-existence oracle. The goal is met only at the string level. | P3 |
| Login-approval sheet shows requesting device, network hint, expiry; Approve/Deny; new session revocable afterwards | standard | Device / Network / Asked / Expires facts and Approve are real (`link/[code]/page.tsx:116-143` → `POST /api/auth/link/approve`) | "Deny" is a client-only no-op (`page.tsx:59-63` toasts and navigates home; no deny/revoke route in `links.ts` or API.md §1.71), so the pending link stays approvable for the rest of its 120 s TTL. The post-approve toast promises the laptop "now shows under Devices" and routes there, but the session row is minted only on the laptop's next 2 s poll while `/settings/sessions` fetches once on mount, so the device can be absent from the list the phone lands on. | P3 |
| Rate-limit errors show the real wait time (Retry-After) | basic | Server sets `retry-after` on every 429 (`app.ts:95`) | The web `ApiError` constructor (`lib/api.ts:5-13`) discards headers, so login prints "try again in a minute" for every limiter (TOTP 5/min, recovery 5/hour) and register prints "try again later" for a 3/hour window. The number is already on the wire. | P3 |
| Security key / passkey usable as a 2FA second factor (not only as the primary login) | advanced | Passkeys only mint sessions (`passkeys.ts:235-305`) | TOTP is the only second factor. Niche. | P3 |
| Multiple personas / profiles under one account | advanced | Per-group masks: pseudonym + emoji avatar (`SPECS.md:136-137`) | No general alt-profile. Masks are the deliberate, scoped version; fine. | P3 |
| Duplicate-account prevention after ban | advanced | Email uniqueness + registration IP capture (`auth/routes.ts:52`) + 3/h/IP | Phone uniqueness, re-registration cooldown. Adequate for a small instance. | P3 |
| Forced client updates / expiring builds | advanced | `sw.js:21 skipWaiting` (PWA self-updates on next load) | No refuse-to-run. Adequate for a web client. | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Minimum-age confirmation / age gate at signup | basic | P0 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Threads | Not collecting birthdays is good (PSYCHOLOGY-aligned), but a public instance with zero 13+/16+ attestation is a legal exposure for the operator; a single checkbox closes it. `RegisterRequestSchema` (`api.ts:783-795`) has no age/consent field. |
| Terms of Service / Privacy Policy pages and acceptance line at signup (incl. the promised instance privacy note on 90-day IP retention) | basic | P0 | all 17 (implicit); Bumble (privacy choices), Reddit, Nextdoor | The app captures IPs and emails and documents a retention window that users are never shown. `FEATURES.md:172` (#54) and API.md §1.41 reference an "instance privacy note" that exists only as admin copy (`admin/users/page.tsx:108`); grep `/terms`, `/privacy`, "By continuing", `terms_accepted` across `apps/web` and `apps/server` = 0 hits. The Help footer is "Bored Chat · self-hosted" (`settings/help/page.tsx:222`). A static /about or /privacy page is a day-one expectation. |
| Change password while logged in (current password required) | basic | P0 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads, Meetup | No route in `auth/routes.ts` (register, login, logout, sessions only); `UpdateMeRequestSchema` has no password; no field on Security or Account pages; `cli.ts:36` even tells the user to "log in and change it". The only way to set a new password is burning a recovery code from the login page, which signs out every device. The most glaring hole in the area. Ship it with the standard "sign out other devices?" choice and a Support-chat alert (`sendSupportMessage` exists). |
| Delete my account (self-serve, in-app, permanent) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup/Luma/Partiful | `users/routes.ts` has GET/PATCH `/me` and avatar POST/DELETE only; no `DELETE /api/users/me`; no UI under `settings/**`; grep "delete account / deactivat" across SPECS, FEATURES, ROADMAP = 0 hits; API.md:1658 says of the admin actions "Neither deletes anything"; admin actions are suspend/unsuspend/ban/unban (`admin/routes.ts:104`), the CLI is reset-password / grant-admin / revoke-admin, and API.md:1642 calls the admin export tooling for "deletion requests" with no delete endpoint behind it. `ON DELETE CASCADE` on auth tables is schema hygiene, not a feature: nothing can trigger it. Universal table stakes, a GDPR erasure right (for the self-hoster too: an erasure request today is hand-written SQL), and an App Store requirement if the native-wrapper backlog item ever ships. Not planned anywhere. |
| Change / add email with re-verification and a revert link to the old address | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads, Meetup, Tinder | Account page renders the email as an unlabelled muted line (`settings/account/page.tsx:226`); no email in `UpdateMeRequestSchema`; no route. Even without a mailer a password-re-proved change is possible today; currently a typo'd email is permanent and squats the address for its real owner. P1 rather than P0 only because email is not yet used for anything on a default install. |
| Change username (with cooldown and/or reclaim hold) | basic | P1 | Telegram, Instagram, TikTok, Discord, Threads, X, Snapchat, Facebook, Signal, BeReal, Luma; Reddit (immutable) | `settings/account/page.tsx:224` renders `<input id="pf-username" disabled readOnly>`; no schema field, no route. Reddit proves immutable handles are survivable because display name is editable, so P1 not P0; but a greyed-out field with no explanation visibly signals "you cannot manage your identity here". Mentions/@-links and share pages make a reclaim-hold design necessary. |
| Signing out removes this device's push subscription | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | `logout()` never calls `disablePush()` (sole caller: `settings/notifications/page.tsx:111`) and there is no `DELETE /api/push/subscriptions` route (`push/routes.ts`); `push_subscriptions` is keyed by `user_id` + `endpoint` with no `session_id` (`schema.ts:675-678`), so revoking a session leaves that browser's endpoint live and `sw.js:140` shows notifications unconditionally. A shared device keeps popping message previews after sign-out until the endpoint fails. Privacy leak, not polish. |
| In-app ban appeal / "Request a review" reachable while locked out | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Hinge, TikTok, Snapchat | Help page is inside `AppShell` (`settings/help/page.tsx:231`), so it requires a session; no unauthenticated appeal route. Pairs with the suspended-login bug: a banned user currently has no path at all. |
| Deletion grace period (hidden immediately, cancel by logging in within 14–30 days) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Threads, BeReal, Nextdoor | Depends on delete-account; no scheduled-deletion column. Design it together with deletion; regret protection is cheap. |
| Deactivate / pause account temporarily (profile hidden, reactivate on login) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Discord, Nextdoor, Threads, Meetup, X, Tinder, Hinge, Bumble | `suspended_at` (`schema.ts:82`) is admin-only; no self-serve route or settings row. A "take a break" switch is exactly the anti-compulsion control `PSYCHOLOGY.md` argues for; the column and hide-everywhere enforcement already exist for suspension. |
| App lock: biometric / PIN with auto-lock timer and content hidden in the app switcher | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram | grep "app lock / passcode / biometric / screen lock" across `apps/web` and docs = 0 hits. The two messengers the app most resembles both have it; a PWA can do a local PIN or WebAuthn user-verification. Pairs with the logout-cache leak. |
| Birthday capture with locked DOB and teen-account defaults | basic | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, Discord, Threads, Bumble, Hinge, Tinder | Deliberately not collected (no `dob` column). The P0 is the attestation checkbox above, not DOB storage. |
| Anti-bot registration challenge (CAPTCHA / proof-of-work / honeypot) | standard | P2 | Signal, WhatsApp (OTP throttling), BeReal, Discord (phone gate) | Only 3/hour/IP. A self-hosted PoW or honeypot fits the no-SaaS ethos; today a public instance can be scripted at 3 accounts/hour per IP. |
| Auto-terminate inactive sessions after a user-chosen window / idle device expiry | standard | P2 | Telegram (1w–6m), WhatsApp (14-day idle), Signal | Fixed 30-day sliding `SESSION_TTL_MS`, no per-user setting. A forgotten library browser stays logged in as long as it is opened monthly. |
| "This wasn't me" flag on a session that secures the account | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Reddit | Devices page offers only Sign out. Meaningful only once change-password exists. |
| Security checkup wizard (confirm contact, review sessions, enable 2FA, password strength) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok | No such page. Polish; the security page is already well structured. |
| Timed snooze with durations and optional "away" status shown to contacts | standard | P2 | Bumble, Tinder ("Show me" off), Hinge (pause) | Depends on deactivate; moods exist but never hide the profile. Nice extension of pause. |
| Export a single chat's history | standard | P2 | Telegram, WhatsApp (chat export), Signal (backup) | No per-conversation export route. Natural sibling of the full data export. |
| Add / change phone number | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, BeReal, Partiful | No phone column. Blocked on the SMS-gateway decision; no phone identity exists. |
| Multiple accounts on one device with quick switching | standard | P2 | Telegram, Instagram, TikTok, X, Reddit, Threads, WhatsApp, Facebook (profiles) | Single `sid` cookie; no switcher in AppShell. Anonymous-group masks already cover the alt-persona need; low value for a community app. |
| Gate posting / messaging until the contact point is verified | standard | P2 | Nextdoor, Luma, Discord, Reddit | Depends on email verification; `auth/guard.ts` has no verified gate. Useful anti-spam once verification exists. |
| Reserved / blocked usernames (admin, support, boredchat, moderator, …) | standard | P2 | every mainstream app | `USERNAME_REGEX` is the only rule; the Support brand is `sender_id NULL` with no reserved handle, so @support, @admin, @boredchat are registrable today. The in-app "Bored Chat Support" brand makes @support a live phishing vector. |
| Credential-change alerts (passkey added/removed, 2FA on/off, recovery codes regenerated) | standard | P2 | Google, GitHub, Apple; mainstream social apps | Passkey add/delete, TOTP enable/disable and code regeneration write the row and return: no Support-conversation notice, no push, no WS frame to other sessions. Combined with the absent step-up re-auth, a stolen unlocked phone can add a passkey or disable 2FA with zero trace for the owner. |
| In-app QR scanner on the phone (camera view inside the PWA for linking a desktop) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | The phone side is reached only via the OS camera app (which opens the browser, not the installed PWA, so the user lands on /login first; `link/[code]/page.tsx:6-7` admits this) or by typing the 6-char code. grep `BarcodeDetector`/`jsQR` = 0 hits. A BarcodeDetector-based scanner adds no dependency and removes the login-first detour on iOS. |
| Sign-out confirmation prompt (warns that queued unsent messages are discarded) | basic | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram | Both Sign out buttons call `logout()` directly (`settings/page.tsx:194`, `sessions/page.tsx:85-93`; no `confirm(` in either file); `logout()` runs `db.clearOutbox()`, so messages queued while offline are destroyed silently. A data-loss action with no warning. |
| "Remember me" / session-duration choice at login | standard | P3 | Facebook, Instagram (saved-login toggle), TikTok | Every login is a 30-day sliding session with no opt-out; matters mainly on shared devices. |
| Account chooser / one-tap "Continue as @user" on the login page | standard | P3 | Facebook, Threads, Instagram | Distinct from the banned member cards (`SPECS.md:98`) since it would show only this device's prior accounts; low value. |
| Account change history (log of username / email / password / privacy changes) | standard | P3 | Instagram ("Account History") | Nothing identity-related is changeable yet; no user audit table. Moot until identity edits exist. |
| Hacked-account recovery with identity proof (video selfie / ID upload) | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, Hinge, Bumble | No vendor (golden rule 7); the operator CLI reset is the self-hosted equivalent and its missing request path is the Half-built last-resort row. |
| Optional reason survey on delete/deactivate | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Tinder, Bumble | Depends on delete-account. Low value for a self-hosted instance. |
| Multiple contact points with a primary selection | standard | P3 | Facebook | Single citext email column. Niche. |
| Connected third-party apps (OAuth grants) list with revoke | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Snapchat, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, TikTok | No oauth tables, no public API. Nothing to connect yet. |
| Switch to a Business / Creator account type | standard | P3 | Instagram, TikTok | No `account_type`; listings exist without a business identity. No ads or insights to unlock; skip. |
| Linked-device / concurrent-session cap | standard | P3 | WhatsApp (4 linked devices), Telegram, Signal (evict oldest) | `createSession` inserts unconditionally; `PASSKEYS_MAX` (10) exists but there is no `SESSIONS_MAX`. An account accumulates unlimited 30-day-sliding sessions that the Devices page then lists as raw UA strings. |
| Organizer / host personas and team accounts under one login (co-hosts on a plan, shared organizer identity) | standard | P3 | Luma (calendars with multiple hosts), Meetup (co-organizers), Partiful (co-hosts), Facebook Events, Discord (server roles) | A beacon has a single `creator_id` (`schema.ts:707`) and every mutation is creator-only (`beacons/routes.ts:360,444,576`); `beacon_roles` (`schema.ts:834-852`) are chore labels on the card ("brings snacks"), not permissions. The only substitute is a group chat's owner/admin/member roles (`schema.ts:377,393`; admin-gated routes at `conversations/routes.ts:320-436`), which the product never names as the way to co-run anything. Fine for friends-and-neighbours plans; a named "co-host" on a beacon is the whole ask, not team accounts. |
| Real-name policy / home-address verification (postcard, billing match) | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | Nextdoor-specific and in tension with the pseudonymous masks. Not wanted. |
| Password-reset protect (confirm email/phone before a reset is allowed) | advanced | P3 | X | Moot until email reset exists. |
| Operator policy: require 2FA for admins or all members | advanced | P3 | Instagram (high-reach), Reddit (mods), Luma (organizers), Facebook | Admin settings have no `require_2fa` flag. Cheap once 2FA exists; low urgency. |
| Name / label a session or linked device ("Work laptop") | advanced | P3 | Signal, WhatsApp | `SessionInfoSchema` (`api.ts:837-841`) has no nickname (passkeys do, `schema.ts:2332`). Nice-to-have. |
| Per-session capability toggles (accept calls / secret chats) | advanced | P3 | Telegram | No such fields on `sessions`. Niche. |
| Account access history (log of past logins with app, IP, date) | advanced | P3 | X, Telegram | Sessions table holds live rows only; gc deletes expired rows. Conflicts mildly with the 90-day IP-retention minimalism; skip. |
| Security-code / encryption-key-change notifications | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Signal, Messenger | Not applicable until E2EE (backlog). |
| Encrypted-backup PIN / secure storage for E2EE history on new devices | advanced | P3 | Messenger, WhatsApp, Signal (PIN), Telegram (cloud password) | Not applicable until E2EE. |
| Self-destruct account after N months inactive | advanced | P3 | Telegram | No such job in `jobs/gc.ts`. Niche. |
| Transfer your information to third-party services (Data Transfer Project) | advanced | P3 | Facebook, Instagram | Golden rule 7; a local file download covers the need. Not wanted. |
| Account merge for duplicate identities | advanced | P3 | Luma | Not applicable with a single email identity. |
| Act as an identity provider ("Sign in with …" for other sites) | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Discord, TikTok, Facebook | Federation backlog. Far future. |
| Developer API keys | advanced | P3 | Luma, Meetup | Not in scope. |
| Delegated / shared account access without sharing the password | advanced | P3 | X | Groups have admins instead. Not in scope. |
| Selfie / ID verification with a verified badge | advanced | P3 | Hinge, Tinder, Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok | No vendor (golden rule 7); `FEATURES.md:64` (#44) plans account-age + mutual-friend trust signals instead, and `created_at` is unused in the UI. The planned trust signals are the right substitute; ship them. |
| Regional age assurance (ID / selfie age estimation) | advanced | P3 | X, Reddit, Discord, Facebook | No vendor; operator liability instead. Not applicable to a self-hosted instance. |
| Legacy contact / memorialization | advanced | P3 | Facebook | Niche. |
| Family Center / guardian link | advanced | P3 | Discord | Conflicts with the "no private-chat monitor" standing decision. Not wanted. |
| Passkey-first / passwordless signup (create the account with a passkey, no password ever set) | advanced | P3 | Google, Apple, Amazon, GitHub | `RegisterRequestSchema` requires a password; `POST /api/auth/passkeys/options` is behind `requireAuth` (`passkeys.ts:64`). The v0.9 auth pack stops one step short of its own ADR-021 passwordless story. |

### Planned already

- **Email verification by code/link before the account is live** (basic, P1 today, P0 the day a mailer exists): `email_verified_at` is never written (`lib/dto.ts:224` is the only non-schema reference), `email_verified` is always false, the admin toggle is inert. `docs/ROADMAP.md:114`; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:138` ("cut"). Email is mandatory and unique yet never confirmed, and nothing can fix a typo because email cannot be changed. Gated on a mailer, but it is the hinge of the whole email lifecycle.
- **Forgot password: self-serve reset via email code/link** (basic, P1 today, P0 the day a mailer exists): admin toggle `password_reset` inert (`admin/auth/page.tsx:39`); `ROADMAP.md:114`; `ARCHITECTURE.md:138-140` ("cut"). Recovery codes are a sound substitute for power users; mainstream users lose them and then need the operator's shell.
- **Magic-link / one-time-code email login** (standard, P1): `docs/SPECS.md:175-180`; API.md §1.74. Toggle persists but nothing reads it; becomes configuration once a mailer exists.
- **2FA via email one-time code** (standard, P1): `docs/SPECS.md:175-180`; admin toggle `email_otp` inert. Configuration once a mailer lands.
- **Download my data / self-serve export** (standard, P1): only trace is `docs/adr/ADR-013-admin.md:62` ("a user-initiated takeout of their own history is future user-facing scope"); the admin-only export (`admin/routes.ts:357-409`) excludes message bodies. Not in SPECS or FEATURES. GDPR/CCPA access right; the actor is wrong (operator, not user) and messages are excluded.
- **Phone-number signup / login with SMS or voice OTP** (basic, P2): `admin/auth/page.tsx:318-329` "SMS gateway" block with a "Coming later" pill; API.md §1.74 ("no column, no key"). Basic everywhere else, but an SMS gateway is a third-party SaaS (golden rule 7); the self-hosted community audience is not phone-first.
- **2FA via SMS codes** (standard, P2): same SMS-gateway placeholder. TOTP + passkeys are stronger anyway.
- **"Member since" / account age on public profiles** (standard, P3): `docs/FEATURES.md:64` (#44: "account-age/mutual-friend signals remain 📋"). `created_at` serializes only on `UserPrivate`. This is the documented substitute for ID/selfie verification and it is not surfaced anywhere yet.
- **Cross-app / federated identity hub** (advanced, P3): `CLAUDE.md` backlog (federation). Far future.

### Deliberately not built

- **Social sign-in (Google / Apple / Facebook)** — `CLAUDE.md` golden rule 7, ADR-021 first-party-only posture. Right call for this product: a self-hosted instance that phones home to Google at login would undercut its one real trust story, and passkeys + QR login cover the convenience case. The cost is real, though: without social login *and* without a working forgot-password, the only recovery for a mainstream user is a .txt file or the operator's shell. Excluded from the basic-tier denominator.
- **Paid verification / premium subscriptions / promo codes / billing** — golden rule 7 ("Money never moves through the app"), SPECS standing decision (no payments). Agree; nothing in the account area should ever gate on money.
- **Collectible / marketplace usernames** — golden rule 7. Agree; a reclaim-hold on changed usernames is the only username economy this app needs.
- **Login streaks / daily-login rewards / "come back" guilt mechanics** — `docs/PSYCHOLOGY.md:32-33` (never build streak counters). Agree without reservation.
- **Suggestion / member cards on the login page** — `docs/SPECS.md:98` ("NEVER user cards on the login page"), `constants.ts:1144`. Agree, and not only on taste: showing members to a logged-out visitor is an enumeration leak that would undo the byte-identical error work. A device-local "Continue as @you" chooser (Missing, P3) is a different thing and would not violate the rule. Excluded from the standard-tier denominator.

### How it looks

- **Register (`phone-register.png`, `register.png`)** — Zero trust or legal signals at account creation: no "By continuing you agree to…" line, no Terms or Privacy page anywhere in the web app, no age attestation, no statement of who runs the instance, all under the heading "Join the fun" (`register/page.tsx:175`) for an app that lets strangers meet nearby and stores photos, email and a home area. Instagram puts DOB plus a Terms/Privacy line under the button; Hinge and Bumble gate 18+ before anything else; Telegram asks for terms acceptance on first launch. Fix: one consent sentence under Create account linking to `/terms` and `/privacy` (operator-editable markdown with a sane default that includes the 90-day IP retention), an "I'm 16 or older" checkbox, and "Hosted by <operator>" on the auth pages.
- **Register (`phone-register.png`)** — No inline validation: the username rules and the 8-character minimum appear only as a post-submit error, availability is only known after a full round-trip failure, Display name is not marked optional, no strength indication. Instagram and Telegram show live username availability with explicit available/taken text. Fix: helper text under Username ("3–24 characters: a–z, 0–9, _"), a debounced availability check with a tick/cross, "(optional — defaults to your username)" on Display name, a 3-segment strength bar.
- **Register (`phone-register.png`)** — Email is required but the app cannot verify it, never sends to it and cannot use it for reset on a default install; the form gives no reason for asking. Fix: helper text in all cases ("Only for account recovery once this server has email set up. Never shown to anyone."), and either make it optional or verify it when SMTP is on.
- **Login (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`)** — The only recovery door is a small muted link reading "Lost everything?" (`login/page.tsx:706`); there is no "Forgot password?" under the password field, and the recover form only accepts a code, so someone who lost both password and the .txt hits a dead end with no "ask the operator" route. Instagram/Hinge/Bumble put "Forgot password?" directly under the field. Fix: inline "Forgot password?"; the recover screen explains both routes (a code, or "ask the person who runs this server" with the operator contact from admin settings, and email reset automatically once SMTP is configured).
- **Login at phone width (`phone-login.png`)** — "Log in with phone · QR" (`login/page.tsx:685`) is shown on a 390px screen with no pointer/touch gating (grep `matchMedia`/`pointer` in the page = 0 hits), where it renders a QR you cannot scan with the same device and a "Waiting for your phone…" that never resolves for a one-device user. Telegram shows QR login only in desktop/web clients. Fix: hide the QR door on touch-only devices, or relabel it "Approve from a device that's signed in" and lead with the typed-code instructions.
- **Login / register wordmark (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`; `AuthScene.tsx`)** — The brand name rolls through Bored / Happy / Sad / Drunk every 2.6 s (`WORDMARK_WORDS`, `WORDMARK_FLIP_INTERVAL_MS`, `constants.ts:773-774`) on the only pages a stranger sees before handing over a password; a quarter of the time the product is called "Drunk" while someone types their email. No mainstream app animates its name on the login screen. Fix: static "bored." plus one tagline on /login, /register and /join; keep the mood wordmark for signed-in surfaces.
- **Login / register (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`)** — Nothing tells a newcomer what the app is or why to trust it beyond a seven-word tagline; the self-hosted story (your messages live on a friend's server, no ads, finite feeds) is the strongest trust lever the product has and it is unused. Hinge runs a 3-slide value carousel, Telegram a 5-slide one. Fix: three short lines under the card ("Chats, plans with friends, a local board." / "Hosted by <operator>, not a company. No ads, nothing sold." / "Feeds end. Nobody sees when you were last online.") and a "What is this?" link to an about page.
- **Login / register footers (`phone-login.png`, `phone-register.png`)** — "New here? Join the fun" (`login/page.tsx:692-694`) hides the verb; a visitor scanning for "Sign up" will not see it. Register has a serif "Join the fun" h1 inside the card while Login has none, so sibling screens have different internal hierarchy. Fix: "New here? Create an account" as the footer link; either both cards carry a heading or neither.
- **Settings → Account (`settings-account.png`)** — Username is a disabled text input "@maya" (`settings/account/page.tsx:224`): looks editable, greyed at low contrast on iOS, no reason given. Below it the email is an unlabelled muted line (`:226`) with no Verified state and no Change action. The page is reached from a pill that says "Edit profile" but is titled "Account", and it is actually a long profile editor (avatar, bio, GIF strip, interests, work/skills) while credentials live under "Devices & security". Telegram shows username as an editable row with live availability; WhatsApp's Account page holds Change number and Delete my account. Fix: rename this page "Edit profile" and keep it identity-only; reserve "Account" for username, email (labelled, Verified/Unverified, Change), password, Download my data and Delete account at the bottom in danger red.
- **Settings → Account / Security (`settings-account.png`, `settings-security.png`)** — No way to change password, change email, delete the account or export data anywhere in Settings. Every comparison app has Settings → Security → Change password and Delete account. Fix: the routes and rows in the one-week list below.
- **Settings → Security (`settings-security.png`)** — Dev-speak and raw values in user copy: "Passkeys are bound to this server's domain (localhost)" (`settings/security/page.tsx:747`); recovery codes "generated 3:58" with no date (same-day times print as bare hour:minute, `lib/format.ts:9`); the 2FA card says "Authenticator codes" OFF plus "Turn on" (`:756,779`; two states, two words). Passkeys shows an always-visible "Name for the new passkey (optional)" input (`:718`) and a dark Add button even with zero passkeys (form before intent); "Approve a laptop" is buried at the bottom (`:832`) while the Active sessions card sits at the top (`:593-603`) with the list itself one tap further away. Telegram's Privacy & Security rows are label + state, "Link Desktop Device" first, label asked after creation. Fix: hide the domain line unless the origin differs from the RP ID; "today 15:58" / "12 Oct" dates; a single toggle row for 2FA; one "Add a passkey" button → OS prompt → name sheet after success; move Approve a laptop to the top of Devices; a direct "Devices" root row with the count.
- **Settings root (`phone-settings.png`)** — Sign out is bare red text with no confirmation (`settings/page.tsx:194` calls `logout()` directly), and it silently wipes the outbox. WhatsApp confirms log out. Fix: confirm sheet that mentions unsent messages.
- **Devices & security with the tour on top (`phone-settings-security.png`)** — The spotlight tour ("Chats — Message friends, make groups…", step 1 of 4) fires on top of the security page, dimming a security screen with a tooltip about a different tab. `SpotlightTour` is mounted by `GlobalOverlays` on every AppShell route and keys only on a device-local "seen" flag (`SpotlightTour.tsx:4-27`), never on the route. Fix: start the tour only on the Chats tab root, never on settings/security routes; persist "seen" before navigating.
- **Help & about (`settings-help.png`)** — Contains no about: no version, no operator identity ("Send to the operator" — who?), no Terms/Privacy links, no data-retention statement (the admin page claims the 90-day IP window is "documented in the instance's privacy note", which does not exist for users). Footer is "Bored Chat · self-hosted" (`settings/help/page.tsx:222`). Telegram's Help has FAQ, Privacy Policy and Ask a question; Instagram's has About, Terms, Privacy, Data policy and Download your information. Fix: an About block with operator name/contact from admin settings, server version, "What we keep", Terms and Privacy links, licence line.

### If I had one week

1. **Ship the four lifecycle routes and an Account page that uses them.** `POST /api/auth/password` (current + new, argon2 re-verify, optional "sign out other devices", Support-chat alert via the existing `sendSupportMessage`); `PATCH /api/users/me/email` (password re-proof, writes `email_verified_at = NULL`, verification link when SMTP is configured, revert notice to the old address when a mailer exists); `DELETE /api/users/me` (password or recovery-code re-proof, `scheduled_deletion_at` + 14-day grace with cancel-on-login, then a job that tombstones messages and cascades profile/posts/listings/beacons/media/sessions/push); `POST /api/auth/logout-all` reusing `revokeAllSessions`. Rename `/settings/account` to "Edit profile" and build a real Account page: username row with reason, labelled email with Verified state and Change, Change password, Download my data (reuse the admin export with message bodies included), Delete account in danger red. Fix `cli.ts:36`. Add an admin "delete user" action so erasure requests stop needing hand-written SQL.
2. **Make sign-out actually sign out.** `logout()` clears the `messages`, `conv_meta` and `outbox` stores, calls `disablePush()` and a new `DELETE /api/push/subscriptions` for this endpoint, and goes behind a confirm sheet that mentions unsent messages. Add `session_id` to `push_subscriptions` so server-side revoke (Devices page, admin suspend, recovery) kills that browser's push too. Then a local PIN/WebAuthn app lock with an idle timer is a day of work on top.
3. **Put the legal and trust floor under signup.** `/terms` and `/privacy` pages rendered from admin-editable markdown with a default that states what is stored (email, IPs for 90 days, messages, media) and who operates the instance; a "By continuing you agree…" line and an "I'm 16 or older" checkbox on register, stored as `terms_accepted_at`; "Hosted by <operator>" on the auth pages and an About block in Help & about. One checkbox and two static pages close the biggest operator-liability gap in the product.
4. **Make the login page tell the truth.** Branch on 403 `account_suspended` / `account_banned` with a reason screen and an unauthenticated "request a review" form (routes to the Support conversation); carry `?reason=revoked|expired|suspended` on every bounce and show a one-line banner; surface `Retry-After` in `ApiError` so 429 copy says the real wait; "Forgot password?" inline under the password field with the operator-contact fallback; hide the QR door on touch-only devices; fix the "Settings → Security" copy; static wordmark.
5. **Close the quiet security gaps in the good half.** Step-up re-auth (password or TOTP within the last 5 minutes) for passkey add/delete and TOTP enable; a Support-chat + push notice on every new-device login and every credential change; per-account lockout (e.g. 10 failures → 15-minute delay keyed by account, in addition to the IP limiter); a dummy argon2 verify when the user is not found so timing stops leaking existence; a reserved-username list (`admin`, `support`, `boredchat`, `moderator`, `help`, `security`); require UV on passkey assertion for credentials that reported it at registration; `needsRehash` on the login path. None of these need a mailer and all reuse code that already exists.


---

## Profile, identity and account

The profile area is a polished hero wrapped around an account core that is missing day-one basics. Three design decisions deserve one sentence of credit: per-viewer mood audience, an activity strip whose per-viewer audience can only narrow and never widen past the object's own audience, and a trust panel that hides averages under three reviews — no benchmark app does any of them. The headline is everything underneath. There is no change password, no change email, no delete account, no private-account mode, no who-can-message policy, no share link, no posts grid or per-user posts endpoint, and iPhone HEIC photos 415 on the single most important profile action. Counting from the tables below, duplicates removed and the deliberately-not-built list excluded: basic tier 14 has ÷ 29 (14 has + 8 half-built + 5 missing + 2 planned) = 48%; standard tier 17 ÷ 73 (17 + 17 + 31 + 8) = 23%; advanced 6 ÷ 34 (6 + 3 + 23 + 2) = 18%. Everything the benchmark calls a profile "surface" (pinned posts, cover photo, pronouns, links, joined date, QR, verified badge, mutual faces, groups in common, who-can-message, mute/restrict, view-as) is absent, and most of it is not on the roadmap. FEATURES #15's "public profile pages" are member-only with no SSR or OG tags; the own Profile tab and the /u/ page are two different components rendering different content (bio hidden behind ritual lines, no Work/Skills, no Set-mood); and the plan-karma counters the UI labels "only you see this line" ride `UserPublic` to every viewer and search result. Contact import is not a privacy virtue here, it is a growth gap (P1): hashed matching is implementable self-hosted and the app otherwise has no way to find the people you already know. The bans are correctly held (no counts, no view history, no streaks), but calm design does not excuse a profile that shows strangers your bio, pictures and reviews with no way to lock it and no way to leave. Fix order: account lifecycle routes (password/email/delete), private-account + who-can-message, per-user posts endpoint feeding real profile tabs, profile share link with QR, then the cheap polish (pronouns, links, joined date, verified chip, HEIC, mutual faces). Plumbing, not counted as features above: live `user.updated` fan-out over WS (`connection.ts:256`, `dto.ts:1048 publishUserUpdated`; recipients are owner sockets plus conversation co-participants, so an open `/u/` page of a non-conversation peer does not refresh) and the block/privacy integration suites (v03/v05/v05b).

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Display name (editable) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Meetup/Luma | 1–100 chars (`UpdateMeRequestSchema.display_name`, `PATCH /api/users/me`); fans `user.updated` to co-participants. |
| Unique username handle | basic | Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal | `users.username` citext unique, `USERNAME_REGEX` (`constants.ts:73`). Set once at signup, shown as @handle on the hero line; cannot be changed afterwards (see Missing). |
| Bio / about text | basic | All 17 apps (Hinge replaces it with prompts) | `users.bio`, max 1000 chars — generous (IG 150, X 160, Discord 190). On the own Profile tab the bio is hidden whenever recent activity exists (`settings/profile/page.tsx` ritual fallback), so the owner rarely sees what they wrote. |
| Preset status/about lines (Available, Busy, At the movies...) | standard | WhatsApp, Signal, Discord | `MOOD_PRESETS` (`constants.ts:871`) via `MoodSheet.tsx`. Covered by the mood chip presets. |
| Interest tags on profile | standard | Threads, Facebook, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Meetup; TikTok/X keep them private | `users.interests` + check constraint; `INTERESTS_MAX` 10 tags × 24 chars; chips on `/u/[username]`. Matches Threads. |
| Shared-interest highlight for the viewer | standard | Tinder, Bumble, Luma | `myInterests` / `intChipShared` ('· shared') on `/u/[username]/page.tsx`. Works, but re-fetches the viewer's own full profile on every profile open; read `state.me` instead. |
| Custom status / mood (emoji + text, presets, auto-expiry) | standard | Discord, Telegram (emoji status), Instagram Notes, WhatsApp/Signal about, Snapchat | `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/users/me/mood` (API §1.57), `MoodSheet.tsx`, `MoodChip.tsx`. Swing mode and drunk mode have no benchmark equivalent. Oddity: no Set-mood entry on the own Profile tab, only on Account and the public page. |
| Status / mood audience control (everyone / connections / only me) | standard | Instagram Notes (close friends / mutuals), Telegram (Premium) | `MoodSheet.tsx:40-42` VISIBILITY; `moodDto` resolves per viewer on every UserLite surface. |
| Animated name style / name colour / profile effects | advanced | Telegram (Premium), Discord (Nitro), Snapchat+ | `users.nameStyle`, `NAME_STYLES` (`constants.ts:949`), `StyledName.tsx`. Free here where benchmarks charge; reduced-motion fallback present. |
| Profile photo upload / take / crop / remove | basic | All 17 apps | `POST /api/users/me/avatar` (202) and `DELETE`; `pickAvatar()` → `ImageEditSheet lockedAspect={1}`, `removeAvatar()`. Crop/rotate, magic-byte sniffing, processing and failed states all present. HEIC is the hole (see Half-built). |
| Initial-letter / colour fallback avatar | basic | Signal, Partiful, Discord, WhatsApp (silhouette) | `Avatar.tsx initialOf()`. One identical colour for every user (see How it looks). |
| Animated / video avatar | standard | Telegram, TikTok, Discord (Nitro) | `jobs/avatar.ts` (512px animated WebP + JPEG poster), ARCHITECTURE §5, `AVATAR_SOURCE_MIME_ALLOWLIST`. Free where Discord charges; rendering contract limits animation to profile/chat header. |

Backed by `GET /api/users/me/profile` (`profiles/routes.ts:44`), `GET /api/users/me/gallery` (`gallery/routes.ts:25`), `GET /api/users/:username/activity` (`activity/routes.ts:80`). Shipped and reachable from the tab bar. |
| Own connections list with accept / decline / cancel / remove | basic | All apps | `app/connections/page.tsx` (:178 accept, :186 decline, :230 cancel pending, :128 unfriend, :75 rows link to /u/); reached from the Profile tab pill. Server `connections/routes.ts` (:207 accept, :222 decline silent delete, :238 DELETE, :259 GET accepted/incoming/outgoing with blocked pairs omitted). Both halves present. |
| Avatar processing / failure feedback (202 + live completion, previous avatar kept on failure) | basic | All apps | `settings/account/page.tsx:194` 'Processing — it will pop in when ready.' (spinner), :198 'Processing failed. The previous avatar is kept.'; `POST /api/users/me/avatar` 202 status 'processing' (`users/routes.ts:71-95`); completion via WS `user.updated` → `store.applyUserUpdated` (`store.ts:681`); `Avatar.tsx` keeps rendering the last good keys while processing/failed. Basic upload UX the 'Profile photo upload' row does not spell out. |

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| iPhone HEIC photo accepted for avatar | basic | `maybeConvertImage` exists and is used by `ProfileEditor.tsx uploadGif()` and `lib/imageUpload.ts` for the GIF strip | `settings/account/page.tsx:171` accept list is gif/webp/apng/png/jpeg/mp4 and the page never imports `maybeConvertImage`, so an iPhone user's first action (set a photo) returns 415. One import fixes it. | P0 |
| Avatar / name links to profile on every surface | basic | Linked in `FeedView.tsx`, `ChatView.tsx`, `ListingDetailSheet.tsx`, `BeaconDetailSheet.tsx`, `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`, `SuggestionCards.tsx` | Not linked (rg '/u/' = 0) in `StoriesStrip.tsx`, `StoryViewer.tsx`, `SeenBySheet.tsx`, `ChannelMembersSheet.tsx`, `PollView.tsx`, `UserSearch.tsx`. FEATURES #60 claims 'every avatar anywhere → profile'; a dead tap on a story author is noticed on day one, but every person is still reachable one hop away via the chat or feed, so P1 not P0. | P1 |
| Cover / banner image (upload, reposition) | standard | `profilepage.module.css:20-28 .cover` is a static 128px CSS gradient | No column in `schema.ts`, no DTO field, no upload route. The band is already drawn and reads empty without an avatar. A static-only upload reusing the avatar job is a small job; or drop the band. Absent on Instagram, Threads, TikTok, WhatsApp, Snapchat, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Nextdoor — so dropping it is a legitimate answer. | P1 |
| Mutual connections named on a profile ('Connected with X, Y') | standard | `mutuals`/`mutual_count` only on Suggestion cards (`api.ts:3509`); the query already exists in `suggestions/routes.ts` | Nothing on `/u/[username]/page.tsx`. Named faces are exactly the PSYCHOLOGY-approved social proof; Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, Threads, BeReal, Discord, Luma, Nextdoor, Telegram all show it. | P1 |
| Email verification flow + verified state shown | basic | `users.emailVerifiedAt`, `UserPrivateSchema.email_verified`, API §1.74 flagged off until SMTP | `settings/account/page.tsx` never shows verified/unverified. Infra-gated (SMTP) so not day-one visible; still, the Account page should at least say which it is. | P1 |
| Owner-only profile stats kept private (no contract leak) | standard | UI caption 'Only you see this line.' on `/u/[username]` | `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` ride `UserPublic` (`api.ts:246-247`) to every viewer and search result via `userPublicDto` (`dto.ts:200`). The promise is not backed by the contract. Strip for non-owners server-side or drop the caption. | P1 |
| Report from profile with reason taxonomy | basic | `ReportSheet.tsx` single optional free-text (`REPORT_REASON_MAX_CHARS`); `POST /api/reports` (§1.14) | A spam / harassment / impersonation / underage / scam picker is standard everywhere and helps the admin queue (§1.41). | P1 |
| Presence / last-seen shown on the profile page itself | standard | `pp.heroSub` reads `state.presence`; `lib/format.ts:18 formatLastSeen` emits 'last seen 43m ago' | Never calls `subscribePresence` (`connection.ts:522` is called only from `ChatView.tsx:251` and `ConversationListView.tsx:72`), so it is unreliable and shows the literal word 'offline' when the owner hid last-seen; exact-minute strings collide with the PSYCHOLOGY never-build. Either subscribe + coarse buckets ('recently', 'today'), or follow Instagram/X/Threads/Signal/Bumble/Hinge/Nextdoor/Luma and show no presence on the profile. | P1 |
| Single edit-profile screen with one consistent save model | basic | One page (`settings/account/page.tsx`) | Two Save buttons ('Save' vs 'Save interests & entries') while the pic strip auto-saves on every change. Easy to lose interest/entry edits by pressing the top Save. BeReal, Instagram, Tinder: one edit screen, one save. | P1 |
| Bio editor polish: character counter, multi-line preview | standard | Textarea rows=3 maxLength=1000 on `settings/account/page.tsx` | No counter; users hit the silent maxLength wall with no feedback. Instagram, X, Discord, Telegram all count. | P2 |
| Location / home city shown on profile | standard | `users.area` owner-private; `UserPrivateSchema.area` 'never serialized into UserPublic'; `same_area` boolean on suggestions only (API :2524) | Deliberate privacy choice (FEATURES #30). Keep hidden by default; an opt-in coarse 'around <area>' line is the only sensible addition. | P2 |
| Work / job / school / education fields | standard | Two entry kinds on both halves: `profile_entries_kind_check IN ('work','skill')` (schema :1580), `ProfileEntryKindSchema` (`api.ts:2256-2262`, :2320), `ProfileEditor.tsx` `#pe-kind` select Work/Skill, rendered as 'Work' / 'Skills' sections on `/u/` | No school/education/job-title kind; education must be smuggled into a free-text 'work' title. Never rendered on the own Profile tab — you must open your public page to see your own entries. Corrected from has by verifier. | P2 |
| Status expiry choices (30m / 1h / 4h / today / never) | standard | Fixed 4h expiry (`users/mood.ts`, `constants.ts:896-899`) | Fixed 4h is a defensible calm default; a 'today' option would be the only worthwhile add. | P2 |
| Built-in avatar builder (emoji-on-colour / sticker / Bitmoji / Snoovatar / 3D) | standard | `Avatar.tsx` letter fallback only | No builder component. A Signal-style emoji + colour picker is cheap and on-ethos; skip 3D avatars. | P2 |
| Story ring on profile / list avatars | standard | `StoriesStrip.tsx:76 storyRing` in the chat-list tray only | `Avatar.tsx` has no ring prop; the profile hero shows none even when the person has a live story. | P2 |
| Story highlights / featured / saved stories on profile | standard | Saved stories are private-only (`gallery/routes.ts`) | No public highlights section on `/u/`. The 3-pic strip is the de-facto 'featured' slot; let a saved story fill one. | P2 |
| Multiple profile photos (6-9 slot gallery) | standard | `profileMedia order_idx 0..2` (3 slots) | Three images is the entire public media surface; six would be a constant lift plus layout. | P2 |
| Events hosted / attended shown on profile | standard | `GET /api/users/:username/activity` (beacons lit/sparked, 30 d, cap 20, links to `/local`) | No upcoming beacons and no deep links to the beacon itself. | P2 |
| Profile actions: message / call / video call | basic | `message()` on `/u/`; calls live in ChatView | No call button on the profile (rg -i call in that file → none). Reachable one tap later via the chat, so not day-one pain. | P2 |
| Own activity log with filters and bulk delete | standard | Own strip in `activity/routes.ts` (20 items, 30 d) | No filters, no bulk delete. | P2 |
| Hide me from guest / 'Who's in' lists | standard | `GET /api/beacons/:id/joins` shows joiner faces to the audience (API :1836) | No opt-out; Luma, Partiful, Meetup all have one. | P2 |
| Profile completeness nudges / empty-state prompts (no percentage meter) | standard | Only the Interests segment prompts on `settings/profile/page.tsx` | The public page hides every empty section instead of inviting the owner to fill it. Quiet 'add a bio' rows, never a progress meter (PSYCHOLOGY). | P2 |
| Search people by name or username (find a profile) | basic | Server half: `GET /api/users?q=` prefix ILIKE on username/display_name, limit 20, blocks + bans excluded (API :622, `users/routes.ts:117-127`); `UserSearch.tsx:55` placeholder 'Search people by name or username' | UI half is a participant picker only: mounted in `/new` (start a chat) and `ChatInfoSheet` (add member); `UserSearch.tsx:12 onSelect(user)` renders `<button>` rows (:65-71) with no `/u/` link; `people/page.tsx` has no search box; no global search bar (SPECS #108 planned). You cannot look someone up without starting a chat. Added by verifier; the verifier's second filing of this row is merged here. | P2 |
| Live username-availability check while typing at signup | standard | Availability learned on submit: `register/page.tsx:121-122` catches 409 `username_taken`; `auth/routes.ts:30-38/67` checks uniqueness only inside register; `register/page.tsx:195` has pattern="[a-z0-9_]{3,24}" but no inline rules hint | No `GET /api/auth/check` or debounced lookup (rg 'available\|username/check' API.md → none). Any live check is an enumeration oracle; the §1.44 doctrine (API :2800) deliberately avoids those elsewhere — a deliberate 'never' is defensible, but then say so in the field's helper text and show the rules inline. Added by verifier; the verifier's second filing (Missing, P3) is merged here. | P2 |

| Profile theme colours / background pattern | advanced | Single fixed gradient in `profilepage.module.css` | No user choice. Discord/Telegram sell this; low value here. | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Private account / connections-only profile mode | basic | P0 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Threads, Snapchat (default), BeReal (friends-only), Signal (connections model), Facebook (profile lock), Luma | No profile-visibility column on `users`, no row on the privacy page; SPECS v1.0d covers only per-tab toggles (#107). Today bio, interests, work/skills, the 3-pic strip and trust reviews are visible to every logged-in member regardless of relationship, and nothing on the roadmap changes that. For a 'local community' app this is the gap users feel first. |
| Change password while logged in | basic | P0 | All apps | No route in API.md (only `POST /api/auth/recover` via recovery codes, :2859); nothing under `settings/security`. A logged-in user with a weak password has no fix short of burning a recovery code, which also signs out every device. |
| Change email | basic | P0 | All apps | No route, no module; `settings/account/page.tsx` shows email read-only. Email is required at signup and becomes the recovery address once SMTP is on — and it can never be corrected. |
| Delete / deactivate account | basic | P0 | All apps (GDPR/CCPA requirement; Apple requires in-app deletion) | No `DELETE /api/users/me`; only admin suspend (API :1632); `users` has no `deleted_at` (the one at schema :428 is messages). Legally required in most markets and expected day one. |
| Change username (rate-limited, e.g. once per N days) | standard | P1 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Snapchat (yearly); Reddit never allows | `UpdateMeRequestSchema` (api.ts:855) has no username field; `#pf-username` is disabled readOnly; no route. Immutable handles are defensible for @mentions/forwards, but every mainstream app lets you rename with a cooldown. At minimum document it as a decision. |
| Rich bio: clickable links, @mentions, #hashtags, line breaks | standard | P1 | Telegram, Instagram, TikTok, X, Discord, Threads, Facebook | `/u/[username]/page.tsx:469` renders `<p>{shown.bio}</p>` as plain text; no linkify helper in `apps/web/lib`. @mention linking already exists in ChatView (:2040); reuse it. |
| Pronouns field with show/hide toggle | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, Discord, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup (absent on WhatsApp, Telegram, X, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Snapchat) | rg -il pronoun apps packages docs → no hits; not in SPECS v1.0d. Half the benchmark has it and it is a one-column, one-chip feature. Add with an audience toggle. |
| External links / website (1-5 links with titles) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, Reddit, Discord (in bio), Meetup/Luma, TikTok (gated), Telegram (in bio); Snapchat/Nextdoor business only | No column on users or profile tables; `ProfileSchema` (~api.ts:2266) carries media/interests/entries only; no roadmap item in FEATURES or SPECS. Plain typed URLs are ethos-compatible (no OAuth). Add 'links' to the §1.37 editor; render with rel=noopener. |
| Joined date / member since / about-this-account panel | standard | P1 | X, Reddit (cake day), BeReal, Nextdoor ('neighbor since'), Discord, Meetup/Luma, TikTok | `UserPublicSchema` (api.ts:233-251) has no `created_at`; `/u/` shows no join date. Cheapest trust signal there is (the column already exists). Also enables a Nextdoor-style 'new here' label. |
| Profile photo privacy (everyone / contacts / nobody, with public fallback) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook, Snapchat, Signal (contacts only) | API :658 'avatars of any user are readable by authed users'; no privacy row, no column. Every messenger in the benchmark has it; the proxy already auth-checks, so a per-viewer gate is feasible. |
| Pinned posts on profile (1-4) | standard | P1 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal | rg -i pinned api.ts → only message pins (§1.32) and disappear TTL; no post pin column. Add once profile tabs exist. |
| Groups in common / mutual servers on profile or DM info | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Meetup | `shared_groups` count only on suggestions (API :2524); nothing on `/u/` or `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`. The query exists; the surface does not. |
| Verified / official badge | standard | P1 | WhatsApp (biz), Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, BeReal, Reddit (Official), Nextdoor (verified neighbor), Luma | rg 'is_admin\|verified' `/u/[username]/page.tsx` → none; `users.isAdmin` exists (ADR-013) but is never rendered. An operator-granted boolean plus a chip; trivially small and the only impersonation defence a small server has. |
| Profile QR code (show / scan to add) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, Threads, BeReal, Luma (absent: X, Discord, Reddit, Tinder, Nextdoor) | `QrCode.tsx` exists but is used only by QR login (`login/page.tsx:481`) and TOTP (`security/page.tsx:182`). SPECS #110 (`docs/SPECS.md:274-284`) specifies a share link and copy-link button, not a QR, so this is Missing, not Planned. In-person adding is the natural flow for a local app; wire it to the profile share sheet when #110 lands. |
| Per-field profile privacy (photo / bio / about audience selectors) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook, Hinge (per-vital toggle), Tinder/Instagram (pronouns toggle) | Only mood (`MoodSheet.tsx:40`), last-seen (`users.showLastSeen`) and activity kinds (`users.activity*`) have audiences. The pattern exists three times; extend it to photo/bio/links/pronouns. |
| Who can message me (everyone / connections / nobody) + message requests inbox | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Discord, Telegram, TikTok, Signal, Snapchat | `users` has only `group_add_policy`, `anon_messages`, `invites_paused`; DM create (§1.14) checks blocks only; `POST /api/conversations {type:'dm'}` from any profile. With /people suggestions, Discover and share pages this is the largest safety gap around profiles; the anon-message policy shows the pattern to copy. |
| Discoverability opt-out (find by username / suggest my account to others) | standard | P1 | Instagram, TikTok, Snapchat (Quick Add), Telegram, Reddit (allow follow), Luma (guest lists) | `GET /api/users?q=` has no opt-out; `suggestions/routes.ts` excludes only blocks/dismissed/ghost; no privacy row. You can be suggested to strangers on the same server and cannot say no. |
| Mute / restrict a person (soft block) | standard | P1 | Instagram (Restrict), X (Mute), Facebook (Snooze), Snapchat, Discord (Ignore) | `blocks/routes.ts` has only PUT/DELETE block + list; no 'restrict' concept in schema or docs. In a local community a hard block is socially loud; a quiet mute is what people actually want. |
| View-as / preview my profile as others see it | standard | P1 | Facebook, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Discord (live preview card) (absent: Instagram, X, TikTok, Threads, Telegram, Meetup) | rg -i 'view-as\|preview.*profile' → nothing. The own tab hides the bio behind ritual lines and omits Work/Skills, so users literally cannot see what others see without the small link icon. Essential once #107 per-tab toggles ship. |
| Self-serve data export / takeout | standard | P1 | Facebook, Instagram, X, Reddit, Discord, Telegram, Snapchat, TikTok | Admin-only `GET /api/admin/users/:id/export` (API :1642, omits profile_entries/profile_media); API.md calls user takeout 'future user-facing scope'. Legal expectation alongside delete; the job already exists server-side. |
| Onboarding profile setup step (avatar / bio / interests) | standard | P1 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Nextdoor, Meetup, TikTok, Instagram, WhatsApp (photo prompt) | `register/page.tsx` collects username/email/password/display name; `welcome/page.tsx` collects area only (rg -i 'interest\|avatar\|bio' → none). New accounts land as an initial-letter circle with an empty profile; /people suggestions then have nothing to show. |
| Contact-info / profile sheet aggregating shared media, links, files and groups in common | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord (popout) | rg 'conversations/:id/(attachments\|media\|links)' API.md → none; `ChatInfoSheet.tsx` offers theme/wallpaper/background only. Every messenger in the benchmark has it; it is the profile surface messenger users open most. |
| Find friends via contact import (hashed phone / email matching) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Snapchat, Instagram, TikTok, Facebook | No phone column (schema :35-128) and nothing in API/SPECS. This is a growth gap, not a privacy virtue: the only ways to find someone you already know are a username you must be told, an invite link (#70) or a suggestion engine that has nothing to work with on a fresh account. It is implementable fully self-hosted with no SaaS: the client normalises and hashes numbers/emails (salted, server-side pepper), the server matches against hashes of `users.email` (already stored) and an optional phone column, both sides opt in, and raw contacts never leave the device. Signal's model, not Facebook's. Added by verifier; priority raised from P3 and the 'deliberate gap' framing withdrawn. |
| Edit an existing work/skill entry in place | basic | P2 | Facebook, LinkedIn, Hinge, every app with structured fields | `ProfileEditor.tsx` exposes only addEntry(), moveEntry(↑/↓) and a '×' remove per row (rg 'edit' → none); fixing a typo means remove, re-add at the end, re-order. `PATCH /api/users/me/profile` (`profiles/routes.ts:52`) is full-replace so no new endpoint is needed. Pure client gap on a basic editor interaction. |
| Photo captions / alt text on profile media | standard | P2 | Hinge, Instagram (alt text), Facebook | Strip images render alt=''; `profileMedia` has no caption column. Accessibility plus the 'why this photo' context Hinge gets right. |
| Search within a profile's posts | standard | P2 | Threads, X (from:) | No per-user post search in API.md. Low until the posts tab exists. |
| Role / tenure badges (operator, moderator, founding member, new here) | standard | P2 | Reddit (admin/mod), Discord (staff/booster), Nextdoor (roles, new-neighbor label), Telegram | `users.isAdmin` never rendered; no `created_at` in UserPublic. Not a ranked signal, so psychology-safe; tells a newcomer who runs the place. |
| Copy username / handle from profile | standard | P2 | Telegram, Discord, Snapchat ('Share Username') | rg -i 'clipboard\|copy' `/u/[username]/page.tsx` → none. Thirty-line change; the stopgap until share-profile ships. |
| Close Friends / custom audience lists | standard | P2 | Instagram, Snapchat (private story), Facebook (custom lists) | `POST_AUDIENCES = friends_of\|public` (constants.ts:711); FRIENDS-OF primitive at :371. Profile and post audiences stop at 'connections'; there is no inner circle. |
| Interest picker with suggested / preset tags (tap-to-add) and autocomplete instead of free text only | standard | P2 | Threads, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup, Nextdoor | `ProfileEditor.tsx:87-98` bare free-text `tagDraft` + Add (24 chars, up to 10, case-insensitive dedupe); no datalist, no `INTEREST_PRESETS` in constants, no `api/interests` or `interests/suggest` source anywhere — only `DISCOVERY_SHARED_INTERESTS_MAX` (constants.ts:1163) for suggestion cards. Free tags fragment ('climbing' vs 'Climbing ' vs 'bouldering') and weaken the shared-interest highlight the has table credits, since the profile compares lowercased strings only. Added by verifier; the separate 'autocomplete' filing (P3) is merged here. |
| Minimum-age attestation at signup (no DOB stored) | standard | P2 | Every mainstream app gates signup on age | `register/page.tsx` collects username/email/password/display name only (rg -i 'age\|birth\|13\|18\+' → none). FEATURES #78b forbids age/gender FIELDS, which does not preclude a non-stored 'I am 16+' checkbox or terms line. A self-hosted operator still carries this liability. Added by verifier. |
| Tap profile strip (Pics) images to open the full-screen pinch-zoom viewer | standard | P2 | All apps | `ProfileGif` renders `<img>` in a span with only onMouseEnter/onMouseLeave — no onClick, no MediaLightbox (the only lightbox on the page is the avatar `picZoom`). The avatar got a #93 viewer but the three strip images, the only other media on the profile, cannot be opened at all; own-tab Posts/Gallery tiles likewise not tappable. |
| Unsaved-changes guard on the profile/account edit screens | standard | P2 | Instagram ('Discard changes?'), Telegram, Facebook | rg beforeunload apps/web → none; `settings/account/page.tsx` tracks only `saved`/`busy` (lines 65, 94, 107), no dirty flag or navigation prompt; `ProfileEditor.tsx:41/71/76` same. Leaving via the Back link silently drops name/bio/area edits while the pic strip auto-saves, and it compounds the two-Save-buttons problem: edits in the lower card are lost without warning. Added by verifier; the second filing is merged here and the priority raised from P3 because the loss is silent. |
| Hide an individual post from profile (profile curation) | advanced | P2 | Instagram, Reddit | No per-post profile-visibility flag on `posts`. Needed the moment #107 tabs ship, otherwise the per-tab toggle is all-or-nothing. |
| Tagged photos / tag review | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook | No photo-tagging primitive anywhere in schema. Depends on tagging existing at all; low fit. |
| Search-engine indexing toggle | standard | P3 | Reddit, Facebook | Moot until logged-out pages exist. |
| NSFW / mature profile flag | standard | P3 | Reddit | Niche to Reddit. |
| Connections-only profile field (Hinge 'Match Note' — a disclosure shown only after you connect) | advanced | P3 | Hinge | No profile field carries a connections-only audience apart from mood (`MoodSheet.tsx:40-42` 'connections'); bio, entries and pics are all-or-nothing; nothing in SPECS. Cheap once per-field audiences exist (Missing, P1): one more text column with the same selector. Low fit outside dating, so P3. |
| Structured lifestyle/basics chips (relationship goals, height, languages, drinking...) | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | Nothing in `users`/`profileEntries`. Dating-specific; out of scope for a community chat app. |
| Profile prompts Q&A (text / voice / video / poll) | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, TikTok (legacy Q&A) | No prompt table or DTO. A tasteful 'three questions' section would fit the Go-Deeper deck idea in PSYCHOLOGY, but it is not on any roadmap. |
| Profile song / music clip | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Telegram, Tinder/Bumble (Spotify) | Not found; no audio profile column. Spotify is barred by CLAUDE.md rule 7; a user-uploaded clip is possible but low value. |
| Business / professional / creator profile type (category, hours, catalog, contact buttons) | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Nextdoor, Telegram | No profile type column; marketplace listings + TrustPanel already cover the local-seller case. |
| Full-size photo screenshot / download protection | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Facebook (profile lock) | Media proxy serves originals with immutable cache (API :658). Unenforceable on the web; not worth it. |
| Temporary profile picture / frames / avatar decorations | advanced | P3 | Facebook, Discord (shop), Telegram (Premium) | Not found in schema or `Avatar.tsx`. Cosmetic; benchmarks sell it, you would give it away for nothing. |
| Playlists / series / curated collections | advanced | P3 | TikTok, X (Articles/Highlights) | Not found. Video-platform specific. |
| Follow a profile / per-profile post notifications | advanced | P3 | Threads, Reddit, Facebook (follow), Luma | Only symmetric connections in `connections/routes.ts`. Asymmetric follow drags in follower counts (banned); mention-override already exists. |
| Friends' endorsements / takes shown on profile | advanced | P3 | Hinge (Friends' Takes), Snapchat (charms) | Not found. Named (never counted) endorsements would be psychology-safe; not on any roadmap. |
| Photo / selfie liveness verification badge | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | Needs face ML; outside the self-hosted ethos. |
| Government ID verification | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Instagram (age) | Not a dating app; no third-party verifier allowed. |
| Owner-only profile insights / analytics | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Threads, Facebook, Bumble, TikTok | Not literally banned but contradicts the PSYCHOLOGY north-star (plans completed, not reach). Skip. |
| Online status selector (online / idle / DND / invisible) | advanced | P3 | Discord | No such row on the privacy page. 'Nobody' last-seen plus mute is the calm equivalent. |
| Memorialized / legacy-contact account state | advanced | P3 | Facebook | Not found. Irrelevant until delete-account exists. |
