| Year-in-review recap | advanced | P3 | Reddit, BeReal | Not found. Fine as a once-a-year story; the streak-calendar half of BeReal's version is banned. |
| Opening move / conversation starter on profile | advanced | P3 | Bumble | Not found; anonymous send and waves cover the intent. |
| Life events timeline | advanced | P3 | Facebook | Not found. Facebook-shaped; skip. |
| Multi-account / switch account | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Discord, X, Telegram, TikTok | rg -i 'switch account\|multi-account\|add account' → none; auth is a single httpOnly `sid` cookie (CLAUDE.md rule 8), so one identity per browser profile. Low priority for a community-scale app. Added by verifier. |

### Planned already

- **Public profile content tabs (posts / reposts) backed by a per-user posts endpoint** — basic, P0. FEATURES #107, SPECS v1.0d. Today `/u/` has no tabs and `settings/profile/page.tsx` derives 'Posts' client-side from the FIRST page of `GET /api/feed?lane=friends` filtered to author===me — misses older, text-only and public-lane posts, and tiles are not tappable. There is no `GET /api/users/:username/posts`. The single most visible gap versus every social app: a profile that shows nothing the person has posted.
- **Share profile link / copy link / OS share sheet** — basic, P1. FEATURES #110, SPECS #110 ('profiles get a standalone share link'). `SHARE_OBJECT_TYPES` (`constants.ts:1192`) = post|beacon|listing|story|group, no 'profile'; no share button on `/u/`. The §1.67 share-link machinery exists for five types; profile is the obvious sixth. P1 rather than P0: the invite link already carries identity, so nobody is blocked, just inconvenienced.
- **Tappable interest tags → discovery / search** — standard, P1. API §1.37 'tapping one is a future discovery seam, not a link in v0.5a'; chips are plain `<span>`. `/people?interests=` already exists; wiring the chip is small.
- **Media-only photo / video grid tab** — standard, P1. FEATURES #107. Ships with the per-user posts endpoint.
- **Groups / communities on profile (opt-in, public only)** — standard, P1. FEATURES #107; SPECS (107) 'ghost: never; private: never'.
- **Connections list on profile with owner privacy** — standard, P1. FEATURES #107; `GET /api/connections` (`connections/routes.ts:259`) is viewer-own only today.
- **Logged-out public profile page with OG / Twitter unfurl** — standard, P1. FEATURES #110. `/u/[username]/page.tsx` is 'use client' inside `<AppShell>` (redirects to /login); `GET /api/users/:username` requires auth; OG meta exists only on `/join/[token]` and `SharePageRoute.tsx`. FEATURES #15 calls these 'Public profile pages'; they are member-only. Fix the label or ship #110.
- **Per-tab content privacy (posts / gallery / groups / connections)** — standard, P1. FEATURES #107; SPECS v1.0d.
- **Profile peek / bottom-sheet preview on avatar tap** — standard, P1. FEATURES #106; SPECS v1.0d. No `ProfileSheet`/`ProfilePeek` component yet.
- **Share someone else's profile with a friend** — standard, P2. SPECS :274-284 (#110 Share audit: every shareable surface incl. profile carries the share button; not restricted to own profile); FEATURES :162. Nothing shipped; `lib/share.ts` has no user path. Corrected from missing by verifier.
- **Fediverse / federated handle on profile** — advanced, P3. CLAUDE.md backlog 'federation'.
- **Contact verification via safety numbers (E2EE)** — advanced, P3. CLAUDE.md backlog 'E2EE'; nothing in schema.

### Deliberately not built

- **Birthday / age / gender fields (with separate visibility)** — FEATURES #78b 'no age/gender fields exist to filter on'; rg -i birthday → only `EFFECT_TRIGGERS` (`constants.ts:245`). A standing product decision, not a PSYCHOLOGY ban: discovery filters only narrow by what people chose to share. Agree for profile fields. Disagree that it should extend to signup: a non-stored 'I am 16+' attestation is a different thing and its absence is a liability (see Missing).
- **OAuth-connected accounts (Instagram / Spotify / YouTube) with verified check** — CLAUDE.md golden rule 7 'no third-party SaaS APIs'. Agree. Plain typed links (Missing: External links) are the ethos-compatible substitute and should exist.
- **Public follower / following / friend / like counts** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; API §1.37 'No endorsement counts, no connection counts'; SPECS 'Explicitly NOT building'. Agree, this is the product's spine. Correctly absent everywhere except the `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` leak in `UserPublic`, which is a bug against the ban and must be fixed.
- **Algorithmic lead-photo ordering (Tinder Smart Photos, Bumble Best Photo, Hinge Top Photo)** — not written down anywhere as a decision (rg -i 'smart photo\|best photo\|top photo' docs → none); the strip order is owner-chosen (`profile_media.order_idx` 0..2, schema :1600-1604; `ProfileEditor.tsx moveGif`). It reorders photos by a hidden approval score — a score-driven signal PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 bans and an engagement optimisation rule 1 rejects. Verdict: ban it, and add it to PSYCHOLOGY's never-build list so a later contributor does not add it as a 'helpful' default.
- **Achievements / trophies / donor / booster badges** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 (no ranked signals) + CLAUDE.md rule 7 (no money). Agree on ranked and paid badges. A role/tenure badge (operator, moderator, new here) is not ranked and should not be swept up in this ban.
- **Snap score / karma / streak counters** — PSYCHOLOGY never-build 'Streak counters or loss-framed ritual warnings'; rule 3. Agree without reservation.
- **Best-friends ranking / friend emojis / friend solar system** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 (no leaderboards). Agree; a Close Friends list the owner curates (Missing, P2) is the non-ranked version and is fine.
- **Who viewed my profile / post view history** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 'public view counts'; no `profile_views` table. Agree; this is the single most anxiety-generating feature on TikTok and the app is right to refuse it.
- **Gifts / paid subscriptions / creator monetization on profile** — CLAUDE.md golden rule 7 'Money never moves through the app'. Agree.

### How it looks

- **phone-profile-arjun.png / desk-profile-arjun.png / phone-profile-options.png — other-user header.** A 128px blurred gradient cover (`profilepage.module.css:22`) with a beige blotch top-right (`:25` radial rgba(176,148,94)) that reads as a rendering artifact in light mode; a hard 1px edge (`:28 border-bottom`) where the cover stops mid-avatar (the ring overlaps it by 51px, `:111`); back and kebab as mid-grey 36×36 circles (`.orbBtn`, `:54-55`; below the 44px minimum) that look like disabled buttons; a double ring on the avatar (3px `--ring-live` conic ring + 3px `--bg` gap ring, `:114-125`) that reads as a stuck focus ring when no story exists; a 👻 emoji round button beside two text pills. On desktop the cover is a 680px band (`.page` max-width, `globals.css:172`; the cover is a direct child of `.page`, `page.tsx:247-250`) floating in the 1280px desktop capture. Instagram has no cover, a smaller avatar ringed only when a live story exists, nav buttons as plain glyphs; Hinge uses a full-bleed photo with a soft bottom fade and translucent controls. Fix: drop the cover (or make it full-bleed from the user's own avatar blurred with a 48px fade), 44px rgba(255,255,255,.7) nav circles with ink glyphs, ring only on a live story, anon action as a line icon in the kebab.
- **phone-profile-arjun.png — Friends-state action row.** '✓ Friends' and 'Message' are equal-weight outline pills; nothing is primary; the anon orb has the same visual weight; no voice/video call shortcuts although calls exist; tapping '✓ Friends' fires `window.confirm("Remove … as a friend? They won't be told.")` (`/u/[username]/page.tsx:232`) — a destructive action behind a status-looking button. WhatsApp puts Audio / Video / Search under the name; Instagram's unfollow is an explicit sheet. Fix: Message as filled primary, 'Friends ✓' as a status chip opening a sheet (Remove friend, Mute, Block), voice/video icon buttons, anon orb tertiary.
- **phone-profile-arjun.png — hero sub-line.** Prints 'last seen 43m ago' (`formatLastSeen`) — the exact-minute seen-at the product's own never-build rejects, defaulting to Everyone, and unreliable because the page never subscribes to presence. Signal shows nothing; Instagram's 'Active 2h ago' is the midpoint. Fix: coarse buckets ('active now', 'today', 'this week') or a dot only; keep the privacy setting.
- **phone-profile-arjun.png — Back orb.** `<Link href="/">` (`/u/[username]/page.tsx:252`): open Arjun from Connections, Feed, a beacon face pile or Find People, tap Back, land on the chat list. This is the one page that sends every origin to `/`; `blocked/page.tsx:61` and `sessions/page.tsx:61` hard-code their back targets too, but to their logical parents (`/settings/privacy`, `/settings/security`), which is tolerable. Every mainstream app returns to the originating screen. Fix: `router.back()` with a logical-parent fallback when `history.length` is 1.
- **phone-profile-arjun.png / phone-things.png / phone-new.png — no tab bar.** `/u/[username]`, `/settings` hub, `/things`, `/new`, `/anon`, `/puzzle` render no `TabPage` (rg TabPage apps/web/app → only connections, feed, gallery, local, people, settings/profile); tapping a person from Feed or Local drops you into a bare page with only a floating chevron; the Settings hub has no Profile tab under it. Instagram keeps the tab bar on every profile and on Settings. Fix: wrap `/u/`, `/settings`, `/things` in `TabPage` with Profile active; hide the bar only inside an open conversation and full-screen viewers.
- **phone-settings-profile.png / desk-settings-profile.png — own Profile tab vs public page.** Two different components with different content: the tab has POSTS/GALLERY/INTERESTS segments, ritual lines, a 'My gallery' card, Things and a `RescueCard` (`settings/profile/page.tsx:29,382`), no bio, no Work/Skills; the public page has a cover band, bio, Pics, Work/Skills, trust panel and no posts grid. The 'View your public profile' chain-link icon shows the owner a page they cannot see while editing. Instagram renders own and others' profiles with one screen; only the action row differs. Fix: one `ProfileView` used by both routes, `isMe` swaps the action row only; same section order everywhere (hero → bio → interests → pics → Work/Skills → Posts/Pics segments → Recently → trust); move the Gallery card, Things and Rescue out of the profile body (Rescue stays reachable from the Chats list sheet, `ConversationListView.tsx:15,303`, and Local, `local/page.tsx:37`).
- **phone-settings-profile.png — activity strip under the handle.** Two centered bullet lines where line 1 is centered and line 2 is ellipsized and left-flush, two bullet colours (ink and green) with no legend, and the same two activities repeated further down the same capture under RECENTLY; meanwhile the bio the user wrote ('coffee, climbing, bad puns. koramangala.') never renders because code shows it only when there is no activity. Instagram: bio under the name unconditionally; nothing on a profile appears twice. Fix: always render the bio; drop the ritual lines or reduce to one distinct sentence RECENTLY does not repeat.
- **phone-settings-profile.png — segments and empty states.** POSTS/GALLERY/INTERESTS labels are 12px uppercase in `--inactive` (`settings.module.css:375-379`; light `--inactive` #8a93a6 on `--bg` #f4f6fb, `globals.css:99/:86`, = 2.86:1, fails AA); Gallery has three entry points on one screen (segment, 'My gallery ›' inside it, 'My gallery' card below); the big initial-letter avatar has no 'Add photo' affordance; 'Nothing posted yet' has no CTA; the two top-right icons (chain-link, sliders) are unlabeled and the link one reads as copy-link. Instagram: one grid, labeled hamburger for Settings, 'Share profile' as a labeled button, 'Complete your profile' chips. Fix: `--muted` for inactive segments, one gallery entry, camera badge on the avatar, a dismissible 'Finish your profile' checklist (Photo · Bio · 3 interests), labeled 'Share profile' + gear.
- **settings-account.png — the 'Edit profile' destination is titled 'Account'.** One long scroll holding avatar, display name, bio, area, name style, motion preference, mood card, GIF strip, interests, work/skills; email/password/sessions live under 'Devices & security'. Three save models on one page: a mid-page 'Save' (name/bio/area/style/motion), a bottom 'Save interests & entries', and auto-save for the GIF strip and mood; an entry added via 'Add entry' is silently lost if you tap the top Save or leave; no dirty guard. Username is a disabled text input (looks editable, greyed, no reason given); email is an unlabelled muted line with no verified state; no character counters on name (100) or bio (1000 in a 3-row textarea); 'Remove photo' is a red danger button under the avatar whenever a photo exists; interests have no autocomplete; the 'GIF STRIP' eyebrow is Marcellus serif while 'MOOD' on the same screen is sans; 💼 emoji on work entries. Instagram: single Done in the header, 'Discard changes?' on back, counters; Telegram: editable username row with live availability. Fix: rename to 'Edit profile' and keep it identity-only; one header Done enabled when dirty issuing both PATCHes, 'Discard changes?' on back; username as a read-only value row ('Can't be changed') or editable with cooldown; email as a labelled row with Verified/Unverified + Change; counters; Remove photo inside the camera-orb sheet (Take / Choose / Remove); one eyebrow token; move name style, motion, mood to Appearance / the Profile hero.
- **settings-privacy.png / desk-settings-privacy.png — privacy controls (last-seen, receipts, typing, group-add, anon, activity kinds, invites).** One long scroll of seven sections; the five booleans (`ToggleRow` at `privacy/page.tsx:96,113,123,166,233`) are word-pills ('On' filled / 'Off' outline) that are pixel-identical to secondary buttons like 'Open' and 'Regenerate' elsewhere, so toggles cannot be told from actions — the semantics are already right (`role="switch"` + `aria-checked`, `:289-290`), the gap is purely visual; eight native `<select>`s (`SelectRow` at `:102,129,144,179,186,193,200,218`) styled like text inputs (browser chevron and grey gradient on desktop), four of them the identical per-kind Activity selects (`:179-200`); two labels carry emoji ('🎭 Who can send you anonymous messages', '👀 Wave nudges'); 'Blocked people' is a card with an 'Open' button instead of a chevron row. WhatsApp/Telegram: a switch for every boolean, value-rows ('Last seen and online — Everyone ›') opening radio lists, Blocked contacts as a chevron row with the count. Fix: restyle `ToggleRow` as a track + thumb switch (keep the existing role/aria) and replace `SelectRow` with one `PickerRow` (label / current value / chevron → sheet with radio rows); four cards max (Visibility, Who can…, Activity on profile, Safety); one-sentence hints; drop emoji from labels; Blocked people as a chevron row with count.
- **settings-security.png.** Passkeys shows an always-visible 'Name for the new passkey' input and a dark Add button with zero passkeys; 'Passkeys are bound to this server's domain (localhost)' leaks environment detail; 'generated 3:58' is a time with no date; 'Approve a laptop' Continue sits disabled with no hint it needs the 6-char code; 2FA reads 'Authenticator codes OFF' + 'Turn on' (two states, two words). Telegram: each row is a label + state, no implementation notes. Fix: single 'Add a passkey' → OS prompt → name sheet after success; hide the domain line unless it differs from the current origin; 'today 15:58' / '12 Oct'; enabled Continue validating on tap; one toggle row for 2FA.
- **phone-connections.png / phone-people.png / phone-chats-more.png — people management.** Four homes with drifting terminology (Friends / Connections / Your people): Chats ⋯ → 'Friend requests' sheet, Profile → Connections page, Chats magnifier → 'Find people', Feed audience 'Connections ›'. The Find people Back is hard-coded to `/connections` (`people/page.tsx:35`). 'No more requests' implies requests existed; '+ FIND' and 'Copy' are bare text taps with no button affordance; the bare Copy explains nothing about the invite link (7-day expiry, lands in a DM). Instagram: one Followers/Following/Requests surface under Profile with Discover People as a tab. Fix: one 'People' page (Requests · Your people · Suggestions as segments) reached from Profile and the Chats ⋯ menu; `history.back()` everywhere; one word (Connections); 'No requests yet'; 'Share invite link' as a 40px pill with a one-line caption; promote '+ FIND' to a button when the list has fewer than 3 people.
- **phone-chats.png / phone-new.png / phone-people.png / phone-feed-list.png — letter avatars.** Every fallback avatar is the same #e2e5ec circle with a dark initial, so no list can be scanned by colour; 'F', 'A' and 'M' are visually one person. WhatsApp, Telegram and Google Messages hash the user id to a hue. Fix: hash(user_id) % 6 → six low-saturation tinted gradients in the palette.
- **phone-profile-arjun.png (👻), settings-account.png (💼), settings-privacy.png (🎭 👀) — emoji as chrome.** Emoji render as Noto on Android/Linux and Apple on iOS; every one clashes with the monochrome charcoal/silver palette and the 1.7px line-icon set (`strokeWidth="1.7"`, `/u/[username]/page.tsx:257`). Instagram and Hinge: zero emoji in chrome. Fix: one `<Icon>` component and replace every chrome emoji on profile and settings; emoji stay in user content, reactions and vibe chips.
- **phone-settings-profile.png / phone-profile-arjun.png — RECENTLY rows.** Bare '3:47' for today's items switching to 'Oct 18' tomorrow (ambiguous AM/PM); rows are links with no chevron and no touch hover state. Instagram activity and Telegram recent actions use relative, coarse times. Fix: 'today' / 'yesterday' / 'last week' right-aligned plus a trailing chevron on linkable rows.
- **desk-settings-profile.png / desk-settings-privacy.png / desk-profile-arjun.png — desktop.** Every settings sub-page is a full-page navigation with a back chevron in a 680px column (`.page` max-width, `globals.css:172`) leaving roughly half of the 1280px capture empty; the own profile is a phone layout centred on the desktop screen; the 72px rail (`social.module.css:82`) is absent from the `/settings/*` and `/u/[username]` captures, so primary navigation disappears. Telegram Desktop: settings in a side panel with categories; Instagram web: horizontal profile header, nav on every route. Fix: ≥900px Settings as two-pane (categories left, content right, no back buttons); profile header on desktop with avatar left and name/handle/bio/actions right; mount the rail in the shell for every route.

Not captured, so not critiqued visually here: the Devices (sessions) page. Its code-level problems are in the has-table row (raw user-agent + IP, no 'sign out all other devices').

### If I had one week

1. **Account lifecycle (days 1–2).** `POST /api/auth/password` (current + new, argon2id, optional revoke-others), `PATCH /api/users/me/email` (re-auth; verify via SMTP when `features.email_verification` is on, otherwise accept with re-auth), `DELETE /api/users/me` (re-auth, sets `users.deleted_at`, 7-day grace with cancel-on-login, then the existing tombstone/GC paths), and `GET /api/users/me/export` reusing the admin export job with `profile_entries`/`profile_media` added. New migration `0013`. Settings → Account becomes identity + credentials only, with Change password, Change email (showing Verified/Unverified), Download my data, and a red Delete account row at the bottom. This also closes the two Onboarding/Settings blockers.
2. **Private account + who-can-message (days 2–3).** Two columns: `users.profile_visibility` ('everyone' / 'connections') and `users.dm_policy` ('everyone' / 'connections' / 'nobody'). Enforce in `userPublicDto` / `GET /api/users/:username` (non-connections get hero + Connect button only; bio, interests, entries, pics strip, trust panel and activity withheld) and in `POST /api/conversations {type:'dm'}` (403 with the same byte-identical shape as `group_add_denied`). Two `PickerRow`s on the privacy page. In the same DTO pass, strip `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` for non-owners so the 'Only you see this line' caption becomes true.
3. **Per-user posts endpoint + one ProfileView (days 3–5).** `GET /api/users/:username/posts?cursor=` with the feed's audience filter (friends_of visible to connections, public to everyone, blocks → empty), paginated, cap per page. Replace the first-page client hack in `settings/profile/page.tsx`. Build one `ProfileView` component mounted by both `/settings/profile` and `/u/[username]` with `isMe` swapping only the action row; bio always rendered under the name; Posts / Pics segments backed by the new endpoint; ritual-line duplication removed; strip images and tiles wired to `MediaLightbox`. This unblocks #107 tabs, pinned posts and view-as for free.
4. **Share profile + QR + copy handle (day 5).** Add `'profile'` to `SHARE_OBJECT_TYPES`, a share sheet on `/u/` and the own tab (copy link, OS share via `navigator.share`, Copy @username, QR from the existing `QrCode.tsx`). Logged-out `/u/` renders hero + 'Join to see more' with OG tags via the `SharePageRoute` pattern; the full page stays member-only. Then relabel FEATURES #15 honestly.
5. **Cheap polish day (day 6).** Import `maybeConvertImage` in `settings/account/page.tsx` and add `image/heic` to the accept list (HEIC 415 gone); `created_at` in `UserPublic` → 'Joined Oct 2026' line; `pronouns` column + chip with audience toggle; `links` (1–5 typed URLs, rel=noopener) in the §1.37 editor; verified chip rendered from `users.isAdmin` or a new operator-granted `verified_at`; mutual faces on `/u/` by reusing the suggestions mutuals query; `/u/` links in `StoriesStrip`, `StoryViewer`, `SeenBySheet`, `ChannelMembersSheet`, `PollView`, `UserSearch`; `router.back()` on the profile Back orb; `ReportSheet` reason picker (spam / harassment / impersonation / underage / scam / other); a dirty flag + 'Discard changes?' on the account editor.


---

## Privacy & safety

**Verdict.** The enforcement layer is solid and invisible: blocks are 404-shaped across fifteen-plus modules so a 403 never confirms one, media only ever leaves MinIO through an auth-checked proxy with EXIF/GPS stripped, report context is forwarded only with explicit unchecked-by-default consent and every moderator view is ring-logged, and no admin route reads message bodies outside that consented viewer. Be clear about what the last point is: a policy boundary, not cryptography. There is no E2EE, DMs sit server-readable in Postgres, and the operator has SQL; "the operator cannot read chats" is a promise the code keeps, not one it can prove to a user. None of the enforcement is visible to a user anyway, and the control layer every mainstream app ships is missing in one block: no who-can-message policy or requests inbox, no appear-offline, no chat mute at all (a dormant column with no route and no UI is not a feature), no mute/restrict person, no hidden words, no comment or mention controls, no delete-for-me. Coverage after dedupe and recount: basic 40% (17 of 43, banned items excluded), standard 31% (27 of 86). That is below every benchmark app including Signal and BeReal, and the hits are mostly cheap items or properties the product gets by construction (no email search, no counts, no contact sync). The legal and consent surface is literally zero: no Terms, no privacy policy, no guidelines, no age attestation, no consent checkbox, while four docs cite an "instance privacy note" that was never written, and a banned user is told to check their connection (`apps/web/app/login/page.tsx:148-154` handles only `bad_credentials` and 429). Reporting is lopsided: the consent mechanics are careful, but there is one free-text box instead of a taxonomy (so no off-platform, self-harm or IP reason), no report+block, no outcome feedback, and members cannot report posts, comments or stories while logged-out strangers on share pages can. Most P0s are afternoon-sized (mute route + row, login 403 copy, group kick UI, legal pages, age/consent line, report taxonomy, report-from-feed, delete account); the two real builds are who-can-message plus a requests inbox, and delete-for-me plus clear chat.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Block a person + blocked-people list with unblock | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | `PUT/DELETE /api/users/:id/block`, `GET /api/users/me/blocks` (`apps/server/src/modules/blocks/routes.ts:22-80`); `apps/web/app/settings/blocked/page.tsx`; profile menu `apps/web/app/u/[username]/page.tsx:135-146,287`. Polish: no confirm dialog on the profile path (the discovery-card path has one); blocked-list copy should say shared-group messages still show. |
| Silent, server-side block enforcement at every surface (no "you are blocked" signal) | basic | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Instagram, Bumble, Tinder | `apps/server/src/lib/blocks.ts`; ADR-009; 404-shaped at DM create, profile, search, calls, feed, comments, listings, stories, discovery, anon, rescue, push (`docs/API.md` §1.14 table, confirmed in each module's `requireVisible*`/`isBlockedPair` guard). A 403 never confirms a block; `group_add_denied` shares the policy-denial shape. |
| Remove a connection / follower silently | basic | Instagram, Snapchat, BeReal, Facebook | `apps/server/src/modules/connections/routes.ts:98-259` remove/decline silent to the other party; block deletes pending requests (`blocks/routes.ts:41-50`). |
| Who can add me to groups (everyone / people I chat with / nobody) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, TikTok, Signal (as requests) | `apps/server/src/modules/conversations/routes.ts:84-106,226,454` (403 `group_add_denied`); UI `apps/web/app/settings/privacy/page.tsx:129` SelectRow. Polish: WhatsApp/Signal turn a denied add into an expiring invite/request; here the add silently fails. |
| Report a user from their profile | basic | all 17 benchmark apps | `POST /api/reports` kind `user` (`apps/server/src/modules/reports/routes.ts:42-50`); `apps/web/app/u/[username]/page.tsx:298-314`. |
| Report a single message | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, X, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, BeReal | `reports/routes.ts:50-72`; `apps/web/components/ReportSheet.tsx` reached from `ChatView.tsx:1783-1810`. |
| Reporter stays anonymous; the target is never told | basic | Instagram, Facebook, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Signal | `reports/routes.ts` header "No fan-out, no notification to anyone, ever"; `ReportSheet.tsx:94` "No one is told you reported this." |
| Last-seen visibility (everyone / people I chat with / nobody) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook, Instagram | `users.show_last_seen` CHECK (`schema.ts`); `apps/server/src/lib/dto.ts` `lastSeenVisible()`; gateway omits per subscriber (`apps/server/src/ws/gateway.ts:550-562`); UI `privacy/page.tsx:102`. Polish: no "contacts except…" exceptions and no reciprocal hiding; it hides only the timestamp, not the online dot (see Missing). |
| Typing indicator toggle | basic | Signal, Telegram, Instagram | `users.show_typing`; dropped at ingest before rebroadcast (`ws/gateway.ts:498-505`); UI `privacy/page.tsx` "Show when you're typing". |
| Email / phone never exposed: not searchable, not serialised to other members, no phone numbers at all | basic | X, Snapchat, Facebook, Telegram, Signal, Discord, BeReal, Partiful (as opt-out toggles); Meetup/Luma/Partiful, Nextdoor (contact details hidden) | `GET /api/users?q=` matches username/display_name only (`apps/server/src/modules/users/routes.ts:117-137`); `userLiteDto`/`userPublicDto` carry no email (`apps/server/src/lib/dto.ts:185-210`). By construction rather than a toggle; acceptable. |
| Help center / contact support / report a problem | basic | all 17 benchmark apps | `SUPPORT_CATEGORIES` bug/question/idea/`safety` (`constants.ts:1245`); `POST /api/support/tickets` (`apps/server/src/modules/support/routes.ts:52`); `apps/web/app/settings/help/page.tsx`; replies land as a Support chat. No legal category (see Missing). |

Default-off; nothing to pay for. |

Polish: the SOS trigger is an unlabeled emoji (see How it looks). |

The only soft-block in the product; the pattern should generalise to feed/story mutes. |
| EXIF/GPS stripped on upload; media served only through an auth-checked proxy | standard | Signal, WhatsApp, Instagram (EXIF strip) | EXIF strip + auto-rotate (`uploads/pipeline.ts:199-212`); `GET /api/media/*` requireAuth with membership ACL (`uploads/routes.ts:110-135`). No user control and none needed; listed because every benchmark lists it. |
| Hide like / reaction / follower counts | standard | Threads, Facebook, Instagram, X (as toggles) | `docs/PSYCHOLOGY.md` rule 3; no `*_count` field for likes/reactions/followers in `api.ts` or `feed/routes.ts`; profile has no counts. By law rather than toggle. |
| Re-authentication before sensitive security changes (sudo mode) | standard | GitHub/Google-style | Disabling TOTP requires a current code or a recovery code, rate-limited (`auth/totp.ts:154-166`); regenerating recovery codes re-proves the password (`RegenerateRecoveryCodesRequestSchema` `api.ts:4095`; `settings/security/page.tsx:382-389`). Present for the two sensitive surfaces that exist; there is no password/email change yet to gate. |
