| Moderator report queue (list open/closed, view consented context, close with note) | standard | (operator side of every benchmark) | `GET /api/admin/reports` (`admin/routes.ts:161`), `POST /api/admin/reports/:id/close` (`:206`), context viewer ring-logged; UI `admin/reports/page.tsx:10,28,44,115` with open/closed filter. Reporter-facing status is absent (see Missing). |
| No admin route reads private messages; consented report context is the single bounded exception | advanced | Signal (by cryptography); as a policy boundary, unusual among non-E2EE apps | `apps/server/src/modules/admin/*` contains no query over `messages` outside `content.ts:203-295` (the consented context viewer, 404 without consent, every view ring-logged); `admin/reports/page.tsx:165` "(bodies are never shown here)"; `docs/SPECS.md` standing decision. Policy, not cryptography: no E2EE, bodies are plaintext in Postgres, the operator has SQL. Worth something only once the legal pages say exactly that. |
| Ghost groups: pseudonym masks anonymous to members but never to the server; reports attach identity | advanced | unique (Reddit-style anonymity with accountability) | `conversations/visibility.ts`; `lib/ghost.ts:3,34`; `conversationMasks` (`schema.ts:2230`). |
| Anonymous-game anti-deanonymization (mascot blur pre-consent, never real pixels) | advanced | unique | server renders the blur (`anon/routes.ts:375-429`, `ANON_BLUR_SIGMA_MAX` / `ANON_AVATAR_CACHE_TTL_S` `constants.ts:1107-1114`). |
| Drunk-mode guardrails (longer undo, "you sure?" before cold DMs/public posts, caring banner, no trace after expiry) | advanced | unique | `DrunkConfirmSheet.tsx`; `DrunkBanner.tsx`; `settings/routes.ts` drunk state. |

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Block / report reachable from inside the chat (DM header, chat-info sheet, message menu) | basic | Message menu offers Report (`ChatView.tsx:1783-1810`); block lives on the profile page and discovery cards | `ChatInfoSheet.tsx` has no Block, Report or Mute row at all (zero matches). The chat is where harassment happens; avatar → profile → menu is three taps mid-incident. WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Messenger, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble all put it in chat info. | P0 |
| Report a post / comment / story / listing / group as an object while logged in | basic | `CreateReportRequestSchema` kinds `user|message|beacon|anon_message|story_overlay` (`api.ts:1301-1344`); `ListingDetailSheet.tsx:292` reports the author; strangers can report a post via `POST /api/share/:token/report` | No report path in `FeedView.tsx` (no `ReportSheet` import; own-post menu is "Delete post" only, `:991`) or `StoryViewer.tsx`; no post/comment/story/listing/group kinds. A logged-out stranger can report a post, a member in the feed cannot, and the admin takedown tool has no in-app report feeding it. | P0 |
| Suspended / banned notice at login with reason and next step | basic | Server returns 403 `account_suspended` / `account_banned` (`auth/routes.ts:114-118`; `recovery.ts:191`; `totp.ts:148`; `passkeys.ts:301`) | `login/page.tsx:148-154` handles only `bad_credentials` and 429 and falls back to "Could not sign in. Check your connection and try again." A banned user is told it is a network problem. Two-line client fix, plus an operator contact field that `InstanceSettingsSchema` (`api.ts:2893-2897`) does not have yet. | P0 |
| Group admin can remove (kick) a member and promote/demote admins from the UI | basic | `DELETE /api/conversations/:id/participants/:userId` admin-only (`conversations/routes.ts:505-535`); `PUT …/role` (`channels.ts:36-60`); UI exists for channels (`ChannelMembersSheet.tsx:95-130`) | Group `MembersSection` in `ChatInfoSheet.tsx:748+` has no remove/role control. A plain-group admin cannot kick a harasser without the operator. | P0 |
| Delete chat for me / clear chat history / remove a DM from my inbox | basic | Server-side a DM self-leave works: the participants DELETE has no DM guard (`:534-536`), sets `left_at`/`left_seq` (`:540-545`), and inbox queries filter `isNull(cp.leftAt)` (`:123, :252`), so the DM drops out and later history is floored. | It emits a `participant_left` system message to the other party (`:553-560`, not silent); there is no clear-history; the web Leave row renders only for channel/group (`ChatInfoSheet.tsx:710-719`). A user cannot make an abusive DM disappear except by blocking, which freezes it rather than hiding it. | P0 |
| Unmatch / end a conversation for both sides | basic | Roulette keep-or-vanish hard-deletes unless both keep (`RouletteBanner.tsx:4-7`; `docs/API.md` §1.45) | No equivalent for ordinary DMs or mutual-wave DMs; after a mutual wave creates a DM there is no "undo the match" other than block. Tinder, Bumble, Hinge all have it. | P1 |
| Invite link controls (expiry, use cap, revoke a single link) | basic | Fixed defaults: `INVITE_TTL_S` = 7 days, `INVITE_MAX_USES` = 50 (`packages/shared/src/constants.ts:77-80`); `DELETE /api/invites/:id` exists server-side (`invites/routes.ts:257`); instance-wide `invites_enabled` (`admin/routes.ts:289-291`) and per-user `invites_paused` kill switches | No per-link expiry/use choice and no revoke-one UI: `InviteSheet.tsx:67` mentions "Revoke one" only in an error string and no `api.del('/api/invites…')` exists in `apps/web`. Pause-all is the only reachable kill switch. WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord, Luma. Merged from a has row and a standard half-built row; defaults without a control are not a feature. | P1 |
| Password strength / breached-password check | basic | `PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH = 8` (`constants.ts:74`; enforced `api.ts:786, :4118`); argon2id storage | No zxcvbn/entropy meter, no common-password or HIBP k-anonymity check. "password1" is accepted; accounts are guessable before the login rate limit matters. | P2 |
| Hide / delete comments on my post + block the commenter in one tap (bulk select) | standard | Author can delete any comment (`FeedView.tsx:1547`) | No "hide" state (visible only to its author), no delete-and-block shortcut, no multi-select. Instagram, Facebook, Threads, X, TikTok, Nextdoor. | P1 |
| Per-field profile visibility (photo / bio / area / sensitive fields) | standard | Area is owner-private by construction (`schema.ts users.area`; `dto.ts` "NO area") | Avatar and bio are visible to every logged-in member with no audience setting (`userPublicDto`). WhatsApp, Telegram, Hinge, Nextdoor, Snapchat. | P1 |
| In-chat safety tips card for stranger conversations (discovery / roulette / cold DMs) | standard | `DiscoverySection.tsx:588` explains the 5-minute vanish; `LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE` (`constants.ts:490-495`) renders on gig/ride/room listings | Nothing in the conversation itself. Bored Together explicitly arranges meeting strangers; the "tell a friend / meet in public" card belongs there. Hinge, Bumble, Tinder, Messenger. | P1 |
| Verified-email requirement | standard | `users.email_verified_at` (`schema.ts:41`); admin Authentication settings flag | Flagged off until SMTP exists (`docs/FEATURES.md` #86); `POST /api/admin/settings/auth/test-send` always 503 `mailer_unavailable`. Reddit, Discord, most apps at signup. | P1 |
| Change password / change email from settings | standard | Logged-out forgot-password path: `POST /api/auth/recover` consumes a recovery code, revokes all sessions, returns a one-shot `reset_token` (`recovery.ts:135-168`); `POST /api/auth/reset-password` writes a new argon2id hash (`:170-199`); web `login/page.tsx:45-46, :320-338`. | No in-settings change-password (`settings/account/page.tsx:213-258` edits only name/username/bio/area/style; security page covers TOTP/passkeys/codes); email change does not exist (`UpdateMeRequestSchema` `api.ts:855-865` has no email/password). A logged-in user cannot rotate credentials after a suspected compromise without burning a recovery code and signing out every device. All 17 benchmarks have it. | P1 |
| Block an anonymous-message sender without revealing identity | standard | `AnonThreadView.tsx:238-241` offers report; `DELETE /api/anon/threads/:id` deletes the thread; server knows the sender (reports stamp `target_user_id`, `reports/routes.ts:73-100`) | No server-side "never again from this sender" that keeps the identity hidden. Instagram restrict / hidden requests analogue. | P1 |
| Report after unmatch / after the roulette chat vanished | standard | Profile route works while the chat exists | Roulette banner has no report/block affordance (`RouletteBanner.tsx`); once hard-deleted (`docs/API.md` §1.45) the counterpart is unreachable for a report. Bumble, Hinge. | P2 |
| Disable link previews toggle | standard | Per-message dismiss: `Composer.tsx:96` `previewDismissed`; `:575-581` `<PreviewCard onDismiss>`; unfurl skips re-attaching (`:221-227, :241`). | No global per-user switch: `UserSettingsSchema` (`api.ts:1237-1270`) has no `link_previews` key, no privacy-page row. Less urgent than in Signal because the server fetches previews (recipients never hit the URL), but the sender cannot opt out. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal. | P2 |
| Exit a group silently (only admins notified) | standard | Channels already hide leaves (`conversations/routes.ts:540-553`) | Groups emit `participant_left` to everyone (`:558`). WhatsApp, Telegram (channels). | P2 |
| Scam / "keep it on the app" warnings in marketplace and DMs | standard | `LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE` verbatim on listings (`ListingDetailSheet.tsx`) | Nothing in DMs. Tinder, Hinge, Nextdoor, Messenger, Telegram. | P2 |
| "Not interested" / dismiss a discovery card server-side | standard | `suggestionDismissals` (`schema.ts:2077`) shows the server pattern already exists for people suggestions | `DiscoverySection.tsx:305` "a LOCAL skip only; the server hears nothing"; the card returns on refresh. Tinder/Bumble/Hinge pass, Threads, TikTok. | P2 |
| Email-enumeration resistance at signup | standard | Login, recovery and link-poll are byte-identical on failure (`recovery.ts:132-134`) | `POST /api/auth/register` answers 409 `email_taken` vs `username_taken` (`auth/routes.ts:38,67`), confirming which emails hold an account. Username collisions are public by nature; email ones are not. Signal/Apple posture. | P2 |
| Cold-outreach throttle for DMs (distinct new stranger recipients per day) | standard | Anon game caps new threads at `ANON_NEW_THREAD_RATE_PER_DAY = 5` (`constants.ts:1117-1119`) | Ordinary DMs have only `CONVERSATION_CREATE_RATE_PER_HOUR = 20` (`constants.ts:50`; `conversations/routes.ts:142-147`), not stranger-aware: 20 new DMs/hour to non-connections, 480/day. Without who-can-message this is the only spam brake. Instagram, LinkedIn, Discord. | P2 |
| Durable, queryable admin audit log of moderation actions | standard | `adminLog()` on suspend/ban (`admin/routes.ts:137`), report close (`:222`), settings (`:303-321`), export (`:393`), takedown and context view (`admin/content.ts:188,292`); viewer `GET /api/admin/logs` reads the ring newest-first (`admin/routes.ts:279-287`) + `admin/logs/page.tsx` | It is "the Redis ring tail" (`logs/page.tsx:3-4`; ADR-013): a bounded in-memory ring, not a table; entries age out, cannot be filtered by target user, vanish on Redis loss. Who banned whom, when and why needs to outlive a log ring. Discord, Slack, Mastodon (mod log). | P2 |
| Revoke public share links (post / beacon / listing / group / story), auto-revoked on audience narrowing | standard | `DELETE /api/share-links/:token` (`sharepages/routes.ts:166`) and route-side auto-revoke (`feed/service.ts:512-513`, `sharepages/service.ts:91-92`, `stories/service.ts:183-184`); UI only for stories (`StoryViewer.tsx:371-387`). | `FeedView.tsx:969`, `BeaconDetailSheet.tsx:283`, `ListingDetailSheet.tsx:476`, `GroupSettingsSection.tsx:208` call `copyShareLink` only; no revoke button; no "list my open share links" endpoint. A post/beacon/listing/group author cannot kill a link short of deleting or narrowing the object. Instagram, Luma/Partiful, Telegram. | P3 |
| Pre-send kindness nudge ("Are you sure you want to post this?") | advanced | Drunk-mode "you sure?" before cold DMs / public posts (`DrunkConfirmSheet.tsx:1-10`) only while drunk mode is on | No content-based trigger. Self-hosted version is a keyword-list nudge, not a classifier. Instagram, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge. | P2 |
| Relay calls through the server to hide my IP | advanced | TURN credentials minted (`calls/routes.ts:137` `mintTurnCredentials`); `CallOverlay.tsx:577` warns when no relay; the self-test probe already passes `iceTransportPolicy` (`apps/web/lib/selftest.ts:90`) | The real call `RTCPeerConnection` is built with `iceServers` only and no `iceTransportPolicy` (`apps/web/lib/webrtc.ts:141`); no "always relay" toggle. When TURN is configured, forcing `relay` there is a one-line toggle. WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, X. | P2 |
| Minimal metadata retention (IP addresses nulled after 90 days by nightly GC) | advanced | `runIpRetention` (`jobs/gc.ts:49-60`) nulls `users.registration_ip` and `sessions.ip`. | `reports.reporter_ip` (`schema.ts:989`), written by the unauthenticated share-page report (`sharepages/routes.ts:406`) with the schema comment "aged by the IP_RETENTION_DAYS sweep" (`schema.ts:967`), is never nulled anywhere. Share-page reporters' IPs are retained indefinitely, contradicting the documented contract, which itself points to a privacy note that does not exist. | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Who can message me (everyone / connections / nobody) | basic | P0 | TikTok, X, Snapchat, Discord, Instagram, Messenger, Threads, Telegram, Reddit, Meetup; Tinder/Bumble/Hinge/BeReal by design | `POST /api/conversations {type:'dm'}` checks only existence + `isBlockedPair` (`conversations/routes.ts:153-163`); no `dm_policy` column, no `UserSettingsSchema` key, no privacy row. Biggest gap in the area: with /people suggestions, discovery and share pages any member can open a DM with any member; only blocks stop it. |
| Mute a chat's notifications (8 h / 1 week / always) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, every chat app | `conversation_participants.muted_until` (`schema.ts:387`) exists and the push guards read it (`lib/pushGuard.ts`; `jobs/push.ts:173,238,395`), but nothing writes it: zero writers in `apps/server/src/modules` (`calls/service.ts:120` only reads), no `PATCH …/mute` route, no UI (`ChatInfoSheet.tsx:508` is a device-local sound override). `docs/FEATURES.md` #9 claims "muting". A dormant column is not a feature; counted as missing, matching the Chat section. One route plus a sheet row. |
| Report reason taxonomy (spam, harassment, hate, nudity, self-harm, scam, impersonation, underage, off-platform, IP, other) | basic | P0 | Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup | `reports.reason` is one optional free-text ≤500 chars (`schema.ts:990,1006`; `ReportSheet.tsx:121` "What happened?"); `reports.kind` encodes only the target type (`schema.ts:1004`); admin queue shows "no reason text" (`admin/reports/page.tsx:150`). Without a taxonomy there is no triage, no self-harm hook, no underage fast-lane, no off-platform or IP lane, no stats. |
| Hide online / active status (appear offline) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook/Messenger, Instagram, Discord, Bumble (Snooze) | `ws/gateway.ts:171,861` always send `online`; `show_last_seen` hides only the timestamp; no column, no UI. Anyone sharing a conversation sees the green dot; the privacy page does not warn about this. |
| Terms of Service + Privacy Policy pages (in-app and at signup) | basic | P0 | all 17 benchmark apps | `apps/web/app` has no terms/legal/privacy route; the "instance privacy note" cited in `docs/API.md:1649`, `ADR-013:118`, `docs/FEATURES.md` #54 and `admin/users/page.tsx:109` does not exist. Zero legal surface; docs promise a document the repo never shipped. The BeReal-style "DMs are not end-to-end encrypted" disclosure is also missing, and without it the no-chat-reader policy earns the product nothing. |
| Community guidelines / rules page | basic | P0 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup | No guidelines, house rules or code of conduct anywhere. Takedown notices carry a required reason but there is no published rule set to cite; reports and any future appeal need one. |
| Consent to terms/privacy at registration + re-prompt on policy change | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Snapchat, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Nextdoor, Meetup | `register/page.tsx` fields: username, display name, email, password only; `RegisterRequestSchema` (`api.ts:783-795`) has no consent field. |
| Minimum-age attestation at signup (13+ / 16+ / 18+) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor (18+), Tinder/Bumble/Hinge (18+), Meetup | No DOB/age field anywhere (`constants.ts:1150` "no age/gender fields exist"); no checkbox on register. ADR-015's no-age-filters decision does not forbid a plain attestation; PSYCHOLOGY.md is silent on it. An app that arranges meeting strangers and has a drunk mode must at least ask. |
| Delete my account / deactivate temporarily | basic | P0 | all 17 benchmark apps (GDPR/CCPA baseline) | `users/routes.ts` has GET/PATCH/avatar only (`app.delete` exists only for `/api/users/me/avatar`); `settings/account/page.tsx` has no delete. Only path is the operator editing rows by hand; the admin export route's own comment mentions "deletion requests". |
| Mute a person (hide their posts/stories from my feeds without blocking; they are never told) | basic | P1 | Instagram, X, Threads, Snapchat, Nextdoor, TikTok, Facebook (unfollow/snooze), Meetup | Feed query filters only blocks/banned (`feed/routes.ts:281-287`; `stories/routes.ts:292`); no mutes table. Only levers today are block or remove-connection, both relationship-visible. Canvas bans already prove the soft-block pattern. |
| Hide a single post from my feed | basic | P1 | Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads, TikTok, Facebook, X | No hide state in `FeedView.tsx`, feed module or schema. A plain per-post hide is fine; a "see fewer like this" ranking signal would collide with the no-algorithmic-ranking rule. Keep it a hide, not a signal. |
| Private account / account-wide default post audience / "limit past posts" | basic | P1 | TikTok, Instagram, Facebook, Threads, BeReal (hide from FoF), Reddit (follow toggle) | No users column or UserSettings key; only the per-post chip (`FeedView.tsx:469-509`), which flips to public on the Discover lane (`:505-509`). Partly moot because connections are request+accept, but public posts are reachable by any logged-in member and there is no one-tap way to re-narrow old ones. |
| Who can send me connection requests (everyone / connections-of-connections / nobody) | basic | P1 | Facebook, Discord, Snapchat, Reddit | Request path checks only block/existence (`connections/routes.ts:98-259`); no setting. Requests are accept-gated so the harm is request spam, not contact; still the standard lever is missing. |
| Sign out of all other devices in one tap | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Google | Only per-session revoke: `DELETE /api/auth/sessions/:id` (`auth/routes.ts:150-160`); `settings/sessions/page.tsx:42-51` revokes one row at a time. `revokeAllSessions` is called only by the recovery path (`recovery.ts:153`). The first response to a suspected compromise here is to burn a recovery code. |
| Forgot-password via email link (self-serve reset without a pre-saved recovery code) | basic | P1 | every mainstream app | `password_reset` exists only as stored config defaulting false (`api.ts:2877-2882`; `admin/service.ts:62,80`) with no route reading it; mailer always 503 `mailer_unavailable`; `POST /api/auth/recover` requires a one-time recovery code (`recovery.ts:135-151`, "recovery is credentials compromised, not forgot"); "The CLI reset remains the operator path" (`docs/API.md:611`); login offers only "Use a recovery code" (`login/page.tsx:447`). A user who loses password and codes is locked out until the operator runs a CLI reset. Recovery codes alone are a power-user posture. |
| Message-send flood limit (per-user cap on `POST /api/conversations/:id/messages` and comments) | basic | P1 | every mainstream chat app | `fixedWindow()` (`lib/ratelimit.ts:9`) is called from 25 module files but not from `apps/server/src/modules/messages/*`; only per-socket WS signaling guards exist (`ws/gateway.ts:341,402,580,706,729,767,805`), which do not cover REST; no `MESSAGE_RATE` constant, no cap in `docs/API.md`. One account can flood any DM/group via REST at network speed; blocks and reports are the only brake. |
| Host control of who sees the guest list / face pile (everyone / joiners only / host only) | basic | P1 | Partiful, Luma (host toggle "guests can see guest list"), Meetup (per-group attendee-list setting), Facebook Events | Every beacon DTO carries `joiners` + `joiner_count` to the whole audience (`beacons/service.ts:273-275`), and `GET /api/beacons/:id/joins` returns the full joiner list to anyone in the audience (`beacons/routes.ts:461-500`; `docs/API.md:1836`); the beacons table has only `audience` (`schema.ts:716`), `BeaconCreateSheet.tsx` offers no guest-list option, and no `guest_list`/`show_joiners` key exists in server, web or shared. On an `area` beacon that is every member in the area seeing who is showing up to meet strangers; the host cannot narrow it. The Local section covers only the joiner-side "hide me" and address-after-RSVP gaps; this is the host side. |
| Archive / hide a chat from the inbox | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram | `settings/chats/page.tsx:5` states "No archived row"; `conversation_participants` has `muted_until` but no `archived_at`/`hidden_at`. The only way to tuck a sensitive chat away is a one-sided leave that notifies the other party. |
| Message requests inbox (strangers land in Requests; accept / delete / block / report; no read state or calls before acceptance) | standard | P1 | Signal, Instagram, Messenger, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Telegram (non-contact banner) | No `message_request` concept anywhere; every new DM lands in the main list. Pairs with who-can-message; the requests inbox is what makes "everyone can message" tolerable. |
| Who can call me / silence unknown callers | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, X, Signal (via requests) | Calls allowed to any DM/group participant, blocks only (`calls/routes.ts:55-104`); no setting; ring push honours `muted_until`, which nothing sets. Follows from the DM gap: a stranger who opened a DM can ring you. |
| Who can @mention me (everyone / connections / nobody) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Snapchat, Threads, TikTok | Mentions parsed server-side against active participants with no recipient setting (`constants.ts:363-368`); mention push may override mute (`constants.ts:404`). Because mentions override mute, a mention control is the only way to stop a persistent pinger short of block. |
| Who can comment on my posts (global) + per-post "turn off comments" | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X (who can reply), Threads, Nextdoor | `feed/routes.ts:566` "any viewer of the post"; no `comments_enabled` column. Author can delete afterwards (`FeedView.tsx:1547`) but cannot prevent; for a finite calm feed a per-post off switch is the obvious lever. |
| Opt out of username/name search and of "people you may know" suggestions | standard | P1 | Meetup, Snapchat (Quick Add), Facebook, Discord | `GET /api/users?q=` prefix ILIKE finds every non-blocked, non-banned account (`users/routes.ts:117-137`); suggestions have per-card dismissal (`suggestionDismissals`) but no "don't suggest me" toggle. |
| Report + block (and/or mute) in the same flow | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Snapchat, Hinge, Bumble, Signal, Telegram | `ReportSheet.tsx:96` done-state offers only "Done"; no block checkbox. One checkbox. |
| Report status tracking / outcome notification to the reporter | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit, Threads, TikTok, Nextdoor | `reports/routes.ts` has only POST; no `GET /api/reports` (mine); admin close ring-logs only (`admin/routes.ts:160-235`). Users never learn anything happened; even a "received → reviewed" pair would do. |
| Off-platform / "something happened in person" report reason | standard | P1 | Hinge (standard), Tinder ("offline behaviour"), Bumble ("Behavior off Bumble"), Meetup (report a member after an event) | Nothing in `ReportSheet.tsx` asks whether it happened at a beacon or roulette meetup; `reports.reason` is free text (`schema.ts:990`) and `reports.kind` only names the target type (`:1004`), so a moderator cannot tell an in-person incident from a rude DM. For an app whose core loop is meeting strangers at a beacon this is the one reason every dating benchmark carries. Ships inside the taxonomy row; listed separately so the enum does not drop it again. |
| Hidden words: custom keyword/phrase/emoji filter + offensive-content filter for comments, DMs and requests | standard | P1 | Instagram, Threads, TikTok, X (mute words), Hinge, Facebook | Nothing of the kind in server, web or shared. Half the benchmark lacks it too (WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Reddit, Tinder), but this app has public comments and anonymous messages, which is exactly where it matters. |
| New non-connection DM safety banner (who this is, groups in common, Report spam / Block / Accept) | standard | P1 | Telegram, Messenger, WhatsApp (2025 safety tools), Discord (Teen Safety Assist), Hinge/Bumble | No stranger/unknown-sender/first-message handling in `ChatView.tsx`. Cheapest mitigation for the open-DM gap while who-can-message and the requests inbox are built. |
| Hide message preview in push notifications | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Instagram | `jobs/push.ts:42-62` `snippetFor()` puts `body.slice(0,140)` in every text push; no `UserSettingsSchema` key, no notifications-page row. Media/audio/location pushes being content-free is a fixed design, not a control. One UserSettings key. |
| View-once / timed media | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Snapchat (by design) | No `view_once` anywhere; MESSAGE types have no view-once kind. Feasible through the auth-checked media proxy (single-fetch tokens); screenshot blocking is not, and the copy should say so. |
| Safety Center / safety tips hub (what blocking, reporting, ghost groups and discovery actually do) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup, Reddit | `settings/help/page.tsx` is a ticket form only; `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` (`constants.ts:1057`) has no privacy/safety entry besides `rescue`; `/things` has no safety section. The scattered copy ("they are never told", "meet in public…", reciprocal receipts) already exists; collecting it is an afternoon. |
| Formal warning as a moderation outcome | standard | P1 | Tinder, Snapchat, Nextdoor, Reddit, Hinge, Bumble, Discord | `docs/FEATURES.md` #54 claims "warn/suspend/ban" but `admin/routes.ts:104,146` registers only suspend/unsuspend/ban/unban; no endpoint or UI. Docs overclaim; the Support-chat channel used by takedowns is the obvious delivery path. |
| Takedown of individual messages in public groups, comments, stories and canvas overlays | standard | P1 | Discord, Reddit, Facebook, Nextdoor, Telegram | `ADMIN_CONTENT_TYPES = post|beacon|listing` only (`constants.ts:1240`); no admin message/comment/story delete endpoint. Public-group share pages are "reportable" (SPECS 79d) but the only remedies are suspend the author or revoke the link. Must stay inside the no-chat-reader boundary: act on a reported id, never browse. |
| Appeals / request review of a suspension, ban or takedown | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, Snapchat, TikTok, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup, Telegram | No appeal concept anywhere; a suspended user cannot log in to open a support ticket (`auth/routes.ts:118` returns 403 before a session exists). Even a "write to the operator" address on the 403 screen would be an appeal path, and no operator contact field exists yet. |
| Self-serve "download my data" (takeout) | standard | P1 | TikTok, X, Facebook, Instagram, Snapchat, Reddit, Discord, Telegram | No `/api/users/me/export`; no export in `settings/account/page.tsx`. GDPR access right; the operator export already strips the right fields and could be reused. |
| New-device / new-login alert | standard | P1 | Facebook, Instagram, X, Discord, Telegram, Snapchat | No new-device/new-login handling in server or web; the only account alert is "recovery code was used" (`recovery.ts`). The Support-chat + push pipe already exists; one message on session mint from a new user-agent/IP closes it. |
| Sensitive-content controls (author content warning, blur interstitial, NSFW toggle) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Threads, X, Reddit, Telegram, Discord, TikTok (Restricted Mode), Snapchat | No nsfw/sensitive/content-warning concept in feed, `FeedView.tsx` or constants (only the anon-game avatar blur). No classifier needed: an author-set "sensitive" flag that blurs media in feed and share pages is the self-hosted version. |
| Restrict / Ignore a person (their comments visible only to them, DMs silently parked, no read receipts, they are not told) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Messenger, Threads, Discord (Ignore) | Nothing besides canvas bans. The anti-bullying tool for when blocking would escalate; the product's psychology brief is exactly the audience for it. |
| Closed / invite-only registration (instance signup gating) | standard | P1 | Mastodon, Matrix/Synapse, Rocket.Chat (open / approval / invite-only) | `InstanceSettingsSchema` (`api.ts:2893-2897`) carries only `invites_enabled`, `release_notes`, `auth`; `POST /api/auth/register` (`auth/routes.ts:27-97`) performs no instance gate; "register-with-token degrades to invite: null" (`admin/routes.ts:289-291`). Anyone who reaches the host can create an account. It is the first anti-spam and child-safety control an operator reaches for. |
| Copyright / IP report reason + legal-request intake (DMCA / takedown notices) | standard | P2 | Reddit (standard; copyright form), Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, BeReal (IP in the report taxonomy) | No `dmca`, `copyright` or `intellectual` hit anywhere in `apps/server`, `apps/web`, `packages/shared` or `docs`; `SUPPORT_CATEGORIES` (`constants.ts:1245`) is bug/question/idea/safety with no legal category; share pages are public and crawlable, so a US-hosted instance has DMCA exposure with no designated-agent or notice path. Realistic version: `ip` in the reason enum, a `legal` support category and an operator contact on the About block. |
| Who can tag me in photos + tag review | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X | No photo tagging exists. Moot until tagging ships; if it ships it must ship with this control on day one. |
| Story reply / resharing controls | standard | P2 | Instagram, Snapchat, WhatsApp | No `allow_replies`/`reply_policy` in stories module or `api.ts`. |
| Automated spam / scam / abuse detection (classifiers, SCAM/FAKE labels, link blocklists) | standard | P2 | Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Discord, Bumble (Deception Detector), Tinder, Nextdoor | Only rate limits (`lib/ratelimit.ts`) and SSRF-guarded unfurls (`modules/previews`, `lib/urls.ts`); no classifier. Self-hosted, no ML; a plain domain blocklist plus duplicate-message throttle for new accounts is the realistic version. |
| Suspicious / external link warning interstitial | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Discord, Messenger, Telegram | No interstitial in `ChatView.tsx`; links open directly. |
| Group context card when added by someone you don't know (who added you, created when, member count, Exit, Safety tools) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp | No `added_by` anywhere. Lower risk here because the who-can-add-me setting exists. |
| Chat lock / app screen lock (PIN or biometric, hidden folder) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, Snapchat (My Eyes Only), Telegram (passcode) | No app_lock/passcode/biometric in web or shared. A PWA can do a local PIN overlay + WebAuthn re-auth cheaply. |
| Privacy checkup wizard | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Facebook, Instagram (Privacy Center) | `settings/privacy/page.tsx` is a flat list of thirteen rows (five ToggleRows, eight SelectRows); no guided flow or onboarding step. |
| Crisis / self-harm resources (on report reason, on search terms) | standard | P2 | Facebook, Instagram, Reddit, Snapchat (Here For You), TikTok, X | No self-harm report reason (see taxonomy); no crisis/helpline copy anywhere. Depends on the taxonomy landing first; self-hosted instances need an operator-configurable resource list, not a hardcoded hotline. |
| Timeout / temporary suspension with a duration | standard | P2 | Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Facebook | Suspend is indefinite and manual (`admin/routes.ts:104-158`). |
| Account status page (removed content, warnings, restrictions, strikes) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, Discord (Account Standing), TikTok | No `/api/users/me/moderation` route or settings page; takedown notices are one-off Support messages. |
| Graduated enforcement / strike system | standard | P2 | Snapchat, Nextdoor, TikTok, Reddit, Bumble, Tinder | Admin actions are binary suspend/ban with a free-text note; no strike count on users. |
| New-account restrictions (account-age / activity gates before DMs, posting, group creation; verification levels) | standard | P2 | Reddit, Nextdoor, Discord, Luma, WhatsApp (unknown-account limits) | No `account_age`/captcha logic in server or constants; `docs/FEATURES.md` #44 lists account-age signals as planned. |
| CAPTCHA / proof-of-work at signup | standard | P2 | TikTok, Reddit, Discord, Facebook | Register is rate-limited 3/h/IP only (`auth/routes.ts`). Self-hosted ethos rules out reCAPTCHA; an invite-only registration mode or offline PoW challenge would fit. |
| Group ban list (kicked members cannot rejoin; host blocklist) + purge a member's messages | standard | P2 | Discord, Luma, Meetup, Telegram (delete all + ban), Facebook groups | No `group_bans`/`conversation_bans` in `schema.ts`. A kicked member holding the invite link can walk back in. |
| Photo / identity verification badge in discovery | standard | P2 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Nextdoor (address), Snapchat/X (ID age assurance) | No verified/verification concept in discovery, profiles or web. Conflicts with the no-third-party ethos; an operator-granted vouch badge is the self-hosted analogue. |
| Quiet hours / Do-Not-Disturb schedule for notifications | standard | P2 | Telegram, Slack, Discord, iOS/Android | `settings/notifications/page.tsx:171-217` offers ringtone/ringback/message-sound only; no quiet-hours key in `UserSettingsSchema`. With no per-chat mute either, the only off-switch is the OS. |
| Search-engine noindex / robots.txt on public share pages | standard | P2 | Signal/Telegram link-only surfaces are noindex | `apps/web/app/s/[token]/page.tsx:15` `generateMetadata` emits OG/twitter tags but no robots directive; `apps/web/public` has no robots.txt; no `noindex` anywhere in `apps/web/app`. Revocation exists but an indexed copy outlives it. For a privacy-first app, token-URL posts should not be crawlable. |
| Group rules acceptance / membership screening on join | standard | P3 | Discord, Meetup (event questions), Luma | Join-request queue has no rules text or questionnaire (`visibility.ts:227-302`). |
| Hide a blocked person's messages inside shared groups | standard | P3 | Instagram, Discord, Messenger (hide or collapse) | `docs/API.md:792` "Does NOT leave shared groups or delete history"; `:807` both stay; `:1430` "Blocked users are serialized normally here"; no client-side hide in `ChatView.tsx`. WhatsApp matches this behaviour; the current design choice is documented, not accidental. |
| Default disappearing timer for all new chats | standard | P3 | Signal ("default timer for new chats") | Per-chat disappearing is shipped (has table); no default: no key in `UserSettingsSchema` (`api.ts:1237-1270`), nothing written by `settings/routes.ts:60-77`, no row in `settings/chats/page.tsx`; `DISAPPEAR_TTL_OPTIONS_S` is only ever applied per conversation. |
| Temporary "Limits" (auto-park comments/DMs from non-connections for N days during a harassment spike) | advanced | P2 | Instagram | No such setting; depends on who-can-message and who-can-comment existing first. |
| Block new accounts the blocked person may create / pre-emptive block of phone contacts | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Threads, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | No phone numbers or device fingerprints stored; blocks are user-id pairs (`blocks/routes.ts`). No identity signal to key on in a phone-free self-hosted app; invite-graph provenance is the only lead. |
| Inbound harmful-message prompt ("Does this bother you?" → one-tap report) | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Hinge, Bumble, Discord (Teen Safety Assist) | No classifier; message menu offers Report only (`ChatView.tsx:1783-1810`). |
| Nudity blur in DMs (Private Detector / explicit image filter) | advanced | P3 | Bumble, Instagram, Discord | `uploads/pipeline.ts` does EXIF strip + resize only; no image classification. Open models exist (Bumble released theirs) but it is a dependency the project forbids by default. |
| Screenshot detection / blocking / screenshot indicator | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, Signal (screen security), Instagram (view-once), BeReal (indicator), Telegram (secret chats) | Only the support-ticket "screenshot" attachment exists. A web PWA cannot detect or block screenshots; the honest move is to say so in the Safety Center. |
| Safety numbers / key verification / key transparency | advanced | P3 | Signal, WhatsApp, Telegram (secret chats) | No E2EE yet, so nothing to verify. |
| Age verification / age assurance (ID, facial estimation) | advanced | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Discord, Snapchat, Bumble, Hinge | No age field at all (`constants.ts:1150`). Out of scope for a self-hosted instance; attestation is the realistic bar. |
| Teen accounts / parental supervision (Family Center) | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, Threads, Discord | No age known; no supervision model anywhere. An instance either admits minors (then needs this) or declares 18+ in the missing ToS; the owner must decide. |
| Ban-evasion detection (device / alt-account linking) | advanced | P3 | Reddit, TikTok, Snapchat, Bumble, Tinder, Hinge | Bans are user-row flags only (`admin/routes.ts:146`); IPs are nulled after 90 days. Invite-graph provenance (who invited the banned user) is the cheap, privacy-respecting signal here. |
| Transparency report / published moderation statistics | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor, Reddit, X, Snapchat, Discord, Meta | No aggregate reports endpoint beyond the admin queue. |
| Community Notes / fact-check / AI-generated content labels | advanced | P3 | X, Instagram, Threads, TikTok, Facebook | Nothing of the kind. Feeds are finite and connection-scoped; low relevance. |
| Group-level AutoMod (keyword/regex filters, mention-spam, raid protection: pause invites / pause DMs) | advanced | P3 | Discord | No group-level filters; `invites_paused` exists only per-user and instance-wide. Group admins can kill a public share token via visibility switch-back, which partly covers the raid case. |

### Planned already

- **Story audience (public / connections / close friends / hide-from)** — standard, P1. Stories are fixed to friends-of (`stories/routes.ts:60` `isFriendOf`); `docs/SPECS.md` v1.0b #98 designs public/connections/exclusive/hide-from. Design written, not built. Snapchat, Instagram, WhatsApp Status, Facebook.
- **End-to-end encryption** — standard, P2. `docs/FEATURES.md` #57 backlog; `docs/SPECS.md` standing decision "E2EE… stay backlog"; session/device registry exists. The compensating control (no admin route reads chats, ADR-013) is policy, not cryptography; the legal page must say chats are not E2EE until this ships.
- **Fediverse / third-party-chat interoperability with an explicit opt-in consent screen** — advanced, P3. `docs/FEATURES.md` backlog "federation". When built, the Threads-style consent screen ("posts leave this server; deletes may not propagate") is mandatory.
- Adjacent, noted in the has-table: v1.0d #107 (owner toggles to widen the connections list) and v1.0d #110 (public profile share link, which needs an owner toggle on day one).

### Deliberately not built

- **Private dislike / downvote used as a ranking signal** (Instagram test, TikTok). `docs/PSYCHOLOGY.md` forbids algorithmic engagement ranking and variable-reward mechanics; feeds are finite. The ban is right: a downvote only has value as a ranking input, and this product has no ranker. A plain per-post hide is the allowed cousin and should be built (see Missing, P1).
- **"Seen X minutes ago" timestamps, public story view counts, "active now" / "X and Y are hanging out" pings** (WhatsApp, Instagram, Snapchat, Facebook). `docs/PSYCHOLOGY.md` "Never build"; ADR-009 "no receipt payload anywhere carries a timestamp"; `docs/FEATURES.md` "Explicitly NOT building" under v1.0d. The ban is right and it is the product's strongest privacy stance. It is also currently violated by the product itself: the profile hero prints "last seen 43m ago" (`apps/web/lib/format.ts:18-26` `formatLastSeen`, rendered at `u/[username]/page.tsx:400`) with a default of Everyone, and the green online dot cannot be turned off. Enforce the rule on presence the way it is enforced on receipts.
- **Operator private-chat monitor / bulk message reader as a moderation tool** (Discord server mods read channels; Telegram/Meta review reported DMs). `docs/SPECS.md` standing decision "No private-chat monitor in admin"; ADR-013, ADR-020. The ban is right for a self-hosted app where the operator is often a friend of the members; it is the one thing that makes "hosted by a person, not a company" trustworthy, as long as the legal pages admit it is a promise and not encryption. Recorded so nobody "fixes" the comment/message-takedown gap by adding one: takedowns must act on a reported id, never browse.

### How it looks

- **Register (`phone-register.png`, `register/page.tsx:175`).** Heading "Join the fun", four fields, Create account, nothing else: no "By continuing you agree to…", no age line, no "hosted by". Instagram puts the terms sentence under the button; Hinge and Bumble gate on DOB first. Fix: one consent sentence linking /terms and /privacy (operator-edited markdown with a sane default), an "I'm 16 or older" checkbox stored on the user row, and "Hosted by <operator>" on the auth card.
- **Login (`phone-login.png`, `login/page.tsx:148-154`).** A suspended or banned user sees "Could not sign in. Check your connection and try again." Bumble, Tinder and Discord show the reason and a contact. Fix: branch on `account_suspended`/`account_banned` and show an operator contact, which first needs a field in `InstanceSettingsSchema` (`api.ts:2893-2897` has none). Also: the only recovery door is a muted "Lost everything?" link with no "Forgot password?" under the field; a user without a saved code hits a dead end with no "ask the operator" path.
- **Settings → Account and → Devices & security (`settings-account.png`, `settings-security.png`).** No Delete account, no Download my data, no Change password, no Change email, no Sign out all devices; email is an unlabelled grey line with no verified state. WhatsApp Account and Telegram Privacy & Security put Delete at the bottom in red; Telegram Devices has "Terminate all other sessions". Fix: an Account group with Change password (re-auth), Change email, Download my data (reuse the admin export), Delete account (re-auth, grace period, explains tombstones); a "Sign out all other devices" button on Devices.
- **Privacy & safety (`settings-privacy.png`, `settings/privacy/page.tsx`).** Thirteen rows: five `ToggleRow`s whose control is an "On"/"Off" word button (`btn`/`btn-primary`, `:283-298`) that reads as a command, with the grey "Off" looking disabled; it already carries `role="switch"` and `aria-checked` (`:289-290`), so the problem is visual, not semantic. Eight native `<select>`s styled as text inputs (`SelectRow` `:102,129,144,179,186,193,200,218`), four of them identical selects for the Activity strip (`:179-200`). Helper copy is `muted small` (`--muted` #9aa0ae at 12.5px, `globals.css:61,315-321`), legible (about 6:1 on `--panel`) but the smallest type on the page. "Mood effects" (`:167`) and "Default beacon audience" (`:146`) are filed under Privacy; two labels carry emoji (🎭 `:220`, 👀 `:234`); Blocked people is a card with an "Open" button (`:244-250`) instead of a chevron row with a count; nothing says the green online dot cannot be hidden. WhatsApp and Telegram use one switch for every boolean and a value-row ("Last seen — Everyone ›") that opens a radio list. Fix: a visual Switch and a PickerRow component, four cards (Visibility, Who can…, Activity, Safety), misfiled rows re-homed to Appearance, and a one-line note under Last seen that online status is always visible until appear-offline ships.
- **Chat settings sheet (`chat-info.png`, `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`).** Opens on nine theme swatches and seven wallpapers; no identity row, no Mute, no Block, no Report, no Clear chat (zero matches for any of them in the file); disappearing messages is buried below the wallpapers (`:674-702`); the group members list (`:748+`) has no remove or role control although `ChannelMembersSheet.tsx:95-130` already has both. WhatsApp contact info: hero → media → mute → disappearing → wallpaper far down → block/report at the bottom. Fix: identity row → Mute (8h / 1w / always) → Disappearing → Members (reuse the channel sheet) → Media → Appearance (collapsed) → Clear chat / Block / Report / Leave. Also: chat-list rows have no long-press or swipe, so mute/archive/mark-unread are unreachable from the list.
- **Message delete (`chat-msg-longpress.png`, `ChatView.tsx:1334`).** A native `window.confirm('Delete this message for everyone?')`; there is no "Delete for me". WhatsApp, Telegram and Signal offer both in an action sheet. Fix: bottom sheet with Delete for me / Delete for everyone / Cancel.
- **Feed cards (`phone-feed-list.png`, `feed-post-menu.png`, `FeedView.tsx:991`).** Other people's posts have no ⋯ at all; the ⋯ renders only on own posts and its single item is "Delete post"; `FeedView.tsx` does not import `ReportSheet`. The Discover lane shows strangers' public posts with no report path. Instagram, Threads, X and Facebook put Report / Not interested / Mute / Block behind ⋯ on every post. Fix: ⋯ on every card; others get Report (new post/comment kinds) · Hide this post · Block @user.
- **Rescue / SOS (`phone-local.png`, `phone-chats-more.png`).** The emergency control is a 13px 🆘 emoji (`discovery.module.css:504-507`) inside an 8px-padded `btn-icon` ghost button with no visible label (`RescueSheet.tsx:35-40`; `globals.css:442-444`) on the Local top bar, duplicated in the Chats ⋯ overflow (`ConversationListView.tsx:232-235`); from Feed or Profile it is three taps and a guess. Uber, Lyft and Bumble use a persistent labelled pill. Fix: one labelled 44px "Rescue" pill with an SVG life-ring on every tab root; remove it from the overflow.
- **"Make me discoverable" (`phone-local.png`, `DiscoverySection.tsx:132,213-290`).** The stranger-meeting opt-in is the most prominent CTA on the screen; the sheet says what it does but there is no first-time explainer of what strangers see, that it lapses in an hour, or how to report. Bumble and Hinge show a safety sheet before the first match. Fix: a one-time per-account consent sheet with Continue / Not now.
- **Help & about (`settings-help.png`, `settings/help/page.tsx:222`).** The "about" is one line, "Bored Chat · self-hosted": no operator identity or contact, no version, no Terms/Privacy, no "what we keep". Telegram Help has FAQ, Privacy Policy and Ask a question. Fix: an About block with operator name and contact, version, retention summary, links to the legal pages and a Safety section.
- **Profile hero (`phone-profile-arjun.png`, `u/[username]/page.tsx:400`, `lib/format.ts:18-26`).** "last seen 43m ago" is minute-precise presence, the exact pattern the never-build list rejects for receipts, with a default of Everyone. Fix: coarsen to "active today / this week" or dot only.
- **Unread badge (`phone-chats.png`, `list.module.css:169`).** `pulseGlow 2.4s ease-in-out infinite` on the unread count (stilled only under `prefers-reduced-motion`, `:159`), the attention hook PSYCHOLOGY.md forbids. No mainstream messenger animates unread counts. Fix: static, animate once on change.

### If I had one week

1. **Day 1: the legal and consent surface, end to end.** Add `/terms`, `/privacy` and `/guidelines` pages rendered from operator-editable markdown in instance settings (ship real defaults that state: no ads, no contact sync, no admin route reads chats but chats are not E2EE and the operator holds the database, 90-day IP retention, what a report forwards). Add an operator contact field to `InstanceSettingsSchema`, the consent sentence and the "I'm 16 or older" attestation to `RegisterRequestSchema` and the register page, an About block in Help with that contact, and the `account_suspended`/`account_banned` branch at login with the contact as the appeal path. This also fixes the four docs that cite a privacy note that does not exist.
2. **Day 2: build mute and fix reporting.** `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/mute` writing `muted_until` (8h / 1w / forever / null), a Mute row at the top of the chat-settings sheet plus a list-row long-press, muted glyph on the row. Add a `reason` enum to reports (spam, harassment, hate, nudity, self-harm, scam, impersonation, underage, off_platform, ip, other) and `post|comment|story|listing|group` target kinds; put ⋯ → Report / Hide / Block on every feed card and StoryViewer; a "Also block @user" checkbox on the ReportSheet; a `GET /api/reports/mine` with received/reviewed states shown in Settings → Privacy. Admin queue filters by reason; `off_platform` and `ip` get their own lanes, and a `legal` support category covers DMCA-style notices until a real intake exists.
3. **Days 3–4: who-can-message plus a requests inbox.** `users.dm_policy` (everyone / connections / nobody), enforced at `POST /api/conversations {type:'dm'}` and at call creation (404-shaped like blocks); a `conversation_participants.accepted_at` so a stranger's first DM lands in a Requests section with Accept / Delete / Block / Report, no read receipts, no ring and no typing before acceptance; a stranger banner inside the chat (who this is, groups in common) as the first thing a recipient sees. Add `MESSAGE_RATE_PER_MINUTE` with `fixedWindow()` on the message POST while in that file.
4. **Day 5 morning: account controls.** `DELETE /api/users/me` with re-auth and a grace period (tombstone messages, delete profile/posts/media, revoke sessions), `GET /api/users/me/export` reusing the admin export, `POST /api/auth/password` for logged-in change, "Sign out all other devices" (the `revokeAllSessions` helper already exists), and an `appear_offline` flag honoured by `gateway.ts` where `online` is emitted. Null `reports.reporter_ip` in `runIpRetention` so the documented contract is true.
5. **Day 5 afternoon: give group admins, DM participants and beacon hosts their own tools.** Reuse `ChannelMembersSheet` for groups so admins can kick and promote; add "Delete for me" to the message menu and "Clear chat" / silent DM leave (suppress the `participant_left` system message for DMs) to the chat-settings sheet; make the Leave row render for DMs. Add a `guest_list` column on beacons (everyone / joiners / host) read by the DTO pile and `GET /api/beacons/:id/joins`, with a row on the create sheet. Add the `noindex` meta to `/s/[token]` while touching share pages.


---

## Notifications: a good engine with no dashboard

**Verdict.** The delivery pipeline is complete for a PWA: presence-aware enqueue, read-elsewhere suppression, debounce, block belt re-checked at execution, drunk-mode undo hold, identity-free anonymous copy, and a ring push with Answer/Decline actions, TTL and silent retraction. The user-facing control layer on top of it is close to nonexistent: one push switch, one category toggle filed under Privacy, no mute (the `muted_until` column has existed since migration 0000, the worker honours it and the DTO already exposes it (API.md:53, `lib/dto.ts:321`), but no route writes it and no UI reads it), no lock-screen preview control, no app-icon badge, no unread divider, no mark-unread, and no activity tab, so kudos, matches and plan changes evaporate the moment push is off. Two of the gaps are security/privacy, not polish: message bodies always ride the push, and logout does not revoke the device's push subscription, so a signed-out browser keeps receiving chat text. The docs are inconsistent with the product: API.md forbids an all-off switch citing a digest that was never built, while Settings lets you go fully silent anyway, and reactions and comments consequently notify nobody in any form. **Basic-tier coverage is 42% (18 of 43 basic rows in the three tables below are "has"; half-built counts as not shipped), standard-tier is 14% (10 of 70); advanced is 1 of 12.** The seven P0s (mute end to end, logout-revokes-push, per-category toggles, preview hiding, comment notifications, app-icon badge, subscription keep-alive) are mostly cheap because the server already distinguishes every trigger it needs.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

Fires once per device on first login from `GlobalOverlays.tsx:40`, before the user has a reason to want it; contextual asking (first chat opened, first plan joined) would convert better. `apps/server/src/modules/push/routes.ts:14-69`, `apps/web/lib/push.ts:29-49` |

Engineering plumbing that is present but is not a user-facing feature and is not counted above: pure push-guard unit tests (`apps/server/test/v05.integration.test.ts:51-86`, `test/v071.integration.test.ts:58-80`, run without infra), stale-subscription pruning (404/410 or 5 consecutive failures deletes the row, `jobs/push.ts:86-99`), and multi-device fan-out (`sendToSubscriptions` loops every row for the user, endpoints coexist via upsert).

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Push status honesty (ON / OFF / Blocked / Not supported) with unblock steps and re-askable Enable | basic | UI states and unblock copy (`app/settings/notifications/page.tsx:57-59,137-157`); re-checks on focus/visibilitychange (:79-83); iOS copy correctly demands A2HS first | "ON" is computed from the browser's PushSubscription alone (`sub !== null && permission === 'granted'`) and never reconciled with the server. The server silently deletes the row on 404/410 or 5 failures; the client POSTs only inside `enablePush` (`lib/push.ts:29-49`), never on boot; `sw.js` has no `pushsubscriptionchange`; there is no GET to read server state (API.md §1.9:678-684 lists VAPID, POST, DELETE only). Result: the row can read ON while the server will never push. | P1 |
| Durable, readable record of private appreciation (kudos) after the fact | standard | `BeaconStrip.tsx:276-281` banner, populated from the live `beacon.kudos` frame; `lib/store.ts:191` is "received this session"; POST only (API.md:848) | No GET, no persistence surface. Offline recipients with push off never learn they were liked, which undercuts PSYCHOLOGY §9. | P1 |
| Tab-bar badges with numeric counts (not just a dot) | basic | Dot for Chats and Profile via `.some()` boolean, aria-label "new activity" (`components/Tabs.tsx:25-45`) | No count, no Feed/Local dots. | P1 |
| Unread indicator on secondary inboxes (anonymous inbox, requests) | standard | Requests dot on the overflow button (`ConversationListView.tsx:36,100`) | The Anonymous inbox link sits inside the same overflow menu (:244-255) with no dot of its own; a new anonymous message is invisible in-app unless the push landed. | P1 |

A user cannot mute a chat today. Biggest hole in the area and a contract gap. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, Discord, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor and TikTok all have it. | P0 |
