For a classifieds board the inquiry is the conversion event (FB Marketplace, OLX-style boards all push it); the offline seller learns nothing until they reopen the app. | P1 |
| Game "your move" notification (tic-tac-toe) | standard | The challenge is a real `game_ttt` message and pushes "Tic-tac-toe challenge" (`modules/games/routes.ts:163-183`, `jobs/push.ts:53-54`) | Each move is `publishMessageUpdated` only (`games/routes.ts:267`): no push, no unread bump, so an offline opponent is never told it is their turn. | P3 |
| Scheduled-message outcome notice to the author (sent / cancelled because of leave, demotion, block, failed voice note) | standard | `scheduled.updated` WS frame to the author's own live sockets (`modules/scheduled/service.ts:59-66`) | Cancellation branch (:140-150) sets status, orphans attachments and returns; no push job, no system row (`grep enqueue` returns nothing). An offline author never learns a scheduled send silently failed. | P2 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Per-category push toggles (messages, groups, calls, mentions, plans, requests, waves, anon, support, reactions) | basic | P0 | all 17 benchmarks | Single Enable/Disable (`app/settings/notifications/page.tsx:137-157`); UserSettings has only `wave_nudges` (`packages/shared/src/api.ts:1238-1270`); the push queue already carries 7 job kinds (`queues.ts:30-113`) and roughly twenty distinct user-visible triggers (every row of the Has table) but the user gets one switch. The explainer discloses only 4 of them (messages, calls, sparked plans, waves). |
| In-app notification center / Activity tab (bell) listing mentions, replies, kudos, plan changes, requests, waves grouped by time | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Discord, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | Tabs are Chats/Local/Feed/Profile (`components/Tabs.tsx:16-21`); kudos are "received this session" in `lib/store.ts:191`. Nothing durable records what happened while you were away outside chats; kudos, nudges, matches and plan-change notes vanish with push off or iOS without A2HS. Not in SPECS or FEATURES (`grep -i 'activity center|notification center|digest'` returns nothing). P1 rather than P0 because it needs a new table and a new surface; the cheap P0s (comment push, durable kudos) cover the worst losses first. |
| Comment / reply notification on my feed post (push or in-app) | basic | P0 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | Comments fan out nothing (`packages/shared/src/ws.ts:319-332` has `story.new`/`post.new` only, API.md §1.36/§2.3). Comments are conversation, not an approval score, so PSYCHOLOGY does not forbid this; today an author finds replies only by reopening the post. |
| App icon badge count (Badging API) | basic | P0 | all 17 | `grep setAppBadge` in `apps/web` source returns nothing. An installed PWA shows no unread indicator on the home-screen icon; `navigator.setAppBadge` is a ten-line change. |
| Hide message preview on the lock screen (Name & message / Name only / Nothing) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Facebook, Instagram, Snapchat; OS-only in the rest | Body is always the first 140 chars and title is always sender/group (`jobs/push.ts:62,419-427`); no setting in UserSettings. A PWA cannot rely on OS-level preview hiding the way native apps can, so the in-app switch matters more here, not less. |
| Unread divider ("New messages" line) with jump-to-first-unread | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Facebook, Instagram | `grep -i 'unread|new messages'` in `ChatView.tsx` finds only `jumpToSeq` (:377) and a public-group warning. `last_read_seq` already gives the exact position. |
| Push subscription keep-alive: SW `pushsubscriptionchange` handler + re-POST on app boot/login | basic | P0 | Telegram Web, Twitter Lite, Messenger web | `sw.js` has only install/activate/fetch/push/notificationclick (lines 16, 26, 36, 98, 143); the only POST is inside `enablePush` from the explainer's Enable button (`PushExplainer.tsx:77`). Combined with server-side pruning, a device's push dies silently and permanently until the user manually Disables and re-Enables. This is the mechanism behind the "ON can lie" row above. |
| Cross-chat Mentions list / mentions inbox | standard | P1 | X, Discord, TikTok, Threads, Reddit | In-chat highlight only (`ChatView.tsx:539-554`); mentions are server-stamped in `metadata.mentions` (API.md §1.33). Data already exists; cheap, and the first thing a group-heavy user asks for. |
| Replies in threads I participated in | standard | P1 | Threads, Reddit, Nextdoor, Discord, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | Needs comment-notification plumbing first; a digest row would satisfy PSYCHOLOGY §6. |
| New-posts indicator on the Feed tab (quiet dot, resolves on open) | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, TikTok | `post.new` already arrives live and is adopted silently (`lib/connection.ts:339`); dots exist only for Chats/Profile (`Tabs.tsx:34`). One boolean. Position, stated once for the whole audit: build it. A dot on a tab the user is already looking at is user-pulled information, not an app-pushed nudge (rule 4) and not a withheld-then-released teaser ("Never build"); it tells someone who opened the app that people they follow posted, and rule 5's hard "you're all caught up" end still applies once they tap. The Feed and Stories sections should carry the same position. |
| Unread-@mention badge on chat rows | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Discord, Telegram | Count badge only (`ConversationListView.tsx:202-206`); data exists. |
| Document title / favicon unread count on the web client | standard | P1 | WhatsApp Web, Telegram Web, Discord, Signal Desktop, Reddit web | `grep document.title` in `apps/web` source returns nothing. Desktop users have no unread signal when the tab is in the background except the ding. |
| Group notification mode: All / Mentions only / Nothing | basic | P1 | Discord, Snapchat, Facebook, Telegram | No `notification_level` column, no setting in API.md §1.4. Plan groups and squads are the core loop; "mentions only" is the first thing a busy group needs. Mute-forever + mention override is a crude stand-in once mute ships. |
| Mute a person's stories / posts (soft mute, distinct from block) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, X, Snapchat, Telegram | Block is the only way to quiet someone (`modules/blocks`, hard 404 both ways); too blunt for a community app. |
| Pause all notifications for a duration (15 min / 1 h / 8 h / 24 h) or manual DND | standard | P1 | Facebook/Messenger, Instagram, Threads, Discord, Signal | No setting; API.md:760-762 says "no all-off switch, by design". A timed pause is the honest middle ground §6 asks for; users already reach "off" via push Disable + ringtone Off + sound Off, so the rule is inconsistent with the UI. |
| Inline reply from the notification | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Facebook/Messenger, Telegram, Discord | Actions only on `call.ring` (`sw.js:133-141`). Text actions are Chrome-Android only; worth doing there since the SW already does durable POSTs for call reject. |
| Mark-as-read action from the notification | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Facebook | An explicit user tap is not a display-side receipt, so it does not violate the receipt-honesty rule at `sw.js:1-6`; same fetch pattern as Decline. |
| Mark chat as unread / mark all read | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook/Messenger, Instagram, Discord, Signal | No endpoint (API.md §1.4/§1.5), no row actions. Receipts are `last_read_seq` integers, so "mark unread" needs a separate flag, not a receipt rewind. |
| Unread filter chips in the chat inbox | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook/Messenger, WhatsApp, Telegram | No filter UI; cheap client-side filter on `unread_count`. |
| Per-device push subscription list / revoke in Devices & security | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Discord | Sessions page shows sessions only; `user_agent` is stored on `push_subscriptions` (`db/schema.ts:681`) but there is no list endpoint (API.md §1.9). The data to show it is already stored. |
| Calendar invite / ICS export for plans | standard | P1 | Meetup, Partiful, Luma, Facebook events, Discord events | `grep '.ics|text/calendar|add to calendar'` in BeaconDetailSheet and beacons/routes returns nothing. Zero-dependency, offline-first, lets the user's own calendar do the reminding; fits the ethos. |
| TTL on time-sensitive non-call pushes (starting-soon T-30, beacon lit, ritual re-up) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal | `sendToSubscriptions` options are passed only for the ring and ring-stop (`jobs/push.ts:300-306,318-322`); `processBeaconPushJob` (:107-127) sends with none, so "Starting in about 30 minutes" (`jobs/beacons.ts:102`) can be delivered hours later after the plan has started. Violates PSYCHOLOGY rule 2. |
| Desktop notification while the tab is open but hidden/unfocused (in-page Notification API fallback) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp Web, Telegram Web, Slack, Discord | A push job is enqueued only for participants with no live socket (`messages/service.ts:166-183`); a background tab holds a socket, so no push is ever sent, and the client never calls `new Notification` or `showNotification` itself. On desktop the most common state is "tab open, other window focused" and this app is silent except for a chime. |
| Install-to-home-screen prompt tied to push enablement (beforeinstallprompt on Chromium, A2HS steps on iOS) | standard | P2 | Telegram Web, Twitter Lite, Messenger web, most installable PWAs | `grep -i 'beforeinstallprompt|add to home'` in `apps/web` source hits only the unblock copy on the settings page (`notifications/page.tsx:42`). iOS users cannot get push at all until installed, and nothing in the product asks them to install before they discover that in Settings. |
| Category granularity: Off / from connections / from everyone | standard | P2 | Instagram, Threads, Telegram | Depends on categories existing first; moderate value since most triggers are already connection-scoped. |
| Activity grouping by actor with named faces ("Ana, Raj and 2 others replied") | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X, Threads, Snapchat, Nextdoor | No activity model in the schema. Compatible with "faces not counters" as long as rows name people; depends on the center. |
| Activity filters (All / Mentions / Replies / Requests) | standard | P2 | X, TikTok, Threads, Reddit, Discord | Depends on the center. |
| Mark all notifications read; per-item hide / "turn off these" | standard | P2 | Facebook, Reddit, X, Discord | Depends on the center; no endpoint in API.md. |
| Follow a post / turn off notifications for a specific post, beacon or listing | standard | P2 | Reddit, Facebook, Nextdoor, Discord | No per-object notification setting; moot until anything about posts notifies. |
| Show notifications while muted / mute sound only (deliver silently) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram | No `silent` flag in `jobs/push.ts`; mute = no push at all. Web push supports `options.silent`; polish once mute exists. |
| Mute messages and calls separately per chat | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook/Messenger | `muted_until` suppresses rings with no override (API.md:3097); one column silences both. |
| Per-chat exceptions overriding category defaults | standard | P2 | Telegram, Signal, WhatsApp | Depends on categories. |
| Mute a host, creator, group board or community's notifications (non-chat object) | standard | P2 | Nextdoor, Meetup/Partiful/Luma, Reddit, Facebook, WhatsApp channels | Beacon pushes have presence and debounce guards but no mute guard because beacons are not conversations. |
| Quiet hours / scheduled Do Not Disturb (Quiet Mode with status to senders, recap on exit) | advanced | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Signal | `grep -i 'quiet hours|do not disturb|dnd'` across apps/ and SPECS returns nothing. Most benchmarks delegate to OS Focus; the PWA cannot. The natural implementation of §6's "batch, never silence"; rescue/ring need an override rule. |
| DND / profile exceptions (allow calls, allow mentions, allowed people) | advanced | P2 | Signal, Instagram, Discord | No DND; the rescue-overrides-mute rule in `jobs/push.ts:329-338` is the seed of an exceptions model. |
| In-app banner for an incoming DM while browsing another screen | standard | P2 | TikTok, Discord, Instagram, Telegram, WhatsApp | Toasts are own-action confirmations only (`lib/toast.ts`, API.md §1.56); zero `toast(` calls in `lib/connection.ts`. Deliberate restraint; a tappable banner would still be rule-compatible. |
| Custom notification sound upload / sound library | standard | P2 | Telegram, WhatsApp, Signal | Three fixed synthesized patterns in `lib/sounds.ts`. Would need MinIO-hosted audio (allowed by ethos). |
| Moderator / admin notifications (new reports, mod queue) | advanced | P2 | Reddit, Discord, Facebook groups | Reports create no job (`grep enqueue` in `modules/admin/*.ts` returns nothing). A self-hosted owner is usually one person and will miss reports. |
| Admin / ops visibility of push state (VAPID configured, delivery failures) | advanced | P2 | Reddit, Discord, TikTok | `grep -i vapid` in admin modules and `app/admin` returns nothing; TURN state is surfaced (API.md §1.55). An instance with push silently disabled looks healthy from the panel. |
| Email notifications with per-category toggles | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Discord, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | No mailer exists (`grep nodemailer|sendMail` hits only config/migration comments; test-send is always 503 at API.md:2925). The closest peers (chat apps) send no email either. |
| Email digest / weekly "what you missed" | standard | P2 | Facebook, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Meetup/Luma, Hinge | Not in SPECS or FEATURES; no mailer. PSYCHOLOGY §6's digest is in-app/push; email is a separate, unplanned channel. |
| Mandatory account-security emails + unsubscribe links for the rest | standard | P2 | Instagram, TikTok, Snapchat, X, Discord, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | No mailer; SMTP fields save in `app/admin/auth/page.tsx`, nothing sends. Tied to the security-alerts row. |
| Rich push content: sender avatar as the notification icon (and media thumbnail where the no-leak rule allows) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Messenger, Telegram, Signal | `icon`/`badge` are hard-coded to `/icons/icon-192.png` (`sw.js:129-130`); payload carries only title/body/tag/data.url (`jobs/push.ts:438-444`). The avatar icon does not conflict with the placeholder-body rule. |
| renotify / vibrate pattern on collapsed notifications | standard | P2 | Telegram Web, Slack | Notifications collapse by tag but set no `renotify` and no `vibrate` (`sw.js:124-131`), so a second message from the same chat updates the card silently on most platforms. |
| "Send a test notification" button in notification settings | basic | P2 | WhatsApp Web, Telegram Web, Slack, Discord, most PWAs | The page offers Play for tones only (in-tab Web Audio, not a push); §1.9 defines no test-send endpoint. The only way to learn push is broken is to miss a real message or call. |
| Per-account / per-friend bell: notify me when X posts | standard | P3 | Instagram, Threads, X, TikTok, Snapchat, Telegram, Reddit, Bumble | `story.new` / `post.new` are WS-only by design (`ws.ts:319,332`). A user-opted bell is rule-4 compatible but low priority. |
| Badge counter settings (count chats vs messages, include muted) | standard | P3 | Telegram, WhatsApp | Polish after a badge exists. |
| Toggle for red dots on tabs separately from counts | standard | P3 | Facebook | Single-app feature. |
| Automatic overnight push pause for teens / Sleep Mode | advanced | P3 | TikTok, Instagram | No age tiers in the users table; out of scope. |
| Android notification channels / LED / popup settings per category | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Reddit, BeReal | Web push cannot declare channels; moot until native wrappers. |
| Conversation shortcuts / bubbles / Chat Heads | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Facebook/Messenger | Not possible from a PWA. |
| In-chat sent / received tick sounds for the open conversation | standard | P3 | Signal, Telegram, WhatsApp | `lib/sounds.ts:406-437` explicitly excludes the open chat and own sends. Deliberate; low value. |
| Silent send (recipient gets no sound) | standard | P3 | Telegram | No flag in the message create schema; scheduled send covers "don't wake them" partly. |
| Reset notification settings to defaults | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | Little to reset today. |
| Hide muted chats / fold muted into a section | standard | P3 | Discord, Telegram | After mute + archive (archiving does not exist either). |
| Pinned-message notification toggle | standard | P3 | Telegram | Pins fan out `conv.updated` silently (API.md:214). |
| Suppress @everyone / role mentions | standard | P3 | Discord | No broadcast mention exists, so nothing to suppress. |
| "Missed messages while inactive" email | standard | P3 | Discord, Bumble | No mailer. |
| SMS notifications (urgent alerts, reminders) | standard | P3 | Facebook, Nextdoor, Partiful, Luma | FEATURES #86 is an auth-scoped SMS-gateway slot only; ethos makes a gateway unlikely. |
| Per-category channel matrix (push / email / SMS) | standard | P3 | Facebook, Nextdoor, Meetup/Partiful/Luma | No categories, no email, no SMS. Far downstream. |
| Waitlist / spot-opened notification | standard | P3 | Luma, Meetup, Partiful | Beacons use thresholds, not caps; the "One more for X?" re-ping is the closest analogue. |
| Saved-search / interest-match alerts for listings and posts | standard | P3 | Nextdoor, Meetup, Luma | No saved-search model. User-initiated so rule-4 compatible; low demand. |
| Per-community / board post alerts (Frequent / Low / Off) | standard | P3 | Reddit, Nextdoor, Facebook groups, WhatsApp channels | Design chose ambient feeds; leave it. |
| Birthday reminders | standard | P3 | Facebook, Snapchat, Partiful, BeReal | No birthday field; not a chat-app expectation. |
| "Remind me" about a message | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp (2025) | Not in API.md §1.5. Nice-to-have. |
| Screenshot notification | advanced | P3 | Snapchat | Not detectable from a PWA anyway. |
| Friend-nearby / location-triggered alerts | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, Tinder | Surveillance-adjacent; the consent-based beacon lit push is the better pattern. |
| Community-wide urgent alert mode (severity takeover) | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | No admin broadcast push; admin replies go via the Support conversation. Rule-compatible for genuine emergencies; low priority. |
| Notification quality filters / muted words | standard | P3 | X | Single-app feature; blocks are the only filter. |

Coverage arithmetic for this section (half-built counts as not shipped; the Deliberately-not-built list is excluded): basic 18 has / 9 half-built / 16 missing = 18 of 43 = 42%; standard 10 / 17 / 43 = 10 of 70 = 14%; advanced 1 / 1 / 10 = 1 of 12 = 8%.

### Planned already

- **Batched reaction / recap digest (2-3 per day)** as the sanctioned alternative to "off": PSYCHOLOGY §6 names it a v0.3 build target, but it is absent from SPECS and FEATURES and `grep -i digest` across `apps/` finds only crypto `.digest()` calls. So reactions and comments notify nobody in any form, and the "digest, never all-off" rule protects nothing. P1: either build it or drop the rule from API.md §1.13.
- **Story reply / quick reaction lands as a DM and notifies the author**: FEATURES #99, SPECS v1.0b. Reuses `msg.new` + the normal push guards. P1.
- **Story live comments thread (author opt-in)**: FEATURES #100, SPECS:230-236, queued for v1.0b. P2.
- **Call history** (FEATURES #102, SPECS v1.0c) is what the "coming soon" line on Settings → Calls is waiting for; it is the missing half of the missed-call row above. P1.
- **Fediverse / federated interaction notifications**: FEATURES:176 ("federation (someday)"). Backlog.

### Deliberately not built

- **Reaction / like notifications in real time** (PSYCHOLOGY §6: reactions belong in digests; API.md §2.3 "no approval pings"). Agree, with one condition: the digest that is supposed to carry them has to exist. Right now the ban plus the missing digest means a chat reaction on your message is only ever a live `reaction.updated` chip. Build the digest or the ban is just a gap.
- **Promotional / marketing / product-announcement pushes** (PSYCHOLOGY rule 4; `WhatsNewCard.tsx` is pull-based, dismissible, in-app only). Agree. Correct for a self-hosted app with no growth team.
- **Re-engagement nudges** ("Your turn", "X is waiting", "what you missed", trending) (PSYCHOLOGY "Never build" + rule 4; no re-engagement job in `jobs/`). Agree. This is the single biggest thing that separates the product from Tinder/Hinge/X and it is worth defending.
- **Memories / "On this day"** (standard tier in Facebook, Snapchat, BeReal). Same class as the above: a nostalgia hook fired by the app, not pulled by the user. Not counted in the Missing denominator.
- **Blurred / withheld "someone liked you" teaser upselling a paid tier** (PSYCHOLOGY "Never build"; CLAUDE.md rule 7, money never moves). Agree. The non-paywalled, batched wave nudge is the right counterpart.
- **"Your post is getting attention" / milestone / upvote-count nudges** (rule 3: no scores, no tallies). Agree.
- **Streak-expiring / loss-framed reminders** (PSYCHOLOGY "Never build"; `PushExplainer.tsx:113-115`; rituals are "forgiving calendars, never streaks", API.md:880). Agree.
- **"Is typing" push** (typing is an ephemeral WS signal, `ws.ts:94`; rule 4). Agree; attention bait.
- **Expiring-match / countdown pressure reminders** (PSYCHOLOGY "Never build"). Agree.
- **Daily time-critical "post now" prompt** (rule 4: no unprompted "start a Beacon!" nudges). Agree.
- **Cake day / achievement / XP unlock** (rule 4: no XP or points). Agree.

One disagreement with the docs, not the bans: API.md:760-762's "no all-off switch, by design" is not a rule the product honours. Push Disable + ringtone Off + sound Off is all-off. Either ship the timed pause (P1 above) and the digest, or delete the sentence.

### How it looks

Screenshot findings that bear on this area, from the captured settings-notifications, settings-privacy, settings-calls, chat-settings-sheet, chats-list, conversation and feed screens:

- **Notifications page, boolean controls.** The page uses a green uppercase "ON" / grey "OFF" status word next to each title (`ok-text` / `muted`, `notifications/page.tsx:137-139,202-204,239-241`) with red reserved for "Blocked by browser" (:141), plus an Enable/Disable button; Privacy uses "On"/"Off" word pills; the chat sheet uses a Default/On/Off segmented control. Three vocabularies for one concept, and the "Off" pill is pixel-identical to action buttons like "Open" and "Regenerate". WhatsApp, Telegram and Instagram use one switch for every boolean. Fix: one `<Switch>` (role=switch, aria-checked) used on Privacy, Notifications and the chat sheet; drop the inline status words.
- **Notifications page, content.** Push on/off plus three sound rows, nothing else. No categories, no quiet hours, no preview toggle, no digest option, although PSYCHOLOGY §6 promises digests and the footer line "We never notify you about something you can't act on" (:261) is the only policy exposed. WhatsApp splits Messages / Groups / Calls with tone, vibrate, reactions, priority; Telegram adds per-chat exceptions. Fix: a category list, each row a switch plus Instant / Digest choice (Messages, Calls, Plans & beacons, Feed replies & reactions, Mentions, Wave nudges, Support), Quiet hours (from/to), Show message preview; keep the browser-permission row at the top as the master.
- **Wave nudges toggle is filed under Privacy** ("👀 Wave nudges", emoji in the label, `privacy/page.tsx:234`). It is a notification setting and the only per-category one in the product. Fix: move it to Notifications as the first category row; drop the emoji.
- **Ringtone and Vibrate for calls live under Notifications**, while Settings → Calls is a self-test and "Call history is coming soon" (`calls/page.tsx:258`). Telegram puts ringtone under Notifications → Calls; nobody ships placeholder copy. Fix: move ringtone and call vibrate into a Sounds group on Calls; delete the "coming soon" line.
- **Chat settings sheet** opens on nine theme swatches (Default + 8 `CHAT_THEMES`, `ChatInfoSheet.tsx:434-446`, `constants.ts:292-301`) and seven wallpaper tiles (None + 6 `CHAT_WALLPAPERS`); the per-device "Message sound: Default / On / Off" is the only notification control and it is below the fold; there is no Mute row. WhatsApp contact info puts Mute near the top, wallpaper near the bottom. Fix: Mute (8 h / 1 week / Always, with Unmute state) as the first action row, above an "Appearance" row that collapses the swatches.
- **Chat list rows** have no long-press, swipe or right-click actions and no muted-bell glyph; the unread badge pulses forever (`list.module.css:169`, `pulseGlow 2.4s ease-in-out infinite`, stilled only under `prefers-reduced-motion`), which is exactly the attention hook PSYCHOLOGY tells you to avoid and no mainstream messenger does. Fix: swipe/long-press sheet with Mute / Mark unread / Pin / Archive; muted bell glyph next to the title with the badge greyed; animate the badge once when the count changes.
- **Conversation view** has no "N unread messages" divider and no jump-to-bottom pill, so entering a chat with 40 unread lands you at the bottom with no marker of where you left off. WhatsApp, Telegram and Signal all mark it. Fix: insert the divider above the first unread seq from `last_read_seq` and scroll to it; floating ↓ pill with the count of messages arrived since you scrolled up.
- **Tab bar** shows a dot only, on Chats and Profile (`Tabs.tsx:34`); no count, no Feed dot even though `post.new` arrives live. Labels are 10.5px (`social.module.css:30`) in `--inactive`, which is #8a93a6 on #f4f6fb in the light theme = 2.85:1 and #6d7282 on #12141c in dark = 3.83:1 (`globals.css:48,62,86,99`); both fail WCAG AA for text that size. Fix: numeric badge on Chats, quiet dot on Feed when new posts arrived (position stated in the Missing table), inactive label contrast raised to 4.5:1.

### If I had one week

1. **Ship mute end to end (days 1-2).** `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/mute { until: iso | null }` writing the existing `muted_until`; add the endpoint to API.md §1.4 (the `ConversationDTO` already exposes `muted_until`, API.md:53); a Mute row at the top of ChatInfoSheet (8 h / 1 week / Always / Unmute) and the same action on a long-press/right-click sheet on the chat row; muted-bell glyph on the row and in the header; grey the unread badge for muted rows. Extend the pure guard tests in `test/v05.integration.test.ts` with an endpoint test. The worker already honours the column, so this is UI plus one route.
2. **Close the two privacy holes (day 2).** On logout and session revocation, delete the `push_subscriptions` rows for the device: either add a nullable `session_id` to `push_subscriptions` (new migration) or have the client DELETE its endpoint before calling logout and have the server revoke-all-for-user on remote revocation. Add a `message_preview: 'full' | 'name' | 'none'` user setting and respect it in `jobs/push.ts:419-427` (body becomes "New message", title becomes "Bored Chat" at the strictest level).
3. **Make push trustworthy (day 3).** `pushsubscriptionchange` listener in `sw.js` that re-POSTs the new subscription; re-POST the current subscription on app boot when permission is granted; `GET /api/push/subscriptions` so the settings row reflects server state; a "Send me a test notification" button backed by `POST /api/push/test`; TTL on beacon and reminder pushes (starting-soon TTL = lead time, lit/one-more TTL = beacon expiry) so rule 2 holds; `renotify: true` on message notifications.
4. **Badges and dividers (day 4).** `navigator.setAppBadge(totalUnread)` wherever the store recomputes unread, cleared on zero; `document.title` prefix "(3) Bored Chat" on desktop; numeric count on the Chats tab; quiet Feed-tab dot on `post.new` (the audit's single position, see the Missing row); "N unread messages" divider in ChatView from `last_read_seq` with initial scroll to it; floating jump-to-bottom pill.
5. **Per-category toggles and the first durable activity surface (day 5).** Extend `UserSettings` with a `notify` map keyed by user-visible trigger, not by job kind (the push queue has 7 kinds, `queues.ts:30-113`, and one `social` kind carries requests, accepts, matches, waves and anonymous threads): messages, calls, mentions, plans, requests, waves, anon, support, comments; check it at enqueue time; move Wave nudges into this list on the Notifications page; replace the On/Off pills with a Switch. Add comment-on-my-post as a new push trigger that also writes an `activity` row; render those rows (comments, kudos, matches, plan changes, requests) in a bell sheet off the Chats topbar so nothing evaporates when push is off. The digest can be built on the same table later, which finally makes API.md §1.13 true.


---

## Chat and direct messages

Basic-tier coverage is 34/68 (50%): 34 basic rows in "What it has" against 68 basic rows across has, half-built and missing (nothing in the banned list is basic-tier). Standard-tier coverage is 14/79 (18%): 14 standard has-rows against 79 standard rows, counting the two planned items. The message pipeline covers the standard verbs — reply, forward, edit, unsend with undo, reactions, pins, voice notes, link previews, scheduled sends, disappearing timers — and the psychology choices (faces not counts, no seen-at) are enforced server-side rather than in UI copy. The surface around the pipeline is half-finished. The gaps users hit on day one are not exotic: no Copy, no draft persistence, no mute anyone can set, no date separators, no sender clustering, no Delete-for-me, no way to delete or archive a DM, no unread divider, no scroll-to-bottom, no group rename, a Photo tile that is a bare file input and a fixed 40-emoji picker. The chat list is the barest in the comparison set — sorted by last message only, with no search, pin, archive, filters, mark-unread or swipe actions. "Who can message me" plus a requests inbox is missing even though Instagram, Messenger, X, Threads, Reddit and every dating app in the set gate stranger DMs; with public share pages and the Bored Together pool it is the highest-severity gap in the area, even though it fails the first-hour test for P0. Three features are server-only halves (group rename, poll creation, remove-member) and one is a dangling column with no writer and no control (mute). The five P0s — Copy, drafts, mute, date separators, sender clustering — are each a day or less: four are pure client work and the fifth is one endpoint over an existing column. Ship them before any new novelty feature.

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Drafts persisted per chat with "Draft" label in chat list | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Tinder, Bumble, Partiful | Typed text dies on any navigation; db.ts:33-46 stores messages, conv_meta, outbox, seen_fx but no drafts; the Composer body is React state only (the only "draft" in Composer.tsx is the schedule sheet's, 542). Hit the first time someone switches chats mid-sentence. |
| Copy message text | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | The menu is Reply/Forward/Pin/Edit/Delete/Message info/Report (ChatView.tsx:1703-1790); no clipboard call anywhere in ChatView.tsx. The single most basic long-press action; native text selection in a touch bubble is unreliable. |
| Mute chat (8h / 1 week / always) | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, TikTok, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Discord | Nothing can set muted_until: only reads exist (dto.ts:321,335; push.ts:173,238; sounds.ts:428); no mute endpoint in conversations/prefs/settings routes; no UI anywhere (ChatInfoSheet.tsx:507-531 is the device sound toggle). Counted as missing, not partial: a column with no writer and no control is not a server half. First noisy group and users look for Mute. |
| Date separators / sticky day headers | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, Hinge | Every bubble carries only a clock (ChatView.tsx:1620 formatClock); nothing in chat.module.css for Today/Yesterday. "Which day was this" is unanswerable. |
| Message clustering by sender (name/avatar once per run, grouped timestamps) | basic | P0 | Signal, Telegram, Discord, WhatsApp, Messenger, Instagram | Sender name on every group message (ChatView.tsx:1357-1375, 1455-1475), no sameSender/previous-message logic anywhere in ChatView.tsx, clock on every bubble. Group chats read as a wall of repeated names and clocks; noticeably noisier than every peer. |
| Paste image from clipboard / drag-and-drop into composer | basic | P1 | Discord, WhatsApp desktop, Telegram, Signal desktop, Messenger web, Instagram web | The app ships a desktop two-pane (TwoPane.tsx); desktop users expect Ctrl+V/drag. No onPaste/onDrop/dragover in Composer.tsx. Desktop-only, hence P1. |
| Delete for me (hide a message from my own view only) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, TikTok, X, BeReal | messages/routes.ts:555-645 only does author tombstone/undo; no per-user hide table in schema.ts. You cannot remove anyone else's message from your view; combined with no delete-chat, nothing unwanted ever leaves the screen. |
| Delete / clear a DM conversation (for me) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, BeReal, Nextdoor, Discord (close) | conversations/routes.ts:505 is leave/remove only; ChatInfoSheet.tsx:718 is Leave group/channel only. A DM can never be removed from the list. With open DMs, a spam thread is permanent. |
| "Who can message me" setting and message-requests inbox | basic | P1 | Instagram, Messenger, X, Threads, Reddit, Snapchat (pending), Meetup, Tinder/Bumble/Hinge (match-gated), Discord (friends-only DMs) | POST /api/conversations type dm accepts any user_id (only blocks refuse); no who_can_message in UserSettingsSchema (api.ts:1237-1270 — read_receipts, show_last_seen, show_typing, invites_paused, group_add_policy, anon_messages and friends, nothing for DMs). With public share pages and the Bored Together pool, anyone discoverable can land in anyone's inbox. Fails the first-hour test for P0 (WhatsApp, Telegram and Signal do not gate by default) but is the highest-severity gap in the area. |
| Unread divider ("N new messages") when opening a chat | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Messenger | ChatView.tsx:784-805 is a plain messages.map; last_read_seq is already tracked, so this is pure rendering. Noticed the first time a group has 30 unread. |
| Scroll-to-bottom chip with unread count | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Messenger, Instagram | ChatView.tsx:403 only scrollIntoView for jumps; stickRef auto-scroll only. After a "jump to reply" there is no way back except scrolling. |
| Multi-select messages for bulk forward/delete/copy | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Discord | ChatView.tsx has no selection state; the menu is single-message. Basic by consensus, not a day-one blocker. |
| Share a message/media to another app (OS share sheet) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord | navigator.share is only used in InviteSheet.tsx:100. The Web Share API is already in; trivial to extend. |
| Search/filter chats by name on the chat list | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Threads | ConversationListView.tsx is a single flat list; the only "search" is the /people link (81-84). Hurts once a user passes a couple of dozen chats. |
| Archive chat | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Nextdoor, Meetup, Hinge (hide) | settings/chats/page.tsx:3-6 says "archiving does not exist yet"; schema.ts has no archived column. The list only ever grows. |
| Pin chat to top of list | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Reddit | ConversationListView.tsx:21-23,60 sorts purely by last_message_at; no pinned column on participants in schema.ts. |
| Mark chat as unread / mark all read | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord | No markUnread anywhere in web or server. Basic triage tool. |
| Optimistic local echo with a "sending" (clock) state on the bubble | basic | P1 | Every mainstream messenger | The message is applied to the store only after the POST resolves (connection.ts:541-543); TickState is sent/delivered/read with no pending (Ticks.tsx:3); the composer just disables Send. On a slow link the bubble appears late with no clock tick. The single most felt latency difference. |
| Rich text formatting (bold, italic, strikethrough, monospace, lists, quotes) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord, Messenger (desktop) | Bodies render plain text (ChatView.tsx:1980); no markdown/strikethrough anywhere. Every major messenger has at least *bold* / _italic_. |
| Voice recording: lock to hands-free, pause/resume, preview before send | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram | Composer.tsx:887-905 is hold-only; no lock/preview state. Long voice notes require holding the button the whole time. |
| Sticker packs (install, recents, suggest while typing, create your own) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, Discord | No sticker table in schema.ts; API.md §1.21 says doodles and uploads are the only sticker sources; avatar stamps are reactions, not stickers. User-uploaded packs are fully self-hostable; nothing exists. |
| Contact card / share a user's profile into a chat | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Snapchat (profile card) | No vcard/contact message type (api.ts:320-333). "Introduce A to B" is a core social action; absent. |
| Star / save / bookmark a message with a Saved screen | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Snapchat (save in chat), Discord (favourites) | No starred/favorite anywhere in schema.ts or web. No way to keep an address, a time, a plan detail. |
| Report reason taxonomy (spam / harassment / impersonation / self-harm) | standard | P1 | Reddit, Nextdoor, Bumble, Tinder, Instagram, TikTok, X | ReportSheet.tsx:37 is a single free-text reason. Moderators and users expect categories; also a trust signal for a dating-adjacent pool. |
| Coarse presence ("Active today" / "Recently active") instead of minute-precise "last seen 5m ago" | standard | P1 | Tinder, Bumble (none), Hinge (none), Discord (no last-seen), X (none) | format.ts:18-26 renders relative minutes; PSYCHOLOGY.md's never-build list rejects "Seen X minutes ago" and rule 6. Minute-precise last-seen is the same response-deadline mechanism the docs ban on receipts. Coarsen it. |
| Search within a chat (text, result navigation) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, X, Discord | ListMessagesQuerySchema has after_seq/before_seq/limit only (api.ts:954-960); no search row in ChatInfoSheet.tsx. Finding "what was the address" is impossible beyond scrolling. |
| Chat-list filter chips (Unread / Groups / Channels) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Messenger, Instagram, Reddit, Bumble, Discord (Unreads) | Single flat list, no chips. Cheap and high-use. |
| Swipe actions on chat rows (archive / mute / unread / pin) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Instagram | Rows are plain `<Link>` elements. Depends on the actions existing first. |
| Per-chat media / links / docs gallery | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat | ChatInfoSheet.tsx has no media section; gallery/routes.ts:25 is only my own uploads. Six majors have it; the data (attachments by conversation) is already there. |
| View-once photos/videos | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat | No view_once anywhere in apps, packages or docs. Six majors; no screenshot detection is possible in a PWA, so ship it honestly without alerts. |
| Chat export (.txt / .json, with or without media) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger/IG (bulk DYI), X (archive) | Only GET /api/admin/users/:id/export (admin-side); FEATURES.md:176 backup backlog. The self-hosted ethos makes "my data" export a credibility item. |
| Unknown-sender safety banner (Block / Report / Accept before replying) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Instagram, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder (safety cards) | No stranger/first-message handling in ChatView.tsx. Pairs with message requests. |
| Hide message preview in notifications | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Instagram | settings/notifications/page.tsx:130-245 is push on/off, ringtone, sound, vibrate only. Basic privacy on lock screens. |
| Per-category notification toggles, quiet hours and digest mode | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Discord | A single Enable/Disable (settings/notifications/page.tsx:130-160); no digest in jobs. PSYCHOLOGY.md rule 6 wants "digest, never all off" — the app's own psychology doc asks for a digest option that does not exist. |
| Story replies/reactions land in the DM thread | basic | P2 | Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, BeReal | No story reply rider in ChatView.tsx. Belongs partly to the stories area; chat has no quoted-story bubble. |
| Sender avatar beside incoming group bubbles | basic | P2 | Telegram, Messenger, Slack, Discord | Group rows render only a StyledName sender line inside the bubble (ChatView.tsx:1455-1475); Avatar is used only for stamp replies and on-my-way faces. Pairs with the missing clustering item. |
| Chat-list snippet shows the sender name in groups ("Alice: …") | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram | messageSnippet() returns the body only (format.ts:91-118); the row prefixes nothing for groups. Without it the list is not scannable. |
| Emoji-only messages render large (jumbo emoji) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Messenger, Discord | No emojiOnly/jumbo logic in ChatView.tsx or chat.module.css. Rendering-only polish. |
| GIF search picker (first-party index) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Reddit, Tinder, Bumble, Partiful | Most of the set has it, but Giphy/Tenor is forbidden by golden rule 7; only a self-hosted community GIF library (Telegram-style first-party index) is viable, hence P2 not P1. |
| Live location sharing (15m/1h/8h) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat | API.md §1.52: "No live location sharing (by design)". Owner decision; "On my way" covers the main use case. Would need no tile keys anyway. |
| @everyone / @all group-wide mention | standard | P2 | Messenger, Discord, Telegram | Mentions are user-id arrays (api.ts:527); no @all handling in messages. Useful for plan groups; must respect PSYCHOLOGY (no spam pings). |
| Mention badge (@) on the chat-list row | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord | ConversationListView.tsx has no mention awareness; row shows unread count only. |
| Forward options: hide sender name / drop caption | standard | P2 | Telegram, WhatsApp | ForwardSheet.tsx has no options; attribution snapshot always applied. Minor. |
| Customisable quick-reaction row | standard | P2 | Instagram, Messenger, Signal, Telegram | ReactionPicker.tsx uses the fixed EMOJI_GRID. Polish. |
| Double-tap to quick-react | standard | P2 | Instagram, Messenger, Telegram, X | Hover smiley / long-press only (ChatView.tsx:1448, 1686-1703). Polish. |
| Copy link / deep link to a specific message | standard | P2 | Discord, Telegram, Reddit | No seq-anchored route in apps/web/app; jumps are in-component only (ChatView.tsx:375-403). Nice for plan groups. |
| Viewer: swipe between chat media / filmstrip | standard | P2 | Signal, Telegram, WhatsApp, Discord, Instagram | MediaLightbox.tsx takes a single src (16-27); no prev/next, no swipe. Polish. |
| Typing/recording preview on the chat-list row | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | ConversationListView.tsx:145-206 renders the snippet only. Polish. |
| Jump to date and media-type / from: filters in chat search | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord | No search endpoint at all (api.ts:954-960). Depends on search landing first. |
| Note to self / message yourself | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram (Saved Messages) | No self-conversation type (api.ts; conversations/routes.ts:136). Useful cross-device clipboard; moderate demand. |
| Global default disappearing timer for new chats | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal | settings/chats/page.tsx is a placeholder; no default_disappearing in UserSettings. Settings → Chats is literally empty; this is its first natural resident. |
| Member permissions / admin-only posting for groups | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord | Only channels restrict posting (ChatView.tsx:883-887; API.md §1.35). Channels cover broadcast; group-level slow mode / send permissions are absent. |
| Ignore / move conversation to spam silently | standard | P2 | Messenger, Reddit, Threads (hidden requests) | Not in conversations/routes.ts or ChatInfoSheet.tsx. Softer than block; useful once requests exist. |
| Hidden-words / keyword DM filtering | standard | P2 | Instagram, Threads, TikTok | No filter settings in settings/privacy/page.tsx; no filter in messages/service.ts. Harassment mitigation. |
| Spam/scam detection and warnings on messages | standard | P2 | Nextdoor, Reddit, Tinder, X (low-quality filter) | No classifier/warning rider in messages/service.ts. Relevant to listings-driven DMs. |
| Enter-to-send toggle (mobile newline vs send) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Discord | Composer.tsx:858-861: Enter always sends; no sendOnEnter setting. Mobile users who want multi-line notes have no way. |
| Quick reply from the notification | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Snapchat, Messenger | sw.js:132-137 has actions only for call rings. Web push inline reply is Android-only; limited win. |
| App icon badge (navigator.setAppBadge) / unread count in document.title | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger (PWAs badge the icon) | No Badging API call in sw.js or lib/push.ts; no title badge. The installed PWA icon never shows unread. Cheap to add (set on push, clear on read). |
| Admins/owners can delete other members' messages | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord (group admins and channel owners) | DELETE throws forbidden() unless row.senderId === user.id, channels included (messages/routes.ts:574); client canDelete = own (ChatView.tsx:1275-1280). Without it moderation is report-only. |
| Transfer group/channel ownership; delete a group or channel | standard | P2 | Every mainstream group-capable app | conversations/routes.ts:526-527 says "transfer/deletion is future scope" and throws owner_cannot_leave; no DELETE /api/conversations/:id. A channel owner is permanently stuck. |
| "I got home" safety check-in ping to trusted contacts | advanced | P2 | Snapchat (Home Safe) | The inverse directions exist — Rescue me (rescue/routes.ts; RescueSheet.tsx) and the drunk-mode "Check on them" banner that sends DRUNK_CHECK_IN_BODY (constants.ts:927; API.md §1.57) — but there is no arrival ping: nothing matching home-safe/arrived/check-in in server modules or web. Beacon meetups with strangers from the Bored Together pool are exactly the case it was built for. |
| Chat lock / app lock (PIN or biometric) | advanced | P2 | WhatsApp, Messenger, Telegram (app passcode), X (XChat) | Passkeys are login-only (API.md §1.70). Passkey infra exists; a WebAuthn re-auth gate per chat is feasible. |
| Suspicious / look-alike link warning before opening | advanced | P2 | Signal | ChatView.tsx:1980 linkifies without domain checks. Cheap client-side check; security win for a community app. |
| HD/original quality toggle or "send as document" for originals | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger, Signal | The server never downscales (pipeline.ts:204-222), so originals already go by default. The real gap is a "compress / data saver" choice, not an HD toggle. Low demand; the file tile is a workaround. |
| Download progress / pause / resume for large received files | standard | P3 | Telegram, WhatsApp (progress ring on media), Signal | The file row is a plain `<a download>` through the auth proxy (AttachmentView.tsx:107) and images/videos load inline; no progress, pause or resume anywhere in apps/web for a 100 MB (MAX_FILE_BYTES) download. The browser's own download UI covers the file row; inline media just stalls with no feedback. |
| Favourites list for chats and calls | standard | P3 | WhatsApp (Favourites filter + calls tab), Discord (favourites folder) | No favourite/favorite column, route or control anywhere in apps/server, apps/web or packages/shared. Distinct from pin-to-top, which is also missing; a curated shortlist only earns its place once pin, filters and a call history exist. |
| Emoji shortcode autocomplete (":smile:") | standard | P3 | Slack, Discord, Telegram desktop | detectMention only recognises an "@" token (Composer.tsx:112-150); no ":" trigger. Ties to the fixed picker. |
| Up-arrow to edit your last message (desktop) | standard | P3 | Slack, Discord, Telegram desktop | onKeyDown handles ArrowUp only while the mention list is open (Composer.tsx:817-858). |
| Trim / edit video before sending | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, iMessage | The edit sheet is image-only; the edit button renders only for kind === 'image' (ImageEditSheet.tsx:3-8; Composer.tsx:640-650). |
| Photo markup (draw / text / stickers on a photo before sending) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Snapchat, iMessage | ImageEditSheet offers crop, 90° rotate and aspect presets only; DoodleSheet is a blank canvas, not an overlay. |
| Reply quote shows a media thumbnail | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | ReplyContextSchema carries seq/author/type/excerpt/deleted only (api.ts:625-636); replying to a photo renders the text label "Photo". |
| "Recording a voice note…" indicator for the other side | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | TypingIndicator only knows "is typing…"; startRecording never emits a signal (Composer.tsx:385-430). |
| Group setting: who can add members / edit group info (admins vs everyone) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | Adds and renames/avatars are hard-coded admin-only (ChatInfoSheet.tsx:404; conversations/routes.ts:436); group settings expose only visibility, description and join requests. |
| Long-message collapse ("Read more") | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Messenger | The only line-clamp is on .previewDesc (chat.module.css:754-760); 8192-char bodies render uncollapsed. |
| Chat text size / font size setting | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | No chat font-size preference anywhere in settings; only OS/browser zoom. Accessibility item. |
| Spoiler text and spoiler-blurred media (tap to reveal) | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Signal, Discord | Only puzzle "no-spoiler" comments mention the word. Niche. |
| Voice message transcription | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram (Premium) | No transcription anywhere. Would need a self-hosted model; rule 7 forbids SaaS. |
| Round/short video messages recorded in chat | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Snapchat (video notes), Bumble | No video-record UI in Composer.tsx; video only via file upload. Nice-to-have. |
| Quote a selected part of a message / reply into another chat | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Reply is whole-message (ChatView.tsx:146). Telegram-only. |
| Reply privately from a group into a DM | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp | Not found in web or docs. Single-app feature. |
| Visible edit history | advanced | P3 | Signal, Messenger | No edit-history table in schema.ts. Niche. |
| Custom chat folders / labels | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Signal, WhatsApp (custom lists) | No folder table in schema.ts. Advanced. |
| "Keep in chat" for disappearing messages with sender veto | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp | Disappearing sweep is unconditional (API.md §1.25). Single-app feature. |
| Vanish mode / screenshot alerts | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, Telegram (secret chats) | Not in client or API.md. Screenshot detection is impossible on the web platform. |
| In-chat message translation | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram | Nothing beyond CSS transforms; FEATURES.md:176 i18n backlog. Needs a self-hosted MT model. |
| Home-screen shortcut to a chat | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Signal (Android) | No manifest shortcuts for conversations. PWA-limited. |
| Message threads / topics inside a chat | advanced | P3 | Discord, Telegram (topics), Meetup (comment replies) | API.md §1.29 is flat threading only. Out of character for the product. |
| AI assistant / AI characters in chats | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, Discord (summaries) | Rule 7 (no third-party SaaS); nothing in apps. Only a self-hosted model would fit; not a priority. |

### Planned already

- **Global search: messages, people, groups** (standard, P1) — designed in docs/SPECS.md:266-269 (#108, trigram/FTS); docs/FEATURES.md:159. Not built; the Chats-tab Search icon currently routes to /people (ConversationListView.tsx:81-84) and misleads.
- **Self-serve backup / restore** (standard, P2) — docs/FEATURES.md:176 (#58, one-click backup/restore at operator level). Server-stored history makes device backup moot; operator backup is the real need.
- **End-to-end encryption for DMs** (advanced, P2) — docs/FEATURES.md:175 (#57); docs/SPECS.md:298 backlog; CLAUDE.md backlog. Owner decision; DMs are server-readable today, like Instagram and Discord.

### Deliberately not built

- **"Your turn" / "Their move" reply-owed labels** (Tinder, Bumble, Hinge) — PSYCHOLOGY.md rule 6 and the never-build "Seen X minutes ago" (the response-deadline mechanism). Agree: it is a guilt timer, and this product is explicitly anti-guilt. The in-game "Your turn" on tic-tac-toe is game state, not a social debt, and is fine.
- **Match expiry countdown timers / extend-a-match** (Bumble) — never-build "loss-framed ritual warnings". Agree; loss-framed urgency has no place in a friends-and-neighbours app.
- **Streaks** (Snapchat) — PSYCHOLOGY.md:33 never-build. Agree without reservation.
- **Real-time push for reactions** (WhatsApp, Instagram, Messenger, Tinder) — rule 6 puts reactions in digests; API.md §2.3 fans reaction.updated to sockets only. Agree with the ban, but the digest that is supposed to carry them does not exist either, so reactions currently reach nobody who is off-device. That is a bug hiding behind a principle; build the digest.
- **Message info with per-recipient seen-at timestamps** (WhatsApp, Signal, Messenger) — PSYCHOLOGY.md:36 never-build; API.md §1.18 faces in username order. Agree; faces answer "did they see it" without starting a clock.
- **Peeking / "in this chat" presence and typing-as-push** (Snapchat) — rule 6 calm defaults. Agree; it is surveillance presence.
- **Payments / gifts / paid reactions in chat** (Messenger, Telegram Stars) — CLAUDE.md rule 7 "Money never moves through the app"; SPECS.md owner decision. Agree; it also removes a whole class of scam and compliance work from a self-hosted operator.

### How it looks

**Conversation screen (phone-conversation.png, desk-conversation.png)**

- Incoming messages are not bubbles. In light mode --bubble-in #e8ecf5 sits on --chat-bg #eef0f6 (globals.css:89,108) at 1.04:1, so "always. what are you thinking" floats as loose text while outgoing messages (--bubble-out #1c2130, globals.css:109) are near-black slabs; dark mode (#232838 on #171a24, globals.css:51,71) is 1.18:1, also too close. WhatsApp light is a white bubble on #efeae2; iMessage is #e9e9eb on white. Fix: light --bubble-in #ffffff with a 1 px shadow, dark #2a3042, and pull the outgoing bubble down to a mid-navy so both sides sit in the same weight band.
- No day dividers, no unread divider, no jump-to-bottom. Five consecutive bubbles each carry their own "03:47 PM" and ticks (.meta, chat.module.css:218-226) and an incoming tail corner (chat.module.css:176), so a week-old chat has no way to tell Tuesday from Saturday and a rapid exchange reads as five unrelated events. Every peer groups by sender/time and shows a day chip; WhatsApp and Telegram add a floating "↓" with the unread count. Fix: cluster consecutive same-sender messages within 5 minutes (tail and stamp on the last only, name on the first only), insert day pills (Today / Yesterday / weekday / date) that stick at the top, add an "N unread messages" divider derived from last_read_seq and scroll to it on open, and a floating "↓" pill when stickRef is false.
- Timestamps are formatted two ways: formatClock in bubbles uses hour:'2-digit' and forces the leading zero ("03:47 PM"), while formatTime in the list hand-builds "3:47" with no meridiem (format.ts:3-16). Bubble meta is 10.5 px (chat.module.css:222), below any readable floor. Fix: one formatter in lib/format.ts with hour:'numeric', 11.5 px meta at ~65% opacity, wrapped in `<time dateTime>`.
- Delivered vs read is nearly indistinguishable inside outgoing bubbles (ticks at a 55% colour-mix of --accent-ink vs 100% for read, chat.module.css:203-209) while the list paints the same read state --ok green (list.module.css:128-142). Two colour languages for one receipt. Fix: pick one — read ticks in --ok in both places, or Signal's circle/check/filled model.
- Group chats have no sender identity cues: every sender name is the same colour, no avatar beside incoming bubbles (ChatView.tsx:1455-1475), and the list snippet shows the body with no "Arjun:" prefix (format.ts:91-118). Fix: hashed per-user hue for names, a 28 px avatar on the first bubble of a run, and a first-name prefix in group snippets.
- Text inside own bubbles uses page tokens on the wrong surface: "Your turn · you are X" (.tttStatusMine, color: var(--accent), expression.module.css:939-942) is ink on the dark bubble at ~1.1:1 and the game is unusable in light mode; "0 votes" (.pollMeta, var(--muted), beacons.module.css:873-876) is ~2.95:1. Fix: an --on-bubble-out-muted token and audit every var(--accent) inside .own .bubble.
- The ListingReviewNudge is a white card rendered outside the scroll container, wedged between the message pane and the composer (ChatView.tsx:813-816), slicing a system line and a bubble in half in the capture. Fix: render it as a dismissible system card inside the scroll.
- The typing indicator is its own row between the scroll and the composer (typingRow, ChatView.tsx:822-830), so the layout shifts whenever someone starts or stops. WhatsApp and Telegram put it in the header subline. Fix: header subline, plus an in-stream dots bubble only when the user is at the bottom.
- Composer placeholder reads "Message… try @" in a 1:1 DM where there is nobody to mention — mentionCandidates is built from participants for every non-channel chat, DMs included (ChatView.tsx:541-553; Composer.tsx:824). Fix: "Message" in DMs, "Message Friday crew" in groups, one-time @ tooltip.
- Enter sends on every device, including phone keyboards (Composer.tsx:858-861 has no pointer or settings check), so a mobile user cannot write a multi-line message. Fix: newline on coarse pointers, send button sends, Enter-to-send behind a desktop setting.

**Chat list (phone-chats.png, desk-chats.png)**

- The magnifier in the Chats top bar is a Link to /people, not a search; the component comments "Search routes to /people until the global search page exists" (ConversationListView.tsx:81-84). On the most-used screen of a WhatsApp-style app the primary affordance lies. Fix: an inline field that filters the loaded list client-side by title and snippet today; message search when #108 ships.
- Rows have zero row-level actions — no swipe, no long-press, no right-click, no mute, pin, archive, mark-unread or delete — and no All/Unread/Groups filters. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal and iMessage all offer swipe plus long-press. Fix: swipe-left Mute/Archive, swipe-right Pin/Mark unread, a long-press action sheet, a desktop right-click, and a chip row under the search field.
- The unread badge pulses forever (pulseGlow 2.4 s ease-in-out infinite, list.module.css:160-169; only reduced-motion turns it off). That is the attention hook PSYCHOLOGY.md tells you to avoid; no mainstream messenger animates unread counts. Fix: one 200 ms scale-in when the count changes.
- The stories strip reserves a full row at the top even with zero friend stories (StoriesStrip.tsx returns null only when logged out, line 38) — conversations lost on every phone-height screen. Fix: collapse to a chip row when empty and hide it on scroll.
- Every letter avatar is the same neutral tint in the capture, so "F", "A" and "M" are visually one person; the presence dot on Arjun's avatar is navy for an offline user and signals nothing. Fix: hash(user_id) to six low-saturation tints; show the dot only when online.
- Anonymous messaging is split across "New anonymous thread" on /new and "Anonymous inbox" in the ⋯ overflow (a separate list without the tab bar). Telegram keeps secret chats in the same list with a badge. Fix: show anon threads in the main list with a mask badge, or a pinned "Anonymous inbox" row.

**Composer and attach menu (phone-chat-plus-menu.png, desk-chat-plus-menu.png)**

- The "+" grid is Photo, Doodle, Vibe check, Game, Puzzle, Location, Schedule (Composer.tsx:519-528) — no Poll, no Camera, no File, no Contact — while the onboarding tour promises "voice notes, doodles and polls" (constants.ts:1011) and the feature directory advertises Polls with a Try-it link that goes to "/" (constants.ts:1039; 15 of its 25 links are dead). On desktop the grid opens as a full-width bottom sheet across a 1280 px window instead of a popover anchored to the button, and there is no paste or drag-and-drop. Fix: Poll and Camera tiles (plus File), rename Photo to Gallery, drop the Schedule tile (hold-send already schedules), 4-column grid, a 2-column popover on desktop, onPaste/onDrop with a "Drop to send" overlay.

**Message actions (chat-msg-longpress.png, desk-conversation.png)**

- Long-press is bound to onTouchStart only (ChatView.tsx:1448), so trackpads, pens and the Playwright mouse long-press open nothing; desktop has no onContextMenu and relies on discovering a hover smiley. The menu has no Copy, no Select, no Star, no Share. Delete uses window.confirm (1334) and pin-full uses window.alert (435). Fix: onContextMenu on the bubble, pointer events for long-press with a 10 ms vibrate on commit, add Copy / Select / Save, and replace the native dialogs with the app's own sheet ("Delete for me" / "Delete for everyone").

**Chat settings sheet (chat-info.png)**

- For a DM the sheet opens on nine unlabeled theme tiles (default + 8) and seven wallpapers (none + 6) that fill the first viewport (ChatInfoSheet.tsx:433-492), repeats "ONLY YOU SEE THIS" four times, never shows who the chat is with, and has no Mute, no Block/Report, no media/links, no search, no clear chat; disappearing messages is buried under the wallpapers; the selected theme has no check mark. WhatsApp's order is hero, media, mute, disappearing, wallpaper, block/report. Fix: identity hero with Message/Call → Mute → Search → Media & links → Disappearing → Appearance collapsed into one row with named swatches and a check badge → Clear / Block / Report / Leave.

**Settings → Chats and Calls (settings-chats.png, settings-calls.png)**

- Settings → Chats is two rows that both say "this lives somewhere else" (settings/chats/page.tsx:41-66); Settings → Calls is a self-test plus "Call history is coming soon." (settings/calls/page.tsx:258). Two of seven categories are placeholders, which teaches users the categories are unreliable. Fix: fill Chats (archived chats, text size, Enter sends, media auto-download, export, clear all) or remove the row; delete the "coming soon" line and fold a Calls list in when it ships.

**Tap targets and touch (all phone shots)**

- Header icon buttons (search, +, ⋯, call, video) are .btn-icon with 8 px padding around a 22 px glyph (globals.css:442-445), i.e. 38×38, below the 44 px minimum on both HIG and Material. Fix: a ::before inset hit box without changing the visual.
- The only haptics in the app are the ring pattern and its cancel (sounds.ts:313,321, gated by the vibrate pref); the interactions that deserve a short vibrate — long-press commit, swipe-reply arm, voice-note send — fire none. Fix: 10 ms vibrates at those three points.

### If I had one week

1. **Days 1–2: ChatView rendering pass, pure client.** Day pills with sticky header, "N unread messages" divider from last_read_seq with initial scroll-to-it, floating "↓" pill with count, same-sender clustering (tail/stamp on the last, name on the first, 28 px avatar on incoming group runs), one time formatter, bubble contrast tokens (--bubble-in white/#2a3042, --on-bubble-out-muted for poll/game status), group snippet prefix. Two of the five P0s (date separators, clustering) and three P1s die here, and the app stops looking three years behind.
2. **Day 3: Mute, done end to end.** A PATCH /api/conversations/:id/mute that writes participants.muted_until (8 h / 1 week / always / null), a "Mute notifications" row at the top of the chat settings sheet, a bell-slash glyph on the row, and a long-press / right-click sheet on chat rows that carries Mute plus Mark unread (a one-field write to last_read_seq). pushGuard and sounds.ts already honour the column; the pulsing badge animation gets removed in the same commit. Third P0.
3. **Day 4: Long-press menu completion and the native dialogs.** Copy (clipboard — fourth P0), Delete for me (a message_hides table keyed (conversation_id, seq, user_id), filtered in list and sync), Share (navigator.share, already used for invites), onContextMenu for desktop, pointer-event long-press with haptic, and the app's own sheet replacing window.confirm/alert. Then Delete/clear a DM as a per-user hide of the conversation row using the same table pattern.
4. **Day 5: Wire the server-only halves, plus drafts.** Poll tile in the "+" grid posting to the existing /polls route; group rename from the sheet calling the existing PATCH title; Remove member and Make admin / Demote buttons in the group MembersSection using the existing DELETE participant route and a one-line port of the channel role route to groups; Camera tile via the capture attribute; a Save path for video bubbles alongside the existing image Save; drafts persisted per conversation in the IndexedDB db.ts with a "Draft" label on the row (fifth P0). None of this needs a migration.
5. **Days 6–7: "Who can message me" and a requests inbox.** A who_can_message setting (everyone / people I'm connected with / nobody new), enforced in POST /api/conversations type dm using the existing friends.ts helpers; DMs from outside the allowed set land as pending with a stranger banner (Accept / Block / Report) and no push until accepted. This is the one item that is not cosmetic: with public share pages and the Bored Together pool, the inbox is wide open today, and every dating-adjacent app in the comparison set gates it.


---

## Groups and channels

**Verdict.** Groups look finished from the outside and are hollow in the middle. Counting the rows in the tables below (banned items excluded), basic-tier coverage is **17 of 45 (38%)** and standard-tier coverage is **13 of 67 (19%)**. The novel parts are real — four visibility modes including ghost masks, a join-approval queue, beacon-sparked plan groups with roles and rituals, cleanly scoped broadcast channels — but the day-one admin verbs every reference app ships (rename, kick, promote/demote, mute, create a poll, revoke an invite, delete the group) exist as server routes with tests and have **no button anywhere**. `docs/FEATURES.md` #9 ("muting"), #48 ("group admins") and the feature directory's "Polls — settle where/when/what in any chat" (`packages/shared/src/constants.ts:1040`) all describe controls a user cannot reach. The join loop is half-built: admins can approve requests, but no signed-in user can request to join or join a public group from inside the app (`apps/web/lib/groups.ts:35 joinGroup()` has zero callers; `SharePageView.tsx` gates every interaction to `/register?next=…`), and a private group's only invite button lives in the empty-chat block and disappears after the first message. For a product whose pitch is a local community board, the gaps that matter are chat-list hygiene (archive / pin / mark unread), mute, a member-visible description, in-group events with RSVP, a media gallery, and any moderation lever for group admins (delete others' messages, ban list, sole-admin guard). Advanced parity is mostly irrelevant: Telegram/Discord/Reddit tooling is either banned by `PSYCHOLOGY.md` (counts, boosts, badges, streaks, ads, AI) or premature at a 256-member cap. There are six P0 rows below, all afternoon-to-two-day jobs: one release that wires the existing server surface into `ChatInfoSheet` (mount `ChannelMembersSheet` for groups; add rename / mute / invite / revoke UI; make `SharePageView` auth-aware) plus chat-list row actions, then fix the member-count doc contradiction and the silent rename / role-change system messages.

Cap is `MAX_GROUP_PARTICIPANTS = 256` (Instagram-class; channels 10k). A solo group cannot be created (400 `group_needs_members`, `routes.ts:216`). |

| Instance-admin moderation of public groups | standard | Share-page reports reach the queue; FEATURES #79d says public groups are "moderated / reportable" | `admin/content.ts:35` takedown type enum = post, beacon, listing only; operators cannot browse a public group or take it non-public | P2 |
| Invite link options: expiry, use cap, single-use, named, approval-required | standard | `invites.expiresAt` / `maxUses` columns with defaults (7d / 50) | `CreateInviteRequestSchema = {conversation_id?}` (`api.ts:1177-1181`) — no request fields | P2 |
| Poll options: multiple answers, anonymous / public, quiz, add option, close | standard | `closes_at` accepted and enforced (409 `poll_closed`) | `poll_votes` PK `(poll_id, user_id)` → single choice only; nothing else | P2 |
| Three-tier roles (owner / admin / member) in groups | standard | `participants_role_check` has `'owner'`; used for channels (`routes.ts:200-212`) | Groups are binary admin / member; the same column could carry it | P3 |
| Enable / disable (pause) invite links per group | standard | `invites_paused` user-level privacy toggle (API §1.13) | No per-group switch | P3 |
| Forwarded channel posts link back to the channel | standard | "Share invite" (channel admin) + invite links | `ForwardSheet` forwards carry no channel link-back | P3 |
| Block and leave / leave and prevent re-adding | standard | Blocks + `group_add_policy` exist separately | No combined flow or per-group "never re-add" flag | P3 |
| Pin for everyone vs just me, pin notify toggle | standard | Conversation-wide pins (`pins/routes.ts`) | No personal pin, no notify flag. Fine | P3 |
| Hide member list from members | standard | Channels never expose members; ghost masks identities | Plain groups have no toggle. Covered well enough | P3 |
| Leave silently (no system message) | standard | Channels leave without a message (`routes.ts:544-557`) | Groups always post `participant_left`. Fine as is | P3 |
| Live location sharing with the group | advanced | `LocationPicker.tsx` static drops + "On my way" headcount | No live tracking — also a privacy hazard, fine to skip | P3 |
| Geographic scope for groups / local groups | advanced | Beacon area audience + Local board listings | Groups carry no location. Fine | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Chat-list actions for groups: archive, pin-to-top, mark unread, swipe / long-press | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, X, Discord | Nothing in `ConversationListView.tsx` (no `onContextMenu`, touch or swipe handlers; the only glyph is "has pinned messages", `:178-181`). Day-one hygiene every messenger has; groups pile up with no way to tidy them. |
| Delete group / end chat for everyone (and close an abandoned channel) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, TikTok, BeReal, Nextdoor, Discord, Meetup | No `app.delete` for conversations; `routes.ts:526` says "transfer/deletion is future scope". A dead or abusive group lives until the last member leaves; a channel owner cannot leave (`owner_cannot_leave`), so an abandoned channel cannot be closed at all. |
| Unread divider / jump to first unread message | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger, Discord, Slack | `ChatView.tsx:271` always pins to the bottom; `last_read_seq` is on the DTO and `store.ts:329-333` computes `unread_count`, but nothing anchors to it. In a busy 256-member group the reader lands at the bottom and scrolls up blind. |
| Toggle: who can mint / share a group invite link | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | `invites/routes.ts:205` hard-wires admin-only for groups; no setting column. Ordinary members cannot bring friends in except by asking an admin. |
| @-mention indicator on the chat-list row | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger, Discord, Slack | `ConversationListItemSchema` carries `unread_count` + `last_message` only (`api.ts:773-776`); mentions exist as `metadata.mentions` and drive the mute-override push rule, but no unread-mention flag reaches the list. |
| Scroll-to-latest button with new-message count | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Discord | `onScroll` only sets `stickRef` (`ChatView.tsx:755`); the only scroll control is "Load older messages" (`:760`). Needed exactly where reading history while messages arrive is common. |
| Per-conversation draft persistence | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger, Slack, Discord | IndexedDB stores are messages, conv_meta, outbox, seen_fx only (`lib/db.ts:33-45`); no draft field. Typed text dies on back-navigation. |
| Delete chat for me / clear chat history | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger | Only destructive row is Leave (`ChatInfoSheet:708-720`); no per-user clearing route. The only way to tidy a group off your list is to leave it with a public system message. |
| Notification when you are added to a group | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger | `participant_added` has `senderId: null`; `fanOutNewMessage` enqueues push only inside `if (msg.senderId)` (`messages/service.ts:167-172`). The add route sends `conv.new` to live sockets only. An offline user can be put in a group and miss the first hours. |
| Copy message text | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, iMessage, Discord | No `clipboard` call in `ChatView.tsx` or any message menu (`grep clipboard apps/web` → recovery codes, invite sheet, share.ts only). Sharing a line from a group conversation means retyping it. |
| Group info shows creator and creation date | basic | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | `created_by` / `created_at` ride every Conversation DTO (`dto.ts:383`) but the sheet hero shows avatar + title only. Cheap trust cue. |
| Chat-list "X is typing…" preview for groups | basic | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | Typing state lives in the global store; `TypingIndicator` is mounted only inside the open chat. Render-only gap. |
| Ban / removed-users list that blocks re-joining via invite link | standard | P1 | Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Bumble BFF, Meetup | No ban table (`schema.ts` has only user-level `banned_at` and canvas bans); `invites/routes.ts:422` accept has no removed-user check. A removed member rejoins on the same open link immediately — which makes the missing kick button only half the problem. |
| Admin can delete any member's message for everyone | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Messenger, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, X Communities, Bumble BFF | `messages/routes.ts:556-575` author-only (`if (row.senderId !== user.id) throw forbidden()`); API §1.30 "nobody edits or deletes anyone else's words". Public groups exist but their admins cannot moderate content; everything funnels to instance-admin reports. |
| Transfer ownership of a group or channel (so an owner can leave) | standard | P1 | Telegram, Signal, Discord, Nextdoor, Meetup, WhatsApp, Instagram | `owner_cannot_leave` with "future scope" (`routes.ts:524-527`). Channel owners are bound for life; groups have no owner concept to transfer. |
| Admins notified / badged when someone requests to join an invite-only group | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook, Discord | `visibility.ts:200-212` parks the request and returns 202 with no publish and no push; no `group.*` frame in `packages/shared/src/ws.ts`; `ConversationListItemSchema` has no pending count. `GroupSettingsSection` fetches the queue only while the sheet is open and visibility is invite_only. The shipped approve / deny queue is invisible until an admin happens to open settings. PSYCHOLOGY "never notify about something you cannot join" does not apply — admins can act. |
| Invite QR code for a group / channel (show + scan) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, TikTok, FB Groups, Luma | `QrCode.tsx` already exists (login and security pages); `InviteSheet.tsx` renders text only. Trivial wiring, and in-person onboarding is the core loop of a local community app. |
| Per-group media / links / files gallery | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Facebook Groups, Discord, Meetup | `/gallery` is personal media only (API §1.75); nothing in `ChatInfoSheet`. Expected on every group info screen. |
| Channel rename, photo and description | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Messenger, Discord | Title and avatar routes throw 400 `not_a_group` for channels; `canEditGroupAvatar = plainGroup && groupAdmin` (`ChatInfoSheet:244`). Channels are permanently named and show a bare initial forever. |
| Toggle: who can edit group info | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Discord | Every info gate checks `role === 'admin'`; single flag, no per-capability toggle. |
| Default member permissions matrix (text, media, links, polls, pin, add) | standard | P2 | Telegram, Discord, Reddit | No permission columns; Composer gates only on `canPostIn` (`lib/format.ts:53`). |
| Per-member restrictions: temporary mute / suspend a member | standard | P2 | Telegram, Messenger/FB Groups, Discord, Reddit | Only self `muted_until` exists. Admins have no lever between "nothing" and "remove" — and remove has no UI either. |
| Channel discovery / follow public channels | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, BeReal, Discord | FEATURES #108 names groups only; channels are invite-link-only. A channel nobody can find is a newsletter without a sign-up page. |
| Structured group rules / guidelines shown on join | standard | P2 | Facebook Groups, X Communities, Reddit, Discord, Meetup, Bumble BFF | Only the ≤300-char description. Needed once public groups get discovery. |
| Notification mode: mentions-only | standard | P2 | Signal, Snapchat, Discord, X Communities | Only the unwritable `muted_until` and a device-local sound override. Falls out once mute ships. |
