| Group topics / categories / interest tags | standard | P2 | Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Bumble BFF, Meetup, Luma, Threads | No tags on conversations. Prerequisite for useful discovery (#108). |
| Group safety / context card before first read of an unknown group | standard | P2 | WhatsApp | `ChatView.tsx:706-717` public banner only; no "who added you / N mutuals" card. Pairs with `group_add_policy`; cheap and user-protective. |
| Granular admin rights and custom admin titles | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Reddit, Meetup | `conversation_participants.role` is the only permission field. For a 256-cap group a simple admin / mod split would do; the matrix is overkill. |
| Slow mode (per-member send cooldown) | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Messenger, Reddit | Absent. Only matters once public groups / channels have real traffic. |
| Membership questions / rules gate on join | standard | P3 | Messenger/FB Groups, X Communities, Discord, Reddit, Meetup, Luma | `groupJoinRequests` has no answers / rules fields. Defer behind the basic join loop. |
| Toggle: chat history visible to new members | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord | `join_seq = last_seq` always (`routes.ts:470-492`), 0 only for beacon / team-up groups. Defensible privacy default. |
| Threaded replies | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Reddit, Messenger, Facebook Groups, Meetup | API §1.29 flat by design. WhatsApp, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat also lack threads; flat is legitimate for chat-first. |
| @all / @everyone / @admins with permission control | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Facebook Groups | API §1.33 resolves literal usernames only. Needs an admins-only abuse guard if added. |
| Linked discussion group / comments under channel posts | standard | P3 | Telegram, Facebook, Instagram | No `linked_conversation` column. Members can only react in channels. |
| Group feed / posts inside a group | standard | P3 | Facebook Groups, Nextdoor, BeReal, Reddit, X Communities, Meetup | Conversations are flat dm / group / channel; posts are user-level. The Local board partly covers it; a group-scoped feed is a different product direction. |
| Report to group admins (separate from platform report) | standard | P3 | Facebook Groups, X Communities, Reddit, Discord | `ReportSheet.tsx` routes everything to the instance queue. Depends on admins first being able to act on anything. |
| Pending invites list (invited-but-not-joined, revocable) | standard | P3 | Signal, Discord | Invites are bearer links, no per-person rows. Model difference; low value. |
| Silent (no-notification) sends and draft posts in channels | standard | P3 | Telegram | No silent flag in send schemas. Telegram-only polish. |
| Allowed-reactions set / reaction toggle | standard | P3 | Telegram, WhatsApp channels, Discord | Absent. Low value. |
| Scheduled voice / video chat with countdown | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord | No scheduled-call object. Covered better by in-group events if those ship. |
| Broadcast lists (one-to-many DMs) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp | Channels cover the use case. |
| Welcome message for new members | standard | P3 | Reddit, Discord, Instagram channels | No `welcome_message`. Nice-to-have. |
| Age-restricted / NSFW flag | standard | P3 | Discord, Reddit | No flag. Low priority for a local community app. |
| Reply privately to a group message | standard | P3 | WhatsApp | Absent. WhatsApp-only convenience. |
| Who can create events / event submission approval | standard | P3 | Meetup, Luma, Discord | Blocked on in-group events. |
| Multi-select messages (bulk forward / delete) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, iMessage, Discord | No `selectMode` in `ChatView.tsx`; `ForwardSheet` forwards one message to many targets, never many messages. |
| Communities / groups-of-groups / subgroups | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp Communities, Facebook, Snapchat, Threads, Discord, Telegram folders | Absent and unplanned; Local board + public groups is the nearest analogue. |
| Topics / forum mode / multi-channel server | advanced | P3 | Telegram topics, Discord, Reddit, Bumble BFF rooms | No parent / child conversation relation. Only matters past ~50 active members. |
| Admin action log / audit log | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Reddit | The `reports` audit log (`schema.ts:955-960`) is instance-admin scoped. Would also fix the silent-admin-action problem. |
| Chat folders / shareable folders | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Threads | No filters / folders. Nice-to-have. |
| Anonymous admin posting / send as channel identity | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Ghost masks everyone; no admin-only anonymity. Low value. |
| Ambient voice rooms / stage channels | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp voice chat, Telegram, Discord Stage, Facebook | Mesh ≤8; needs the SFU first. |
| Live streaming into channels | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Facebook Live | Out of scope. |
| Group stories (24h, members only) | advanced | P3 | Signal, Snapchat, Telegram | `StoryAudienceSchema` is `friends_of` only (`api.ts:1851`). |
| Restrict saving / forwarding content | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Skip. |
| Custom emoji / sticker / soundboard packs per group | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Reddit, Threads | Reactions are unicode + avatar stamps. Skip. |
| Collaborative collections / shared saves | advanced | P3 | Instagram | Skip. |
| Channel DM inbox / suggested posts from subscribers | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Skip. |
| Automoderation: keyword filters, crowd control, approval queue, strikes | advanced | P3 | Reddit, Facebook, Discord AutoMod, Telegram | Far ahead of the basic admin tools that are missing. |
| Bots, webhooks, integrations | advanced | P3 | Discord, Telegram, Reddit | Self-hosted bots would be allowed, but out of scope. |
| Teen / age-based group-chat protections | advanced | P3 | TikTok, Instagram | Depends on an age model that does not exist. |
| Server onboarding / screening / templates at creation | advanced | P3 | Discord | Discord-scale. |
| Community status, wiki, FAQ, Q&A | advanced | P3 | Reddit, Meetup, Luma | Description is the only prose field. Skip. |
| Linked roles / role gating by criteria | advanced | P3 | Discord | Skip. |

### Planned already

- **Public group / channel discovery, search and directory** (standard, P1) — `docs/FEATURES.md` #108, 📋 v1.0d. Covers public / invite-only groups only; channels are not in the plan. `conversations_public_idx` (`schema.ts:362`) is currently unread by any route.
- **In-group message search** (standard, P1) — `docs/FEATURES.md` #108, v1.0d, scoped to "my own messages" only. That is not what anyone expects from in-chat search; widen the scope before building.
- **Search / filter the chat list by group or channel name** (basic, P2) — `docs/FEATURES.md:159` #108 global search, v1.0d. Today the list's magnifier is a Link to `/people` (`ConversationListView.tsx:80-85`).
- **Groups in common shown on a profile** (standard, P2) — `docs/FEATURES.md` #107 profile groups tab, v1.0d. The signal already feeds `SuggestionCards.tsx:27` ("shares a group") but is never shown as a list.
- **E2EE / server-blind group membership** (advanced, P3) — `CLAUDE.md` backlog. No implementation.

### Deliberately not built

- **Algorithmic shared recommendation feed in a group (Blend) / similar-channel recommendations** — PSYCHOLOGY design rules 1 and 5, never-build "infinite scroll". Agree. This is the engagement-ranking machine the product exists to not be.
- **AI assistant / AI-generated icon in group chats** — CLAUDE.md rule 7 (no third-party SaaS APIs). Agree for now, but this is a dependency rule, not a product rule: a self-hosted model would pass rule 7. If it ever comes up, it is a product decision to make on purpose, not one the ethos settles.
- **Channel / group statistics, view counts, subscriber counts** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; API §1.35 ships no counts even to the channel owner. Agree on every public surface. Hiding the subscriber count from the owner is stricter than it needs to be, but with a 10k cap and named-face receipts it is not worth fighting; leave it.
- **Boosts, levels, giveaways, paid media, paid membership, server shop** — CLAUDE.md rule 7 "money never moves through the app", SPECS owner decision "no payments", PSYCHOLOGY rule 4. Agree, twice over.
- **Sponsored messages / ads in channels** — self-hosted ethos, PSYCHOLOGY north-star rule 1. Agree.
- **Member badges (Top contributor, Rising star) and achievement badges** — PSYCHOLOGY rules 3 and 4. Agree; ranking people in a 50-person local group is corrosive, not motivating.
- **Group streaks** — PSYCHOLOGY never-build "streak counters or loss-framed ritual warnings". Agree; rituals auto-pausing instead of nagging is the right call for this product.

### How it looks

- **Group chat-settings sheet — not captured; described from `apps/web/components/ChatInfoSheet.tsx` only.** (The captured `chat-info.png` is the DM sheet, which opens on theme swatches; the group sheet does not.) For `conv.type === 'group'` the render order is: 84px avatar hero with the name as a static `<strong>` and an admin-only "Photo or GIF · tap to change" target (`:347-387`) → "Group" block from `GroupSettingsSection` (visibility radio, description textarea, "Copy the group's public link", join-request queue, mask editor — all admin-gated except the ghost tag and your own mask) → "Members" with "+ Add people" for admins and rows that link to a profile and carry an Owner / "Mod" chip (`:748-968`) → Theme (9 swatches) → Wallpaper → Message sound (device-only) → Shared background → Personal background → Nicknames → Disappearing → Leave (`:427-720`). So the skeleton is right and the verbs are missing: no tap-to-rename, no Mute, no read-only description for members, no member actions (remove / promote / search), no media, no open-invites list. The chip says "Mod" (`:858`) while the API, the settings copy and every reference app say "Admin"; pick one word. Fix: rename field on the hero, Mute row directly under it, description paragraph for everyone, mount `ChannelMembersSheet` from the group block (it already has search, Make admin / Demote, Remove), Media row, collapse Theme / Wallpaper / Sound / Backgrounds / Nicknames into one "Appearance" group.
- **No mute anywhere (`phone-chats.png`; sheet from code)** — "Message sound: Default / On / Off" (`ChatInfoSheet:508-530`) silences the in-browser tone on this device only; a 256-member group keeps pushing to your phone with no escape short of leaving. WhatsApp 8h / 1w / always, Telegram, Signal, iMessage all surface mute from both the row and the info screen. Fix: a Mute row at the top of the sheet that PATCHes `muted_until`, a muted-bell glyph on the list row, greyed badge for muted rows.
- **Group bubbles have no sender identity (`phone-conversation.png`)** — every sender name is the same ink colour (`.sender { color: var(--accent) }`, `chat.module.css:211-215`), no avatar beside incoming group bubbles, and the list snippet shows the body only ("perfect 🙌") with no "Arjun:" prefix. WhatsApp colours sender names and prefixes the list; Telegram shows avatars beside group bubbles. Fix: hash(user_id) → stable hue for sender names, 28px avatar on the first bubble of a run, "Arjun: …" / "You: …" prefix in group and channel snippets.
- **Chat-list rows have zero actions (`phone-chats.png`)** — no swipe, long-press, right-click, or filter chips (All / Unread / Groups). Every mainstream messenger has these. Fix: long-press / right-click action sheet (Mute, Pin, Mark unread, Archive) and a chip row under the search field.
- **Composer "+" grid has no Poll (`phone-chat-plus-menu.png`)** — Photo, Doodle, Vibe check, Game, Puzzle, Location, Schedule (`Composer.tsx:519-527`). WhatsApp and Telegram put Poll in the attach menu. The server route and `PollView` exist; the feature directory advertises it. Fix: Poll tile (question + 2–6 options sheet) posting to the existing endpoint; reorder the grid by frequency.
- **No unread divider, no jump-to-bottom (`phone-conversation.png`)** — opening a group with unread messages lands at the bottom with no marker; scrolling up to read means new arrivals land unseen. WhatsApp "N UNREAD MESSAGES" + ↓ badge, Telegram the same. Fix: divider above the first unread seq from `last_read_seq`, floating ↓ pill with a count when `stickRef` is false.
- **No day dividers, no message grouping (`phone-conversation.png`)** — five consecutive bubbles each carry "03:47 PM" and a tail. Busy groups are where this hurts most. Fix: day pills (Today / Yesterday / weekday / date), 2px gaps and tail only on the last bubble of a same-sender run.
- **Composer placeholder "Message… try @" in every chat (`phone-conversation.png`; `Composer.tsx:824`)** — in a group it should read "Message Friday crew"; in a DM just "Message". Surface the @ hint once as a tooltip on the first group message.
- **All letter avatars are the same grey circle (`phone-chats.png`, `phone-new.png`)** — a group member list or chat list cannot be scanned by colour. WhatsApp / Telegram hash the user id to a hue. Fix: six low-saturation tinted gradients keyed on user id.
- **Poll meta ("0 votes", "tap yours to unvote") is near-invisible on own bubbles (`chat-msg-longpress.png`)** — `.pollMeta` is `var(--muted)` and the chosen option's count is `var(--accent)` (`beacons.module.css:861-875`), painted over the outgoing `--accent-grad`. In the captured light scheme that is `#5e6a81` over `#1c2130→#3a4152` = 1.9–2.9:1 and `#12141c` over the same = 1.2–1.8:1 (tokens from `globals.css:98-104`, WCAG formula); dark scheme is no better (1.6–2.6:1 and 1.0–1.6:1). Group polls are the thing people look at most. Fix: use the on-bubble foreground token (`color-mix(var(--accent-ink) 70%)`) for every secondary string inside `.own .bubble`, as `.meta` already does (`chat.module.css:199`).
- **Delete uses `window.confirm`, pin-full uses `window.alert` (`chat-msg-longpress.png`)** — `ChatView.tsx:1334,435`, and the same native dialogs guard Leave (`ChatInfoSheet:186`) and channel Remove (`ChannelMembersSheet:119`). Alien against the styled sheets; no "Delete for me". Fix: bottom sheet with Delete for me / for everyone.
- **"Friday crew" row has no timestamp while "Arjun Rao" does (`phone-chats.png`)** — `last_message_at` is null for an empty group so the time column collapses (`ConversationListView.tsx:185-190`). Reserve the column; render the created time for empty groups.

### If I had one week

1. **Wire the admin verbs into `ChatInfoSheet` for groups (2 days).** Mount `ChannelMembersSheet` from the group block too (it already has search, Make admin / Demote, Remove — the server accepts group admins). Add a rename field on the hero calling the existing PATCH title. Add a read-only description row for every member. Move "Share invite" out of the channel-only block, and list / revoke open invites via the existing GET / DELETE `/api/invites`. Add the sole-admin guard server-side (refuse the last admin's leave / demote with a named 403, matching the channel `owner_cannot_leave` pattern) and emit `title_changed` / `role_changed` system messages. Relabel "Mod" → "Admin".
2. **Mute (1 day).** One route: PATCH `/api/conversations/:id/mute {until}` writing `participants.muted_until`. A Mute row at the top of the sheet (8h / 1 week / always / unmute), the same action on a long-press of the list row, a muted-bell glyph and greyed badge on the row. Everything downstream (pushGuard, sounds.ts, DTO) already works. Then correct FEATURES #9.
3. **Close the join loop (1.5 days).** Make `SharePageView` and the join page auth-aware: signed-in viewer sees Join / Request to join calling `joinGroup()`; render the 202 "Requested" state with a withdraw. Publish a `group.join_request` WS frame plus a push to admins when a request lands, and carry a `pending_requests` count on the conversation DTO so the row and header show a badge. Show group avatar, description and size on the invite page.
4. **Poll tile in the composer "+" (0.5 day).** Question + 2–6 options + optional close time, posting to the existing `/polls` route; `PollView` renders it. Then the directory's "Polls" entry stops lying.
5. **Group chat-list and read hygiene (1 day).** Long-press / right-click row actions (Mute, Pin, Mark unread, Archive — the last two need per-participant columns), "Arjun: …" sender prefix in group / channel snippets, sender name in group push bodies (`push.ts:419-443`), the unread divider + jump-to-bottom pill in `ChatView`, and a "N members · created by X" line on the sheet hero. Fix the member-count contradiction in `ADR-020:67-69` / `API.md` §1.68 (the header shows it; either document that or remove it) and correct FEATURES #48.

Not in the week, but next: delete-group route, ban list on invite accept, invite QR (component exists), per-group media gallery, and the ability to raise a beacon plan inside an existing group — the one feature that would make "local community board" true.


---

## Feed and posts

The feed's skeleton is sound — gapless finite lanes, poll/game/join-card kinds, faces-not-counts end to end, block enforcement on every post surface — but the day-to-day surface a normal user touches is a third of the basic bar: **basic-tier coverage is 32% (22 of 68), standard-tier is 22% (16 of 73)**. Denominators are every basic- or standard-tier row in the four lists below (Has, Half-built, Missing, Planned) minus the rule-7 'never' rows; none of those are basic-tier and three are standard. The gaps are not exotic: no edit post, no drafts, no bookmark, no carousel, no threaded replies, no comment timestamps, no mentions, no in-app permalink, no posts tab on a profile, and feed images bypass the lightbox the chat already has. Safety is inverted — a logged-out stranger can report a public post from the share page, but a member cannot report a post or a comment (`reports/routes.ts` and `ReportKindSchema` accept `user | message | beacon | anon_message | story_overlay | share_page`, nothing else), and a stranger's post has no options menu at all, contradicting `docs/DESIGN-HANDOFF.md:128`. A video post renders as a broken image in list view while it transcodes, the grid-by-default turns a friends feed into author-less 118px tiles, and authors are never told anyone reacted or commented even though PSYCHOLOGY §6 explicitly allows daily digests. Only five rows here are P0 (a user hits it in the first hour, every mainstream feed app has it, and its absence breaks or endangers the core post/see/react/reply loop or safety): the ⋯ menu on others' posts and Report-a-post (one fix), the lightbox, the broken pending-video state, and the posts tab on a profile. Everything else is P1 to P3, and most of the P1 list is plumbing the app already has (mention infra, link unfurl, lightbox, blurhash, share sheet, scheduler) that the feed never received.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text post (body ≤2000) | basic | X, Threads, Facebook, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, WhatsApp channels, TikTok, Discord forum, Meetup | Body renders as inert pre-wrap text (`social.module.css:699`) — no links, mentions, hashtags or counter. |
| Photo post (multi-image, up to 4) | basic | Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, Threads, TikTok, Snapchat | `POST_MAX_ATTACHMENTS=4`; fixed 2x2 grid, `max-height: 340px; object-fit: cover` (`.postImages img`) — single photos are cropped, no aspect preservation. EXIF is stripped on re-encode except for animated GIF/WebP, which are stored verbatim (`pipeline.ts:199-220`). |
| Poll post (2–6 options, vote/revote/unvote, option tallies) | standard | X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, Threads, Facebook groups, WhatsApp channels, Discord, Partiful | `post_polls`/`post_poll_votes`. The card renders body AND question (`FeedView.tsx:1000`, `:1132`), so the question shows twice when users fill both composer fields. |
| Open game-challenge post (first claim wins → DM board) | advanced | Bored Chat only | Advisory lock + DM create-or-dedupe; one winner under concurrent claims (`test/v07.integration.test.ts:433`). |
| 'Join my…' cross-post card (beacon/listing → feed with Join button) | advanced | Facebook share event, Nextdoor share to feed, Luma cross-listing | `validateJoinCardRef` (400 `ref_audience_mismatch`), per-viewer `resolveRefSummary`. |
| Location tag on post | basic | Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok, Snapchat, Nextdoor, BeReal | `posts.location` jsonb; link-out only (OSM/Google/geo:) — correct under golden rule 7. |
| Composer entry point (header icon opens inline composer card) | basic | all apps | Collapsed by default; the friends-lane empty state says 'post something first' but offers no button (`FeedView.tsx:288`). |
| Post kind picker (Post / Poll / Challenge / Join my…) | standard | Facebook composer types, Nextdoor categories, Reddit post-type tabs | `KIND_PICKS` radiogroup (`FeedView.tsx:473-477`). |
| Caption on media posts (body + attachments together) | basic | all apps | Kind 'text' allows body AND `attachment_ids`; card renders body then images. |
| Audience selector (Connections vs Public) | basic | Facebook, Instagram, TikTok, BeReal, X reply controls, Meetup | `POST_AUDIENCES`, DB check constraint. Only two values (see Missing); the control is a tap-to-flip row, not a picker (see How it looks). |
| Multi-select photo picker with thumbnails and remove-before-send | basic | all apps | `<input type=file multiple>` + photoStrip 'Remove image'. |
| Crop / rotate before posting | basic | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Reddit, Luma cover crop | `ImageEditSheet.tsx` wired from 'Edit image' (FEATURES #92). |
| HEIC → JPEG client conversion | standard | Instagram, Facebook | `lib/imageUpload.ts` (FEATURES #91). |
| Drunk-mode 'you sure?' guard before a public post | advanced | Bored Chat only | `isSelfDrunk() && audience==='public'` → `DrunkConfirmSheet` (FEATURES #73). |
| Post rate limit with friendly 429 copy | standard | Nextdoor urgent-alert limits, Reddit/Discord anti-spam | `POST_CREATE_RATE_PER_HOUR=10`, `fixedWindow('postcreate:')` (`feed/routes.ts:140`). Posts only — comments and reactions have none (see Missing). |
| Poll builder UI (question, add/remove options) | standard | X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, Partiful | `POLL_OPTIONS_MIN/MAX`, `POLL_OPTION_MAX_CHARS`, `POST_POLL_QUESTION_MAX_CHARS=120`. |
| Join-card picker (my live beacons/listings, audience-nesting disabled rows) | advanced | Bored Chat only | Rows disabled when `!allowed.includes(audience)`. |
| Upload error handling (413 / 415 / HEIC unreadable) | standard | all apps | `upload()` catch branches; `uploadAttachment()` for comments. |
| Like / react on a post (emoji toggle, multiple reactions) | basic | all apps (Facebook/Nextdoor/Telegram multi-reaction; IG/X/Threads single heart) | 8-per-user cap, advisory lock. Not optimistic — each toggle refetches the whole post (`lib/feed.ts:92 refetchPost`). |
| Comment on a post (≤500) | basic | all apps | `POST /api/posts/:id/comments` (`feed/routes.ts:566`); `PostComments`. |
| Delete a comment (own, or post author) | basic | all apps | `DELETE /api/posts/:id/comments/:commentId` (`routes.ts:616`, hard delete); '×' when `canDelete` (`FeedView.tsx:1547`, `:1722`). No confirmation step. |
| Comment reactions (emoji + avatar stamp, faces not counts) | basic | Instagram comment likes, Facebook, TikTok, Reddit, Discord | `comment_reactions`; `patchCommentReaction` (`lib/feed.ts:204`) is optimistic, unlike post reactions. |
| Image / GIF in comments | standard | Facebook, Reddit, Instagram GIF, Nextdoor photo, Threads | `attachment_id` binding, image kind only (API §1.51); no video/audio comments. |
| Inline comment box under the card + sheet composer | basic | Threads, TikTok comments sheet, Facebook | 'Say something back…' row under every card; grid tiles open a Portal bottom sheet. |
| Delete own post | basic | all apps | `DELETE /api/posts/:id` cascades and revokes the share token. UI is native `window.confirm` (`FeedView.tsx:941`) while every other destructive flow uses a sheet (`DrunkConfirmSheet`, `ReportSheet`; FEATURES #90a); no WS `post.deleted`, so other viewers keep the stale card. |
| Chronological friends feed | basic | Instagram Following, Facebook Friends, X Following, TikTok Friends, BeReal, Threads Following | `lane='friends'`, `created_at DESC`. |
| Public / Discover feed (chronological, instance-wide) | basic | Reddit All, Nextdoor nearby, BeReal Nearby, Snapchat Discover, Instagram Explore | `lane='discover'`, `NOT EXISTS blocks/banned`. Pull-only; no WS fan-out to strangers (API §1.49). |
| Hard end-of-feed card ('You're all caught up') | standard | Instagram, Facebook Friends, TikTok Following, BeReal, Hinge, Meetup/Luma, Bumble Discover | `all_caught_up=!hasMore`; honoured in both lanes. The card has nothing to tap (see How it looks). |
| Grid / mosaic layout toggle | standard | Reddit card/compact, Discord list/gallery, Instagram Explore grid, Snapchat Discover tiles, Partiful tiles | Grid is the DEFAULT (`useState<FeedLayout>('grid')`, `FeedView.tsx:94`); text posts become 118px tiles (`.feedGrid grid-auto-rows: 118px`). No benchmark app defaults a mixed friends feed to a grid. |
| List layout (full post cards) | basic | all apps | `layout==='list'` → `PostCard`. |
| Layout preference remembered | standard | Reddit (per device), Discord forum | `FEED_LAYOUT_KEY` in localStorage (try/catch). Device-local only. |
| 'Show older posts' explicit pagination | basic | Discord finite history, BeReal, Meetup | Keyset cursor; `loadMoreFeed`. |
| Manual 'Check for new posts' refresh control | standard | sanctioned substitute for pull-to-refresh | `.laneRefresh` → `refreshFeed(lane)` (`FeedView.tsx:260-265`). |
| People-you-may-know cards inside Discover | standard | Instagram, Facebook, Reddit recommendation cards, BeReal FoF | `<SuggestionCards moreHref="/people" />` (FEATURES #78). |
| Live arrival of friends' new posts (WS `post.new`) | standard | X, Facebook, Threads | `publishToUsers(friends-of + self)` (`feed/routes.ts:226`). Prepends immediately (see Half-built). |
| Public SSR share page with OG/Twitter-card meta | standard | X, Facebook, Threads, Reddit, Telegram | `app/p/[token]/page.tsx`; non-users can play videos too — FEATURES #110 still lists that as planned (docs lag code). |
| Admin takedown of public posts with reason | standard | Reddit mods, Discord mods, Nextdoor reviewers, Telegram admins | `admin/content`, `hardDeletePost` (FEATURES #81). Public surfaces only; friends-only posts are moderated only via user reports — which members cannot file. |
| Block / ban enforcement across every post surface (faces, commenters, reactors, claimers, joiners) | basic | all apps | `requireVisiblePost/requireVisibleComment`, `blockedSetOf` filters, identical 404 for unknown/out-of-audience/blocked; a chip whose reactors are all blocked vanishes. |
| Mixed image + video in one post (up to 4) | standard | Instagram carousel, Facebook, X, Telegram albums | `feed/routes.ts:191-197` binds any kind but 'file'; composer `accept` includes mp4/webm (`FeedView.tsx:829`). |
| Audience label on each post card ('· public') | basic | Facebook audience glyph, Instagram close-friends badge | `PostCard` header appends ' · public' (`FeedView.tsx:957-960`); friends-only posts carry no label (implicit default). |
| Per-lane empty-state cards | basic | all apps | Friends: 'Nothing here yet' with a /connections link; Discover: 'No public posts yet' (`FeedView.tsx:285-293`). Copy is wrong for zero-connection accounts (see How it looks). |
| Comment pagination ('See earlier comments' / 'More comments') | basic | all apps | Keyset cursor (`routes.ts:775`); inline preview is the latest `POST_COMMENTS_PREVIEW_MAX` (3). |

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Video post with feed-first player (muted autoplay, sound toggle, duration badge, pending slate) | basic | Composer accepts mp4/webm; `<video controls>` renders when `status==='ready'` (`FeedView.tsx:1010-1011`). | A 'pending' video falls into the `<img>` branch (`:1013-1015`) — a broken image while transcoding, in list view. The add button is still labelled 'Add images' (`:839`). No autoplay, duration badge or pending slate. | P0 |
| GIF in post animating in the feed | basic | GIFs upload fine (API §1.51 'a GIF is just an image upload'). | Card renders `thumb_url` (static frame); the GIF only moves when the raw file is opened in a new tab. | P1 |
| Event post with RSVP (standalone template) | standard | Kind 'join_card' referencing the author's OWN live beacon. | `posts_kind_check` has no 'event'; you cannot post an event without first lighting a beacon. Join cards are a proxy, not the thing. | P2 |
| Voice / audio post | advanced | Server binds any attachment kind but 'file' (API §1.36 `POST /api/posts`); the Attachment DTO carries kind 'audio' + waveform; `AudioBubble.tsx` exists for chat. An audio post can be created via REST today. | Web never offers it (`accept="image/*,video/mp4,video/webm"`) and renders a non-ready-video attachment through `<img>`, so an API-created audio post shows as a broken image. Close the latent broken state even if the feature is skipped. | P2 |
| Per-file upload progress | standard | The add tile becomes a spinner while any upload is in flight. | No per-file bar or percent; a 4-file batch gives one spinner. | P2 |
| Location picker with venue search | basic | `LocationPicker.tsx`: typed label or one-tap coords. | No place search. Places APIs are banned by rule 7; a self-hosted Nominatim is the only route. | P2 |
| Share from another app into a post (PWA share-target → composer) | standard | `app/compose/page.tsx` routes share imports to a CHAT conversation only. | No 'post to feed' option on the share-target page. | P2 |
| Full emoji grid + avatar-stamp reaction on posts (parity with comments) | standard | Comments get `ReactionPicker.tsx` (stamp + 40-emoji grid). | `PostCard` uses only `QUICK_REACTIONS` (6 emoji, `FeedView.tsx:1078`). Inconsistent within the same card; the 'react with your face' idea is hidden on the thing people react to most. | P1 |
| Who-reacted list with names (tap chip → sheet) | basic | `faceChip` aria-label lists names (`FeedView.tsx:1036`); beacons have a 'Who's in' sheet (FEATURES #60). | Tapping the chip toggles MY reaction (`:1037`) instead of opening a list; API caps at 12 + `has_more` with no list endpoint. Trying to see who reacted accidentally reacts. Faces-not-counts doctrine favours a names list. | P1 |
| Comment timestamps | basic | `PostCommentSchema` carries `created_at`. | `CommentRow` never calls `formatTime` (no match in `FeedView.tsx:1640-1730`). One line of UI. | P1 |
| Author and commenter display names link to profile | basic | Avatars are wrapped in `<Link>` (`FeedView.tsx:952`, `:1654`). | The `<strong>` name in the header (`:956`) and in `CommentRow` (`:1671`) is plain text — the most natural tap target is dead. | P1 |
| Copy link to post | basic | Server complete: `POST /api/share-links`, `DELETE /api/share-links/:token`. Public posts show an `IconLink` button (`FeedView.tsx:964-969`). | Friends-only posts (the composer default) have no link control and no in-app permalink substitute. `copyShareLink` returns the link so callers can offer Revoke and `PostCard` discards it. | P3 |
| Report a post (logged-in member) | basic | Strangers CAN report via `POST /api/share/:token/report` (`sharepages/routes.ts:386`, kind `share_page`). | `ReportKindSchema` (`api.ts:1288`) has no 'post'; `ReportSheet.tsx:22-26` has no post target. Inverted safety coverage; golden rule 5 half-met. Same fix as the options-menu P0 in Missing. | P0 |
| Block author from the post card | standard | Block exists on `/u/[username]`. | No control on `PostCard`; two hops away, and from Discover you cannot even reach a menu. | P1 |
| Copy post text | basic | Browser text selection. | No menu action. | P2 |
| 'N new posts' pill instead of auto-prepending while reading | basic | `adoptIncomingPost` (`connection.ts:338`) → `prependPost` (`store.ts:1391`). | Prepends immediately; content jumps under the reader's thumb. No queued pill. | P1 |
| Stories strip above the feed | standard | `StoriesStrip.tsx` mounted on the Chats tab (`ConversationListView.tsx:115`). | Not on Feed. A design choice (stories live with chats) that diverges from the Instagram model the grid imitates. | P2 |
| Desktop feed layout (wider column / right rail) | standard | Single column inside `TwoPane/TabPage`. | `.feedWrap` is capped at 560px inside a 680px `.page`; no detail pane (see How it looks). | P2 |
| Blurhash / skeleton placeholders for feed images | basic | Attachment DTO carries `blurhash`; `BlurhashCanvas.tsx` is used only by chat `AttachmentView.tsx`. | Feed uses `<img loading="lazy">` with no blurhash; tiles flash grey → image. | P2 |
| In-app single-post permalink / detail page | basic | `GET /api/posts/:id` exists (`feed/routes.ts:272`); public `/p/[token]` share page. | No `/post/[id]` or `/feed/[id]` route (`apps/web/app` has neither); `ActivityStrip.tsx:39-43` links 'posted' rows to `/feed` generically. Nothing can deep-link to a post; friends-only posts have no URL of any kind. | P1 |
| Relative timestamps on posts | basic | `formatTime(post.created_at)` is called (`FeedView.tsx:958`). | `format.ts:3-13` is ABSOLUTE: same-day '9:41' (no AM/PM), otherwise 'Oct 12'. The only relative formatter is `formatLastSeen` (presence). | P2 |
| Full-screen pinch-zoom lightbox for feed images | basic | `MediaLightbox.tsx` (pinch, pan, Save) is wired to chat. | Feed wraps post images (`FeedView.tsx:1013`) and comment images (`:1674`) in `<a target="_blank">` to the raw file; on an installed PWA this throws the user out to the browser. `MediaLightbox` takes a single `src` (`:22`), so wiring it also needs a multi-attachment pager. | P0 |
| Save / download media from a post | basic | `MediaLightbox` bottom bar 'Save' (`MediaLightbox.tsx:230`). | Feed images never open the lightbox. Falls out of wiring it. | P2 |
| Video player controls (scrub, speed, PiP, quality) | standard | Native `<video controls playsInline preload="metadata">`. | Browser defaults only. Acceptable for a non-video-first app. | P2 |
| Aspect-preserving single image (no forced crop in card) | basic | `.postImages1..4` grid. | `max-height: 340px; object-fit: cover` for every count including 1 — portraits and tall screenshots are cropped in the list card. | P1 |
| Feed load failure state with error copy + retry | basic | `refreshFeed(lane).catch(() => undefined)` (`FeedView.tsx:104-108`). | Error is swallowed, `posts` stays null, 'Loading your people…' spins forever. The only retry is the unlabeled refresh icon; no message, no 'Try again', no offline hint. | P1 |
| Reorder photos before posting | standard | Server preserves author ordering by design (`schema.ts:1353-1354`, `service.ts:257-262`). | photoStrip offers only Remove/Edit; order is upload-completion order, and `attachments[0]` becomes the grid hero, so the cover image is whichever upload finished first. | P2 |
| Success feedback after posting (toast / composer closes / scroll to new post) | basic | `store.prependPost` puts the post at the top. | `post()` only clears fields; no toast (no toast import in `FeedView.tsx`), the composer stays open (`setComposeOpen` is only ever called with `true`, `:168`), nothing scrolls. | P1 |
| Poll results hidden until you vote | standard | Fill bar waits for `voted` (`FeedView.tsx:1148`). | Per-option count (`:1153-1155`) and the `total_votes` line (`:1159-1163`) render before the viewer votes, so the tally steers the vote and a fresh poll reads '0 0 0'. X/Instagram hide tallies until you vote. | P2 |
| Loading skeleton placeholders for the feed | basic | Text line + spinner on first load. | No skeleton/shimmer anywhere in `social.module.css` or `globals.css` (grep: 0 hits). | P3 |
| Attachment cap feedback when selecting more than 4 files at once | basic | Server enforces ≤4 (`posts_attachment_ids_check`); 'Up to 4 images per post.' error on a later pick. | `upload()` checks the cap against stale closure state, so picking 6 files uploads all 6 and silently drops the 5th and 6th (orphans go to GC). | P2 |
| Revoke a post's public share link from the card | standard | `DELETE /api/share-links/:token`; `revokeShareLink` in `lib/share.ts`; stories have a Revoke control (`StoryViewer.tsx:375`). | Own-post ⋯ menu has only 'Delete post'; a regretted URL can only be killed by deleting the post. | P2 |

### Missing

Ordered P0 → P3, basic → advanced within each. P0 = first-hour hit, every mainstream feed app has it, and its absence breaks or endangers post/see/react/reply or safety; P1 = expected within the first week on most benchmarks; P2 = ordinary polish; P3 = niche or low fit. The last four rows are rule-7 (self-hosting) bans — final, but not psychology doctrine.

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Per-post ⋯ options menu on OTHERS' posts (report / hide / mute / block / copy) | basic | P0 | all apps | `FeedView.tsx:975` renders the menu only when `own`; `DESIGN-HANDOFF.md:128` claims 'delete/report/share'. There is no affordance at all on a stranger's post in Discover. One fix with the Report-a-post P0 above. |
| Carousel / swipeable multi-photo with dot indicator | basic | P1 | Instagram, Threads, TikTok photo mode, Telegram album (X and Facebook use a collage in the timeline and swipe in the viewer) | `social.module.css:707-740` is a static grid; no swipe/pager code. The 2x2 collage is defensible as the card layout; it is not defensible as the only way to see a post's photos. |
| Swipe between a post's images in the viewer | basic | P1 | Instagram, X, Facebook, Reddit, Telegram, TikTok, Nextdoor, Threads, Discord | `MediaLightbox.tsx` takes a single `src`; no gallery/pager mode. Rides on the lightbox P0. |
| Clickable URLs (linkify) in post body and comments | basic | P1 | Facebook, X, Threads, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, Discord, Meetup (not Instagram captions) | `.postBody` is plain pre-wrap; `ChatView.tsx URL_RE` linkifies chat only. A pasted URL in a post is dead text; the regex already exists. |
| @mention people in post body (autocomplete + notify) | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, TikTok, Snapchat, Nextdoor, BeReal, Discord, Reddit u/ | `CreatePostRequestSchema` (`api.ts:2142`) has no mentions; chat messages do (API §1.33). Posts are the one surface where 'name people' matters most. |
| Drafts / composer persistence | basic | P1 | X, TikTok, Threads, Reddit, Nextdoor autosave, Discord per-channel, Snapchat, Facebook, Meetup | Composer state is React `useState` only. Navigating away loses text and strands uploaded attachments as orphan-GC candidates. |
| Threaded replies to comments | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, Reddit, Discord, TikTok, Nextdoor, Meetup, Telegram discussion | `post_comments` (`schema.ts:1448`) has no `parent_id`; list is flat `created_at ASC`. Flat comments are the most visible 'unfinished' signal in the comments UX. |
| Save / bookmark a post (private) | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads, Snapchat, Telegram, Meetup/Luma | No `saved_posts`; only 'My gallery' of my OWN media (FEATURES #94). Entirely private, so no psychology conflict. |
| Share / forward a post into a chat (DM or group) | basic | P1 | Instagram, X, TikTok, Snapchat, Reddit, Telegram, WhatsApp, Facebook Messenger | `ForwardSheet.tsx` takes a `message` only (`:22`); no post→conversation path. For a chat-first app this is the most natural share and it is absent. Must server-enforce audience nesting (same table as join cards). |
| Edit a post (caption, location, tags) with 'Edited' label | basic | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Reddit, Nextdoor, Telegram, BeReal caption, Threads 5-min, X Premium (absent: TikTok, Snapchat) | No `PATCH /api/posts`; menu has only 'Delete post'; `posts` has no `edited_at` (`schema.ts:1356`). Fixing a typo means delete + repost and losing every face and comment. |
| Report a comment | basic | P1 | Reddit, Nextdoor, TikTok, Instagram, Facebook | No comment kind in `ReportKindSchema`; no control in `CommentRow`. |
| Daily digest of reactions/comments on my posts (named faces) | basic | P1 | all apps notify on likes/comments; Bored Chat's own PSYCHOLOGY §6 sanctions 2–3 daily digests | Only crypto digests exist in the server; nothing in SPECS. Today an author learns about replies only by scrolling back — the doctrine is applied more strictly than its own brief. |
| 'See more' truncation for long post bodies | basic | P1 | Instagram, X, Facebook, Threads, Reddit, Nextdoor | `.postBody` has no line-clamp; only grid tiles clamp (`.gridTileBody` 5 lines). A 2000-char post fills the list view with no collapse. |
| Close / cancel the post composer | basic | P1 | all apps | `setComposeOpen` is only ever called with `true` (`FeedView.tsx:168`); the footer has only 'Post'. A mis-tap on the compose icon leaves a permanent card above the feed until navigation. |
| Hide post / 'not interested' | standard | P1 | Facebook, X, Instagram, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Snapchat, BeReal, Threads, Luma | No hide table/route; no menu on non-own posts. Lighter need in a chronological feed, but Discover has zero per-post control; belongs in the P0 menu. |
| `post.deleted` / `post.updated` live events | standard | P1 | X ('This post was deleted'), Facebook, Telegram, Discord | `packages/shared/src/ws.ts:332` defines `post.new` only; DELETE and admin takedown publish nothing. Other viewers keep a stale card and hit 404 on interaction. |
| Rate limiting on comments and reactions (anti-spam) | standard | P1 | Reddit, Discord, Nextdoor, every large platform | Only `postcreate:` is limited (`feed/routes.ts:140`); `POST .../comments` (`:566`), `.../reactions` (`:497`) and comment reactions (`:682`) have none. A member can flood a post with unlimited ≤500-char or image comments; share-page reports are limited, comments are not. |
| Live character counter in composer | basic | P2 | Threads, X, TikTok, Reddit | Textarea uses `maxLength` only (`FeedView.tsx:674`); users hit the silent 2000 wall with no feedback. |
| Camera capture shortcut in composer | basic | P2 | Instagram, TikTok, Snapchat, BeReal, Nextdoor, Facebook | File inputs have no `capture` attribute. One attribute away; the OS picker usually still offers the camera. |
| Double-tap media to like | basic | P2 | Instagram, TikTok, Threads | No `onDoubleClick` handler on `PostCard`. Gesture polish; less noticed because grid is the default. |
| @mentions inside comments | basic | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, BeReal, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Reddit | `CreateCommentRequestSchema` (`api.ts:2190`) is body + `attachment_id` only. Blocked on the same mention infra as posts. |
| Multi-line comments | basic | P2 | all apps | Comment composer is a single-line `<input>` and Enter submits (`FeedView.tsx:1598-1610`); the server allows newlines. |
| Comment delete confirmation / undo | basic | P2 | Instagram, Threads (swipe/long-press → Delete, confirmed) | `removeComment` calls `deleteComment` immediately from the '×' (`FeedView.tsx:1722`); server-side it is a HARD delete (`routes.ts:616`) with no undo. An accidental-loss trap on phones. |
| Multi-image indicator on grid tiles | basic | P2 | Instagram (stack icon) | `FeedGridTile` shows only `attachments[0]` (`FeedView.tsx:409`) and badges only video; a 4-photo post is indistinguishable from a 1-photo post in the default mosaic. |
| Hashtags / topic tags on posts | basic | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok, Snapchat, Threads (topic), Nextdoor (categories), Telegram, Meetup topics | Nothing in feed module, shared or API.md; FEATURES #108 search excludes posts. No way to group or find posts at all; Nextdoor-style topic chips (no trending) are the form that fits the psychology rules — see Feed filters. |
| Tag people in photos (photo tags, tagged tab) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X (up to 10) | `attachments` table has no tag rows; no tag UI. Needs mention infra first. |
| Audience: close friends / custom lists / friends-except / only-me | standard | P2 | Facebook, Instagram close friends, TikTok only-me, BeReal friends-of-friends, Snapchat private story | Only two audiences; SPECS v1.0b (98) plans a reusable 'close friends' list for STORIES only. Half the benchmark offers at least 'only me' or 'close friends'. |
| Per-post comment controls (turn off / who can comment) | standard | P2 | Facebook, Instagram, X, Threads, TikTok, Nextdoor, Snapchat Spotlight, Luma | No `comments_disabled` column or route; API §1.36 lets every viewer comment. Authors cannot close a thread that goes sideways. |
| Alt text on post images | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit, Threads, Discord (absent: TikTok, Snapchat, BeReal, Nextdoor, Meetup) | No alt column; `<img alt="">` (`FeedView.tsx:1015`). Accessibility gap shared with chat. |
| Schedule a post | standard | P2 | Instagram, Threads, TikTok, Telegram, X web, Luma (absent: Facebook personal, Reddit, Snapchat) | Scheduled sends exist only for chat (`modules/scheduled`, `ScheduleSheet.tsx`); no `scheduled_posts`. The chat scheduler could be reused. |
| Content warning / spoiler / NSFW flag per post | standard | P2 | X, Reddit, Threads spoiler, Discord spoiler, Facebook overlays, TikTok photosensitivity | No flag anywhere. The public Discover lane has no way for authors to self-label. |
| Sensitive-content blur / interstitial on media | standard | P2 | Reddit, Facebook, X, TikTok, Snapchat | No flag on posts or attachments; no blur class. Pairs with the missing content warning. |
| Edit a comment with 'edited' marker | standard | P2 | Facebook, Reddit, Nextdoor, Discord (absent: Instagram, X, TikTok, Threads) | No PATCH route; `post_comments` has no `edited_at`. |
| Post-author comment moderation (hide, restrict commenter, delete-and-block) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat Spotlight, Meetup/Luma hosts | Only hard delete exists; no hidden flag or restrict concept. |
| Pin post to profile | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, Reddit, BeReal, Telegram channels | No pinned column; `/u/[username]` renders only `ActivityStrip`; FEATURES #107 plans profile tabs without pin. |
| Mute / snooze an author (soft block, restrict) | standard | P2 | Facebook snooze, X, Threads, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Discord ignore, Meetup | No `muted_users` concept; only blocks (`lib/blocks.ts`). Block is the only lever and it is nuclear. |
| Feed filters (kind / media-only / topic chips) | standard | P2 | Nextdoor topic chips, Reddit, Discord tag filters, Meetup filters, Luma categories | `FeedQuerySchema` takes only lane/cursor/limit. The Local board has a chip row; the feed has nothing. |
| Search posts by keyword | standard | P2 | X, Threads, TikTok, Reddit, Telegram, Instagram keyword, Nextdoor, Discord forum | FEATURES #108 global search (planned) covers people/groups/listings/my messages — not posts. Combined with no permalink, any post older than the window is gone. |
| Change audience after posting | standard | P2 | TikTok, Threads reply audience, Facebook | No PATCH route; pairs with edit post. |
| Memories / On This Day | standard | P2 | Facebook, Snapchat Memories, BeReal Memories | No resurfacing job. PSYCHOLOGY §8 explicitly wants a past recap resurfaced to bored users — a sanctioned gap. |
| Client-side image downscale / compression before upload | standard | P2 | Instagram, WhatsApp, Telegram (downscale to ~1080–2048px) | `imageUpload.ts` converts HEIC only; the server re-encodes at full dimensions and accepts up to `MAX_IMAGE_BYTES` (25 MB). A 12 MP phone photo uploads raw on mobile data. |
| Offline cache of the last-loaded feed | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Telegram | `lib/db.ts` (IndexedDB) has stores for messages/conv_meta/outbox/seen_fx and no feed/post store; a cold start while offline renders nothing despite the PWA caching messages. |
| Admin takedown of a comment | standard | P2 | Reddit, Discord, Nextdoor, Telegram | `ADMIN_CONTENT_TYPES` is post/beacon/listing (`constants.ts:1240`); an abusive comment on a public post can only be removed by the post author or by taking down the whole post. Moderation stops one level above where abuse usually lands. |
| Shared album with contributors (co-owned photo set as a post object) | standard | P2 | Facebook shared albums, Partiful and Meetup event albums (Google Photos outside the benchmark) | No `album` anywhere in `apps/server/src`, `apps/web`, `packages/shared` or `docs` (grep: 0 hits). The plan group's afterglow recap (API §1.15) bundles photos into one system message, but nobody can add to it afterwards and it cannot be posted as an object. Natural fit for a plans app; the recap is the seed. |
| Card vs compact density toggle for the list view | standard | P2 | Reddit card/classic/compact, Discord cozy/compact | `FeedLayout` is 'grid' or 'list' (`FeedView.tsx:84`) and the list has one density; no `compact` in `FeedView.tsx` or `social.module.css`. With a comment form on every card there is no way to get a dense text list. |
| Feed tab new-activity indicator (quiet dot for new posts from people) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, X | `Tabs.tsx:34` dots only chats (unread) and profile (requests). Recommended: a quiet dot on `post.new` from people you follow is user-pulled information, not a nudge to act (PSYCHOLOGY rule 4 bans unprompted prompts, not the fact that something exists), and it is the same pattern the Chats dot already uses. The Notifications section's week plan adds it; this section takes the same position. |
| Keyboard submit (Ctrl/Cmd+Enter) in the post composer | basic | P3 | X, Facebook, Reddit, Discord | Post textarea has no `onKeyDown`; only the comment input submits on Enter. |
| Paste an image from the clipboard into the post composer | standard | P3 | X, Facebook, Mastodon, Reddit, Discord (desktop) | No `onPaste`/`clipboardData` anywhere in `apps/web/components` (grep: 0 files); files enter only through the hidden `<input type=file>`. Also absent from the chat composer. |
| Poll close time / duration | standard | P3 | X (5min–7d), Reddit (1–7d), Nextdoor, Telegram quiz | API §1.50 'No `closes_at`'; no column. Deliberate, but polls never resolve, which weakens the 'song for the ride' use case. |
| Poll voter faces (who voted for what) | standard | P3 | Telegram (non-anonymous polls), Facebook groups, Partiful | API §1.50 'No voter lists'; tallies only. The faces-not-counts doctrine would actually favour named voters over the numeric tallies ADR-016 allows. |
| Question / ask-for-recommendations prompt post | standard | P3 | Facebook, Nextdoor (≈30% of posts), Hinge poll prompt | Not in `POST_KINDS`; nothing in SPECS/FEATURES. Fits a local-community board; nearest analog is the chat Vibe check. |
| Multi-post thread chain at compose time | standard | P3 | X, Threads | No parent/thread field in `CreatePostRequestSchema`. Text-platform feature; low fit for a media/plans feed. |
| Text post with colored background / styled card | standard | P3 | Facebook, TikTok, Snapchat | No background/style field; grid text tiles are plain panels. Would make text tiles in the default grid less dead. |
| Rich text formatting (bold/italic/lists/code/spoiler) | standard | P3 | Reddit, Telegram, Discord, Luma, Meetup | Body rendered as raw text; no markdown parser in `apps/web/lib`. Not expected on photo-first feeds. |
| Post title / subject line | standard | P3 | Reddit, Nextdoor, Discord forum, Meetup | `posts` has body only. Board listings carry titles; feed posts do not. |
| Video trim / cover-frame selection in composer | standard | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, Reddit, Facebook, Snapchat | `ImageEditSheet.tsx` is static images only; worker picks the poster via `-vf thumbnail`. Low fit. |
| Pin a comment | standard | P3 | Instagram (3), Threads, TikTok, Nextdoor | No pinned column on `post_comments`. |
| 'Author' badge on the post author's own comments | standard | P3 | X, Threads, Instagram, Reddit OP, TikTok creator | `CommentRow` renders name/body only. |
| Bookmark collections / folders | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X Premium, Telegram tags | Depends on bookmarks existing first. |
| Share post as rendered image card | standard | P3 | Threads, BeReal, Partiful, Instagram story sticker | No canvas/OG-image render; the share page only sets meta. |
| Recently deleted / trash restore window | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook | `hardDeletePost` is immediate and irreversible. |
| Archive post (hide from profile without deleting) | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook | No archived flag; no profile posts list to hide from yet. |
| Feed sort (newest vs recent activity) | standard | P3 | Nextdoor, Reddit, Discord forum | Both lanes strictly `created_at DESC`. Activity sort is not engagement ranking but sits close to the line. |
| Mute keywords / content filters | standard | P3 | X mute words, TikTok keyword filters, Reddit NSFW blur, Snapchat content prefs | No filter table or client setting. |
| Drag-and-drop files onto the composer (desktop) | standard | P3 | X, Facebook, Discord, Mastodon | `onDrop`/`onDragOver` exist only in `ChatView.tsx`; nothing in `FeedView.tsx`. |
| Translate post / comment | standard | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Threads, TikTok, Reddit, Telegram Premium, Nextdoor | Needs an external MT API (rule 7) or a self-hosted model. |
| Captions track / auto captions on video | standard | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, X, Telegram | No `<track>`; no ASR step. ASR would need a self-hosted model to satisfy rule 7. |
| Undo window after posting | standard | P3 | Threads, Instagram (brief), Gmail-style | `createPost` POSTs and prepends immediately; the drunk-mode 10s send-undo exists only for chat (FEATURES #73). |
| Multiple-choice (multi-select) polls | standard | P3 | Telegram, WhatsApp, Discord | `post_poll_votes` PK is `(post_id, user_id)`; vote request takes a single `option_idx`. |
| Disappearing / ghost posts (24h) | advanced | P3 | Threads (Oct 2025) | No `expires_at` on posts. Stories already cover the ephemeral case. |
| Collab / co-authored post | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook | Single `author_id`; no collaborators table. Niche; join cards already put multiple faces on a card. |
| BeReal-style dual-camera post with timed window and 'late' badges | advanced | P3 | BeReal | No capture attribute or dual-stream code. Lateness labels and post-to-view gates are guilt/reciprocity mechanics that sit badly with 'no guilt streaks'. |
| AI assist / rewrite suggestions in composer | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor Assistant, X Grok | No LLM call anywhere. Conflicts with rule 7 unless self-hosted. |
| Embed post / QR code for a post | advanced | P3 | X, Facebook, Threads, Telegram, Reddit, Instagram QR, TikTok QR | `QrCode.tsx` exists for other surfaces; no embed route. |
| Private post insights / analytics for the author | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok, Threads, Reddit, Telegram, Snapchat | API §1.36 'no numeric total exists in any payload'. Only a named-faces digest (PSYCHOLOGY §9) would fit. |
| Custom feeds / lists / favorites | advanced | P3 | Reddit multireddits, Threads custom feeds, X Lists, Facebook Favorites | Only two fixed lanes. |
| Music / licensed audio attached to posts | standard | never | Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, Snapchat, BeReal (now-playing chip) | Golden rule 7 (no third-party SaaS). A licensed catalogue is impossible self-hosted; not a psychology ban but equally final. |
| GIF picker (GIPHY/Tenor search) | standard | never | X, Threads, Reddit, Facebook, TikTok stickers | Golden rule 7 'no Giphy'; upload-your-own GIF (API §1.51) is the sanctioned path — which currently does not animate (see Half-built). |
| Ads / sponsored posts in feed | standard | never | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, X, TikTok, Snapchat, BeReal, Nextdoor, Telegram, Reddit | Golden rule 7 'Money never moves through the app'. Absent on purpose. |
| Paid media / awards / tips / creator monetization | advanced | never | Telegram Stars, Reddit awards, X subscriptions, TikTok Series, Snapchat Spotlight payouts | SPECS standing decision 'no payments'; rule 7. Doctrine. |

### Planned already

- **Author's posts tab on their profile (grid/list)** (basic, P0) — `docs/FEATURES.md` #107 profile tabs. `/u/[username]` renders only `ActivityStrip`; no `GET /api/users/:username/posts`. Tap a friend's avatar in the first hour and their profile shows no posts — the one screen every benchmark builds around. Until it ships, a post older than the feed window is unreachable from its author.
- **Link card / URL unfurl on posts** (basic, P1) — `docs/ROADMAP.md:118` 'posts … reusing link_previews'. The unfurl machinery (`PreviewCard.tsx`, API §1.8) exists for chat; `FeedView.tsx` never imports it. Facebook, Reddit, X, Threads, Nextdoor, Discord, Telegram all have it.
- **Share via OS share sheet (`navigator.share`)** (basic, P1) — `docs/SPECS.md` v1.0d 'Share audit' (110) plans it on every surface. Today `navigator.share` is used only in `InviteSheet.tsx:100`; posts are clipboard-only. Owner-emphasised growth loop.
- **'N people were glad you posted this' private author signal** (advanced, P2) — `docs/PSYCHOLOGY.md` §9 (liking-gap research). Not built, not in SPECS. This is the sanctioned shape of the missing digest above; build them as one job.

### Deliberately not built

- **Repost / quote post / crosspost** — API §1.36 'no ranking, no recommendations, no resharing'; PSYCHOLOGY rules 3 & 5. Right call for this product: quote-posts are the virality and dunking engine, and a friends-plus-local-board app does not need reach. The real need (showing a friend a post) is 'send into a chat', which is missing — ship that and the ban costs nothing.
- **Share post to story** — same doctrine. Agree; marginal either way.
- **Public like / reaction counts** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; `PostReactionAggregateSchema` has no count. Agree without reservation; it is honoured end to end and is the product's clearest differentiator.
- **Comment sort / 'top comments'** — API §1.36 'created_at ASC'; rule 5. Agree for friend-sized threads. Threaded replies (missing, P1) are not ranking and should not be swept up in this ban.
- **Public comment count ('View N comments')** — `has_more_comments` is a boolean. Agree, but the replacement must exist: a 'View replies' line with the latest commenters' faces. Today there is neither a count nor faces, just the last 3 comments.
- **Public view counts on posts** — rule 3. Agree.
- **Real-time notification pings for reactions/comments on my post** — API §1.36 'no approval pings, rule 4'. Agree on pings. But rule 6 of PSYCHOLOGY says digest instead of nothing, and nothing is what shipped; applied this way the doctrine is stricter than its own brief and authors simply never find out.
- **Algorithmic ranked 'For You' feed / recommendations** — API §1.36, §1.49; rule 5. Agree.
- **Infinite scroll** — PSYCHOLOGY never-build; `lib/feed.ts` header. Agree; the end card is the right shape (it just needs a CTA).
- **Pull-to-refresh** — PSYCHOLOGY never-build; API §1.36. This is the one I would push back on. Pull-to-refresh is a gesture, not a slot machine; the variable reward comes from what a ranked, bottomless feed returns, and this feed is chronological and finite. Banning the universal mobile gesture buys nothing once a 'N new posts' pill exists. Harmless as long as the manual refresh is made findable, but it is overreach, not doctrine.
- **Trending topics / hashtags module** — API §1.36 'no ranking, no recommendations'; rule 5. Agree; topic chips without trending (Nextdoor style) are the compatible form of hashtags.
- **Live update of reactions/comments on a post I am viewing** — `feed/routes.ts:5-8`, API.md:1486-1489 and the `post.new` row of the WS table ('deliberately fan out NOTHING'). Half agree. No pings is right; refusing to reconcile a card the user is already looking at is staleness, not restraint — an open post sheet can show a comment that was deleted 10 minutes ago. At minimum `post.deleted` should exist, and the detail sheet should refetch on focus.

### How it looks

Feed findings from the captured screens (phone 390x844 @2x and desktop, light scheme as captured) plus the CSS that produces them. Every number below is read from a named screenshot or a named CSS rule.

- **feed-post-menu.png — no ⋯ on others' posts (blocker).** The menu renders only when `own` (`FeedView.tsx:975`) and its single item is red 'Delete post'. Instagram, Threads, X and Facebook put Report / Not interested / Mute / Block behind ⋯ on every post. Fix: render ⋯ on every card — own: Edit · Copy link · Delete; others: Report (ReportSheet with a post target) · Hide · Block @user — and fold the standalone copy-link ghost icon into it.
- **phone-feed-list.png — post card anatomy, top to bottom (major).** 38px avatar (`size={38}`, `FeedView.tsx:953`); name as a 14px bold line (`.postHeadName strong`) and '@handle · 3:47 · public' as a second 12px muted line; body 15px/1.5 (`.postBody`); media as a fixed 2x2 collage, each image `max-height: 340px; object-fit: cover`; poll as a 17px serif question (`.postPollQ`) over 13.5px option rows; a reaction row whose only control when nobody has reacted is an 18px smiley-plus (`IconSmilePlus` default size 18 inside `.btn-icon`, 8px padding → a 36px ghost button); up to three 13.5px comment bubbles (`.commentBubble`, `POST_COMMENTS_PREVIEW_MAX=3`); then a full comment form — image button + 16px 'Say something back…' pill input (`.commentInputRow input`, 8px/14px padding) + Reply — under EVERY card. Summing the CSS for a one-line text post with no comments: 16px card padding + 38px head + 6 + 23px body + 6 + 44px react row + 43px comment form + 16px padding ≈ 190px, of which the react row and the form are ≈90px of chrome. Instagram: 32px header with ⋯, edge-to-edge aspect-preserved media, one 24px action row (heart · comment · send · bookmark), liked-by faces, caption, 'View all comments', time — no input. Threads: 36px avatar, name · time · ⋯ on one line, text, media, one action row, 'N replies'. X: the same with views and bookmark. Facebook: Like / Comment / Share row, inline comment box only on the post you open. BeReal: comments behind a tap. Fix: one meta line, one 40px action row (React · Reply · Send · ⋯), comments opening in the sheet that already exists for grid tiles; keep inline typing only inside that sheet.
- **feed-grid-default.png — grid is the default for Friends (major).** Three columns of 118px rows (`.feedGrid grid-auto-rows: 118px`). The poll tile shows a 13.5px bold question (`.gridPollQ`) and 'poll · tap to vote' with no author; the text tile shows an 11.5px excerpt (`.gridTileBody`) with 'Maya Chen' at 10px (`.gridTileAuthor`); only the 2x2 hero tile gets the 12px author pill (`.gridAuthorPill`). A friends feed where you cannot see whose post it is contradicts 'faces, not counters'. With two posts the third cell is empty and the end card's check icon sits at ≈300px (y≈605 of 1688 @2x) with ≈395px — 47% of the 844px frame — blank beneath it before the tab bar. Instagram uses a grid only on a profile or on Explore; Threads, X, Facebook and BeReal never grid a friends feed. Fix: default Friends to list, make grid the Discover default; if the owner keeps grid for Friends, every tile gets an avatar+name pill, text/poll tiles span 2 columns at ≥13px, the grid is 2-up under 390px, and under 4 posts fall back to list.
- **phone-feed-list.png — images open the raw proxy URL in a new tab (major).** `<a href={a.url} target="_blank">` on post images (`FeedView.tsx:1013`) and comment images (`:1674`); on an installed PWA this throws the user out to Safari/Chrome. Chat, board and profile already use `MediaLightbox`. Fix: open the lightbox with all attachments so it swipes between the up-to-4 images; tap-to-fullscreen on videos.
- **phone-feed-list.png — no permalink (major).** Tapping the body or timestamp does nothing; only the grid sheet shows a post alone; nothing can deep-link a comment or mention. Fix: `/feed/[postId]` (or `?post=` opening the existing sheet) rendering PostCard + full thread; timestamp and body tappable.
- **phone-feed-list.png — no 'Send to a chat' (major).** The only share is copy-public-link, public posts only (`FeedView.tsx:964`). Instagram's paper-plane, X 'Send via DM', Facebook 'Send in Messenger' are primary actions. Fix: 'Send' → ForwardSheet; server creates a message carrying a post reference; enforce audience nesting.
- **feed-audience.png vs phone-feed-compose.png — the Audience row lies (major).** 'Audience … Connections ›' is a chevron row whose `onClick` flips friends↔public (`FeedView.tsx:884`); the captured screen shows a flipped value, not a picker. Facebook/Instagram open a radio sheet with one-line descriptions. Fix: a two-row sheet (Connections / Public — 'anyone on this server; gets a share link') or drop the chevron for an inline segmented control.
- **phone-feed-list.png — poll question rendered twice (major, also an a11y nit).** The card renders `post.body` (`FeedView.tsx:1000`) and then `poll.question` (`:1132`), and the composer offers both fields ('Anything to add under the poll?' and 'The question'), so 'song for the ride?' appears in 15px sans and again in 17px serif and screen readers hear it twice. `.postPollOpt` also hard-codes `border: 1px solid #3a4052` (`social.module.css:1484`) — the DARK `--border-strong` hex — so feed poll rows have 10.3:1 near-black outlines on a white card while every other border in light mode is `--border` at 1.34:1; the chat poll (`beacons.module.css .pollOpt`) uses tokens and does not. X/Threads: the post text IS the question. Fix: when kind=poll, hide the body or relabel it 'Add context (optional)' and render it under the options; dedupe when `body.trim() === question.trim()`; one `<Poll>` component consuming tokens only.
- **phone-feed-list.png — reactions are an 18px ghost smiley (major).** The primary interaction is the least visible element on the card, and posts get only the 6-emoji `QUICK_REACTIONS` row (`FeedView.tsx:1078`) while comments get the full `ReactionPicker` with the avatar stamp (`:1728`). Instagram: 24px heart/comment/share row; BeReal: RealMoji is the headline reaction — this app has the mechanism and hides it. Fix: a proper action row with 22–24px stroke icons and 44px hit targets; long-press/hover opens the same ReactionPicker as comments and chat.
- **feed-grid-default.png — end card has no action (major).** 'You're all caught up — nothing new from your people — go make a plan' with nothing to tap. Instagram's caught-up card offers 'View older posts'. Fix: 'I'm bored — light a beacon' (→ /local with BeaconCreateSheet open) and 'See Discover' on Friends / 'Find people' on Discover. Navigation, not more feed. The Friends copy is also wrong for a zero-connection account — 'You're all caught up' is false when they have nobody; branch on connections count and offer Find people / Copy invite link.
- **feed-grid-default.png — 124px of chrome before the first post (major).** A 24px serif 'bored.' wordmark (`.feedTitle`) instead of a title, the grid/list pill in the header, then boxed Friends/Discover lane buttons (`.laneBtn`, 1.5px border, ≈39px tall: y≈142–220 @2x) beside a 40px outlined refresh square (`.laneRefresh`); the first tile's top edge is at y≈248 of 1688 @2x. Two 'pick one' treatments on adjacent rows (outlined blocks for lanes, a sliding pill for layout). Threads 'For you / Following' and Instagram 'Following / Favorites' are underlined text tabs. Fix: a fixed 56px topbar titled 'Feed', lanes as a 36px segmented control, refresh folded into a 'New posts' pill that appears only when the server reports newer items.
- **phone-feed-list.png — '@maya · 3:47 · public' (minor).** `format.ts:3-13` gives a bare 12-hour clock with no am/pm and no relative time; ambiguous today, becomes 'Oct 18' tomorrow. Threads, X, Instagram, Facebook show '2h' / '3d'. Fix: relative time for posts and comments ('now', '12m', '2h', '3d', then 'Oct 2') wrapped in `<time dateTime>`; keep the compact clock for the chat list only.
- **phone-feed-list.png — author name is plain text (minor).** Only the avatar is a `<Link>` (`FeedView.tsx:952`); the `<strong>` name (`:956`) is dead. Fix: wrap avatar + name + handle in one link with a 44px row hit area.
- **phone-feed-compose.png — composer anatomy (minor x3).** The kind-picker row (`.kindRow`) sits ABOVE the 36px avatar + textarea (`FeedView.tsx:651` before `:678`), so it reads as a form before you see where to type; the fourth chip is labelled 'Join my…' and truncates at 390px; the dashed 86x86 add tile (`.photoAddTile`) is always visible even with nothing attached; with 'Add a place', the audience row, the policy note and the Post button, the empty composer runs ≈820 of the 844px frame as read from phone-feed-compose.png and pushes the feed off-screen; the policy note 'Replies are named faces…' repeats on every open at 12px in `--faint` (#aab2c2 on white = 2.13:1, fails AA). Threads/X: textarea first, poll/attachment affordances as icons beneath. Fix: textarea + avatar first; kind picker as icon chips under the text ('Share a plan' instead of 'Join my…'); a toolbar row (photo · place · audience) with the thumb strip only once an image is picked; note shown once per device then an info icon. Target: empty composer ≤260px.
- **phone-feed-compose.png — disabled 'Post' reads as the darkest element on the card (major, visual + a11y).** `.btn:disabled` is `opacity: 0.55` over the light-scheme `--accent-grad` (#1c2130 → #3a4152) with white ink, which still renders as a charcoal pill while inert; people will tap it and assume the app is broken. Instagram keeps 'Share' brand-blue at ~40% opacity. Fix: `.btn-primary:disabled { opacity: .4 }` keeping the gradient, plus a helper line and `aria-describedby`.
- **feed-post-menu.png — one-item red menu, no Edit (minor).** A menu whose only item is destructive feels like a trap; typos cannot be fixed. Fix: Edit · Copy link · Delete with `PATCH /api/posts/:id` and an 'edited' marker.
- **phone-feed-list.png — poll rows show '0 0 0' before voting; copy 'be the tiebreaker before there's a tie' (minor).** `.postPollCount` (12px bold) renders unconditionally (`FeedView.tsx:1153-1155`) while the bar waits for `voted`; after voting the bold digit reads like a badge. X/Instagram hide results until you vote, then show percentages. Fix: gate the count on `voted` like the bar; after voting show percent primary, count in the meta line; copy 'No votes yet — yours sets the pace.'
- **phone-feed-list.png — bare '×' on every comment the author may delete (minor).** `.commentDelete` is a 13px glyph with 4px padding (≈21px hit box) that hard-deletes on one tap (`FeedView.tsx:1722`). Under your own post every friend's reply carries it. Instagram/Threads: swipe/long-press → Delete. Fix: move delete behind the comment's long-press/⋯ (the ReactionPicker overlay already opens on long-press) with confirm.
- **feed-post-menu.png / phone-feed-compose.png — emoji as UI icons inside cards (minor).** '🎮' at an inline `fontSize: 22` (`FeedView.tsx:1214`), '📍 Add a place', 'Join 🙋', 'needs 2 more 🔥', 'Claim 🎯' (`:1242`, `:1318`, `:1366`, `:1383`) while the header, kind chips and audience row use stroke icons. Emoji render differently per OS. Fix: the existing stroke-icon family; emoji only in user content and reactions.
- **desk-feed.png — nothing lines up (minor).** Header actions align to the 680px `.page` column while the feed is a 560px `.feedWrap` column, so the wordmark sits 60px left of the lane bar and '+' 60px right of refresh ((680 − 560) / 2); at a 1280px viewport the 680px page leaves 300px of empty gutter per side. Fix: one max-width for topbar and `.feedWrap`; at ≥1100px use the spare width for an opened-post pane instead of a sheet.
- **phone-chats.png vs feed-grid-default.png — stories live only on Chats (minor).** `StoriesStrip` is mounted in `ConversationListView.tsx:115` and nowhere on Feed. A new user looking for stories opens Feed first. Fix: render `StoriesStrip` at the top of Feed too.
- **feed-grid-default.png / phone-feed-list.png — tap targets and type floor (a11y, major/minor).** `.layoutBtn` is 34x26; `.laneBtn` ≈39px; `.iconBtn` 38x38; `.gridTileAuthor` 10px; `.gridTileBody` 11.5px; tab labels 10.5px in `--inactive` (#8a93a6 on #f4f6fb = 2.85:1). Fix: ≥44px hit boxes via padding/pseudo-element; no text under 12px; inactive labels in `--muted` (5.45:1).
- **Identical letter avatars (visual, major).** `.avatar` (`globals.css:534-546`) paints every initial-letter fallback with the same `--accent-soft` tint (8% ink on white) and `--accent` text, so a feed of F, A and M is visually one person. WhatsApp/Telegram hash the user id to a hue. Fix: `hash(user_id) % 6` → six low-saturation tints.
- **`alt=""` on every feed image (nit).** `FeedView.tsx:1015`, `:444`, `:1678`; no alt field in the composer or `ImageEditSheet`. Fix: optional alt text stored on the attachment.
- **Light scheme (blocker, app-wide, hits every feed shot).** `globals.css:81-82` says light was 'not designed in the Royal pass — equivalents derived': `--panel-2` (#eef1f8) on a white card is 1.13:1 and `--border` (#d9dfec) is 1.34:1, so reaction chips, comment bubbles and the lane buttons barely separate from the card. Playwright's default is light and so is most phones'. Fix: treat light as a first-class palette and re-shoot the feed in both schemes.

### If I had one week

1. **Safety and the options menu (1.5 days) — both P0s in one pass.** Add `post` and `comment` kinds to `ReportKindSchema`, `reports/routes.ts` and `ReportSheet.tsx`; render ⋯ on every card (own: Edit · Copy link · Revoke link · Delete; others: Report · Hide · Block); replace `window.confirm` with the sheet pattern; move comment delete behind long-press with confirm; add `fixedWindow` limits to comments and reactions. Closes the inverted-safety finding and the doc/code mismatch.
2. **Wire the plumbing the app already has (1.5 days) — the lightbox P0 and the P1 one-liners.** `MediaLightbox` for post and comment images with a multi-attachment pager and Save; a pending-video slate instead of the `<img>` branch (the other P0); `BlurhashCanvas` on feed `<img>`; `URL_RE` linkify plus `PreviewCard` unfurl on bodies and comments; relative time via one formatter wrapped in `<time>`; comment timestamps; avatar + name + handle as one profile link; `capture` on the file input; a character counter. None of this needs a migration.
3. **Permalink and `post.deleted` (1 day).** `/feed/[postId]` rendering `PostCard` + the full thread, with body/timestamp tappable and `ActivityStrip` linking to it; a `post.deleted` WS event published from DELETE and admin takedown so stale cards vanish; refetch the open sheet on focus. The profile posts tab (P0, planned #107) rides on the same `GET /api/users/:username/posts` + card reuse.
4. **Card and composer rebuild (1.5 days).** Replace the per-card comment form with a slim action row (React · Reply · Send · ⋯) that opens the existing sheet; default Friends to list; dedupe the poll question and gate tallies on `voted`; audience row opens a real two-option sheet; Cancel button + localStorage drafts; collapse the add tile into a toolbar; fix the stale-closure attachment cap; close the composer and toast on success.
5. **Edit post + the digest the doctrine already allows (1 day).** `PATCH /api/posts/:id` (body, location, attachments order) with `edited_at` and an 'Edited' marker; a daily job that pushes one 'Maya, Arjun and 2 others were glad you posted this' card per author (PSYCHOLOGY §6/§9) — named faces, no counts, no pings. Share-into-chat via `ForwardSheet` is the next P1 after this week; it needs a new message kind and audience-nesting enforcement, so it does not fit in five days.


---

## Stories — a 2017 photo ring with the lifecycle missing

**Verdict.** Stories are a 2017-grade photo ring with careful privacy plumbing underneath, and the plumbing is not what users meet. The privacy design is right (audience is 404-shaped: unknown = out-of-audience = blocked = expired; `story_views` has no timestamp column; no count on any DTO; share links are author-minted and die with the story) but it is a floor, not a headline. The headline is the missing lifecycle. A viewer of someone else's story has zero actions: no reply, no reaction, no DM, no report, no mute, no block, no profile link. An author cannot delete their account and take their stories with them, because no delete-account route exists (`users/routes.ts` has GET/PATCH `/me` and avatar routes only). **14 of 42 basic-tier** expectations are fully met (**33%**) and **8 of 59 standard-tier** (**14%**), banned items excluded from both denominators and duplicate rows removed. The composer has no text story, no camera, no audience picker and no editing before publish; the viewer has no swipe and no audio mute; a video story fails on the first post because the client posts before the worker marks the attachment ready. Two bugs ship today: `routes.ts` L366 throws 403 `canvas_closed` for the author of a default (`canvas_mode: 'off'`) story while `StoryCanvas.tsx` L78/L351 shows the author the tools, and every "Stories → Try it" pointer in the app (`constants.ts` L1049, `gallery/page.tsx` L111, tour copy L1029) sends users to `/feed`, which has no tray. The unplanned gaps that matter most are report-a-story with admin takedown, mute-someone's-stories, and a real archive (the sweep keeps the pixels and throws away the caption, date, overlays and viewers). The collaborative canvas is the one genuine differentiator, and it is polish on a composer that cannot draw before posting. Ship the P0 list plus #98/#99 before adding a single sticker.

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |

Docs drift. |

"friends-of" = connections plus anyone you ever DMed (board replies, roulette, support), so the composer copy "Friends see it" (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L206) undersells the reach.
