### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Video story (upload, autoplay in viewer) | basic | Server accepts `kind video` once `att.status === 'ready'` (`routes.ts` L122-127); viewer plays `<video autoPlay playsInline>`. | The upload DTO goes out `pending` (worker hydrates) and the composer posts immediately after upload (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L176-187), so the first attempt reliably 400s `bad_attachment` → generic "Could not post the story." No processing state, no auto-retry, no duration cap, no trim. `attachment.ready` only patches the store. | P0 |
| Decorate before posting: draw, text and stickers inside the composer | basic | Composer = file → crop → caption → canvas mode → post. All drawing lives in `StoryCanvas.tsx` after publish. | An editing step before publish. Today the author cannot decorate their own default story at all: `routes.ts` L366 throws 403 `canvas_closed` when `canvas_mode === 'off'` (the default) for everyone including the author, while `StoryCanvas.tsx` L78/L351 shows the author the tools. Toast says "This canvas is closed". UI/server mismatch. | P0 |
| Tray lives where the app points users (feed/updates surface) and is discoverable | basic | `StoriesStrip` renders only inside `ConversationListView.tsx` L115 (Chats tab). | `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` (`constants.ts` L1049), first-run tour copy (L1027-1031, anchored to the Profile tab) and `gallery/page.tsx` L111 all deep-link "Stories" to `/feed`; `FeedView.tsx` has no story code. Three in-app pointers lead to a page with no tray. Trivial fix, day-one confusion. | P0 |
| Text overlay styles: fonts, colours, alignment, outline, animated text | basic | One 80-char note style (`OVERLAY_TEXT_MAX_CHARS`), tap-to-place (`StoryCanvas.tsx` L457-485). | Every style option, drag, and any of it before publish. | P1 |
| Drawing tools: pen, highlighter, neon, eraser, colour picker, undo | basic | Four fixed pens at a fixed width (`StoryCanvas.tsx` L23-24), stroke capture (L112-160). | Eraser, undo, width, colour picker; works only on an opened canvas after posting. | P1 |
| Sticker placement with drag, resize, rotate, delete (emoji / sticker packs) | basic | Ten fixed emoji (`STAMP_EMOJI`, L25), tap-to-place; scale hard-coded to 1 (L167) although `OVERLAY_EMOJI_SCALE_MIN/MAX` allow 0.5-3. | Drag, resize, rotate, packs. FEATURES #79a promises "draggable emoji stamps"; no drag UI exists. | P1 |
| Full-frame preview before posting (whole photo / playable video, caption over the media, the viewer's real framing) | basic | An 84×84 thumbnail (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L245) plus a Swap button; caption in a separate single-line input; the crop editor is the only full-size look and only for JPEG/PNG (L217-219). | A video shows a text chip with no playback, GIFs skip the editor, and the caption is never previewed over the media; the viewer's `object-fit: contain` framing is first seen after posting, when it is already live to friends. Users post a video story blind. | P1 |
| Upload progress indicator in the story composer | basic | Button label flips to "Uploading…" and disables (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L262). | `api.postForm` is a `fetch()` wrapper (`lib/api.ts` L62) with no progress events; a 100 MB video (`MAX_VIDEO_BYTES`) uploads with no percentage and no cancel. Reads as a hang. | P2 |
| EXIF auto-rotate and metadata (GPS) strip on uploaded story media | basic | JPEG/PNG/WebP are re-encoded by `uploads/pipeline.ts` L199-222 (`sharp(buf).rotate()` then JPEG q85 / WebP q90), which drops EXIF; HEIC is canvas-converted client-side so it arrives stripped. | Animated GIF/WebP/APNG and HEIC-on-server are stored verbatim (L205-206, L223-225) with any embedded metadata intact; video is only remuxed (`jobs/media.ts` L196, `-c copy`), so its metadata atoms survive too. The animated and video paths are the privacy hole. | P2 |
| Blocked pairs invisible both ways on every story surface | basic | Tray, view, viewer list, overlay POST and share-link mint all enforce blocks (`routes.ts` L52-64/L201/L292-295/L352; `sharepages/routes.ts` L126-131). GET /api/stories filters overlays by `myBlocked` (L220-234). | Two author-facing paths bypass the overlay filter: `service.ts` L130 (`blocked = viewerId === row.authorId ? new Set() : ...`) and `routes.ts` L336 (`overlaysForViewer(..., new Set())`). An overlay drawn by someone the author later blocks disappears from GET but re-appears in every `story.updated` frame fanned to the author and in the author's own PATCH response. No test covers a block placed after an overlay exists. Golden rule 5 says blocks are law; a one-line filter in each place. | P2 |
| Private story archive (auto-save expired stories with caption, date, overlays, viewers) | standard | Sweep moves the attachment into `gallery_saves` (`service.ts` L186-194); "Saved stories" chip in gallery (`gallery/page.tsx` L21). FEATURES #94. | A bare media grid. Caption, date, overlays and viewer list are destroyed. The pixels survive, the story does not. | P1 |
| Save / download my own story to the device | standard | GET `/api/users/me/gallery` lists uploads bound to a live story (`gallery/routes.ts` L55), and `MediaLightbox.tsx` L228-230 has a Save `<a download>` for images, so Profile → My gallery → tap → Save works while live. | No download control in the story viewer's author footer (`StoryViewer.tsx` L290-305); gallery videos get native `<video controls>` with no explicit download link. Trivial: `<a download>` through the auth-checked proxy. | P1 |

Admin copy promises the banned user's public presence disappears; their overlays do not. | P2 |
| Rich caption: formatting, links, emoji picker, editable after posting | standard | Plain 140-char single-line input. | Formatting, links, emoji picker, edit (`UpdateStoryRequestSchema` = `{canvas_mode}` only, `api.ts` L3576). | P2 |
| Edit a story after posting (caption, audience, media) | standard | PATCH edits `canvas_mode` only (`routes.ts` L308-340). | Caption, audience, media. | P2 |

The crop sheet presets are Free/1:1/4:5/16:9 (`ImageEditSheet.tsx` L14-19) and lack 9:16, the one aspect stories actually use. | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Delete my account and take my stories, views and canvas overlays with it | basic | P0 | All mainstream apps | No delete-account route exists anywhere (`users/routes.ts` L39-140 is GET/PATCH `/me` plus avatar; no web control either). The schema is ready — `stories.author_id` and both `story_views` keys cascade from `users` (`schema.ts` L1294-1345) — but a cascade on an unreachable operation is not a feature. Owned by the Accounts section; listed here because a user who wants their 24-hour photos gone for good has no lever beyond deleting each story by hand. |
| Video audio: mute/unmute toggle, muted-autoplay fallback, scrubber | basic | P0 | Instagram, Signal, Snapchat, Facebook, Telegram | `<video autoPlay playsInline>` without `muted` (`StoryViewer.tsx` L212-219); `play()` rejections swallowed (L91). Where autoplay-with-sound is blocked the poster sits still, `onEnded` never fires and the progress bar never completes. There is no way to silence a loud story. |
| Confirmation or undo before deleting a story | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat (all confirm) | Footer button fires DELETE immediately (`StoryViewer.tsx` L300-302, `removeOwn`); explicit delete deliberately skips the gallery save (`service.ts` L174-200). One mis-tap destroys the only copy of the photo. The undo-pill pattern (#96) already exists to reuse. |
| Report a story (media/caption) from the viewer + admin takedown | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, Telegram, TikTok | `ReportKindSchema` (`api.ts` L1288-1295) has no `story` kind; `ADMIN_CONTENT_TYPES = ['post','beacon','listing']` (`constants.ts` L1240); ban is the only admin lever. A logged-out stranger can report a shared story page but a logged-in viewer cannot report the story itself. Trust-and-safety hole. |
| Text-only story on a colour/gradient background with fonts | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | `CreateStoryRequestSchema` requires `attachment_id` (`api.ts` L1929-1936); no text template anywhere. The cheapest story type and the one that needs no photo. A client-rendered background image through the existing attachment path needs no schema change. |
| In-app camera capture (photo/video) from the composer | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | `<input type=file>` (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L209-222) has no `capture` attribute; no `getUserMedia` in the composer. Every benchmark opens on the camera. One attribute gives a mobile shortcut; a real camera reuses the getUserMedia path calls already have. |
| Swipe gestures: swipe between people, swipe down to close, swipe up to reply | basic | P1 | Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | `StoryViewer.tsx` has pointer tap zones only; the app's only swipe code is chat swipe-to-reply (`ChatView.tsx` L109-110, L1133) and the discovery card. A swipe is a deliberate act, so it is compatible with the no-auto-advance rule; phone users expect swipe-down to dismiss. |
| "Posted X ago" age in the viewer header | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | `created_at` is in the DTO; the header shows only the expiry label (L200). Allowed: PSYCHOLOGY bans seen-at receipts, not post age. Trivial. |
| DM-the-author shortcut from the viewer / per-viewer reply shortcut in the Seen-by list | basic | P1 | Signal (per-viewer reply), Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, WhatsApp | Viewer footer renders only when `isMine` (L290); the viewers sheet rows are plain `<div>`s (L456-462). A one-line seam that would give viewers any action at all before #99 ships. |
| iPhone video formats (HEVC / .mov) accepted and transcoded to web-playable H.264 | basic | P1 | All mainstream apps transcode server-side | File input accepts `image/*,video/mp4,video/webm` (L213; no `video/quicktime`); the media job only remuxes (`jobs/media.ts` L196, `ffmpeg -c copy`) and never re-encodes, so an HEVC .mp4 plays only in Safari. The video twin of the already-fixed HEIC problem; will surface as "my friend's story is a black box". |
| Deleted story disappears from friends' trays live (`story.deleted` event) | basic | P1 | Every mainstream stories product | `packages/shared/src/ws.ts` defines only `story.new` (L319) and `story.updated` (L543); `hardDeleteStory` (`service.ts` L174-200) publishes nothing. Friends keep the ring until the next GET, tap it, get a broken image and a 404 on the view POST. |
| Block the author (or any safety action) from someone else's story in the viewer | basic | P1 | All mainstream apps | The footer renders only when `isMine` (L290). A story is a pushed-to-you media surface; the viewer is where unwanted content is met and needs the safety exit. Blocking is reachable only from `/u/[username]` or settings. |
| Tap the author avatar/name in the viewer header, or a row in the Seen-by list, to open that person's profile | basic | P2 | Every mainstream story viewer | Header is `<Avatar>` + plain `<span>` (L197-201); Seen-by rows are plain `<div>`s (L456-462). The pattern exists in the same module: `StoryCanvas.tsx` L293 links overlay chips to `/u/${username}`. Natural seam for the planned reply/DM items. |
| Blurhash/poster placeholder while story media loads, and preloading the next segment's media | basic | P2 | All mainstream apps | Bare `<img src>`; no `BlurhashCanvas`, no `new Image()` warm-up, although the attachment DTO carries `blurhash` and `BlurhashCanvas.tsx` is used by chat. Each tap shows a black frame on slow links, the 5 s timer starts before the image is visible, and every auto-advance starts with a cold fetch through the auth proxy. |
| Pause the story timer/video when the app or tab is backgrounded | basic | P2 | Instagram, WhatsApp, Snapchat | No `visibilitychange`/blur listener in `StoryViewer.tsx`; segments keep advancing and views keep being recorded while the phone is locked. Users return to find they "watched" stories they never saw. |
| Mute someone's stories (hide their ring without blocking) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | `UserSettingsSchema` (`api.ts` L1237-1265) has no story keys; no hidden-stories list anywhere; absent from FEATURES and SPECS. The only way to drop a ring is a full block. The most common story-privacy control is absent and not even planned. |
| Story view receipts toggle (don't record my views / hide my viewer list) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp (tied to read receipts), Signal (separate toggle) | `routes.ts` L260-277 records a view unconditionally; the `read_receipts` setting is never consulted. Chat honours read receipts, stories ignore them. Inconsistent privacy. |
| Boomerang / bounce looping clip | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | Nothing in code or docs. Needs camera capture first; client-side canvas/WebCodecs can produce the loop. |
| Links in stories (link sticker / link card with preview) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram (Premium), Signal, Instagram, Facebook (Pages), Snapchat | Overlay kinds are stroke/emoji/text (`STORY_OVERLAY_KINDS`); caption renders as plain text. Chat already unfurls links; reuse the unfurler. |
| Multi-select several photos/videos into several segments in one go | standard | P1 | Instagram, Snapchat (Multi-Snap), Telegram, WhatsApp | Single `<input type=file>` without `multiple` (L209-213); one attachment per story row. The '+' badge forces one composer round-trip per photo. |
| Video duration cap, trim and auto-split of long clips | standard | P1 | Instagram (60 s auto-split), Snapchat, WhatsApp (60 s), Telegram (60 s), Facebook (20 s) | No story video duration constant (chat backgrounds have `CHAT_BG_VIDEO_MAX_DURATION_S` = 30); the route checks kind/status only. A 100 MB ten-minute clip is a valid story today. |
| Share my own feed post or chat photo to my story | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Threads, Telegram (forward) | No story reference in `ChatView.tsx` or `FeedView.tsx`; the route accepts only unbound attachments. Only the afterglow recap hook exists. API.md "no resharing" is about other people's stories, not my own content. |
| Mention sticker / @mentions in stories (mentioned user notified and linked) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | Story DTO has no mentions field; chat mention machinery (API.md §1.33) is not reused. Mentions may override mute but never blocks (rule 6); the chat code already encodes this. |
| Poll sticker | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook | Polls exist only in chat and feed posts. Reuse the poll module; results must be named faces to the author, not percentages (PSYCHOLOGY rule 3). |
| Countdown sticker (to an event / plan) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Snapchat, Partiful | Natural fit: countdown to a beacon/plan, shown only to people who can still join (PSYCHOLOGY rule 2). |
| Story ring / entry on the profile page | standard | P1 | Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | No story reference under `app/u/[username]/page.tsx`. The profile is a dead end for stories. |
| Highlights: pinned story collections on the profile | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook (Featured), Telegram (pin to profile), Snapchat (public profiles), BeReal (pinned) | No table, route or doc. Must respect audience rules; with a media-only archive there is nothing to build highlights from. |
| Collage / layout (several photos on one card) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | Parity polish; client-side canvas compositing. |
| Re-post a saved/expired story from the archive | standard | P2 | Telegram, Instagram (archive → highlight) | Gallery is view/delete only. Blocked by the media-only archive. |
| Story drafts | standard | P2 | Instagram | Composer state is in-memory. Nice-to-have once the composer has an editing step. |
| GIF sticker library | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | Rule 7 forbids Giphy; would need a self-hosted, user-uploaded GIF library. |
| Location / place sticker | standard | P2 | Telegram, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | Location drops exist in chat only. No map tiles under rule 7; a text place chip reusing chat location drops is feasible. |
| Question / Q&A box sticker | standard | P2 | Instagram | With replies (#99) a Q&A box is a prompt plus DM replies. |
| Filters, colour grading, AR/face effects | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | `ImageEditSheet` is crop/rotate/aspect only. Simple CSS/canvas colour filters are cheap; AR is out of reach for a dependency-free PWA. |
| Blur / face-blur tool | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal | Not in `ImageEditSheet`. Privacy tool consistent with the app's ethos. |
| Who can reply to my story (everyone / friends / off) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook | Depends on replies (#99). Bundle with it. |
| Viewer list kept after expiry (24-48 h) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Telegram (Premium) | Sweep hard-deletes `story_views` with the story. Conflicts with the clean hard-delete design; low value. |
| Private like (heart visible only to the author) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram | Compatible with PSYCHOLOGY if shown as names, not a tally. |
| Forward my own story into a chat | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram | No story option in the ChatView forward flow. "No resharing" is about other people's stories; forwarding your own is fine. |
| Share-card generator (9:16 image for other networks' stories) | standard | P2 | Partiful, Luma, BeReal, Threads | Share pages have OG tags but export no 9:16 image. The events apps' main acquisition channel; fits beacons more than stories. |
| Post to a story from the OS share sheet (PWA share target) or the global New/compose surfaces | standard | P2 | Instagram, WhatsApp | `/compose` lists conversations only; `/new` offers people/group/channel. The tray '+' on the chats list is the only story entry point; a photo shared from Photos cannot become a story without re-picking it. |
| Music / sound sticker with a licensed library | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok | Licensed catalogues are impossible self-hosted; only a user-uploaded audio track would be allowed. |
| Auto captions (speech-to-text) / voiceover | standard | P3 | Instagram, Snapchat | Self-hosted STT would be a new native dependency. |
| Reply with photo/video to a story | standard | P3 | Instagram | Follows from #99 since the DM composer already takes attachments. |
| Repost / reshare someone else's story (incl. when mentioned) | standard | P3 | Telegram, Instagram, WhatsApp (mentioned contacts) | API.md L1413-1414 "no resharing" is a documented product rule. Deliberate; revisit only if the owner changes the rule. |
| Selfie / cutout / avatar stickers | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Snapchat (Bitmoji) | Low fit. |
| Hashtag sticker | standard | P3 | Instagram | The app has no hashtag discovery surface to link to. |
| Pinch-zoom / double-tap zoom inside the story viewer | standard | P3 | Instagram | `MediaLightbox` (#93) already does pinch, wheel and double-tap for gallery and profile pics but is not imported by `StoryViewer` (imports at L8-19). Wiring, not a build. |
| Offline / retry queue for story posts | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram | The IndexedDB outbox is message-only; `createStory` (`lib/stories.ts` L40-60) is a direct POST whose failure keeps the sheet open and nothing retries after reconnect. A flaky upload on the move is simply lost. |
| "New stories" indicator on the tab bar / app badge | standard | P3 | Every mainstream app | `Tabs.tsx` L34: the dot covers chats-unread and profile-requests only. Position taken here, so the document has one: a count-free dot that appears only when the user opens the app is neither a push (API.md L1400) nor an unprompted nudge (rule 4), so it is allowed; it is just low value. If built, make it the same single Feed-tab dot the Notifications section proposes for `post.new`, fed by `story.new` too — one dot, not two. |
| Story rings on the person's avatar outside the tray (chat list rows, DM header, profile) | standard | P3 | Instagram (ring wherever the avatar appears) | `.storyRing` (`social.module.css` L171) is referenced only from `StoriesStrip.tsx`; the profile `.avatarRing` (`profilepage.module.css` L115-120) is static decoration with no story data. Stories are discoverable from one strip on one tab. |
| Live viewer-list updates while the Seen-by sheet is open | standard | P3 | Instagram, WhatsApp | Sheet fetches once on mount (`StoryViewer.tsx` L403-419); the view endpoint returns 204 and publishes nothing. The author must close and reopen. |
| Deep link to a story / an author's stories (URL that opens the viewer) | standard | P3 | All mainstream apps | No stories route; the viewer opens solely from strip state. Nothing in the app (profile, feed, share page "Draw back" CTA) can land a user on a specific story. |
| Voice / audio-only story | advanced | P2 | WhatsApp | Route accepts image/video only (`routes.ts` L122). Audio messages with waveform already exist; cheap to allow. |
| Turn off stories entirely (hide the tray, stop posting) | advanced | P2 | Signal | No story settings keys. Fits the "user-pulled" ethos better than most parity items. |
| Automated media-safety scanning (NSFW classifier / known-abuse hash matching) on story uploads | advanced | P2 | Every mainstream app | Pipeline imports only sharp, blurhash and the MIME sniffer; moderation is entirely report-driven and there is no story report kind. 24-hour ephemera that auto-delete are the classic abuse vector; nothing sees the media before friends do. Would have to be a self-hosted model/hash list under rule 7. |
| Camera modes: hands-free, dual camera, multi-capture, timer/speed/rewind | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Snapchat, Facebook | Depends on an in-app camera first. |
| Story scheduling | advanced | P3 | Instagram (Business Suite) | Scheduled messages exist; no scheduled stories. Low fit for a personal app. |
| Quiz / emoji-slider stickers | advanced | P3 | Instagram | Results would have to be named, not scored. |
| "Add Yours" chain-prompt sticker / templates | advanced | P3 | Instagram, WhatsApp (2025) | Chain prompts edge toward engagement bait; low fit. |
| Time / weather / temperature / battery stickers | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, Instagram, Facebook | Weather needs a third-party API (rule 7); time only would be allowed. |
| AI editing tools (magic eraser, backdrop, AI captions) | advanced | P3 | Snapchat | Would need self-hosted models; out of scope. |
| Pin sticker/text to a moving object in video | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Snapchat | Out of reach for a PWA without native tracking. |
| Multiple named private story lists + multi-destination posting | advanced | P3 | Signal, Snapchat | Single close-friends list is planned (#98); one exclusive list covers most of the value. |
| Stealth / anonymous story viewing | advanced | P3 | Telegram (Premium) | A paid variant of the view-receipts toggle above, which is the honest version. |
| Screenshot/save restriction toggle and screenshot markers in the viewer list | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Snapchat | Undetectable in a PWA; surveillance-flavoured. |
| End-to-end encrypted stories | advanced | P3 | Signal | Whole-app backlog item (CLAUDE.md). |
| Viewer list search | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Only matters at scale. |
| Memories resurfacing / "On this day" | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Snapchat (Flashbacks) | Needs a real archive first. Mild variable-reward flavour; keep user-pulled if ever built. |
| Custom expiry (1 h to 1 week) | advanced | P3 | Telegram (Premium), Snapchat+ | `constants.ts` L409 says "fixed (no custom story TTLs)". Deliberate. |
| Cross-post stories to other networks | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook | Rule 7. Share link + OS share sheet is the self-hosted substitute. |
| Group / channel stories | advanced | P3 | Telegram (boosted channels), Signal (groups) | Schema has no `conversation_id`. Stories are personal only. |
| Live photo wall / moments during an event | advanced | P3 | Partiful | Afterglow recap is post-event only. Out of category. |
| Novelty stickers (frames, reveal-until-DM, reminder, fundraiser) | advanced | P3 | Instagram | Fundraiser conflicts with the owner's no-payments decision (SPECS). |
| HD / quality option on upload | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp | Single quality path. Low value. |
| User-facing data export that includes my stories (takeout) | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, WhatsApp | The only export is the admin endpoint, whose schema (`AdminUserExportSchema`, `api.ts` L2937-2945) has user/sessions/listings/posts/reviews and no stories, views or overlays; API.md calls a user-initiated export "future user-facing scope". GDPR-style access requests currently need an operator. |

### Planned already

- **Per-story audience picker at compose time** (Everyone / Friends / Close friends / Custom) — basic, P0. `docs/SPECS.md` L222-225, FEATURES #98. No control whatsoever today; `StoryAudienceSchema` has one value.
- **Reply to a story, delivered into the DM quoting the story** — basic, P0. FEATURES #99; API.md L1413 "a future reply is just a DM — seam, not scope". This is THE story interaction. PSYCHOLOGY rule 5 says every broadcast card leads with a reply action; stories currently break the app's own rule.
- **Quick emoji reactions on a story** — basic, P0. FEATURES #99, SPECS L226-228. Must surface to the author as named faces, never a tally.
- **Public (everyone) story audience** — standard, P1. FEATURES #98, SPECS L222-225; public stories feed the existing share pages.
- **Close-friends / exclusive list** — standard, P1. FEATURES #98; no `close_friends` table yet. Reusable list per SPECS.
- **Hide my story from specific people** — standard, P1. SPECS L222-225 "connections-minus-hide-from list". Part of #98.
- **OS share sheet / share to other apps from the viewer** — standard, P1. SPECS L274-284 (#110). `lib/share.ts` copies to clipboard only (L30); `navigator.share` is a few lines in a PWA.
- **Viewer list shows each viewer's reaction/like** — standard, P2. Depends on #99. Named faces with their reaction is PSYCHOLOGY-compatible.
- **Live comments thread on a story (author opt-in)** — standard, P2. FEATURES #100, SPECS L229-232. Designed, not started.

### Deliberately not built

- **Auto-advance to the next author / endless story stream** — PSYCHOLOGY.md L35 never-build "infinite scroll / auto-advancing stories"; API.md L1401. The viewer ends on "That's all from X" with an explicit Next. I agree with the ban: this is the single mechanic that turns stories from "check on friends" into a 40-minute slot machine, and the end card costs one tap. Keep it. Swipe-to-next-author (deliberate) is still compatible with it.
- **Push notification for a new story** — API.md L1400 "no push (ambient, user-pulled; PSYCHOLOGY §6)", rule 4. I agree. Stories are the lowest-urgency content the app has; a push for them is pure re-engagement. The honest cost is discoverability, which the tray-on-Feed fix covers; if anything more is wanted it is the count-free Feed-tab dot described under Missing, not push.
- **"Seen at" timestamps / view order in the viewer list** — PSYCHOLOGY.md L36; `story_views` has no timestamp column (`schema.ts` L1334-1345); tested in `v05` L571-579. Structurally impossible. I agree, and it is the right way to build it: the DB cannot leak what it does not store. Do not add a timestamp column "for the archive" later.
- **View counts, rewatch counts, story insights, link-tap counts** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; API.md L1403 "the list is the information"; `v05` L541 asserts no `view_count`. I agree for this product. The one thing to watch: a viewer list of 80 names is a count in disguise; if the audience ever goes public (#98), consider capping the list display rather than adding a number.
- **Story Boost / relevance-ranked tray** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 5 and L35; API.md L1401 "no recency ranking, a finite tray, not a feed"; username order. I agree. Unseen-first then username is predictable and nobody is being tuned.

### How it looks

The screenshot review captured the strip (`phone-chats.png`), the composer sheet (`phone-story-add.png`), the feed, local, public profile and gallery screens. The story viewer, the Seen-by sheet and the canvas were **not** captured; everything said about them in this section comes from reading `StoryViewer.tsx` and `StoryCanvas.tsx`, not from a screen, and is kept out of the bullets below.

- **Chats tab, stories strip (`phone-chats.png`).** The strip reserves about 109px at the top of the chat list even when nobody has a story: 10px + 14px block padding and a 1px border (`list.module.css` L202-206), a 66px ring (L212-215), a 4px gap and an 11px label (`social.module.css` `.storyItem`/`.storyName`). Conversation rows are `min-height: 72px` (`list.module.css` L27), so that is one and a half rows lost permanently to a dashed "Your story" circle. WhatsApp keeps Status on its own tab; Telegram collapses the stories bar into the header on scroll; Instagram DMs show Notes as a compact row. Fix: collapse to a single-row chip ("+ Your story" with friends' rings inline) when there are zero friend stories, and hide it while the list is scrolled.
- **Feed tab (`phone-feed.png`) has no stories at all.** The "Instagram half" is split across two tabs, and a new user looking for stories opens Feed first — which is also where every in-app pointer sends them. Instagram, Facebook and Snapchat head the feed with stories. Fix: render `StoriesStrip` at the top of `FeedView` (it is already finite and username-ordered) and keep or drop the Chats copy.
- **Dashed ring as the add-story affordance (`phone-chats.png`, `phone-local.png`).** Dashed borders are the app's "empty slot" look (add-story ring `.ringMine` 1.5px dashed, `social.module.css` L204-207; media add tile; invite card; "No more requests" box; the "+ I'm bored" hero). The add-story ring therefore reads as a placeholder rather than a button. Instagram uses a solid avatar with a small filled "+" badge. Fix: own avatar with a filled '+' badge (the 22px `.storyAdd` badge already exists for the has-a-story state, L224-238), dashed only for drop targets and empty states.
- **Composer sheet (`phone-story-add.png`).** Three problems on one sheet. (1) "📷 Pick a photo or video" (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L262) uses an emoji as a UI icon, which renders differently on iOS/Android and clashes with the stroke-icon set used elsewhere; Instagram and Telegram never put emoji on chrome. Fix: the shared Icon component. (2) The disabled "Post story" primary is the darkest, most button-like element on the sheet while inert: `.btn:disabled` is `opacity: 0.55` (`globals.css` L420-423) over the near-black `--accent-grad`, which in the light theme reads as a solid mid-grey pill with white text; Instagram keeps "Share" brand-blue at roughly 40% opacity. Fix: a lower opacity or an outline variant for the disabled primary, plus a helper line saying what is missing. (3) The sheet has no grabber and no detent, and there is no shared sheet primitive to give it one: all 25 `*Sheet.tsx` components plus the two inline story sheets hand-roll `beacons.module.css` `.sheet` (L428-436). iOS and Telegram sheets have a grabber and two detents. Fix: one Sheet component with grabber + detents and a sticky footer CTA, then migrate.
- **Composer content (`phone-story-add.png`).** After upload the user sees an 84×84 thumbnail (`StoriesStrip.tsx` L245) and a separate caption field. Instagram, WhatsApp and Snapchat compose on the full 9:16 frame so you see what friends will see. Fix: a full-frame preview stage with the caption overlaid, and a 9:16 preset in the crop sheet.
- **Profile avatar ring (`phone-profile-arjun.png`).** The public profile wraps the avatar in a permanent `--ring-live` conic ring with a 3px gap (`profilepage.module.css` L114-120, applied unconditionally at `app/u/[username]/page.tsx` L343) whether or not a story exists, so it reads as a stuck focus ring and devalues the real ring in the tray. Instagram shows the ring only when a live story exists. Fix: ring only on a live story, and make it tappable into the viewer (this also fixes the "profile is a dead end for stories" gap).
- **Gallery empty state (`phone-gallery.png`).** The empty card and the page footnote say the same thing twice ("stories save themselves when they expire", `gallery/page.tsx` L111; "Stories land here automatically when they expire", L182-183) and there is nothing to tap. Fix: one sentence plus "Add a story" and "Send a photo" buttons.
- **Light theme (`phone-story-add.png`).** On the composer sheet (background `--panel`, `beacons.module.css` L433) the light palette's chips are `--accent-soft` = rgba(18,20,28,0.08) over white, which computes to 1.18:1 against the sheet, and the sheet border `--border` #d9dfec is 1.34:1 (tokens at `globals.css` L83-105; WCAG relative-luminance ratios). Not story-specific, but the sheet is where it shows; the viewer is dark by design and is unaffected.

### If I had one week

1. **Day 1 — kill the two shipping bugs and the docs drift.** In `routes.ts` L366 let the author through when `canvas_mode === 'off'` (or auto-open the canvas for the author only), matching what `StoryCanvas.tsx` L78/L351 already renders. Mount `StoriesStrip` in `FeedView` and point `constants.ts` L1049, `gallery/page.tsx` L111 and the tour copy (L1029) at a surface that has a tray. Mark #97 shipped in FEATURES.md. While in the files, close the three server leaks that are each a one-line filter: pass the author's block set on `service.ts` L130 and `routes.ts` L336, and add a `banned_at IS NULL` join to `loadOverlayRowsFor` and the share-page overlay read.
2. **Days 2-3 — give the viewer a footer for other people's stories.** Wrap the header avatar/name in a `Link` to `/u/[username]`. Add a footer with Reply (opens the DM with the author, carrying a story reference — the #99 seam) and a ⋯ menu with Report (add `story` to `ReportKindSchema` and `ADMIN_CONTENT_TYPES` so an admin can take one down), Mute stories (a `hidden_story_authors` list, enforced in the tray query), and Block. This is the whole "zero actions" problem, and the mute and report rows are the two unplanned gaps that matter most. Account deletion is the Accounts section's Day 1; the story cascades are already in the schema and need nothing here.
3. **Day 4 — make video actually work.** Keep "Post" disabled with a "Processing…" state until `attachment.ready` arrives (the store already receives it), accept `video/quicktime`, re-encode HEVC to H.264 in `jobs/media.ts` instead of `-c copy`, add a `STORY_VIDEO_MAX_DURATION_S` of 60 enforced server-side, and render `<video muted>` with a tap-to-unmute button plus a visible fallback when `play()` rejects.
4. **Day 5 — the composer's cheapest wins (all P1, all small).** Text-only story rendered client-side to a 1080×1920 PNG (gradient + text) and posted through the existing attachment path, so no schema change. Add `capture="environment"` to the file input. Replace the 84px thumb with a full-frame preview stage and add a 9:16 preset to `ImageEditSheet`. Route delete through the existing undo-pill (#96) instead of firing DELETE on tap.
5. **Leftover hours — hygiene that users notice.** Publish a `story.deleted` WS frame from `hardDeleteStory` and handle it in the store; pause the timer and video on `visibilitychange`; render `BlurhashCanvas` under the `<img>` and warm `stories[si+1]` with `new Image()`; make the '+' add-more badge a real button for keyboard users; change "Friends see it" to "Your connections and people you've chatted with see it" until #98 ships.


---

## Discovery, meeting strangers and dating parity

Judged as what it claims to be — calm stranger-meeting, not dating — the core loop is complete and tested: hourly opt-in, symmetric visibility, silent single waves, exactly-one-DM under concurrency, anonymous nudges with pool guards, roulette keep-handshakes, private feedback feeding an anonymous encouragement stat, and public beacons that turn into plan groups. Counted from the tables below (has ÷ has + half-built + missing + planned, with the deliberately-not-built list excluded), basic coverage is 32/49 = 65%, standard is 13/66 = 20% and advanced is 11/29 = 38% — the advanced figure is flattered by the product's own inventions (timed window, keep handshake, nudge, drunk mode) counting as advanced, and most of the standard misses are dating-monetisation mechanics the docs rightly forbid. What is actually missing is every safety and trust basic a stranger surface needs: no message-requests gating or who-can-message setting (anyone who can prefix-search your name can DM you), no report categories, no age confirmation at signup, no unmatch or in-chat safety sheet, no selfie verification or account-age/mutuals on the profile, no safety copy on the surfaces where you meet strangers, no share-my-plan, and passes that evaporate on reload. Two half-built edges undercut the deck itself: cards cannot open a profile and show one avatar plus 80 characters, and onboarding never asks for interests, so the only thing that makes a stranger card non-generic is empty for every new user. All five P0 rows are safety or legal (message requests, report reasons, age gate, unmatch + safety sheet, account deletion); the next release should be that set rather than more mechanics, plus the roulette pairing that ignores "My area" scope.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| People-you-may-know suggestions (mutuals, shared groups, same area) | basic | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat Quick Add, TikTok, X, Threads, Telegram, Nextdoor, BeReal, Discord | `GET /api/suggestions` (suggestions/routes.ts, SUGGESTIONS_MAX=20); SuggestionCards.tsx; API.md §1.64. Pull-only, evidence-first order; excludes blocked / friends-of / dismissed / banned. Rendered in Feed-Discover, Connections, /people, /welcome. |
| Dismiss a suggestion | basic | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | `POST /api/suggestions/:userId/dismiss` → suggestion_dismissals (schema.ts:2077); x button in SuggestionCards. Permanent and silent; no undo, no review list (see Missing). |
| Search people by username / display name | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, BeReal, Nextdoor, Meetup | `GET /api/users?q=` ILIKE prefix (users/routes.ts:117-138); UserSearch.tsx. Prefix only; any signed-in account can enumerate members — no discoverability toggle. |

Link only — no SMS/contacts invite because there is no contacts model. |

A single wave notifies nobody (standing decision); "Said hi" chip only on the waver's side. |

For a brand-new account the page is an area input and "Done": SuggestionCards is mounted with defaults (welcome/page.tsx:93), so showEmpty and inviteCard are false and it renders null (SuggestionCards.tsx:36-38, 74) — no photo step, no interests, no invite card. Seen-flag is localStorage-only, so a second device re-prompts. Not captured in a screenshot. |
| Bio on the public profile | basic | all 17 | UpdateMeRequestSchema bio ≤1000 (api.ts:855-865); rendered at u/[username]/page.tsx:467-469. The own Profile tab does not render the bio (earlier audit); the public page does. |
| "Open to all" default — filters only narrow, never required | basic | Hinge, Bumble, Tinder | DiscoveryCardsQuerySchema optional area / interests / vibe; API.md §1.65 "filters narrow, never re-rank". |
| Basic discovery filters (my area / shared interests / vibe) | basic | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating | `?area=1 ?interests=1 ?vibe=` in visibleCards(); filterRow chips in CardStack. The interests filter is case-sensitive SQL overlap while the card's "like you" line is case-insensitive. |
| Who can add me to groups (everyone / people I chat with / nobody) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | users.group_add_policy CHECK (schema.ts:70, 140-143); privacy/page.tsx:128-139; enforced on group create / add / team-up with a 403 group_add_denied that never confirms a block (conversations/routes.ts:81-106, 223; API.md:782, 1580). The only contact-gating privacy setting in the product — contrast the missing "who can message me". |
| Pause my invite links | basic | WhatsApp (reset link), Telegram, Discord | users.invites_paused (schema.ts:68); "Pause your invite links" toggle (privacy/page.tsx:120-126); `POST /api/invites` → 403 invites_paused; accept on a paused inviter's token refused (API.md:780). |
| Presence privacy: hide last-seen, reciprocal read receipts, hide typing | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram | users.read_receipts / show_last_seen / show_typing (schema.ts:65-67, 129-132); privacy/page.tsx:97, 104, 114; gateway drops typing frames and last_seen serialises null unless the viewer qualifies (API.md:777-779). |
| Online / last-seen on the profile with "who sees it" control | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram | u/[username]/page.tsx:398-401 renders "online" or formatLastSeen; SelectRow show_last_seen everyone / dm_partners / nobody (privacy/page.tsx:102-111); UserPublic.last_seen_at nullable per viewer (api.ts:239-241, ADR-009). Default is Everyone with minute precision — see How it looks. |
| Attach surrounding chat context to a report, with explicit consent | standard | WhatsApp (forwards recent messages), Instagram, Discord | include_context + REPORT_CONTEXT_CONSENT_COPY beside an unchecked checkbox (api.ts:1312-1315); REPORT_CONTEXT_RADIUS=5 (constants.ts:1257,1261); ReportSheet.tsx:57; admin context viewer API.md §1.69. |
| Unauthenticated "report this page" on public share pages | standard | uncommon; Telegram's abuse form for public links is the nearest | `POST /api/share/:token/report`, no auth, reason required, stored as kind "share_page" with reporter_id NULL; SHARE_REPORT_RATE_PER_DAY_PER_IP=5 (API.md §1.67; constants.ts:1201; schema.ts:1003-1005); ShareReportLink.tsx on the share page. |
| Short "what I'm up for" note on the discovery card (≤80 chars) | basic | Hinge (prompts), Tinder (bio on card), Bumble | discovery_optins.note CHECK ≤80 (schema.ts:1847,1864); DISCOVERY_NOTE_MAX_CHARS=80 (constants.ts:602); OptinSheet input #disc-note (DiscoverySection.tsx:272-280); HingeCard renders it first in the meta line (:441). The card's only free-text self-description. |
| Silent pass / decline everywhere | basic | Hinge, Bumble, Instagram (silent follow-request decline) | Decline: no WS frame, no push (API.md:1243); dismissed user never told (API.md:2525; suggestions/routes.ts:9-10); discovery_waves "DELIBERATELY no read model" (schema.ts:1878-1882); group-join deny silent (API.md:2645). |
| Moderation queue with warn / suspend / ban behind reports | standard | all 17 (operator side) | Admin reports queue (FEATURES #54; API.md §1.41); users.suspended_at / banned_at with public invisibility (schema.ts:76-84); banned excluded from search, profiles, discovery (users/routes.ts:134,151; discovery/routes.ts:117). |
| Profile photo upload / remove | basic | all 17 | `POST`/`DELETE /api/users/me/avatar` (users/routes.ts:71,97); settings/account/page.tsx:124-146; renders on cards (DiscoverySection.tsx:446-458). Single photo only; multi-photo is Half-built. |
| Coarse area on profile as the gate for area discovery, beacons and suggestions | basic | Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Meetup | `PATCH /api/users/me` area (citext, schema.ts:35; users/routes.ts:44); written by welcome/page.tsx:45-50 and settings/account/page.tsx:241; consumed by discovery/routes.ts:88-92,180 (400 area_required) and suggestions/routes.ts:105; never serialised (api.ts:258-262). |
| Open the full profile from a suggestion card | basic | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat | Avatar and name are Links to /u/:username (SuggestionCards.tsx:169-174); `GET /api/users/:username` (users/routes.ts:140). The discovery HingeCard has no such link — the profile-from-card gap is discovery-only. |
| Suggestion "why" as named faces and words, never counts | standard | Instagram, Snapchat, Facebook, TikTok, BeReal, Discord | whyLine() + mutual faces strip in SuggestionCards ("friend of X", "likes hiking, like you", "from your area", "shares a group"). |
| Interest tags on profile used as a discovery signal | standard | Threads, Instagram, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating | users.interests ≤10 (schema.ts:74); `PATCH /api/users/me/profile` (profiles/routes.ts:52); overlap in cardDto() and suggestions/routes.ts. |
| Nearby people by coarse area, opt-in, no GPS | standard | Telegram People Nearby (discontinued), Facebook Nearby Friends (discontinued), BeReal, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | DISCOVERY_SCOPES ['area','public'] (constants.ts:598); same-area citext equality; 400 area_required when scope=area without an area. Roulette pairing ignores scope entirely (runRoulettePair in discovery/service.ts:209) — an undocumented inconsistency. |
| "Recently active" signal without timestamps | standard | Tinder, Bumble | 60-min opt-in TTL means every card is live by construction; cardDto() omits last_seen. A stronger guarantee than a badge, with no seen-at leak. |
| Prompt to widen preferences when the pool runs dry | standard | Hinge, Bumble, Tinder | showEmpty branch in SuggestionCards links to set area / add interests / see who is bored; CardStack end copy. Honest, no fake fill. |
| Friend-finding mode distinct from dating | standard | Bumble BFF, Facebook (Dating separate from friends) | ADR-015 and DiscoverySection copy frame Bored Together as meeting bored strangers; no romance intent anywhere. The whole product is the BFF mode; there is no dating mode to separate from. |
| Activity intent chip (food / walk / game …) | standard | Tinder Explore "Free Tonight", Bumble Discover collections | DISCOVERY_VIBES (constants.ts:1151-1160); vibe chips in OptinSheet and the filterRow; discovery_optins.vibe CHECK; API.md §1.65. |
| Mood / status chip with visibility control | standard | Snapchat, Bumble, Telegram | MoodChip (u/[username]/page.tsx:369-380); suggMood in SuggestionCards; API.md §1.57; mood is null on stranger cards (cardDto). |
| Icebreakers in a new match chat | standard | Facebook Dating, Bumble, Hinge, Tinder, Partiful, Luma, Meetup | DISCOVERY_ICEBREAKERS (constants.ts:609-616) chosen by hash in discovery/service.ts; BEACON_ICEBREAKERS (:153). One hard-coded English line per chat; no second prompt, nothing profile-derived. |
| Voice / video call from a match chat | standard | Bumble, Facebook Dating, Tinder (wound down), Hinge (removed) | CallHeaderButtons on every conversation incl. origin discovery / roulette (ChatView.tsx:2060-2100); API.md §1.42. Nothing frames it as a pre-meet safety step, and the 5-min roulette window is too short for it. |
| Random timed 1:1 chat with a stranger (roulette) | advanced | Tinder (video speed-dating beta); none of the social apps | `POST /api/roulette/join` (discovery/roulette.ts); runRoulettePair() every 10 s; RouletteBanner 5-min clock (not captured, from code); test v06:582. Pairing checks blocks / friends-of / dm_key only (service.ts:188-199), so a "My area" user can be paired instance-wide. |
| Post-chat mutual keep handshake | advanced | none of the 17 (Bored Chat original) | `POST /api/roulette/keep` (kept_by guarded UPDATE, 410 roulette_expired); test "keep by one stays provisional and never leaks" v06:608; RouletteGoodbye (GlobalOverlays.tsx:117-160). |
| Secret admirer with mutual reveal | advanced | Facebook Dating Secret Crush | anon/routes.ts threads, request-reveal, set-reveal, answer-riddle, blurred-avatar proxy; AnonSendSheet.tsx; privacy "Who can send you anonymous messages"; API.md §1.62. Different shape (timed riddle-reveal game) but covers the use case. |
| Local activity-spike signal | advanced | Tinder Swipe Surge | `GET /api/beacons/radar` → active_visible_count (beacons/routes.ts:333); "N plans live around you" line in BeaconStrip.tsx:55-60; FEATURES #26. Aggregate count only, no identities, no priority placement. |
| Peer trust panel on a profile | advanced | Nextdoor (business reviews only) | TrustPanel.tsx; TrustSchema (api.ts:2302); API.md §1.40; average hidden under a minimum. Sourced only from board deals, never discovery chats; hidden at 0 reviews. |
| Rescue / SOS: ping friends to call me out | advanced | Tinder Noonlight (discontinued) | `POST /api/rescue` (friends-of only, RESCUE_MAX_PER_DAY=3); three mounts: RescueButton on /local (local/page.tsx:13), RescueSheet from the Chats ⋯ overflow (ConversationListView.tsx:15, 303) and RescueCard on the Profile tab (settings/profile/page.tsx:29, 382); not on Feed. Push bypasses mute (API.md §1.46); test v06:733. |
| Contact guard before messaging a cold contact (drunk mode) | advanced | none of the 17 (Bored Chat original) | DrunkConfirmSheet.tsx; API.md §1.57; FEATURES #73. |
| Private post-chat feedback feeding an anonymous encouragement stat | advanced | Hinge We Met (feeds ranking), Bumble Bee roadmap | `POST /api/discovery/feedback`, `GET /api/discovery/encouragement` (ENCOURAGEMENT_MIN_SAMPLES=10); ExpectationFeedback.tsx; test v06:719. The only stat on the surface; anonymous; shown only pre-opt-in. |
| Events → plan group with other attendees | advanced | Facebook Dating events/groups, Luma connect, Meetup | Spark → plan group with icebreaker + venue poll (beacons/routes.ts; FEATURES #23-#25). The app's strongest meet-people mechanic. |
| Anonymous "someone waved" nudge with small-pool suppression and opt-out | advanced | Bumble's blurred Liked-You count is the closest, and it is the opposite stance | discovery/nudge.ts (NUDGE_MIN_POOL=4, once per opt-in hour); wave_nudges toggle (privacy/page.tsx:233-238); test v08b:285. |
| Timed discoverability window (an hour, extend, live countdown) | advanced | none of the 17 (Bumble Spotlight is the paid inverse) | `PUT`/`GET`/`DELETE /api/discovery/optin` (DISCOVERY_OPTIN_TTL_S); OptinSheet + liveChip in DiscoverySection (not captured, from code); ADR-015. The product's defining visibility model: a moment, not a setting. |

Not counted as features above: the per-user rate limits (wave 30/h — DISCOVERY_WAVE_RATE_PER_HOUR, constants.ts:606; DM create, connect, anonymous threads, listing contact, rescue, beacon, report — constants.ts:50, 377, 1116, 1119, 498, 648, 140, 193) are anti-abuse plumbing returning 429 + Retry-After with no user-visible quota. They are necessary, not a feature.

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Report with reason categories (spam / harassment / impersonation / underage / scam) | basic | ReportSheet.tsx on every card and profile; one optional free-text reason (REPORT_REASON_MAX_CHARS=500; CreateReportRequestSchema api.ts:1301-1341, `reason` defaults to ''); `POST /api/reports` | No category enum in packages/shared; moderators triage prose. "Underage" is the one category a stranger pool needs. | P0 |
| Unmatch / end a match DM for both sides, with one in-chat safety sheet (Report user · Block · End chat) | basic | Server: `DELETE /api/conversations/:id/participants/:me` is a soft self-leave with no DM exclusion (conversations/routes.ts:504-506); createMatchedDmTx revives a left DM. UI: ChatView.tsx:1786-1810 long-press "Report message"; the DM header links to /u/:username where Block / Report live | ChatInfoSheet.tsx:710-711 renders the Leave row only for channels and groups (non-owner), never a DM; `rg -i 'block\|report' ChatInfoSheet.tsx` → none. The sheet leads with themes, wallpapers and nicknames; the only remedy for a bad match is Block from the profile, two taps away, and nothing softer than a block exists. | P0 |
| Pass / skip that is remembered | basic | CardStack onPass → a local `passed` Set (DiscoverySection.tsx:305-307, 393), commented "a LOCAL skip only; the server hears nothing"; apply() resets it on filter change | Passed cards come back on reload, filter change and next opt-in; the only remedy for a recurring face is Block. A server-side silent hide would not violate ADR-015. | P1 |
| Multiple photos on profile and card | basic | 3-GIF strip in ProfileEditor.tsx:189-243 (PROFILE_MEDIA_MAX) | DiscoveryCardSchema (api.ts:3054-3073) carries avatar + note only; cardDto() never sends the strip. The card you wave on shows one avatar. | P1 |
| Open the full profile from a discovery card before waving | basic | DiscoveryCardSchema.user is UserLiteSchema (api.ts:3054), so `username` is already on the wire and cardDto() fills it (discovery/routes.ts:136-139); `GET /api/users/:username` exists; FEATURES #106 tap-photo → profile sheet planned | HingeCard imports no Link and renders no username (`rg 'Link\|username\|href=' DiscoverySection.tsx` → none). The server half is done; waving on avatar + 80 chars + 2 tags is an undocumented privacy stance. | P1 |
| Mutual connections on the profile page | standard | Mutual faces strip on SuggestionCards.tsx; FEATURES #44 | u/[username]/page.tsx renders no mutuals. The one trust signal a stranger could check before replying is absent exactly where they would look. | P1 |
| Pre-meet safety tips on stranger surfaces | standard | LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE (constants.ts:493) rendered on gig / ride / room listings (ListingDetailSheet.tsx:77) | None in DiscoverySection, MatchOverlay, RouletteBanner or BeaconDetailSheet (`rg -i safety` → only the cardSafety CSS class). The copy exists and is shown on the one surface where you least need it. | P1 |
| Public group / channel directory | standard | conversations.visibility private / invite_only / public / ghost (schema.ts:298-304; API.md:2632-2636); any non-banned user may join a public group server-side (API.md:2642); SSR share page with name, description, member count, join CTA (API.md §1.67; sharepages/routes.ts:300,360) | No list-public-groups endpoint, no in-app browse or search; global search is FEATURES #108 planned. Shared groups feed PYMK, but you cannot find a group you are not invited to. | P1 |
| Request-to-join invite-only groups with admin approve / silent deny | standard | Server only: `POST /api/conversations/:id/join` → 202 requested for invite_only, `GET …/join-requests`, `POST …/join-requests/:userId/approve`, silent `DELETE …/join-requests/:userId` (conversations/visibility.ts:178-302; API.md:2642-2646); group_join_requests table; GROUP_JOIN_RATE_PER_HOUR | No web call to `/api/conversations/:id/join` anywhere (`rg '/join' apps/web` → beacons only) and no join-requests UI (`rg -i 'join.?request\|approve' apps/web` → story canvas only). The share page's Join CTA is a link to /login?next= (SharePageView.tsx:108) that lands on a page with no request button. Docs call it shipped; users cannot reach it. | P1 |
| Post-meet check-in ("We Met" / how did it go) | standard | `POST /api/discovery/feedback` + ExpectationFeedback.tsx asks better / same / worse after a stranger chat (ChatView.tsx:166-174) | Never fires after a beacon meetup or a roulette-kept chat. Extending it to "did you meet, were you safe" after a beacon is a small step. | P2 |
| Revoke individual invite links | standard | Server only: `GET /api/invites` + `DELETE /api/invites/:id` (invites/routes.ts:240, 256-258) | No revoke control in the web (`rg 'DELETE\|revoke' InviteSheet.tsx` → only the error copy "Revoke one or let an old link lapse", which points at a button that does not exist). The only user-reachable kill switch is the all-or-nothing "Pause your invite links". | P2 |
| People search filters | standard | /people chips Mutual friends / Shared groups / My area / Shared interests narrow the suggestion grid (people/page.tsx; suggestions/routes.ts) | Filters do not apply to name search; no school/workplace fields exist (work/skills are free text). | P2 |
| Hide my card / profile from specific people (softer than block) | standard | Bilateral Block only (blocks/routes.ts); friends-of auto-hidden from the pool; story hide-from lists planned (FEATURES #98) | Nothing for discovery. The ex-colleague case has no answer. | P2 |
| Mark a connection request as spam / block from the request | standard | Requests section (connections/page.tsx:168-190) offers Accept / Ignore | Block / Report require opening the profile. | P2 |
| Anti-bot signup defences (velocity, challenge, fingerprint) | standard | `POST /api/auth/register` rate 3/hour/IP (API.md:600); users.registration_ip captured and GC'd after IP_RETENTION_DAYS (schema.ts:85-87); admin sees IPs (FEATURES #54); per-user fixed windows in Redis | No captcha, challenge or fingerprint module (`rg -i 'captcha\|turnstile\|fingerprint\|proof-of-work' apps/server/src` → one passkey copy string). A disposable-email + VPN signup is unimpeded, which matters for a stranger pool. | P2 |
| "People you met" connections after a shared event | standard | Suggestions include shared non-ghost groups; joiner faces link to profiles with Connect (BeaconDetailSheet) | Not surfaced as "you met at X"; no post-event prompt. | P2 |
| Host-defined icebreaker / introduce-yourself questions in the plan group | standard | Spark → plan group with rotating BEACON_ICEBREAKERS + venue poll (FEATURES #23-#25) | System icebreaker only; hosts cannot ask "what are you bringing?". | P2 |
| Event category / interest dimension on beacons (chip, filter, recommendations) | basic | beacons.vibe NOT NULL with preset set + custom emoji+word pair (schema.ts:710-714, 748-760; beacons/routes.ts:97-113); VibePicker in BeaconCreateSheet.tsx:30-46; rendered in BeaconStrip.tsx:142-143 | `GET /api/beacons` has no vibe / category filter, the web has no filter row, and nothing matches beacon vibes against profile interests — categories are display-only labels. Beacons are the strongest meet-people mechanic and have no interest dimension beyond the label; a one-filter fix, with small local pools softening the loss. | P2 |
| Video intro clip / video prompts | standard | Video upload pipeline exists for posts (uploads module) | ProfileEditor.tsx:196 accepts image/gif, webp, png, jpeg only. Pipeline exists, slot does not. Low value for a non-dating product. | P3 |
| Follow a host / repeat an event with the same crew | basic | Repeat half is real: `POST /api/beacons/:id/ritualize` (beacons/routes.ts:608) with "Make it a ritual" in PlanHeader.tsx:137-141, and `POST /api/beacons/:id/run-it-back` (:650) from AfterglowCard.tsx:100 | No follow/subscription model anywhere (`rg follow.?host` → none); run-it-back re-mints with the source audience (:670) rather than re-inviting the prior joiners. | P3 |
| Travel mode / set location elsewhere | standard | `PATCH /api/users/me` area is free text (api.ts:855-865) | No time-boxed travel badge. Retyping your area to any city is the whole feature minus the badge. | P3 |
| Basics / lifestyle profile fields | standard | Work / skill entries: profile_entries kind IN ('work','skill'), title ≤80, detail ≤160, PROFILE_ENTRIES_MAX=12 (schema.ts:1566-1590; ProfileEditor.tsx:296-346; rendered u/[username]/page.tsx:473-480) | None of height / religion / kids / education / drinking / smoking / zodiac / pronouns / languages. Mostly dating-specific; pronouns and languages are the friend-mode subset worth adding. | P3 |
| Language preference / global mode | standard | scope "public" = whole instance (DISCOVERY_SCOPES) | No language field; i18n is backlog (CLAUDE.md). | P3 |
| Mutual friends (names / faces) on suggestion AND discovery cards | standard | Suggestion half only: SuggestionSchema.mutuals (api.ts:3507-3510) rendered in SuggestionCards.tsx:184-190 | DiscoveryCardSchema (api.ts:3054-3073) = user, scope, note, expires_at, waved, vibe, shared_interests; cardDto() computes no mutuals; HingeCard meta is note · vibe · shared interests. | P3 |
| Video speed dating / timed video rounds | advanced | Roulette is text with an optional call button (ChatView.tsx:2060-2100); ROULETTE_WINDOW_S=300 | No video-first round. Low value for this product. | P3 |

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 18+ age confirmation and terms / guidelines acceptance at signup | basic | P0 | Facebook Dating, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Instagram | register/page.tsx has nothing (`rg -i 'age\|terms\|consent\|birth\|18'` → one unrelated error string at :130); no age column. Nothing stops a minor opting into the stranger pool or roulette. A one-checkbox self-attestation is the minimum and does not require storing a birthday. |
| Message-requests inbox / strangers cannot DM until accepted | basic | P0 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, Discord, Reddit, Meetup, Facebook Messenger | `POST /api/conversations` checks only isBlockedPair (conversations/routes.ts:153-163); no setting, route or inbox (`rg -i 'dm_policy\|message.?request'` → none). Anyone who can prefix-search your name can open a DM. For a product whose discovery surface pairs strangers this is the largest safety hole in the area. |
| Self-service account deletion | basic | P0 | every mainstream app; app-store review and GDPR require it | `rg -i 'delete.?account\|deleteAccount\|close.?account\|DELETE /api/users/me'` across server, web and API.md → only avatar, mood and gallery deletes; API.md §1.2 has no `DELETE /api/users/me`; only admin suspend/ban exists. A social app with discovery, DMs and public share pages cannot lack it. |
| Interest / topic picker at onboarding | basic | P1 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Meetup, Luma | /welcome asks for area only (`rg -i interest welcome/page.tsx` → none); interests are editable only in ProfileEditor.tsx:253-285. Shared interests are the only thing that makes a stranger card non-generic, and every new user has an empty signal until they find Settings → Profile. |
| Address-book / contacts sync | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, BeReal | No hits for contacts sync / navigator.contacts; register is email-only. This is a real growth gap — it is what makes a new install non-empty in every chat app in the union — and it is implementable self-hosted: client-side salted hashes of emails (and phone numbers once there is a phone field) matched against a hashed column, nothing sent to a third party. P1 behind the safety P0s, not P3 as a "deliberate gap": no doc records a decision against it. |
| Change email address | basic | P1 | all 17 | Account page renders me.email read-only (settings/account/page.tsx:226); UpdateMeRequestSchema has no email; no route in API.md §1.2. Email is the recovery identity and there is no verification flow either, so a lost mailbox strands the account. |
| Opt out of appearing in other people's suggestions | standard | P1 | Instagram, Snapchat Quick Add, Discord | No toggle in privacy/page.tsx; only viewer-side suggestion_dismissals. The pool is opt-in but PYMK lists you to mutuals, area and group-mates with no way out short of deleting your area. |
| "Who can find me by username / name" discoverability setting | standard | P1 | Signal, WhatsApp, Discord, X, Facebook | `GET /api/users?q=` filters only blocked / banned; no discoverable / searchable flag in api.ts or schema.ts. |
| Interest taxonomy / autocomplete / tag normalisation | standard | P1 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating, Instagram, Reddit, X | Free-text tag input (ProfileEditor.tsx:253-285); no canonical list; the SQL filter `u.interests && ARRAY[...]` is case-sensitive. "hiking" / "Hiking" / "hikes" never overlap; the whole shared-interest signal is fragile without it. |
| Photo / selfie verification with a verified badge | standard | P1 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating, Instagram, Snapchat (creators) | `rg -i verified` in api.ts / schema.ts → only email_verified_at and passkeys; no badge in UserPublic or DiscoveryCard. The most-cited dating-safety basic that is absent; a self-hosted pose-match flow with admin review fits the ethos. |
| "Who can message me" (everyone / connections / nobody) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, Discord, Reddit | privacy/page.tsx has show_last_seen, wave_nudges, anon-message and activity toggles only; DM create checks isBlockedPair only. The anonymous-message game has exactly this setting; ordinary DMs do not. The setting half of the P0 message-requests row. |
| Restrict / mute a person (softer than block) | standard | P1 | Instagram Restrict, Facebook, X mute, Snapchat | conversation_members.muted_until exists (schema.ts:387; API.md:53) but no route writes it (`rg '/mute\|mutedUntil' apps/server/src/modules` → none) and no UI reads it (ChatInfoSheet.tsx:508 is a comment). A column alone is not a feature: this is Missing, whichever other section calls it Half-built. "Honors mute" is reachable only for system-set mutes. |
| Self-service personal data export | standard | P1 | Instagram, WhatsApp, Tinder (and GDPR subject access) | Only the operator-side export in the admin dashboard (FEATURES #54; API.md §1.41); no users/me/export; no control on the account page. |
| "Contact joined" notifications | basic | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | Depends entirely on contacts sync; `rg -i joined` across push finds only group-join events. Not noticeable without it. |
| Change username | basic | P2 | Instagram, Telegram, Discord (with cooldown) | Username input is disabled / readOnly (settings/account/page.tsx:224); no field in UpdateMeRequestSchema; no route. Usernames are the only public handle since profiles are routed /u/:username. |
| Rewind / undo last pass | standard | P2 | Tinder Rewind, Bumble Backtrack, Facebook Dating Second Look | CardStack `passed` Set has no pop; no server pass model. Benign (no rejection surface); pairs naturally with a remembered pass. |
| Profile prompts / questions ("my ideal Sunday…") | standard | P2 | Hinge, Tinder, Bumble, Facebook Dating, Meetup intro questions | `rg -i prompt` in ProfileEditor / DiscoverySection → only vibe-check and placeholders; ProfileSchema (api.ts:2266) has interests, entries, media only. Self-description is bio, 10 free tags, work/skills and an 80-char note; prompts are what give strangers something to reply to. |
| Connection intent chip (friends / new in town / networking / activity partner) and filter | standard | P2 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating | DISCOVERY_VIBES are activity-only; no intent column on discovery_optins. Even a friends-only product benefits from an honest "new in town / networking" chip; today the 80-char note is the only outlet. |
| "Who can send me connection requests" (everyone / friends-of-friends / nobody) | standard | P2 | Facebook, Discord, Snapchat | Only the anonymous-message setting exists; connections/routes.ts:98 gates by the 30/h window and blocks only. Lower than who-can-message because requests are already silent-ignorable. |
| Unknown-sender safety banner on the first DM from a stranger | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Discord, Snapchat, Signal, Facebook Messenger | `rg -i 'safety\|stranger'` in ChatView / DiscoverySection / GlobalOverlays → nothing for discovery or roulette DMs; roulette copy is "Be kind" only. Mostly subsumed once message requests exist. |
| Safety Center page | standard | P2 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating, Meetup | settings/help/page.tsx:32,136 has a "safety" ticket category only; no /settings/safety route. |
| Adjustable discovery radius / adjacent areas | standard | P2 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Nextdoor, Meetup | users.area is one free-text citext string; no geography model, no neighbouring-area concept. "My area" is string equality and "Anyone" is the whole instance — nothing in between. |
| Hidden words / keyword filter on incoming contact | standard | P2 | Hinge Hidden Words, Instagram Hidden Words | `rg -i 'hidden words\|muted words\|keyword filter'` in FEATURES.md / constants.ts → none. |
| New-account restrictions on contacting strangers | standard | P2 | Reddit (30-day), Nextdoor, Tinder, Instagram | No created_at check in discovery opt-in / wave or DM create; limits are uniform per user. A fresh account can opt in, wave 30/h and open 20 DMs/h immediately. |
| Review / undo dismissed suggestions | standard | P2 | Instagram (reset suggested content), Facebook | suggestion_dismissals is write-only (`POST …/dismiss`, suggestions/routes.ts:184-188); no GET or DELETE route in API.md. A mis-tap permanently hides a person with no recovery. |
| Cooldown / throttle on re-requesting a connection after a decline | standard | P2 | Facebook, LinkedIn | Decline deletes the row and "re-requests stay possible" (API.md:1243); the only gate on `POST /api/users/:id/connect` is 30/h + blocks (connections/routes.ts:105-115). Suggestions hide the person but the profile Connect button can be hammered — a low-grade harassment vector. |
| Browse / search people by interest tag | standard | P2 | Instagram topics, Reddit, X, Tinder / Bumble interest browse | `GET /api/users?q=` is a prefix ILIKE on username / display_name only (:133); interests are used only as a boolean overlap-with-mine filter; profile chips (u/[username]/page.tsx:410-426) are plain spans, not links. The main non-geographic signal is not navigable. |
| Share my meet-up plan with a trusted contact (who, when, where, editable link) | advanced | P2 | Tinder Share My Date, Bumble Share Date, Facebook Dating | `rg -i 'share (my )?(date\|plan)\|check.?in'` → nothing; RescueSheet is the inverse direction (get me out). Beacons already model who / when / where; a "tell a friend" share on a sparked plan is cheap and squarely self-hosted. |
| Recent searches and typeahead suggestions | standard | P3 | Instagram, Reddit, Nextdoor, Threads | `rg -i 'recent\|history\|localStorage' UserSearch.tsx` → none. |
| Verified-only filter in discovery | standard | P3 | Tinder, Bumble | DiscoveryCardsQuerySchema has area / interests / vibe only. Depends on verification existing first. |
| Area member directory | standard | P3 | Nextdoor neighbor directory, Snapchat campus, Meetup group members | Only the hourly pool shows area members; suggestions same_area is one signal among several, capped at 20. Would need an explicit "list me in my area" opt-in to stay consistent with ADR-015. |
| Opening Moves (a pre-set question a match answers first) | advanced | P3 | Bumble | No profile question field; the icebreaker is system-chosen. Would double as the missing profile-prompt feature. |
| Minimum photo requirement to be discoverable | standard | P3 | Tinder | `PUT /api/discovery/optin` requires no avatar or media. Conflicts with the calm, low-pressure ethos; low value. |
| Advanced preferences and dealbreaker toggles | standard | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating | No preference model beyond three query flags. Dating-specific; most underlying fields are banned or absent. |
| Hide from event guest list / incognito RSVP | standard | P3 | Partiful, Luma | beacons/routes.ts:482 always serialises joiners to the audience; no hidden flag. Conflicts with the named-faces "who's in" design. |
| Poke / low-intent nudge to an existing contact | standard | P3 | Facebook Poke | Wave is restricted to live discovery cards (404 discovery_card_not_found for friends). The planned weekly weak-tie card is the calmer version. |
| Dating / meeting tips and coaching content | standard | P3 | Hinge Tips, Bumble, Tinder | No tips beyond the "Things you can do" directory (FEATURE_DIRECTORY constants.ts:1037-1065). |
| City / region landing pages and adjacent-neighbourhood exploration | standard | P3 | Luma, Meetup, Partiful, Nextdoor | Area is a private string never serialised (FEATURES #30); no /area pages. |
| Welcome-new-member action / newcomer card | standard | P3 | Nextdoor | No newcomer surface in suggestions/routes.ts or FeedView.tsx. |
| Friend Check Up / prune prompt | standard | P3 | Snapchat, Instagram | No prune surface; connections/page.tsx offers per-row Remove only. |
| People search pagination / load more | standard | P3 | all with search | USER_SEARCH_LIMIT=20 hard cap, no cursor or offset; UserSearch.tsx:35 sends only q=. Becomes a wall on any instance with a few hundred users sharing a prefix. |
| Double date / pair-to-pair matching | advanced | P3 | Tinder | No pair model; beacons cover group meeting instead. |
| Matchmaker (friends recommend people) | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Facebook Dating | No recommend / introduce endpoint; the profile share link (#110) is the prerequisite. |
| AI matchmaking / profile and conversation assistant | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Facebook Dating, Hinge | No LLM integration; golden rule 7 forbids third-party SaaS. Only a self-hosted model would fit; not worth it here. |
| Discovery modes (music taste, astrology, campus) | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Snapchat | No modes; a single vibe chip per opt-in. |
| Government ID verification badge | advanced | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Instagram | No verification routes; would require a third-party IDV vendor (golden rule 7). |
| Request verification from a match | advanced | P3 | Bumble | Depends on selfie verification, which is absent. |
| Liveness / duplicate-account detection | advanced | P3 | Tinder Face Check | No biometric or device-fingerprint module. |
| Emergency contact registration / check-in timer | advanced | P3 | Tinder Noonlight (ended) | No emergency-contact field; no timer routine. |
| Brand-hosted IRL events / club funding | advanced | P3 | Bumble IRL, Hinge One More Hour | Not applicable to a self-hosted instance. |
| Campus / closed-community directory | advanced | P3 | Snapchat Campus, Tinder College Mode | No community / instance-subset concept beyond the area string. |
| Astrological compatibility reports | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, Tinder Astrology Mode | No birthday field (banned demographics); PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 discourages pairwise scores. Arguably never — a compatibility percentage is a score. |
| AI conversational discovery (ask about people / trends) | advanced | P3 | X Grok, Reddit Answers, Instagram Meta AI | No LLM integration; golden rule 7. |

### Planned already

- Global search (people, public groups, listings, own messages) with typed tabs — basic, P1 — FEATURES #108. Today only prefix user search (users/routes.ts:117) and listing `q=` (API.md:1551); people search sits inside a component, not a destination.
- Email / phone verification at signup — basic, P3 — ADR-021 lines 105-111; SPECS.md:177-180 ("toggles visible but disabled with needs-SMTP notes"). users.email_verified_at exists but nothing writes it; no mailer ships (.env.example:35); the admin "Require email verification" toggle is inert (admin/auth/page.tsx:24, :263). Not user-reachable, so not "has".
- Standalone profile share link (+ second-opinion share, OG unfurl) — standard, P1 — FEATURES #110. Profile ··· menu holds only Block / Report (u/[username]/page.tsx:264-300).
- Add a person via QR code / scannable profile code — standard, P2 — rides on FEATURES #110 (the share link rendered as a code; the Profile section files it the same way). QrCode.tsx exists but is used only for cross-device login (login/page.tsx:481) and TOTP (security/page.tsx:182).
- Account age / tenure trust signal ("member since") — standard, P1 — FEATURES #44 "account-age/mutual-friend signals remain planned"; no created_at in the UserPublic DTO.
- Profile tabs (posts / gallery / groups / connections) with per-tab privacy — standard, P2 — FEATURES #107; SPECS.md v1.0d.
- "Spotted" location pins with interested named faces — standard, P2 — FEATURES #109; SPECS.md v1.0d.
- Escalating question deck / Question Game for new matches and plan groups — advanced, P2 — PSYCHOLOGY.md "Go Deeper" research note (Aron 1997); nothing in code.
- Weekly weak-tie card (resurface a dormant contact) — advanced, P3 — PSYCHOLOGY.md "Weaker but interesting"; nothing in code.
- Fediverse / cross-instance profile discovery — advanced, P3 — CLAUDE.md backlog "federation"; no ActivityPub module.

### Deliberately not built

- **Swipe gestures with stamps and haptics** (Tinder, Bumble, Facebook Dating) — ADR-015 "no swipe-reject mechanic"; PSYCHOLOGY never-build list (variable-reward loops). Tap-based Say hi / Pass is the replacement. Agree on the mechanic: the stamp-and-reward loop is the harm. A plain swipe to advance a card would be harmless, but it is not worth relitigating.
- **Age / gender fields, "show me" and age-range filters** (Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating) — FEATURES #78b; API.md §1.65; no such columns. Agree for profiles and filters. Disagree with reading it as a ban on an age *attestation*: a checkbox at signup stores no demographic and the stranger pool needs it. Keep the field ban, add the gate.
- **Trending / popular events ranking** (Meetup, Luma, Partiful) — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3, never-build list; beacons listed by time. Agree; a popularity sort on a small local pool would just crown one event and empty the others.
- **Distance shown on cards** (Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating) — FEATURES #30 no live GPS; ADR-015 no area text ever serialised. Agree; "same area" is the right resolution for a non-dating product.
- **"Liked you" / who-waved list, free or paid** (Tinder Gold, Bumble, Hinge, Facebook Dating) — SPECS standing decision "single waves invisible; nudges anonymous"; no read model on discovery_waves. Agree; the anonymous nudge with a pool guard is the honest version.
- **Super Like / SuperSwipe / Rose** (Tinder, Bumble, Hinge) — PSYCHOLOGY never-build (paid attention); golden rule 7. Agree without reservation.
- **Boost / Spotlight / paid placement** (Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, X) — API.md §1.65 "never re-rank"; ORDER BY created_at DESC only. Agree.
- **Incognito browsing** (Tinder, Bumble) — API.md §1.44 "you are seen while you see, no lurking"; 403 discovery_not_opted_in. Agree; symmetry is the whole point of the opt-in hour.
- **Live GPS map of people nearby** (Snapchat Snap Map, Instagram Friend Map) — FEATURES #30; LocationPicker only for place chips. Agree.
- **Note attached to a like / message before matching** (Bumble Compliments, Tinder Platinum, Hinge comments, Facebook Dating) — SPECS "single waves invisible"; ADR-015 no pre-match contact; wave accepts no body. Agree; the 80-char card note already carries the one-line intent without the harassment vector.
- **Daily curated pick / Top Picks / Standouts** (Tinder, Hinge, Bumble, Facebook Dating) — API.md §1.65; PSYCHOLOGY never-build. Agree.
- **Algorithmic compatibility ranking** (Hinge, Bumble, Tinder, Instagram Explore) — ORDER BY created_at DESC; suggestions ordered by evidence, never engagement. Agree. Note that a 50-card newest-first deck will need a non-algorithmic shuffle (e.g. random within the live hour) once an instance has more than 50 people opted in at once, or the oldest opt-ins never get seen.
- **Login-page member cards / public member enumeration** — lib/suggestions.ts:1-4; owner standing decision (CLAUDE.md, SPECS); both user routes requireAuth; share-link types exclude "profile". Agree; and the same logic should extend to the authenticated prefix search, which currently is enumeration behind a login.

### How it looks

The screenshot set never captured the live card stack, the OptinSheet, the MatchOverlay or the RouletteBanner — the surfaces this area actually lives on — nor /welcome. Everything this section says about them comes from code, and none of the rows below is about them: the card you wave on (one avatar, 80 characters, two tags, no profile link, window.confirm on Block) has never been looked at in a browser by this audit. That is itself a finding. The /welcome problems are in the Missing table, from code.
