| phone-local.png — "Make me discoverable" | The stranger-meeting feature is the most prominent CTA on the tab with no first-time explainer: what strangers see, that it lapses in an hour, how to report, what Roulette is. For a new user it is the first public write the app offers. | Bumble / Hinge show a safety sheet before the first match; Tinder shows a guidelines interstitial before the first swipe. | One-time consent sheet on first tap ("Who sees you: people nearby who also opted in, never your friends. They see your name, photo, vibe and note — not your area or chats. Ends in 60 minutes. You can block or report anyone."), remembered per account, not per device. |

Four products stacked with no sub-nav; the board starts below the fold on the phone capture. | Hinge: the primary action is always the highest-contrast element. WhatsApp Communities / Telegram folders: sticky segmented control. | "I'm bored" = full-width .btn-primary first; section h2 no larger than the title, or promote the title; sticky segmented control under the title (Plans · Bored now · Board); dashed borders only for drop targets and empty states. |

### If I had one week

1. **Message requests + "who can message me".** Add `users.dm_policy` (everyone / connections / nobody, default *connections* for new accounts, migration 0013), gate `POST /api/conversations` on it (conversations/routes.ts:153-163) so a non-connection's first DM parks as a request (reuse the group_join_requests shape: accept creates the DM, decline is silent and deletes), a Requests row pinned at the top of the chat list, and the setting as a PickerRow in Privacy. Match-created DMs (createMatchedDmTx) and roulette are exempt because both sides opted in. This closes the one hole the discovery surface makes dangerous.
2. **Safety in the chat, with reasons.** `ReportReason` enum in packages/shared (spam / harassment / impersonation / underage / scam / other) with a required pick in ReportSheet and a column on reports; ChatInfoSheet for DMs reordered to identity row → Mute → Safety (Report user · Block · End chat) → Appearance collapsed, where End chat exposes the existing `DELETE …/participants/:me` for DMs; the Block on HingeCard uses the house sheet instead of window.confirm. Reuse LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE as a one-line safety strip on OptinSheet, MatchOverlay, RouletteBanner and BeaconDetailSheet.
3. **Age gate and consent.** `users.age_attested_at` set from an "I'm 18 or older" checkbox on register (attestation only, no birthday — consistent with the field ban), a consent sentence linking to operator-editable /terms and /privacy, and `PUT /api/discovery/optin` + roulette join refusing accounts without the attestation (403 age_attestation_required). One day of work, and the stranger pool stops being open to minors.
4. **Make the card worth waving on.** Add `media` (the existing 3-image strip) to DiscoveryCardSchema and cardDto() — `username` is already there via UserLite (api.ts:3054; discovery/routes.ts:136-139), so the only client change for the profile link is a Link on HingeCard's photo/name to /u/:username (FEATURES #106); add an interests chip-grid step to /welcome and a case-folded `lower()` overlap in the SQL filter so "Hiking" matches "hiking"; add a `discovery_passes (user_id, target_id, expires_at)` table so Pass is remembered server-side for 7 days (silent, filtered in visibleCards(), undo of the last pass via a single DELETE). Every new user then ships a card with a face, interests and a way to read more before saying hi.
5. **Fix the roulette scope bug.** runRoulettePair() in discovery/service.ts:209 must honour the opt-in scope: pair "area" users only with same-area partners and "public" users with anyone, matching visibleCards(). Add an integration test next to v06:582 that two "My area" users in different areas are never paired. Document the rule in API.md §1.65 so it stops being an undocumented inconsistency.


---

## Local: Plans, the Board, and the Neighborhood

This area is two different products wearing one tab. The beacon side ("I'm bored" → atomic spark into a group chat with an icebreaker and venue poll, named micro-roles, afterglow recap, private kudos, guilt-free weekly rituals, a radar that counts plans without naming anyone) is better than anything in the 17-app benchmark set; Bumble BFF Plans and Partiful are the closest and both lose on group formation. It is bolted to a classifieds board that covers four narrow kinds (gig, ride, room, place review) and would lose to Nextdoor or Facebook Marketplace on day one: there is no way to sell a couch, give away a fridge, post a lost cat, ask "good plumber?", or find a neighborhood group without a link someone hands you. Counting the four tables below and excluding the deliberately-not-built list, **24 of 50 basic-tier expectations are fully met (48%)** and **18 of 65 standard-tier (28%)**; advanced is 9 of 28 (32%), almost all of it beacons. The misses are not exotic: keyword search that ignores the body, no bookmark, no renew, no sold/filled outcome, no capacity cap separate from the spark threshold, no exact date/time or plans beyond seven days, no .ics/add-to-calendar, no report-a-listing target, and a scam-safety note that is hidden from the strangers on the public share page who most need it. The foundation under all of it, free-text citext area equality with no autocomplete or hierarchy, means "Indiranagar" and "Indira Nagar" are different neighborhoods, which quietly undermines every "near me" claim the Local tab makes. The bans (payments, maps, push alerts, counts, rankings) are coherent and well documented, but several docs overclaim: FEATURES #41 and #42, SPECS §79d group discovery and PSYCHOLOGY §5 describe things the code does not do, and the "Only you see this line" host-stats caption promises an owner-only fact that the public user DTO ships to every viewer. UI-wise the board cards show expiry but not posted time or an excerpt, the "Plans" chip is a dead placeholder, and the create sheet loses a six-photo listing on an accidental swipe; the beacon sheets are consistent with the faces-not-counters rule. Honest summary: ship a sell/free kind, lost & found, a places entity, canonical areas, .ics and capacity before anything advanced, strip the host counters from the public DTO, and fix the four documented-but-unwired claims so the docs stop writing cheques the code cannot cash.

### What it has

| # | Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Classifieds listing create: photos, title, description, price text, location, area | basic | Facebook Marketplace, Nextdoor Finds | POST /api/listings (listings/routes.ts:108); ListingCreateSheet.tsx:255-310. The weakness is the kind set, not the form. |
| 2 | Multiple photos per listing (≤6) with in-app crop/rotate and HEIC conversion | basic | Facebook, Nextdoor, Meetup/Partiful/Luma (event photos) | LISTING_PHOTOS_MAX (constants.ts:482); ImageEditSheet.tsx. Native editor, no SaaS; needs MinIO at runtime. |
| 3 | Gig / services listings (hire me / work together, ≤5 tags) | standard | Nextdoor Neighbor Services; Facebook (jobs removed 2023) | GigDetailsSchema (api.ts:2387-2397). Offering/seeking direction covers services and informal jobs. |
| 4 | Room / housing rental listings (price, available from, photos) | basic | Facebook rentals/rooms, Nextdoor housing | RoomDetailsSchema (api.ts:2416-2425). Thin fields (no available_to, furnished, deposit) but functional. |
| 5 | Ride offers (from/to legs, departure time, seats) | standard | None of the 17 natively (Meetup/Partiful/Luma: done in chat) | RideDetailsSchema (api.ts:2400-2409). Genuine differentiator versus every benchmark app. |

Not counted above, because they are plumbing rather than features a user can reach: the listing/contact rate limits (constants.ts:466-467,498,707; listings/routes.ts:11,37) and the server-side block/ban/privacy enforcement that every row already assumes (listings/routes.ts:72-95; API.md §1.38, §1.40).

| # | Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Keyword search of listings (title, body, tags, route legs) | basic | ILIKE on title + details->>'place_name' only (listings/routes.ts:229-233); header search icon omitted (app/local/page.tsx:26-27) | Body, gig tags, ride legs, any full-text index. "Koramangala to airport" or a gig tag returns nothing; users notice immediately | P0 |
| 2 | Report a listing (dedicated target with listing pointer) | basic | ReportSheet.tsx:22-26 targets user/message/beacon/anon_message/story_overlay; ListingDetailSheet.tsx:207-211 reports the author as a user (api.ts:1300-1335) | A "listing" target. Beacons have one, listings do not, so admins get a user report with no idea which listing. Safety-critical inconsistency | P0 |
| 3 | Create an event/plan with title, description, date/time, location, cover | basic | Vibe chip + ≤140-char note (BEACON_NOTE_MAX_CHARS constants.ts:127) + now/tonight/weekend presets (BeaconCreateSheet.tsx:208-243); 7-day horizon (constants.ts:135-137) | Title, cover, description. Beacons are superb for "bored right now" but cannot represent "Diwali potluck, Sat 7pm, bring a dish"; users wanting to plan anything named notice day one | P0 |

The control behind #3; ranked under it | P1 |

A 4-seat car trip or a 6-person reservation cannot stop the 7th joiner; hosts hit this immediately | P0 |

| 36 | Host notified when someone RSVPs to their plan | basic | Join emits a live WS beacon.updated card patch (beacons/routes.ts:521-545): the face pile updates if the creator has the app open | No push and no in-app inbox item; the creator is pushed only at spark or the single "one more" re-ping. Likely intentional (PSYCHOLOGY rule 4, faces not counters), but hosts on a closed app learn of RSVPs only at spark time | P3 |
| 37 | "Plans" filter chip on the board | basic | The chip exists in BoardView.tsx | Selecting it sets listings=[] and shows static copy "Plans are the live cards up top" (BoardView.tsx:258-265); beacons never render in the board list. Drop it or render my plans there | P3 |

### Missing

| # | Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | For Sale & Free item listings (second-hand goods, free stuff) | basic | P0 | Facebook Marketplace, Nextdoor Finds (its most-used surface after the feed) | LISTING_KINDS = gig/ride/room/place_review only (constants.ts:459); no sell/item kind anywhere in modules/listings. The single most used local-board surface on every benchmark; users will try to sell a couch on day one and find no kind for it. |
| 2 | Add to calendar (.ics / Google / Apple / Outlook) | basic | P0 | Facebook, Nextdoor, WhatsApp events, Meetup, Partiful, Luma (Discord has none) | rg ".ics / icalendar / add to calendar / text/calendar" across apps, packages, docs → nothing. Zero-dependency, pull-based, and PSYCHOLOGY §2 explicitly asks for a one-tap personal reminder; an .ics route is an afternoon of work. |
| 3 | Renew / bump / repost an expired or closed listing | basic | P1 | Facebook, Nextdoor | API.md §1.48: PATCH on closed/expired → 410 "retire-and-repost instead"; no renew route in listings/routes.ts. The author re-enters everything after 30 days; a one-tap renew is cheap and expected. |
| 4 | Co-hosts with edit rights | basic | P1 | Facebook, Meetup, Partiful, Luma | Beacons have a single created_by; PATCH is creator-only (beacons/routes.ts:342). Micro-roles cover tasks, not editing rights. |
| 5 | Ride requests ("need a ride"), seat booking/decrement, recurring commutes | standard | P1 | None of the 17 (BlaBlaCar-style expectation) | RideDetailsSchema has no direction; rg seats in listings/service.ts → nothing. Rides are offer-only with static seats; the most asked-for gap once people actually use the ride kind. |
| 6 | Sort and filter by price range, date posted, soonest departure | standard | P1 | Facebook, Nextdoor | Fixed created_at DESC (listings/routes.ts:238); ListListingsQuerySchema has only kind/area/q/cursor/limit (api.ts:2550-2557). A 30-day board with one sort order gets stale fast. |
| 7 | Distance-based matching / "N km away" / radius | standard | P1 | Facebook, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | area is a citext string (schema.ts:54,718,1635); no geo columns, no PostGIS; FEATURES #30 coarse area only. Deliberate privacy stance, but rides and rooms are inherently distance-shaped; a coarse optional radius from the pinned snapshot would not break the stance. |
| 8 | Save / bookmark / watchlist a listing | standard | P1 | Facebook saved items, Nextdoor save, TikTok save place, Snapchat Want-to-go | rg bookmark/favourite/saved_listing/watchlist in modules/listings and packages/shared → nothing; no roadmap entry. Zero cost to the psychology rules (private, no counts) and universally expected. |
| 9 | Listing drafts / autosave in the create sheet | standard | P1 | Luma/Meetup/Partiful host drafts | ListingCreateSheet.tsx keeps state in React only; closing the sheet loses the form. A 6-photo, 2000-char listing lost to an accidental swipe-down is a real day-one annoyance on mobile. |
| 10 | Reply to, edit, delete or report a review | standard | P1 | Facebook, Meetup, TikTok | listings/reviews.ts has only POST create + GET list; ReportSheet.tsx has no review target. One angry review is permanent and unanswerable, a fairness problem on a trust panel. |
| 11 | Business / venue pages (claimable: hours, address, contact, CTA, posts) | standard | P1 | Facebook, Instagram, WhatsApp Business, TikTok, Nextdoor, Telegram Business, X professional profiles | rg "business page / venue page" across apps, packages, docs → nothing relevant. Seven of 17 benchmarks have it; the people-first ethos is a reason to keep it light (no ads), not to skip it. |
| 12 | Recommend a business / place (named recommenders with quotes, no stars) | standard | P1 | Facebook Page Recommendations, Nextdoor Recommendations | Not found; place_review is the only adjacent object (api.ts:2430). The named-faces model in PSYCHOLOGY §3 fits this perfectly: "Priya and 3 friends recommend" instead of counts. |
| 13 | "Ask for recommendations" post / thread scoped to my area | standard | P1 | Facebook, Nextdoor (its most common post type), Reddit city subs | Post kinds are text/poll/game/join_card (API.md §1.50); no area audience on posts. The #1 reason people open a neighborhood app ("good plumber?") has no home here; the public lane is global. |
| 14 | Area-scoped community feed lane (posts visible to my neighborhood) | standard | P1 | Nextdoor feed, Reddit city communities, Facebook local groups, BeReal nearby | Lanes are friends / discover(public) only (API.md §1.49); post audience is friends_of or public. The board is the only area surface and it is listing-shaped; there is nowhere to just say "power is out on 5th Main". |
| 15 | Host approval of joiners / request-to-join a plan | standard | P1 | Luma, Partiful, Signal call-link approval, Meetup via membership | POST /:id/join is unconditional for the audience (beacons/routes.ts:498). Public beacons let any stranger in; groups already have a request queue (API.md §1.68 join-requests; GroupSettingsSection.tsx approve/deny) to reuse. |
| 16 | Pre-event discussion / comments / questions on an open plan | standard | P1 | Facebook events, Nextdoor events, Discord events, Meetup | Chat exists only after spark (API.md §1.15); feed join_card comments are on the post, not the beacon. "Is it dog-friendly?" cannot be asked before committing. |
| 17 | Guest list management (remove a joiner, manual add) | standard | P1 | Meetup, Partiful, Luma, Facebook | No remove-joiner route in beacons/routes.ts (the only joins routes are GET /:id/joins and the self DELETE). A creator cannot eject a bad actor from an open beacon short of cancelling it. |
| 18 | Online / virtual event with an attached call link | standard | P1 | WhatsApp (call link on event), Facebook online events, Signal call links, Telegram scheduled video chat, X scheduled Spaces | No call-link field on the beacon schemas; calls exist separately. The app has mesh calls; a "this is a call" beacon is a small join. |
| 19 | Lost & Found board (pets and items with photo + location) | standard | P1 | Nextdoor, Facebook groups/alerts | rg "lost and found / lost_found" across apps, packages, docs → nothing. A fifth listing kind (lost/found, no price, photo, location, "found it" close reason) reuses everything that exists. |
| 20 | Share a plan or listing into a chat (forward / attach the card to a DM or group) | standard | P1 | Facebook, WhatsApp, Marketplace "Send to a friend" | ForwardSheet.tsx and Composer.tsx have no beacon/listing handling (rg hits only a css import at ForwardSheet.tsx:17); the only internal re-share is the feed join_card cross-post (FeedView.tsx:1254-1370; API.md §1.50) and the only chat-side chip is the server-written listing_contact/team_up system message (ChatView.tsx:1231-1249). The workaround, copying a public share link, exists only for is_public listings / public beacons. An area-only card has no in-app way to be handed to a specific person. |
| 21 | Reorder listing photos / choose the cover photo | basic | P2 | Every classifieds app | Photos state is append (uploadPhotos) and remove (photoRemove) only in ListingCreateSheet.tsx and ListingEditSheet.tsx; attachment_ids order is upload order; photos[0] silently becomes the full-bleed hero (ListingDetailSheet.tsx) and the board thumb (BoardView.tsx). With a 6-photo limit and hero rendering this is a visible gap. |
| 22 | Item condition field (new / used / for parts) | standard | P2 | Facebook Marketplace | rg condition in api.ts hits only support categories. Meaningless until a sell-items kind exists; sequence after it. |
| 23 | Expiry warning to the author (quiet push / in-app) before a listing lapses | standard | P2 | Facebook, Nextdoor | No listing references in lib/pushGuard.ts or ws. A notice about your own listing is not a teaser; compatible with ADR-012. |
| 24 | Venue / POI search when attaching a location | standard | P2 | Telegram, WhatsApp nearby places, Facebook | LocationPicker.tsx offers label text + current-position snapshot only. Would need self-hosted geocoding (Nominatim) to stay within rule 7. |
| 25 | Plus-ones / guest count on RSVP | standard | P2 | Meetup, Partiful, Luma | No guest count on beacon_joins (schema.ts:817-834). Only the events apps have it; beacons are small-group by design. |
| 26 | Waitlist with auto-promotion | standard | P2 | Meetup, Partiful, Luma | No waitlist state in beacon_joins. Depends on capacity. |
| 27 | Subscribe to a calendar feed (ICS URL) | standard | P2 | Luma, Meetup | Not found. Follows from .ics support. |
| 28 | Communities with subgroups (building / street / block) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp Communities | No parent/child conversation relation in schema.ts. Nice for apartment complexes; not urgent. |
| 29 | Hide / "not interested" dismiss on a listing or plan card | standard | P2 | Facebook Marketplace/Events, Nextdoor | ListingCard has a single onOpen tap and no menu (BoardView.tsx:372-405); BeaconStrip's menu offers only Take it down / Never mind / Report (BeaconStrip.tsx:226-249); rg hide/dismiss/not interested across listings, beacons, BoardView, ListingDetailSheet → only the kudos banner dismiss; no per-viewer hidden-listings table. The pull-based board has no way to prune. |
| 30 | Video in listings | standard | P2 | Facebook Marketplace, OLX | Picker is accept="image/*" (ListingCreateSheet.tsx:460); non-images rejected client-side ("Listing photos must be images", 109-110; ListingEditSheet.tsx:117); server binds only kind="image" (listings/routes.ts:166-176,374-390); API.md §1.38. Video uploads already exist for chat/stories. |
| 31 | Identity verification / verified-seller badge | advanced | P2 | WhatsApp/Meta Verified, Nextdoor verified neighbors, Tinder/Bumble selfie verification | No verification columns on users in schema.ts. Self-hosted selfie/ID verification is heavy; mutual-friend signals are the ethos-compatible substitute. |
| 32 | Business directory: browse nearby businesses by category | advanced | P2 | WhatsApp (select markets), Nextdoor | Not found in apps/ or docs/. Depends on business/place pages. |
| 33 | Nearby neighborhoods selection / boundaries | advanced | P2 | Nextdoor | No adjacency model; a single area string per user. Follows from canonical areas. |
| 34 | Community notices board (non-push: outage, water cut, weather, safety notice) | advanced | P2 | Nextdoor Alerts tab (2025), Facebook Crisis Response | Not found; closest is a text post in the global public lane (API.md §1.49). A pull-only "notices" kind on the board is ethos-compatible and genuinely useful. |
| 35 | Offers / price negotiation objects in chat | standard | P3 | Facebook Marketplace offers | No offer schema in api.ts; money settles outside (SPECS standing decisions). Plain chat covers it; a structured offer skirts the no-payments ethos. |
| 36 | Open-invite toggle (guests may invite friends) | standard | P3 | Partiful, Luma | Not found. The audience model substitutes. |
| 37 | Check-in / attendance / no-show tracking | standard | P3 | Luma QR check-in, Meetup no-show flags | Not found. No-show flags would violate the guilt-free doctrine; check-in has little value for 4-person hangouts. |
| 38 | Guest-list export (CSV / download) | basic | P3 | Meetup, Partiful, Luma (every event tool) | rg csv/text/csv/export in modules/beacons and the beacon components → nothing; GET /:id/joins is JSON for the detail sheet only (beacons/routes.ts:464). Table stakes for an events tool, close to pointless for a group that caps at 11 and lands in a chat; build it only if beacons grow a named-event mode (Half-built #3). |
| 39 | Structured event prompt blocks (dress code, playlist, parking, "what to bring" as fields) | standard | P3 | Partiful (prompt blocks), Luma | rg "dress code / playlist / what to bring / parking" across modules/beacons, components, packages/shared and docs → nothing. Micro-roles (has #28) cover the bring list; everything else has to fit the 140-char note. Low value until plans can carry a title and description. |
| 40 | Crime & Safety category with extra moderation / anti-profiling prompts | standard | P3 | Nextdoor | Not found. Needs an area feed first; the anti-profiling guardrails are worth copying if ever built. |
| 41 | Scheduled recurring community threads (game-day, weekly meetup megathreads) | standard | P3 | Reddit | Not found; rituals cover recurring plans, not recurring posts. Low value without an area feed. |
| 42 | Public questions / comments on a listing (thread under the card) | standard | P3 | Nextdoor, Facebook Marketplace Q&A | The only comment table is post_comments keyed by post_id (schema.ts:1448); listings have no comment relation or route; the detail sheet's sole CTA is the DM contact (listings/routes.ts:434); attachments.comment_id refers to post comments only (schema.ts:664). DM-first appears deliberate (ADR-012 pull-based), so low priority. |
| 43 | Free Finds dedicated free-items section | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | No sell/free kind (constants.ts:459). Follows naturally once a sell kind with a Free toggle exists. |
| 44 | Garage/yard sale listings and seasonal maps | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | Not in LISTING_KINDS or docs. Niche; a sell kind with a date would cover it. |
| 45 | Local deals / coupons / business offers | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor, TikTok coupons | Not found. Advertising-shaped; low fit with the ethos. |
| 46 | Personal place lists (favorites / visited / want to go) | advanced | P3 | Snapchat Places lists, TikTok saved places | Not found. Depends on a places entity and a bookmark primitive. |
| 47 | Pet directory with lost-pet alerts to nearby neighborhoods | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | Not found. The alert push would be banned; the directory is niche. |
| 48 | Public agency / official accounts posting and polling by neighborhood | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor, Facebook | Not found; no verified-org concept in the users schema. Out of scope for a self-hosted community instance. |
| 49 | Crisis response / mutual-aid help map | advanced | P3 | Facebook Crisis Response, Nextdoor Help Map | Not found. Gig "offering" + a notices kind would cover most of it. |
| 50 | Local news feed / journalist profiles | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | Not found. Publisher partnerships conflict with the self-hosted ethos. |

### Planned already

- **OS share sheet (navigator.share) for listings and plans** (standard, P1): SPECS v1.0d "Share audit (110)". navigator.share is only used in InviteSheet.tsx:33,100; listings use lib/share.ts copyShareLink. Wiring the existing pattern to listings/beacons is small.
- **Trust signals beyond stars: account age, mutual friends, joined-via** (standard, P1): FEATURES #44 marks "account-age/mutual-friend signals" as planned. Cheap, non-ranking, and more useful than stars for a first meeting.
- **Nearby public feed / map of location-tagged content ("Spotted" pins)** (advanced, P2): FEATURES #109; SPECS "Location pins (109)"; DESIGN-HANDOFF.md:101. Planned with interested-faces; the nearest thing to "what is happening around here".
- **Browse / discover public groups by location or interest** (standard, P1): SPECS §79d says PUBLIC groups "appear in discovery" but nothing implements it; global search is FEATURES #108, planned. Spec/code mismatch; the cheapest fix is an area-filtered public-groups list.
- **Plan-group enrichments: Go Deeper deck, Plan Mode, energy selector, squad-split nudge, food poll, daily hype prompt** (advanced, P3): PSYCHOLOGY §3, §5, "Weaker but interesting". rg squad / hype prompt / food poll across apps/ → nothing. Documented intent, none wired; the §5 "Build (v0.3)" items read as shipped but are not.

Coverage arithmetic, from the tables above: basic = 24 has ÷ (24 has + 20 half-built + 6 missing + 0 planned) = 24/50 = 48%; standard = 18 ÷ (18 + 12 + 32 + 3) = 18/65 = 28%; advanced = 9 ÷ (9 + 5 + 12 + 2) = 9/28 = 32%. Half-built counts as not met. The deliberately-not-built list below carries no tier and is excluded from every denominator.

### Deliberately not built

- **Map view of listings / events (embedded tiles, pins)** — CLAUDE.md rule 7 (no map tile keys); API.md §1.52 "rendering is a LINK, never an embed". Half right. The ban is a no-SaaS rule, not a psychology rule, and it is costing the area: rides and rooms are spatial objects. Self-hosted OSM tiles are fully compatible with the ethos and should be on the roadmap; "no map at all" is the wrong reading of "no tile keys".
- **Saved-search / keyword / "new listing near you" alerts** — ADR-012 pull-based doctrine; PSYCHOLOGY rules 2 and 4. Right for push. Note that a private saved search with no notification is not covered by the ban and would recover most of the value for time-sensitive ride offers.
- **Personalized / algorithmic listing recommendations** — API.md §1.49; PSYCHOLOGY no engagement ranking. Right.
- **Boost / promote listing (paid placement)** — CLAUDE.md rule 7; ADR-012. Right.
- **In-app checkout, shipping, purchase protection, tickets** — SPECS "No payments in-app, ever"; CLAUDE.md rule 7; FEATURES #45. Right; it also keeps fraud liability off a self-hosted operator.
- **P2P payments in chat / wallet** — CLAUDE.md rule 7. Right.
- **Listing view counts / "N people interested" badges** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; API.md §1.39 named author-only list. Right.
- **Top-rated lists, sort by rating, leaderboards** — API.md §1.40; ADR-012. Right.
- **Business catalog, cart and in-app shop** — CLAUDE.md rule 7. Right.
- **AI-summarized local recommendations (Faves)** — API.md §1.49, §1.40; CLAUDE.md rule 7 no third-party SaaS. Right.
- **Live / ambient location sharing** — API.md §1.52; FEATURES #30 coarse area + explicit one-tap snapshot only. Right; the snapshot plus "On my way" faces covers the meet-up case.
- **Public host / group star ratings** — API.md §1.40; PSYCHOLOGY rule 3. Right in intent; in practice the host counters leak through UserPublic (Half-built #31), so the ban is not yet enforced at the contract.
- **Event analytics (views, RSVP conversion, traffic)** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; ADR-012. Right for public numbers. Owner-private counts already exist (plan karma), so a host-only "N people saw this" would not break rule 3, but it adds nothing to a 6-person hangout; leave it.
- **Paid tickets, refunds, promo codes, pay-what-you-want, memberships** — SPECS "No payments in-app, ever"; CLAUDE.md rule 7. Right on payments, but the ban is being read too broadly: an external ticket-link field (a URL on the beacon) is allowed and absent.
- **Urgent neighbor safety alerts (push broadcast to an area)** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 4; never-build "teaser notifications"; ADR-012. Right for the push blast. A pull-only notices card (Missing #34) is not banned and should be built.
- **Geo-targeted ads / promoted posts / sponsors** — CLAUDE.md rule 7; ADR-012. Right; no ads product is possible without payments anyway.
- **Fundraisers / donate buttons** — CLAUDE.md rule 7. Right; a link-out to an external fundraiser inside a post is still fine.
- **Location-based trends / trending topics for a city** — PSYCHOLOGY no engagement ranking; ADR-012. Right.
- **Attendance shown on profiles / "N friends are going" social proof outside the plan** — beacon_sparked activity is recorded for the creator only ("a joiner's attendance is the plan group's business, not a strip item", beacons/service.ts:532-537; API.md §1.59; migration 0009:104); ACTIVITY_KINDS has no beacon_joined; joiner faces appear only on the card to its audience and never on the share page. Right. Facebook's "X friends are going" is a FoMO engine; limiting attendance visibility to the card's audience is the correct call for this product and is a binding API.md contract, so "banned" rather than "missing".

### How it looks

The global light-scheme contrast problem (bubbles, chips and borders in the 1.0–1.3:1 band) hits every Local screen too; it is covered, with the colour sources, in the design-system section. What follows is specific to this area and limited to the captured screens (phone and desktop Local, beacon create, beacon detail, listing create, message long-press, chats overflow, Things, public profile).

1. **Local tab (phone-local.png, desk-local.png): the hierarchy is upside down.** The section h2s "Bored together" and "The board" are 22px in the display face (discovery.module.css:21-26; board.module.css:16-22) while the page title is a 17px `.topbar h1` (globals.css:347-348), and the product's primary verb, "+ I'm bored", is a 1.5px dashed ghost pill in `--muted` (beacons.module.css:32-43): the same treatment as the empty-radar box (beacons.module.css:354-357), the add-story ring and the media add tile. Hinge makes the primary action the highest-contrast element on screen; Instagram never lets a section header exceed the nav title. Fix: "I'm bored" as a full-width `.btn-primary` (accent gradient, ≥44px tall) placed first; h2 ≤ the title size; dashed borders reserved for drop targets and empty states.
2. **Local tab: four products stacked, the board below the fold.** Beacon strip + "I'm bored", then the "Bored together" strangers card, then "The board" with seven chips (the seventh clipped to "Pla…" in the 390px capture: `.chipRow` is `overflow-x: auto; scrollbar-width: none` with no edge fade, board.module.css:41-46), then area chip and search. In the 390×844 capture the board, the actual community-board use case, starts below the fold and "Mine" is the last chip; there is no sub-navigation. WhatsApp Communities and Telegram folders use a sticky segmented control. Fix: sticky segmented control under the title (Plans · Bored now · Board), search above the chips, "Mine" second, a right-edge gradient fade on `.chipRow` (or wrap to two rows under 420px).
3. **Board rows (phone-local.png): listings that do not look like listings.** A row ("GIG / Need a photographer for Saturday / ₹2000 / expires in 30d") has no container, no divider, no thumbnail slot when there is no photo, and no posted time or excerpt (BoardView.tsx:338-407); it sits on bare background directly under the search field and reads as left-over text. Facebook Marketplace and OLX always show a card with an image slot (placeholder when empty), title, price and meta. Fix: a fixed image slot with a per-kind placeholder glyph, title at body weight 600, price as a chip, one meta line ("posted 2d · Koramangala · expires in 28d"), a hairline between rows.
4. **"Plans" chip (BoardView.tsx:258-265): a dead control in the primary filter row.** Selecting it empties the list and shows "Plans are the live cards up top". No mainstream app ships a filter that filters to nothing. Fix: remove it, or render my open and sparked plans inside the board list under that chip.
5. **Rescue / SOS: three entry points, three treatments, none on Feed.** On Local (phone-local.png top-right) it is a 🆘 emoji inside a `btn btn-ghost btn-icon` (8px padding, globals.css:442-445; RescueSheet.tsx:35-40) with an aria-label but no visible text, rendering as a red square on Linux/Playwright and a monochrome glyph on Windows. In the Chats ⋯ menu (chats-overflow capture) it is a labeled "Rescue me" row next to Settings and Invite (ConversationListView.tsx:232-241). On the Profile tab it is a full RescueCard with a heading and a primary button (settings/profile/page.tsx:382). From Feed it is two taps and a guess. Uber/Lyft and Bumble use one persistent, labeled safety control in the same place on every screen. Fix: one labeled ≥44px "Rescue" pill with an SVG life-ring in `--danger` outline on every tab root; drop the ⋯ menu copy.
6. **"Make me discoverable" (Bored together card): the most prominent CTA on the screen is a public write with no first-time explainer** of what strangers will see, that it lapses after an hour, or how to report. Bumble and Hinge show a safety sheet before the first match; Tinder shows community guidelines before swiping. Fix: a one-time consent sheet ("Who sees you: people nearby who also opted in, never your friends. They see your name, photo and vibe, not your area or chats. It ends in 60 minutes. You can block or report anyone.") remembered per account, not per device.
7. **Light a beacon sheet (phone-beacon-create.png, desk-beacon-create.png): three defects.** (a) No grabber, no detent, and "Light it" sits at the bottom of an internal scroll: cut at the screen edge in the 390×844 capture and clipped in the 1280×800 one (360×740 was not captured). (b) The disabled "Light it" is a solid mid-grey pill with white text that reads as an enabled secondary button; nothing says what is missing. Instagram keeps "Share" brand-blue at reduced opacity. (c) "Expires on its own. No one is notified when it fizzles." is `.expiryNote`, 12.5px in `--faint` (beacons.module.css:1117-1120); in the light scheme `--faint` is #aab2c2 (globals.css:100), which on white is 2.1:1, so the sentence that states the product's core privacy promise is the least legible text on the screen. The detail footer "Expires in 5h 54m · only Arjun Rao can edit or end it" (`.detailFoot`, beacons.module.css:1224-1228) has the same spec. Fix: sticky footer CTA with safe-area padding; disabled = accent at 40% opacity with a helper line and aria-describedby; helper copy in `--muted` at ≥13px.
8. **Now / Tonight / Weekend (phone-beacon-create.png): one of five "pick one of N" treatments in the app.** These are rounded-rect cards with sublabels (BeaconCreateSheet.tsx:225-243); board filters are filled-ink pills; the feed lane toggle is an outlined block; help categories are white chips. Instagram has one segmented control and one chip; Hinge has one filled-pill chip for every filter. Fix: Chip for the presets, with a fourth "Pick a time" chip that opens the datetime picker the edit sheet already has (Half-built #4).
9. **Beacon sheet labels ("WHAT'S THE VIBE?", "WHEN?", "WHO CAN SEE IT?"): a third eyebrow style.** `.sheetLabel` is 12.5px regular uppercase at 0.05em (beacons.module.css:505-509) next to the serif settings headers and the 12px/700/0.08em `h2.sectionLabel` (identity.module.css:744-748). Telegram and iOS Settings use one grouped-list header on every screen. Fix: one `.eyebrow` token used here and everywhere.
10. **Emoji as chrome on this area's surfaces:** "📍 Add a place" in the beacon and listing sheets (LocationPicker.tsx:88); "Join 🙋", "needs N more 🔥" and "Claim 🎯" on the feed join card that cross-posts plans (FeedView.tsx:1366,1318,1242); sparkle/flame, memo and trophy glyphs in the profile activity strip (ActivityStrip.tsx:27,41,68). They render as Noto on Android and Apple on iOS and clash with the line-icon set. The vibe-chip grid can keep emoji because it is content the user picks. Fix: swap to the stroke-icon family; button labels become plain "Join", "Claim", "Add a place".
11. **Beacon card kebab and "+ LIST SOMETHING" are both well under the 44px minimum.** `.cardKebab` is 28×28 (beacons.module.css:276-282); `.listCta` is 11.5px uppercase text with `padding: 2px 0` (board.module.css:24-35), so its hit box is one line of small caps. Apple HIG is 44pt, Material 48dp. These are the controls people miss and re-tap. Fix: ≥44×44 hit boxes via padding + negative margin; promote "+ LIST SOMETHING" to a 36–40px pill.
12. **List on the board sheet (phone-listing-create.png): opens as a short stub** with the Post button in the header and the form below the fold; an accidental swipe-down discards six photos and 2,000 characters because state lives only in React (Missing #9). Instagram's "New post" flow pins the CTA and never loses the draft. Fix: sheet detents + sticky footer; localStorage draft keyed by kind; "Discard draft?" on dismiss.
13. **ListingReviewNudge in chat (chat-msg-longpress.png): a white card wedged between the scroll and the composer.** It is mounted outside the message scroll container (ChatView.tsx:810-815; `.nudge`, board.module.css:737-743), so it takes height from the thread instead of scrolling with it, and in the capture it sits over an "asked about:" system line and a bubble. WhatsApp renders prompts as centred in-stream system cards. Fix: append it inside the message scroll as a dismissible system card, never between scroll and composer.
14. **Desktop (desk-local.png): a 680px column (`.page` max-width, globals.css:171-173) in the 1280px capture with nothing in the gutters;** opening a beacon or listing is a modal, and the rail disappears on /u/[username] when you tap a face in a plan. Telegram Desktop and WhatsApp Web keep the nav mounted and use the width. Fix: at ≥1100px open beacon/listing detail and Bored-together in a right pane; raise `.page` max-width to ~760px; mount the rail in the authed layout.
15. **"Things you can do" (things capture) deep-links Beacons and Rituals to /plans** (FEATURE_DIRECTORY, constants.ts:1038-1039), which is a bare redirect to /local (app/plans/page.tsx:6). Point the hrefs at /local.

The beacon strip and detail sheet (named faces, countdown, "IT'S ON", "Never mind", "Take it down", the explicit spark-now confirm) are the one place where the faces-not-counters rule is visible in the UI rather than just the docs.

### If I had one week

1. **Days 1–2: a "sell" kind and a "lost_found" kind.** Add `sell` (numeric price + currency + Free flag, condition new/used/for-parts, close reason sold/given-away/withdrawn) and `lost_found` (direction lost/found, photo, location, close reason found/returned) to LISTING_KINDS, the Zod details schemas, the create/edit sheets and the board chips. Both reuse photos, contact, expiry, blocks and the share page as they stand. Add a one-tap renew route (POST /:id/renew → fresh expires_at, same id) and a close-reason field for every kind so contacters see "Sold" instead of a bare 410. This is the single change that moves the board from "four narrow kinds" to a board people will actually use.
2. **Day 3 (half day): the safety-and-privacy quartet.** Add a `listing` target to CreateReportRequestSchema, reports.target_listing_id, ReportSheet and the admin content page so a report maps to the listing; render LISTING_SAFETY_NOTE in SharePageView.tsx for gig/ride/room/sell; make SharePageView deepLink() return /local?l=<id> for listings so the signup gate lands on the card; strip `beacons_created`/`beacons_sparked` for non-owners in userPublicDto (or move them to UserPrivate) so "Only you see this line" is true. Four small diffs, all P0/P1.
3. **Days 3–4: beacons that can represent a planned thing.** In BeaconCreateSheet add a fourth "Pick a time" chip that exposes the datetime-local start/expiry the edit sheet already has (the API accepts any window today); raise BEACON_MAX_HORIZON_S from 7 to 45 days; add an optional `max_joiners` column independent of spark_threshold, enforced in the join path (410 beacon_full) and shown as "N spots left" only to the audience of that card, never on profiles; add an optional title (≤60 chars) above the note. Ship GET /api/beacons/:id/calendar.ics (text/calendar, token-less for members, share-token for public) with an "Add to calendar" row in the detail sheet. Still faces-not-counters, still pull-based.
4. **Day 5: search, cards and drafts.** Extend the board query to ILIKE over title, body, gig tags, ride from/to legs and place_name (a tsvector generated column if you want it fast), put the search field above the chips, and delete the dead "Plans" chip. Add posted-time, a one-line excerpt and the location label to ListingCard with a placeholder image slot. Autosave the create sheet to localStorage per kind and restore it on reopen.
5. **Days 5–6: canonical areas and the docs.** Create an `areas` table seeded from the distinct values already in users/listings/beacons, fold case and whitespace on match, and give the account settings, listing create and beacon create sheets an autocomplete against it; keep free text as a fallback that creates a new canonical row rather than a near-duplicate. Then fix the four overclaims so the docs stop lying: FEATURES #41 (room "looking for" direction), FEATURES #42 (place reviews feeding venue polls), SPECS §79d (public groups in discovery) and PSYCHOLOGY §5 "Build (v0.3)" items, each marked planned until the code exists.


---

## Calls, voice notes, photos and video

The signaling under the calls is competent (ICE restart with backoff, TURN credential refresh, ring-push retraction, honest failure copy) and watch-together is something only Discord matches. None of that engineering is visible to a user, and the surface a user meets first is unfinished. Basic-tier coverage is 73% (30 of 41 basic rows across the tables below, banned items excluded). The 11 basic misses are the loud ones: no call history (the feature is on the roadmap, but a "Call history is coming soon." line ships in Settings → Calls today), answered calls leave no trace in the chat, no in-app camera shutter, no swipe between a post's photos, no speaker/device routing, no alt text, no client-side downscale, originals stored and served at full resolution, uploads behind a spinner with no progress or cancel, a full-screen viewer that the feed cannot reach, and "E2EE" that is only the browser's DTLS default with no verification. Standard-tier coverage is 17% (13 of 78): no screen share, no remote mute indicator, no audio-to-video upgrade, no who-can-call setting, no busy signal, no low-data mode, no video trim, no filters/draw/text on photos, no voice-note lock or preview, no HD/data-saver/auto-download settings, no per-chat media tab. Media editing is crop-and-rotate only, and the video pipeline is a remux, not a transcode, so a 100 MB clip is a 100 MB download on cellular. The bans are the right ones (no viewer counts, gifts, Giphy, licensed music, swipe-video feed), but two tensions are undeclared: YouTube is the single third-party runtime script in a "no third-party SaaS" product, and "no call recording / no filters" are not written down anywhere as decisions. Fix order: inline call-ended events plus the promised call history, then camera shutter and carousel viewer, then the media-quality/data settings, then remote mute + audio-to-video + screen share.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1:1 voice calls started from the chat header | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, X, Discord, Bumble | Shipped. Media is acquired before the durable row exists, so a denied permission never creates a call (`calls/routes.ts:86` POST /api/conversations/:id/calls; `webrtc.ts:509`). |
| 1:1 video calls | basic | Same nine | Shipped (`webrtc.ts:117-121` getMedia; `CallOverlay.tsx` videoSplit; `schema.ts` kind CHECK). |
| Group voice/video calls, one live call per conversation | standard | WhatsApp (32), Telegram (200), Signal (50), Instagram (8), Messenger (8), Snapchat (16/32), Discord (25) | Works, but CALL_MESH_MAX=8 (`constants.ts:561`) is Instagram-level; every other messenger is 25-200. LiveKit SFU is a documented seam only (ADR-014, FEATURES #50). Atomic Lua cap in `service.ts addCallMember`; `calls_live_idx` (`schema.ts:1818`) keeps one live call per conversation, a second caller auto-joins, and "call is full" / "already ended" copy exists (`webrtc.ts:524-532, 579-584`). |

This is the only call event that ever reaches the timeline; see Missing. |
