### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Calls encrypted end to end (server never sees media) | basic | Only the docs half: ADR-014 lines 48-49, 100 assert DTLS-SRTP. The mesh is peer-to-peer, so media is transport-encrypted against an honest server. | Nothing in the app adds to the browser default: `webrtc.ts:117-122` is plain getUserMedia + RTCPeerConnection; SDP with the DTLS fingerprints is relayed verbatim by `gateway.ts:637-653` relayToCallPeer with no out-of-band fingerprint or SAS check, so a compromised signaling server could MITM. No lock badge, no copy in CallOverlay or Settings; FEATURES.md:175 still lists E2EE as backlog. Same posture as WhatsApp/Signal minus their key verification. | P3 |
| Busy signal / call waiting when the callee is already on a call | standard | `webrtc.ts:785-786` silently ignores the invite on the busy device and lets other devices ring. | Caller rings out to "missed" after 45 s; the busy callee gets a missed-call message and push for a call they could never take. No "on another call" copy anywhere. WhatsApp has had this since day one. | P1 |
| Network-quality indicator / call stats | standard | `webrtc.ts:285-323` derives four conn_state words from iceConnectionState. | No getStats() anywhere in apps/web. WebRTC adapts bitrate on its own, but the user gets no "weak connection" warning until the call is already reconnecting. WhatsApp, Signal, Discord, Telegram all show bars. | P1 |
| Hide IP in calls / always-relay (P2P off) toggle | standard | `lib/turn.ts` and GET /api/turn-credentials exist; relay runs under COMPOSE_PROFILES=turn (`docker-compose.yml:111-119`); `selftest.ts:90` already passes an `iceTransportPolicy`. | `webrtc.ts` never sets iceTransportPolicy:'relay' and there is no setting. Mesh P2P exposes peer IPs to each other by design; the relay is there, the choice is not. WhatsApp, Telegram and X all expose it. | P2 |
| Watch-together gating consistency in channels and ghost groups | standard | `gateway.ts:497` drops typing for channels/ghost groups; calls refuse both (`calls/routes.ts:93-100`). | `gateway.ts:725-760` watch.* is only participant-gated and relays host: meta.userId un-masked. In a ghost group a watch session reveals the host's real id; in a 10k-member channel any member can fan a session to everyone. A consistency bug, not a feature gap. | P1 |
| Draw / pen / highlighter on a photo before sending | standard | `DoodleSheet.tsx` is a separate blank-canvas message type; `StoryCanvas.tsx` strokes are vector overlays added by viewers. | `ImageEditSheet.tsx` has no brush. You can doodle, but not on your own photo, which is the first thing every messenger editor does (WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Snapchat, Facebook). | P1 |
| Text and sticker overlays on a photo (burned in) | standard | Stories: caption ≤140 chars (StoriesStrip) plus viewer-added vector text bubbles and emoji stamps (StoryCanvas). | Chat photos get nothing. The "never burned in" design is principled, but it means no edited photo ever leaves the app. WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, Facebook, TikTok all burn text in. | P1 |
| Server-side image compression, re-encode, EXIF strip, thumbnails, blurhash | basic | `pipeline.ts:171-250`: jpeg q85 / webp q90, 512 px thumb, blurhash, EXIF strip via re-encode. | `pipeline.ts:177` resizes only the thumb; there is no max-dimension cap on the original, so a 48 MP photo is re-encoded and served at 48 MP on every full-size tap. Every benchmark caps the long edge (Instagram 1080 px). | P1 |
| Video autoplay preference (always / Wi-Fi only / never) and muted autoplay in feed | standard | FeedView and AttachmentView use preload=metadata and never autoplay; `StoryViewer.tsx:217` autoPlay={!reducedMotion}. | No setting anywhere. The calm default is fine under PSYCHOLOGY; the gap is that it is not a user choice. Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, X, Facebook, Threads all offer it. | P2 |
| Video playback speed / quality picker / background audio / loop | standard | Native `<video>` controls only; desktop Chromium's overflow menu has speed. `AudioBubble.tsx:12, 60` sets playbackRate for voice notes. | No playbackRate control on any `<video>` element (the voice-note speed button is the only one in the app); mobile video has no speed. Single-quality source anyway until transcoding exists. Telegram, X, TikTok have it. | P2 |
| PiP / background play for ordinary feed and chat videos | standard | Native controls expose PiP on Chromium only. | No explicit button: `MediaLightbox.tsx` is image-only, and the gallery's local VideoLightbox (`gallery/page.tsx:203-223`) is a bare `<video controls autoPlay>` with a close button. Telegram, X, Facebook have it; Threads and TikTok lack it too. | P3 |
| Full-screen viewer: pinch-zoom, pan, double-tap, Save, Open original | basic | MediaLightbox is wired into chat attachments, the gallery, the profile page and listing photos (`MediaLightbox.tsx:3-8`). | Feed post images open in a new tab instead: `FeedView.tsx:1013-1016` renders `<a href={a.url} target="_blank">`; FeedView does not import MediaLightbox; FEATURES.md:136 (#93) scopes the viewer to profile/group pics. The viewer is real but unreachable from the feed. | P3 |
| Share / forward / reply from inside the viewer | standard | ForwardSheet works from the message row. | `MediaLightbox.tsx:227-228` admits "Share/Edit have no affordance on a bare viewer, so only Save renders". Forward exists, just not where people look for it (Instagram, WhatsApp, Telegram all forward from the viewer). | P2 |
| Upload progress percentage, cancel, retry, background upload | basic | `Composer.tsx:622-640` uploading counter and a "Video (processing…)" chip. | `api.ts` is fetch-based, so there is no upload.onprogress, no percentage, no cancel, no retry. A spinner for a 100 MB clip on cellular is a day-one annoyance. BeReal does background upload with retry. | P1 |
| External meeting link / livestream embed for online events | standard | YouTube iframe exists only inside watch-together (`gateway.ts:734`). | No online-event join-link field on events. Meetup, Partiful and Luma rely on third parties; this app could reuse the YouTube carve-out or its own calls for online events. | P3 |
| Audio file messages (mp3 / m4a / ogg) beyond recorded voice notes | standard | AUDIO_MIME_ALLOWLIST (`constants.ts:213`) includes audio/mpeg and audio/mp4, so an mp3 picked through the file input becomes kind 'audio' and plays in AudioBubble. | Capped at MAX_AUDIO_BYTES 10 MB / 300 s (a 6-minute track is rejected); the chip labels it "Voice note" (`Composer.tsx:636`); no music-style player (no title/artist, no seek-by-time). Telegram and WhatsApp treat audio files as a separate kind. | P3 |

Not counted above, because it is engineering hygiene rather than a product feature: no Playwright spec files are checked in for calls or media although CLAUDE.md and TROUBLESHOOTING.md:182-184 document the fake-device flags; `test/v06.integration.test.ts` and `v071.integration.test.ts` cover REST/WS only, so browser-level call verification is manual today.

### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Answered / ended call events inline in the chat timeline (with duration) | basic | P0 | Instagram ("Call ended 5:12"), Messenger, Snapchat, X, Discord, Bumble | `service.ts:182-200` is the only system-message insert (call_missed, which also covers cancel-before-answer); POST /api/calls/:id/end from `ongoing` (`calls/routes.ts:193`; API.md §1.42) writes no message; no call_ended handling in ChatView. A 40-minute call leaves zero trace, and with no Calls tab either, answered calls vanish the moment you hang up. Declines are a separate, documented choice (see Deliberately not built). |
| In-app camera capture / camera-shutter shortcut in the composer | basic | P0 | Telegram, Threads, Hinge, Snapchat, BeReal, WhatsApp, Instagram | `Composer.tsx:791-800` is a generic `<input type=file hidden multiple>` with no accept and no capture attribute, so any file is offered and the server 415s the wrong ones. A second input with capture="environment" gives mobile a shutter in an afternoon. |
| Swipe between images in a multi-image message / post carousel | basic | P0 | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, Nextdoor, WhatsApp, Telegram | `MediaLightbox.tsx:16-27` takes a single src; `FeedView.tsx:1008` renders a 1-4 tile grid with no carousel; messages allow 10 attachments. Day-one visible: open photo 1 of 4, close, reopen photo 2. |
| Speaker / earpiece / Bluetooth audio routing | basic | P1 | Signal, Messenger, Snapchat, Discord, WhatsApp | No setSinkId or enumerateDevices anywhere; CallOverlay has no speaker control. A PWA cannot route audio on iOS at all and only desktop Chromium has setSinkId, so the ceiling is the platform, which is why this is P1 not P0. |
| User-provided image alt text and sensitive-media / spoiler flag | basic | P1 | X, Discord | AttachmentSchema (`packages/shared/src/api.ts:278-302`) has no alt or spoiler field; `AttachmentView.tsx:48` uses file_name as alt; `FeedView.tsx:1015` renders alt="". Accessibility gap that most users will not see day one. |
| Client-side downscale of large photos before upload | basic | P1 | Every chat app (WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram downsize to ~1600-2048 px) | `imageUpload.ts:58-66` draws the canvas at natural size (HEIC to JPEG only); `pipeline.ts:177` resizes only the thumb. A 12 MP / 20 MB phone photo is uploaded, stored and served at full size (cap is 25 MB). The main data and latency win; pairs with the upload-quality setting. |
| Add participants / invite someone mid-call | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat | `calls/routes.ts` join requires an active conversation participant; no invite frame among the `ws.ts:359-447` call schemas. The only way to add a person is to add them to the group and hope they see the Join chip. |
| Start a call from a profile page / contact card / people list | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram | startCall( is called from ChatView (4 sites) and DrunkBanner only; `app/u/[username]` has no call button. Every call must begin inside a conversation. Instagram/Snapchat/Bumble are the same, hence standard not basic. |
| Microphone / camera / speaker device picker (desktop) | standard | P1 | Discord, Signal, Telegram, Messenger | No enumerateDevices or deviceId in apps/web, only facingMode constraints. Desktop users with a headset cannot choose it from the app. |
| Remote mute indicator (see which peer is muted) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Discord, Messenger | Call frames (`ws.ts:395-447`) carry call_id/sdp/ice/connection state only; CallOverlay infers "cam off" from a missing video track. A muted peer is indistinguishable from a silent one in group calls; a tiny relayed call.media {mic,cam} frame closes it. |
| Screen sharing (window or screen, with audio) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger, Discord, TikTok LIVE | No getDisplayMedia anywhere in web, shared, server or docs. Absent on Instagram/X/Snapchat/Bumble too, but it is table stakes for any desktop call and is a client-only addition on the existing replaceTrack path. |
| Low-data mode for calls ("use less data") | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | No resolution or bitrate caps in getMedia (`webrtc.ts:117-121`); no setting on the Calls page. Cheap win: cap video constraints and sender maxBitrate behind one toggle. |
| Who can call me / silence unknown callers / "video asks first" | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Instagram, X | Only a code comment at `settings/calls/page.tsx:240-242` ("ship with the Calls pack"); nothing in server, shared, SPECS or FEATURES. Today anyone who can open a DM can ring you (only blocks stop it); ringing is a louder interruption than a message, so this compounds the "anyone can DM anyone" finding. |
| Colour filters / presets with intensity | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok (basic), WhatsApp, Telegram, Snapchat | Nothing in ImageEditSheet (no filter/brightness/contrast/saturate). Table stakes for a stories product; CSS filter on canvas is a day of work. Signal, X, BeReal and the dating apps skip it on purpose, so the owner could declare it a non-goal, but then say so in PSYCHOLOGY.md. |
| Blur brush / auto face blur (privacy tool) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal (auto face blur) | Not in ImageEditSheet. For a privacy-first local-community product this is the one editing tool with a safety story: bystanders' faces in board and feed photos. |
| Video trim before sending | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Snapchat, X, Reddit, Facebook, TikTok, Tinder, Bumble | No video editor component; `StoriesStrip.tsx:216-218` sends videos straight through. The most universal video feature across all 17 benchmark apps; the server already runs ffmpeg (`jobs/media.ts`), so trim can be metadata plus -ss/-to server-side. |
| Voice-note lock (hands-free), pause and listen-back before sending | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | `Composer.tsx:415-423` recorder.onstop sends immediately; release equals send unless cancel is armed. Instant-send-on-release was the number-one voice-note complaint in every messenger until they added lock + preview. |
| Server-side video transcoding / downscaling / adaptive streaming | standard | P1 | Reddit (HLS/DASH), Threads, Nextdoor, Facebook, Instagram (1080p default), Bumble, Hinge, Tinder | `jobs/media.ts:190-196` runs only `ffmpeg -c copy -movflags +faststart`; VIDEO_MIME_ALLOWLIST is mp4/webm (`constants.ts:37`). A 100 MB upload is a 100 MB download on cellular. One scale/crf leg (1080p H.264 crf 23) halves most clips and ffmpeg is already in the worker. |
| Media upload quality setting (Standard vs HD default, per-send "send original") | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, X, TikTok | No upload-quality / compression setting in web, shared, FEATURES or SPECS; Settings → Chats is two pointer rows. Pairs with the pipeline items; Settings → Chats is the natural, empty home. |
| Auto-download rules per network (Wi-Fi / mobile / roaming, by type and size) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram | No auto-download logic anywhere. The bubble already loads only the 512 px thumb; the PWA equivalent is tap-to-load full-res behind the blurhash/thumb when navigator.connection.saveData or cellular. |
| Data saver / "use less cellular data" for media | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, Facebook, X | No data-saver setting. The 512 px WebP thumb and blurhash already exist; the setting just needs to route to them. |
| Per-chat Media / Links / Files tab | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | `ChatInfoSheet.tsx` sections are theme, wallpaper, background, sound, nickname, disappearing, members; no media grid and no server route filtering attachments by conversation. Media others sent me is reachable only by scrolling; My gallery lists only my own uploads. |
| Paste / drag-and-drop media into the composer (desktop) | standard | P1 | Discord, Telegram, WhatsApp Web, Messenger | No onPaste, onDrop or dataTransfer in Composer.tsx. Desktop users must use the picker for every screenshot. |
| Incoming-call OS notification when the app tab is open but in the background (socket alive) | standard | P1 | Every native caller; WhatsApp Web and Telegram Web post a local notification | `service.ts:129` skips the ring push for anyone with a live socket; nothing calls showNotification or new Notification() when document.hidden (sw.js is the only showNotification caller). The callee then gets only the in-app overlay and a WebAudio ringer that needs a prior user gesture (`sounds.ts:372-384`), so a backgrounded tab rings silently and the call goes to missed. The overlap case is the common one on phones. |
| User-facing noise-suppression toggle / voice isolation | standard | P2 | Telegram, Messenger, Discord (Krisp), TikTok | getMedia is `{audio: true}` (`webrtc.ts:118-121`), so the app rides the browser's default AEC/NS/AGC and exposes none of the constraints. Exposing them is trivial; Krisp-grade ML suppression is not. Parity polish. |
| Per-peer local volume / locally mute one participant | standard | P2 | Discord | PillAudioSink renders one audio element per peer with no gain control; no per-tile menu. Useful once group calls are common. |
| Camera background blur / virtual or animated call backgrounds | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Messenger, Discord, Telegram | No canvas.captureStream / MediaStreamTrackProcessor / blur in apps/web; PSYCHOLOGY does not forbid them. Needs an in-browser segmentation model, heavy for a zero-new-dependency rule. Parity polish. |
| Raise hand in group calls | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, X Spaces | Not in CallOverlay or webrtc.ts. Low value at an 8-person cap; matters once SFU calls exist. |
| In-call emoji reactions | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, X Spaces, Messenger (limited) | No reaction frame among the call schemas. Ephemeral WS frame; fits golden rule 1 cleanly. |
| Pre-join lobby (camera/mic preview, join muted) | standard | P2 | Signal, Messenger call links | `webrtc.ts:555` joinCall goes straight in; the Settings self-test is the only preview and is not on the join path. Group joiners get no chance to check their camera or mute first. |
| Host/admin controls in group calls (mute or remove a participant, mute all) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord, X Spaces | `calls/routes.ts` has only self end/leave/reject; no moderator endpoints. Group admins already exist; calls do not honour them. |
| Call links (shareable, join from browser, approve-joiners lobby) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Signal, Messenger, Telegram | No link/token route; join requires an authenticated conversation participant. Lower value in an accounts-only community app; the real ask underneath is "invite someone to this call". |
| Scheduled calls (calendar entry + reminder) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram Live scheduling | Nothing in calls/routes.ts or schema.ts; no call field on events. Could ride the existing events/board feature rather than a new object. |
| Video messages / hold-to-record video clips (round video) | standard | P2 | Telegram (round video), Bumble (video clips), Snapchat | MediaRecorder is audio-only (`Composer.tsx:397-404`). Voice-note infra exists; video is the same MediaRecorder with a camera track. |
| Adjustments (brightness, contrast, warmth, saturation, vignette) | standard | P2 | Instagram, Telegram, TikTok | Nothing in ImageEditSheet. Follows filters. |
| Video mute-audio / cover-frame (thumbnail) selection | standard | P2 | Telegram, X, Reddit | `jobs/media.ts:169` picks the poster automatically (ffmpeg thumbnail=90); no user choice. Follows trim. |
| Voice-note continue-into-next / background play | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram | AudioBubble plays one bubble; no queue or media-session controls. Polish. |
| Voice-note transcription | standard | P2 | WhatsApp; Bumble/Hinge explicitly lack it | No transcript field in AttachmentSchema. Self-hosted whisper would be ethos-compatible; accessibility value. |
| Storage usage per chat, cache size limit, keep media for N days | standard | P2 | Telegram | No storage settings page; disappearing messages exist but no media-retention control. Self-hosted admins will ask for this before users do. |
| Story creative tools: text-only / colour-background stories, mention, link and poll stickers | standard | P2 | Instagram, Snapchat, WhatsApp, Facebook | `StoriesStrip.tsx:213` accepts only image/*, mp4, webm; no text-story mode; SPECS v1.0b covers audiences and reactions only. Music is excluded by rule 7; the rest is ordinary UI work. |
| Auto-captions / subtitle upload for video | standard | P2 | Facebook, X, TikTok | No caption track field in AttachmentSchema. Accessibility; auto needs self-hosted ASR, but SRT upload is just a file. |
| See who is already in a group call before joining (faces/names on the Join chip) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Messenger, Telegram (participant avatars on the join banner) | `ChatView.tsx:2068-2079` renders icon + "Join" only; the live Call DTO already carries participant_ids (`packages/shared/src/api.ts:2964`) but the chip never reads it. PSYCHOLOGY allows named faces, so this is buildable now. |
| Keep the screen awake during a call (Screen Wake Lock) | standard | P2 | Every native caller | No wakeLock anywhere in shared, server or web. A phone will dim and lock mid-video-call. |
| Multi-photo album bubble (grouped grid inside one chat message) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, iMessage | `ChatView.tsx:1506-1515` renders attachments as a vertical sequence of full-width tiles; only the feed has a 1-4 grid; chat.module.css has no album layout. |
| View-once / per-media disappearing photos | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Snapchat | No view_once / viewOnce anywhere; AttachmentSchema has no once/expiry field. Only the per-conversation disappear_ttl_s exists. Table stakes for a private messenger. |
| Silence the ringtone without declining an incoming call | standard | P2 | Phones, WhatsApp | IncomingRing (`CallOverlay.tsx:113-133`) has exactly Decline and Accept; the only way to stop the ringer (`sounds.ts:356-363` evaluate) is to decline, which for a DM is a durable POST /reject the caller sees as "Call declined." |
| Ongoing-call indicator on the chat-list row | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | store.liveCalls (`store.ts:944-959`) exists but only ChatView and `app/c/[id]/page.tsx` read it; ConversationListView, TwoPane and Tabs never do. A group call running in a chat you are not looking at is invisible from the list. |
| Voice-note played / unplayed indicator (persisted) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp (blue/green mic) | `AudioBubble.tsx:77, 112` playedBars is in-session progress only; AttachmentSchema has no played field. Progress resets on remount; nothing marks a note as listened or signals it to the sender. |
| Story video length cap / trim (e.g. 15-60 s) | standard | P2 | Instagram, WhatsApp | `stories/routes.ts:187` copies durationMs with no check; story constants have no duration; only MAX_VIDEO_BYTES 100 MB. A multi-minute mp4 can be posted as a story and the viewer plays it whole. Also a feed-pacing concern. |
| Voice rooms / live audio spaces / drop-in voice channels | advanced | P2 | X Spaces, Discord, Telegram, WhatsApp; retired on Messenger and Reddit | No code; ADR-020:156 is a one-line aside about "LiveKit-era public audio rooms". Needs the SFU first, but a local community board is exactly where ambient audio rooms earn their keep, more than filters would. |
| Co-watch self-hosted uploaded videos (no third-party player) | advanced | P2 | No benchmark; demanded by CLAUDE.md rule 7 | `gateway.ts:734` extractYoutubeVideoId rejects anything else; Caddyfile:26-36 carves CSP for youtube.com/iframe_api. The single third-party runtime script in a "no third-party SaaS" product, while the app already range-serves MP4. |
| Ring specific members only in a group call | standard | P3 | Discord | call.invite fans to every other active participant (`ws.ts:359-363`). Niche. |
| Call quality rating prompt after a call | standard | P3 | Telegram | EndCard shows the outcome only. The self-test page covers most of the diagnostic value. |
| Flip / mirror image | standard | P3 | Telegram, Signal | Not in ImageEditSheet. Trivial to add; low demand. |
| Photo quality / content checks on upload (face detection, blur rejection, nudity detection) | standard | P3 | Bumble, Hinge | `pipeline.ts` sniffs magic bytes and decodes only; no classifier. Dating-app pattern; only a self-hosted NSFW check for public board/feed uploads fits the ethos. |
| Media Session / headset-button handlers (answer or hang up from earbuds, lock-screen voice-note controls) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, every native caller | No mediaSession usage anywhere; `AudioBubble.tsx:39-41` only listens for timeupdate/ended/pause/play. |
| Group-call participant roster sheet (who is on the call, tap to profile) | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Signal, Discord, Messenger | No roster in apps/web; CallOverlay exposes only the tile grid and the mute/camera/flip/end bar, no participants list or overflow menu. |
| Media viewer shows sender, time and caption with jump-to-message | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram DMs | MediaLightbox props are exactly { src, alt, originalUrl?, onClose } (`MediaLightbox.tsx:16-27`); no message, sender or timestamp reaches the viewer, so no metadata strip and no way back to the message row. |
| Voice activity vs push-to-talk, input sensitivity, deafen | advanced | P3 | Discord | Not in webrtc.ts or CallOverlay. Discord-only pattern; irrelevant at an 8-person mesh. |
| AR filters / touch-up / low-light / avatar-as-video in calls | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat (Lenses) | Not found. Lens platforms; arguably off-ethos for an authentic-faces product, but not banned anywhere. |
| Call recording / record video chat | advanced | P3 | Telegram, X Spaces | MediaRecorder is used for voice notes only. Fine for a privacy-first product, but it is not a stated decision; write it down. |
| Live video broadcasting (guests/co-hosts, replays, scheduling, moderation) | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, X, Telegram RTMP | No code; needs the SFU. Any version must ship without viewer counts (PSYCHOLOGY rule 3). |
| Soundboard / sound effects in voice | advanced | P3 | Discord | Not found. Discord-only. |
| Camera tools (flash, zoom, timer, grid, dual camera, BTS buffer) | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, BeReal, Telegram | No in-app camera exists. Only meaningful after the camera capture item ships. |
| Reels-style video editor (speed, split, transitions, green screen, voiceover, templates) | advanced | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, Snapchat Director Mode | Not found. Creator-tools layer; off-ethos for a calm community app. |
| AI image generation / AI effects in camera | advanced | P3 | Snapchat, TikTok, X (Grok) | Not found. Would need a self-hosted model; no pressure that matters here. |
| Save-destination preference / camera-roll cloud backup | advanced | P3 | Snapchat | Gallery is server-side only; no device-save preference. Niche. |
| Voice / video prompts on profiles | advanced | P3 | Hinge | Not in users/routes.ts or the profile schema. Dating pattern; the voice-note infra exists if the owner wants it. |
| Watch-together co-host / pass control / shared queue | advanced | P3 | Discord (shared Activity controls) | `gateway.ts:783, 813`: watch.sync returns watch_not_host for anyone but the host; watch.stop is host-only; frames carry a single video_id and host, no queue, no handoff frame. |

### Planned already

- Calls tab / call history screen (All / Missed, durations, call back from row) — basic, P0. The server half exists (GET /api/conversations/:id/calls at `calls/routes.ts:271`; API.md §1.42); the UI is the line "Call history is coming soon." at `settings/calls/page.tsx:258`. FEATURES #102 (line 150), SPECS.md:236-238. On the roadmap, so it is not counted as Missing, but shipping placeholder text in Settings to real users is itself a smell: either hide the page until the pack lands or ship the list.
- Clear / delete own call history rows — standard, P1. SPECS §v1.0c #102 ("owner-only delete of own history rows"); no DELETE route yet. Depends on the history UI landing first.
- Mid-call switch audio → video (with consent) and instant downgrade — standard, P1. FEATURES #104, SPECS.md:248-250. Impossible by construction today (`webrtc.ts:117-121` requests video:false for audio calls; toggleCam bails unless kind==='video'), but the renegotiation path (replaceTrack + perfect negotiation) already exists.
- Native OS call integration (CallKit / lock-screen incoming call / recents) — standard, P2. "Native wrappers" in the CLAUDE.md/FEATURES backlog (#56). Structural: a closed iOS PWA cannot ring unless installed with push granted; the docs admit the iPhone call UX is weaker.
- Listen together (synced audio) — advanced, P3. FEATURES #51 📋; SPECS standing decision "listen-together deferred".
- Story voice reactions (≤15 s audio reply) — advanced, P3. FEATURES #99 📋; SPECS §v1.0b.

### Deliberately not built

- Live monetisation (gifts, coins, subscriptions, badges, battles) — CLAUDE.md rule 7 "Money never moves through the app"; PSYCHOLOGY: no variable-reward mechanics or leaderboards. Right call. Nothing about a local community board benefits from a tipping economy.
- Viewer counts / "X people watching" on streams, calls or watch sessions — PSYCHOLOGY rule 3 (no public numeric scores). `WatchTogetherSheet.tsx:245-257` shows named avatar chips instead. Right call, and the implementation already honours it.
- Licensed music / sounds library on stories and videos — rule 7 (no third-party SaaS, all media user-generated). Right call. Note that a user-uploaded audio track on a story would be allowed and is not built.
- Third-party GIF search (Giphy / Tenor) — rule 7 names Giphy explicitly; `pipeline.ts:205-218` keeps user-uploaded GIFs animated. Right call. The ethos-compatible substitute, a self-hosted "saved GIFs" shelf, does not exist yet and should.
- Import photos from Instagram / Facebook — rule 7. Right call; nobody will miss it.
- Vertical swipe full-screen video feed — PSYCHOLOGY: finite feeds with a hard end, no variable-reward mechanics; CLAUDE.md rule 6. Right call, and the single most important ban in this area. Do not let "video attachments on posts" grow into Reels through the back door.
- Click-to-call business ads / paid calling tiers — rule 7 (no payments). Right call.
- A declined DM call leaves no system message — API.md:1692 documents it: "No system message is posted (a decline is not an archive entry — the liking-gap rule; the caller's UI just stops)", the same rule that makes connection declines silent (API.md:1243, PSYCHOLOGY §9). Group declines are ephemeral WS `call.reject` frames by the same logic. This is a design choice, not a bug, and the instinct is sound: a durable "declined you" row in the chat is a rejection ping. It is only half-applied, though: the caller's EndCard already says "Call declined." in the moment (`CallOverlay.tsx:930-931`, `webrtc.ts:817, 928`), so the rejection is visible live and hidden only from the archive. Revisit as one decision: either soften the live copy to "No answer." so a decline is indistinguishable from a timeout, or accept that the caller knows and let the history screen (planned) show "Declined" to the caller only. Whichever way, the inline call-ended work above should not add a call_declined event.

Two things are banned in practice but not on paper, and should be written down either way: call recording (fine to refuse, but say so in PSYCHOLOGY.md or SPECS.md), and photo filters/AR lenses (if "authentic faces" is the reason, state it; if not, filters are a P1 gap). The YouTube iframe in watch-together is the opposite case: a declared rule (no third-party runtime) that the code quietly breaks. Either amend rule 7 to carve out YouTube explicitly or build the self-hosted co-watch.

### How it looks

The call overlay, incoming ring, floating pill and end card were not in the captured screen set; every statement about them in this section comes from `CallOverlay.tsx`, `webrtc.ts` and `sounds.ts`, not from a screenshot. The bullets below cover only captured screens.

- Composer "+" sheet (phone and desktop chat plus-menu captures; `Composer.tsx:519-527`): seven tiles (Photo, Doodle, Vibe check, Game, Puzzle, Location, Schedule) with no Camera, no Gallery/File split, no Poll and no Watch together; on desktop the grid opens as a full-width bottom sheet instead of a popover on the button, and there is no paste or drag-and-drop. WhatsApp's attach menu is Document, Camera, Gallery, Audio, Location, Contact, Poll; Telegram opens a gallery preview strip. Fix: Camera (capture attribute), Gallery, File, Poll and Watch together tiles in a 4-column grid; anchor a 2-column popover above the "+" on desktop; add onPaste and onDrop with a "Drop to send" overlay.
- Settings → Calls (settings calls capture; `settings/calls/page.tsx:243-258`): one self-test card and a bare line "Call history is coming soon." Ringtone and vibrate-for-calls live under Notifications. WhatsApp never ships a settings page that announces unshipped features; Telegram keeps call sounds under Notifications → Calls and shows no placeholders. Fix: delete the line, move Ringtone/Vibrate here under a Sounds group, and ship the /calls list it promises.
- Chat settings sheet (chat settings sheet capture; `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`): opens on nine theme swatches and seven wallpapers; no Media/Links/Files, no call shortcuts, no identity row. WhatsApp contact info is hero, Audio/Video buttons, media/links/docs, mute, disappearing, wallpaper far down. Fix: identity hero with voice/video buttons, then Mute, Media & links, Disappearing, then Appearance collapsed.
- Feed post images (feed list capture; `FeedView.tsx:1013-1016`): tapping a photo opens the raw proxy URL in a new browser tab, which throws an installed PWA out to Safari/Chrome; multi-image posts have no swipe; every image renders alt=""; videos are inline with no tap-to-fullscreen. Instagram, Threads, X and BeReal open media in place with pinch-zoom and swipe. Fix: wire MediaLightbox with an items array and index; add an alt field to ImageEditSheet.
- Story add sheet (story add capture; `StoriesStrip.tsx:250-307`): "📷 Pick a photo or video" uses an emoji as the icon, the disabled "Post story" reads as an enabled secondary button, the sheet has no grabber, and there is no text-only story mode. Instagram's story composer is camera-first with a text mode one tap away. Fix: stroke icon, keep the accent hue at 40% for disabled, grabber and detents, add a text/colour-background mode.
- Feed composer media tile (feed compose capture; `FeedView.tsx:835-845`, `social.module.css:576-581`): the 86×86 dashed "Add images" tile is always visible before anything is attached, where Instagram, Threads and X show small photo/poll icons and only show thumbnails after selection. Fix: collapse to a toolbar row (photo, place, audience) under the textarea; thumb strip appears only after a pick.
- Public profile action row (public profile capture; `app/u/[username]`): no voice or video call buttons although calls exist; "Friends" and "Message" are equal-weight outline pills next to a 👻 emoji orb. WhatsApp contact info puts Audio/Video icons directly under the name. Fix: add call icon buttons, make Message the filled primary.
- Stories strip on Chats and nothing on Feed (chats list and feed captures; `social.module.css:203-207`): the strip reserves a full row of the chat list for a lone dashed "Your story" ring when nobody has a story, and the Feed tab, where a new user looks for stories first, has no stories entry at all. Instagram, Facebook and Snapchat head the feed with stories; Telegram collapses the bar on scroll. Fix: a single-line chip when there are zero friend stories, hide while scrolled; render StoriesStrip at the top of Feed.
- Gallery empty state (gallery capture; `gallery/page.tsx:107-112, 181-184`): the empty card and the page footnote both tell the user that stories save themselves when they expire, and there is nothing to tap. Fix: one sentence plus "Add a story" and "Send a photo" buttons.
- Ringer vibrate call on every store change (from code, not a capture; `sounds.ts:309-325, 356-363, 389`): `store.subscribe(evaluate)` runs `evaluate()` on every store change, and whenever no call is incoming it calls `stopRinger()` → `stopVibrate()` → `navigator.vibrate(0)` unconditionally. The pattern vibration itself (`vibrateOnce`) does check `prefs.vibrate`, but neither path checks prefers-reduced-motion, and the cancel ignores the preference entirely. `vibrate(0)` cancels rather than buzzes, so the user-visible symptom is Chrome's "Blocked call to navigator.vibrate" intervention warnings on ordinary page loads with no call in progress, not a buzzing phone. WhatsApp Web never touches the API outside a ring. Fix: track whether a vibration was started and only cancel then; gate both calls behind the preference.
- iOS ring reality (settings notifications capture; `settings/notifications/page.tsx:38-54`, `settings/calls/page.tsx`): the Notifications page already branches its "blocked" copy by user agent, including an iPhone line that says to add Bored Chat to the Home Screen; the Calls page says nothing, so a user who never hits the blocked state is never told that an iPhone cannot ring for a call unless the PWA is installed with push granted. Telegram Web and Instagram web show platform-specific install guidance up front. Fix: the same UA-detected line on the Calls page, and a one-time "Install Bored Chat" card after the first conversation.

### If I had one week

1. Days 1-2, make calls leave a trace. In `calls/service.ts`, write a system message on the terminal transitions that are not declines, the way call_missed already does: call_ended with duration_s (and call_failed), with the call_ref snapshot; leave rejects silent per API.md:1692 and settle the EndCard copy question one way or the other. Render the new events in ChatView beside the existing Call back pill. Add GET /api/calls (my calls across conversations, cursor-paged, reusing the per-conversation query) and a /calls page with All and Missed filters and tap-to-call-back; delete the "coming soon" line. Read participant_ids on the Join chip to show faces, and read store.liveCalls in ConversationListView for a live-call chip on the row.
2. Day 3, fix the two P0 media basics. Composer: add a Camera tile backed by `<input accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment">`, split Photo into Gallery and File, add Poll and Watch together tiles, and add onPaste/onDrop on desktop. MediaLightbox: accept `items[]` plus a start index with swipe and arrow keys; pass all attachments from ChatView, FeedView post and comment images, and the gallery. Lay out 2-4 chat attachments as an album grid.
3. Days 4-5, stop shipping full-size media. Client: canvas downscale to a 2048 px long edge in `imageUpload.ts` behind a per-send "Send original" toggle; server: max-dimension cap in `pipeline.ts` next to the thumb resize. Video: one ffmpeg leg in `jobs/media.ts` (`-vf scale=-2:1080 -c:v libx264 -crf 23 -preset veryfast -c:a aac`) for anything over 1080p or above a bitrate threshold, keep the remux for the rest. Switch uploads to XHR for progress, cancel and retry. Put Upload quality (Standard/HD) and Data saver (tap-to-load full-res on cellular or saveData) on the empty Settings → Chats page.
4. Day 6, four small call frames and two platform calls. Relay a call.media {mic, cam} frame for the remote mute indicator; send a busy reject reason so the caller sees "On another call" and no missed-call message is written; add a Silence button to IncomingRing; post a service-worker notification on call.invite when document.hidden; hold a Screen Wake Lock for the duration of a call. All ephemeral, all inside golden rule 1.
5. Day 7, the renegotiation payoffs. Audio-to-video upgrade with a consent frame (addTrack + the existing perfect-negotiation path), screen share on desktop via getDisplayMedia + replaceTrack, and voice-note lock + listen-back in Composer (hold recorder.onstop output in an AudioBubble preview with Send and Delete instead of sending on release).


---

## UI, design system and navigation

**Verdict.** The shell is right and the skin is unfinished. Of the 166 UI expectations checked, 40 are fully met, 45 are half-built, 71 are missing, 4 are planned and 6 are deliberately banned. Basic-tier coverage is **27/70 = 38.6%** (27 basic items with status *has*, out of 72 basic items minus 2 banned); standard-tier coverage is **13/76 = 17.1%** (13 of 80 minus 4 banned); no advanced item is complete (0/14, 3 partial). The four-tab bar, desktop rail plus two-pane chats, portaled bottom sheets, toasts, the z-index ladder, reduced-motion handling and the serif/sans brand type are all at or above the WhatsApp/Telegram/Signal bar. Everything one layer down is not: the light theme that every screenshot in this audit shows is, by `apps/web/app/globals.css`'s own comment, "not designed in the Royal pass — equivalents derived" (9 light-scheme blocks across 5 CSS files against ~16,000 lines of CSS), and in it incoming chat bubbles (`--bubble-in #e8ecf5` on `--chat-bg #eef0f6`) sit at **1.04:1** — they are not bubbles, they are loose text; tab-bar labels are 10.5px `--inactive` (`#8a93a6` on `#f4f6fb` = **2.85:1** light, `#6d7282` on `#12141c` = **3.83:1** dark, both failing WCAG AA); the helper-text token `--faint` is **2.13:1** on white (10 CSS usages); the tic-tac-toe "Your turn" cue is `--accent` on the outgoing bubble, **1.15:1** in light mode. There is no Dark/Light/System toggle (`data-theme` appears nowhere), zero `rem` units so OS text scaling does nothing, no skeletons, no `error.tsx`/`not-found.tsx`/`loading.tsx`, no day dividers or draft persistence in chat, no pin/mute/archive on the chat list, and the Chats-tab magnifier is a `<Link href="/people">`. The tab bar vanishes on `/u/[username]`, `/new`, `/anon`, `/things`, `/puzzle`, `/welcome`, the `/settings` hub and every `/settings/*` sub-page (only `/`, `/feed`, `/local`, `/people`, `/connections`, `/gallery` and `/settings/profile` mount `TabPage`/`TwoPane`), so the user loses the "where am I" anchor exactly on lateral jumps. Inside an otherwise fully custom Portal/sheet system there are **15 native `window.confirm`/`window.alert` call sites across 12 files** (`ChatView.tsx` ×2, `ListingDetailSheet.tsx` ×2, `AnonThreadView.tsx` ×2, `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`, `FeedView.tsx`, `BeaconDetailSheet.tsx`, `ChannelMembersSheet.tsx`, `DiscoverySection.tsx`, `StoryCanvas.tsx`, `app/connections/page.tsx`, `app/u/[username]/page.tsx`, `app/admin/users/page.tsx`). None of this needs architecture; all of it needs finishing. (The inventory lists the offline app-shell fallback twice, once per tier; numbers above are from the raw list.)

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bottom tab bar on mobile (4 tabs: Chats, Local, Feed, Profile) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram iOS, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Threads, Discord, Reddit, Nextdoor, Hinge +6 | `components/Tabs.tsx`; safe-area padding, press feedback, `aria-current`. Fine where it is mounted. |
| Tab badges as dots, not counts | basic | Snapchat, Tinder, Discord, WhatsApp, Instagram, X | Dots by PSYCHOLOGY rule 3; numeric unread stays on chat rows. Acceptable deviation. |
| Top app bar: title + up to 3 icon actions + overflow menu | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, Reddit, Nextdoor, Instagram | `topbar.module.css`; overflow is `role=menu` with Escape, portaled. Local's only action is a red "SOS" badge nobody can decode. |
| Feed lane switcher (Friends / Discover) | basic | X, Threads, TikTok, Reddit, Instagram, Nextdoor | `FeedView.tsx` `role=radiogroup`. No custom feeds by design. |
| Profile/settings entry reachable from every tab | basic | Snapchat, Signal, Reddit, X, Instagram, Threads, Hinge +3 | Profile tab + Settings row; equivalent to avatar-in-top-bar. |
| Settings hub with grouped categories and user card | basic | Telegram, TikTok, Tinder, Hinge, BeReal, Snapchat, Instagram, X | `app/settings/page.tsx`; seven rows with icons, chevrons, value hint. Two of seven rows are hollow (see Settings). |
| Registration form with validation, show-password, autocomplete | basic | All 17 reference apps | `app/register/page.tsx`; labels, `autoComplete`, eye toggle; mascot and confetti. Most polished screen in the app. |
| Permission priming before the OS notification prompt | basic | Signal, WhatsApp, Instagram, Tinder, Bumble, Meetup, Luma | `PushExplainer.tsx`; one-time, re-askable from Settings. |
| In-app "What's new" release cards | standard | Signal, Discord, BeReal, Telegram | `WhatsNewCard.tsx`, server-backed seen cursor. Better than most. |
| Hard end-of-feed "all caught up" card | standard | Instagram, Threads, Reddit | `FeedView.tsx` endCard `role=status`; product rule, not a nicety. |
| Spinner primitive and inline loading text | basic | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, X, Meetup | `globals.css .spinner`, reduced-motion safe, ~95 usages. |

| Screen-reader labels on icon buttons, lists, menus, tabs, radios | standard | All 17 (uneven) | 436 `aria-label`s, 51 `role=dialog`, radio/radiogroup, menu/menuitem, switch. Broad and mostly correct. |

| Accessible forms: labels, autocomplete tokens, 16px inputs | basic | All | No iOS zoom-stick. Correct. |
| Swipe-to-reply on bubbles | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Messenger, Snapchat, TikTok, Discord | `ChatView.tsx` (48px threshold, `touch-action: pan-y`). Correct. |
| Pinch-zoom, pan, double-tap in the media viewer | basic | WhatsApp, Instagram, TikTok, X, Telegram | `MediaLightbox.tsx`. Good. |
| Story viewer gestures: tap zones, hold-to-pause, keyboard | basic | Instagram, Snapchat, WhatsApp, Facebook, Telegram | `StoryViewer.tsx`. No auto-advance between authors by rule; no horizontal swipe between authors either. |
| Browser/OS swipe-back left to the platform; fixed-parent Back links | basic | All native apps; web apps rely on the browser | 24 `aria-label="Back"` hard links, 0 `router.back()`. Correct for deep links, wrong for lateral entries (see Shell). |
| Self-hosted brand typography (Marcellus + Instrument Sans) | standard | Instagram Sans, TikTok Sans, Chirp, gg sans, Hinge, Bumble | `globals.css @font-face`, `public/fonts/*.woff2`, `font-display: swap`. Distinctive. |
| Mascot / brand illustration system for auth and empty states | standard | Discord, Reddit, Snapchat, Hinge, Tinder | `Mascot.tsx`, `AuthScene.tsx`, `EmptyDoodle`. Charming where used (11 files). |

| Hover-only affordances gated by pointer capability | standard | Discord, Telegram Web, WhatsApp Web | `@media (hover: none)` hides `.reactBtn`. Correct — but see touch-only action access under Conversation. |
