| Drag-and-drop / paste-from-clipboard upload (desktop) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp Web, Telegram Web, Discord, Slack | 0 `onDrop`/`onPaste` in `Composer.tsx`/`FeedView.tsx`; pasted screenshots are the most common desktop share |
| Right-click context menu on messages (desktop) | standard | P2 | Telegram Web, Discord, WhatsApp Web | 0 `onContextMenu`; the overlay exists, only the trigger is missing |
| Browser/Android Back closes sheets and viewers | standard | P2 | All native and most PWA chat apps | 0 `popstate`/`pushState`; hardware Back leaves the page with the composer state |
| Scroll-position memory across tabs and on return from detail | standard | P2 | Instagram, X, WhatsApp | Tabs are plain Links that remount at the top; finite feeds make losing your place worse |
| In-app activity inbox (mentions, replies, requests) | standard | P2 | Instagram, X, Threads, Reddit, Facebook | Requests live behind the Chats ··· popover; nothing collects mentions or replies |
| Screen Wake Lock during calls and stories | advanced | P2 | Native call apps | 0 `wakeLock`; a two-line, self-hosted-safe fix |
| PWA manifest `shortcuts` (New chat, Feed, Local) | advanced | P2 | Snapchat, BeReal, Instagram, WhatsApp (widgets); X/Threads web (shortcuts) | 10-line win; native widgets out of scope |
| Settings search box | standard | P3 | Facebook, X, Discord | Seven categories |
| Side drawer / account menu from avatar | standard | P3 | X, Reddit, Telegram Android, Facebook, Discord | The ··· popover does the same job for a 4-tab app |
| Multi-account switcher | standard | P3 | Telegram, Facebook, X, Instagram, Reddit | Single-identity self-hosted app |
| Extra dark variants (OLED / dim) and accent picker | standard | P3 | X, Discord, Telegram, Signal | Polish after the toggle exists |
| Density setting | standard | P3 | Discord, Telegram, Signal | Not day-one |
| Double-tap to react | standard | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, Threads, Telegram | Minor |
| Quick switcher (Ctrl+K) | standard | P3 | Discord, Telegram/Signal Desktop | Depends on global search |
| Hover cards for users (web) | standard | P3 | Reddit, X, Facebook, Threads, Discord | Tap opens the full profile |
| Storage usage / clear local cache setting | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | IndexedDB message tails with no purge control |
| Landscape / orientation handling | standard | P3 | All store-grade apps | No manifest `orientation`, no landscape rules for calls or lightbox |
| In-app browser / open-in-external toggle | standard | P3 | Telegram, TikTok, X, Threads, Instagram | Not applicable to a PWA |
| GIF search / sticker packs | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord, WhatsApp, Messenger, Signal | Third-party GIF search forbidden by rule 7; only self-hosted packs would fit |
| Markdown / formatting toolbar | standard | P3 | Discord, Telegram, WhatsApp, Reddit | Chat-first; plain text is defensible |
| Spoiler / tap-to-reveal text | standard | P3 | Telegram, Discord | Niche |
| Contacts import | basic | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok +5 | Replaced by invite links and local suggestions on purpose; keep it missing |
| Alternate app icons | advanced | P3 | Telegram Premium, Signal, Snapchat+, Reddit Premium | Vanity |
| Auto-night by schedule | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Needs the toggle first |
| Custom / shareable theme editor | advanced | P3 | Telegram | Skip |
| Automatic alt text / captions | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, Threads, TikTok | Needs a model service; out of scope |
| Auto-translate | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook, Reddit, X, TikTok, Telegram | Out of scope |
| Camera-first launch | advanced | P3 | Snapchat | Irrelevant |
| Multi-column deck layout | advanced | P3 | Threads web, X Pro | Not this product |

### Planned already

- **Global / unified search with type filters** (standard, P1) — `docs/SPECS.md` "Global search (108)", `FEATURES.md #108`; no `app/search` route yet. Until it lands, the Chats magnifier should at least filter the loaded list.
- **UI localisation** (standard, P1) — backlog in `FEATURES.md #58`; `<html lang="en">` fixed, all copy inline in ~90 component files. RTL (above) is moot until this exists.
- **Restore / transfer backup during onboarding** (standard, P2) — backlog; server-side accounts make it mostly moot.
- **Native desktop / iPad / Windows wrappers** (advanced, P3) — PWA is the strategy.

### Deliberately not built

| Feature | Rule | Agree? |
|---|---|---|
| Pull-to-refresh | PSYCHOLOGY.md "Never build" line 34; replaced by an explicit "Check for new posts" icon | Yes. The 76px outlined refresh square it was replaced with is the wrong *shape* (see Feed), but the decision is right. |
| Infinite scroll / auto-advancing stories / prefetch-next feeds | "Never build" line 35; finite lanes with an end card, manual "Load older" | Yes. The end card needs a next step (see Feed), not more feed. |
| Swipe-to-reject / card-stack discovery | `DiscoverySection.tsx` "no swipe-reject mechanic — wave, or just scroll on"; ADR-015; owner's "invisible single waves" | Yes. |
| Time-pressure UI (timers, expiry rings, streak glyphs) | "Never build" lines 33–36 | Yes — with one caveat: the chat list's unread badge pulses forever (`list.module.css:169 pulseGlow 2.4s infinite`), which is the same attention hook by another name. Still it once. |
| Paywall / upsell / premium badges | CLAUDE.md "Money never moves through the app"; SPECS.md "no payments" | Yes. |
| Sponsored cards / ads | Rule 7 self-hosted ethos | Yes. |

### How it looks, screen by screen

Strengths, stated once: the login/register pair (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`) and the feed end card are the two best-executed moments and would not look out of place next to Hinge; the Settings hub (`phone-settings.png`) is a textbook grouped list; the copy voice is short, honest and consistent across 50+ screens ("The feed has a bottom on purpose", "No one is notified when it fizzles"); the psychology rules are visibly in the pixels (named faces, hard feed end, reciprocal receipts without timestamps); custom controls carry real semantics (`role=radio/switch/menu/grid`); the chat two-pane is the right WhatsApp-Web shape; reduced motion is a contract, not a footnote.

#### Shell: tab bar, top bars, Back

- **[major] The tab bar is not in the layout; it is per-page.** `TabPage`/`TwoPane` are mounted by seven routes. `/u/[username]` (`phone-profile-arjun.png`), `/new` (`phone-new.png`), `/things` (`phone-things.png`), `/puzzle` (`phone-puzzle.png`), `/anon`, `/welcome`, the `/settings` hub (`phone-settings.png`) and all its sub-pages render without it. Tap an author from Feed and you are on a page whose only exit is a grey back orb hard-linked to `/` (`app/u/[username]/page.tsx:252`). Instagram keeps the bar on every profile and on Settings; WhatsApp hides it only inside a chat. Fix: mount `Tabs` in the authed layout, hide it only for `/c/[id]`, viewers and calls.
- **[major] Back lies.** 24 hard-coded Back links, 0 `router.back()`. Find people's Back goes to `/connections` (`app/people/page.tsx:35`) even when you arrived from the Chats magnifier; the public profile's Back goes to Chats from anywhere. Fix: `router.back()` with a logical-parent fallback when `history.length === 1`.
- **[major] Primary nav labels fail AA.** `.tab` is 10.5px/600 in `--inactive` (`social.module.css:29–30`): 2.85:1 light, 3.83:1 dark; the same token drives the profile segment tabs (`phone-settings-profile.png` POSTS/GALLERY/INTERESTS) and the grid/list toggle. Active state only changes label weight and colour, so the label reflows. iOS uses 10pt but at ≥4.5:1; Instagram drops labels and fills the icon. Fix: inactive → `--muted`, label 11–12px, filled icon for active, constant weight.
- **[major] Top bars differ per tab.** Chats and Local are titled by name, Feed by the "bored." wordmark, Profile by "Profile"; Local's bar is a single line (title + area), the others two; Feed then stacks lanes, a layout toggle and a boxed refresh before the first post (`phone-feed.png`). The Feed glyph (two stacked rounded rectangles) reads as "database". Fix: one fixed-height topbar token with a text title on all four tabs; wordmark only on auth/about/share pages.
- **[major] Icons are not one set.** Inline SVG stroke widths range 1.1–2.6 (10 distinct values; 99× 1.7, 10× 1.5, 7× 1.8); `ChevronLeft` is re-declared per settings page; Back is a chevron on Settings/Connections/New chat, an arrow on `/things` and `/puzzle`, a grey orb on profiles. 297 emoji characters in 74 `.tsx` files, including as chrome: `<h1>🧩 Daily puzzle</h1>` (`puzzle/page.tsx:191`), `<h1>✨ Things you can do</h1>` (`things/page.tsx:39`), `<h1>🎭 Anonymous</h1>` (`AnonThreadView.tsx:151`), 📣/👻/🛟 row prefixes in the chat list, ⏳ chip in chat, 🫧 end card, 📍 "Add a place", 🔑 "Add a passkey", 👻 as a profile button. Emoji render as Noto on Android and Apple on iOS, outside your control, and clash with the monochrome palette. Instagram and Hinge: zero emoji in chrome. Fix: one `<Icon>` component (24px grid, 1.75 stroke), lint rule against inline `<svg>` outside it, emoji only in user content, reactions and vibe chips.
- **[major] Hit areas.** `.iconBtn` 38×38, `.orbBtn` 36×36, `.cardKebab` 28×28, `.layoutBtn` 34×26; "+ LIST SOMETHING", "+ FIND" and "Copy" are 11.5px uppercase text with ~2px vertical padding. HIG 44pt, Material 48dp; WhatsApp/Instagram header icons are 44–48px squares. Fix: `::before { inset: -8px }` on every icon control; promote the three text CTAs to 36–40px pills.
- **[major] Light theme is derived, and it is what most phones show.** `globals.css:83` says so. Beyond the bubble problem (next section): `--panel-2` chips on white cards 1.13:1, `--border` on bg 1.24:1, the Royal white/silver accent becomes flat charcoal so the brand's one colour move disappears, hard-coded dark hexes leak (`social.module.css:1484`), and the boot gate, status bar and manifest are pre-Royal `#0b0e14` against a `#12141c` body. Instagram, Telegram and Hinge design light first and ship dark as a full second palette. Fix: design light as a first-class palette (bubble-in `#ffffff` with a 1px shadow, panel-2 ≥ `#e6eaf3`, border `#cfd6e4`, a light shadow token), add `data-theme` with a Dark/Light/System row, fix `themeColor` and `manifest.json`, then re-shoot every screen in light.
- **[minor] Five "pick one of N" treatments** on adjacent screens: outlined `.laneBtn` blocks (Friends/Discover), a sliding segmented pill (grid/list, 20px to the right of it), filled-ink chips (board, gallery), rounded-rect cards with sublabels (Now/Tonight/Weekend), outlined white chips selected = grey (help categories). Instagram has one segmented control and one chip. Fix: exactly two components, `SegmentedControl` and `Chip`.
- **[minor] No type or radius scale.** 27 distinct font sizes (214 half-pixel declarations), 23 distinct border radii, 0 `rem`. Fix: `--fs-11/12/13/15/17/20/24`, `--r-6/10/14/20/999`, codemod, stylelint.
- **[minor] `pageFadeUp` (12px slide, overshoot) on 7 settings/profile files only;** the four tabs snap. Apply everywhere as a 160ms opacity fade or remove.
- **[nit] Decorative emoji inside `<h1>` and menu labels** are announced ("sparkles Things you can do"). Wrap in `aria-hidden` or move to the icon slot.

#### Login and register (`phone-login.png`, `desk-login.png`, `phone-register.png`, `shots3/register.png`)

- **[blocker] Zero legal or trust signals at account creation.** No consent line, no `/terms` or `/privacy` route, no age attestation, no statement of who runs this instance, under the heading "Join the fun" — for an app with nearby-stranger discovery, a drunk mode, email and home-area collection. Hinge/Bumble: DOB + 18+ gate + terms before anything; Instagram: DOB + "By signing up you agree…"; Telegram: terms on first launch. Fix: a consent sentence under Create account linking to operator-editable `/terms` and `/privacy` (markdown in Admin → Settings, sane default), a "16 or older" attestation that is never shown on the profile, "Hosted by <operator>" on auth pages.
- **[major] The only recovery door is "Lost everything?"** (`phone-login.png`, bottom). A person who forgot a password does not self-identify that way, and the form behind it accepts only a recovery code — no "ask the operator" path. Instagram/Hinge: "Forgot password?" under the field. Fix: inline "Forgot password?"; recover screen offers the code route and the operator contact (and email reset when SMTP is configured).
- **[major] The wordmark rotates through "happy.", "sad." and "drunk."** every 2.6s on the logged-out pages (`AuthScene.tsx WORDMARK_WORDS`); the capture caught "bored.". No mainstream app animates its name on the login screen. Fix: static wordmark on `/login`, `/register`, `/join`; keep the mood word as a signed-in easter egg.
- **[major] Nothing says what the app is.** Seven-word tagline, no mention of the self-hosted story (no ads, nothing sold, feeds end) — the product's strongest trust lever, unused on the only screens that need it. Hinge/Telegram: 3–5 slide value carousel. Fix: three short lines under the card plus a "What is this?" link.
- **[major] No inline validation.** Username rules (3–24, a–z/0–9/_), the 8-char password minimum and availability appear only as errors after submit; Display name is not marked optional. Telegram/Instagram: live availability with a tick. Fix: helper text, debounced `GET /api/auth/username-available`, a strength bar.
- **[major] "Log in with phone · QR" is shown at phone width.** The flow is for a logged-out computer to be approved by a signed-in phone; on a single phone it shows a QR you cannot scan and waits forever. Telegram shows QR login only in desktop/web clients. Fix: hide below the desktop breakpoint or relabel and lead with the typed-code route.
- **[major] Email is required, never verified, never used** on a default install (`RegisterRequestSchema` requires it; `auth/routes.ts` never verifies; SMTP off). Fix: optional with helper text, or verify when SMTP is on.
- From the code, not a capture: after Create account the flow walls the user with recovery codes (`RecoveryCodes.tsx`), then `/welcome`, then a 4-step tour, then the push explainer, then What's-new — up to five interruptions before a first message (Telegram: zero). Fold into one linear flow; ask for push contextually.
- **[nit]** Register has a serif "Join the fun" heading inside its card; Login has none; the footer link hides the verb ("New here? Join the fun" vs "Create an account").

#### Chats list (`phone-chats.png`, `desk-chats.png`, `phone-chats-more.png`, `phone-new.png`, `shots3/new-chat.png`)

- **[blocker] The magnifier is not search.** `aria-label="Search"` on `<Link href="/people">` (`ConversationListView.tsx:83`): it opens Find people. There is no way to filter the conversation list or search messages anywhere; the desktop sidebar has no search field. WhatsApp/Telegram/Instagram DMs all expand an inline field that filters chats, then messages. Fix: the icon reveals an inline field under the title that filters loaded rows by title + snippet client-side today; message search when global search ships; move Find people to Connections.
- **[blocker] No mute anywhere.** `participants.muted_until` exists and `pushGuard.ts` honours it, but no component references it; the sheet's "Message sound" is device-local. A 256-member group pushes to your phone with no escape short of leaving. WhatsApp 8h/1w/always, Telegram, Signal, iMessage all expose it from the row and the info screen. Fix: `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/participant { muted_until }`, a Mute row at the top of Chat settings, a muted bell in the list, greyed badge on muted rows.
- **[major] Rows have zero actions.** No swipe, long-press, right-click; no pin, archive, mark-unread, delete, mute; no All/Unread/Groups filters. Every reference messenger has at least swipe + long-press. Fix: swipe-left Mute/Archive, swipe-right Pin/Mark unread, long-press action sheet, desktop right-click; pinned section above the sorted list.
- **[major] Every letter avatar is the same grey circle** with a dark initial ("F", "A" in `phone-chats.png`; `globals.css .avatar` uses one `--accent-soft` fill, no per-user hash). WhatsApp, Telegram, Google Messages hash the id to a hue. Fix: `hash(user_id) % 6` → six low-saturation tinted gradients.
- **[major] Group rows give no sender identity.** Snippets show the body only ("perfect 🙌"), never "Arjun:"; group avatars are a bare initial. Fix: prefix with first name / "You:"; stacked member avatars for groups.
- **[minor] The stories strip reserves ~110px** (`phone-chats.png`, y 125–345 at 2x) for a single dashed "Your story" circle when nobody has a story — a row and a half of conversations lost. Telegram collapses it into the header on scroll. Fix: 44px single-row chip when there are zero friend stories; collapse on scroll.
- **[minor] The unread badge pulses forever** (`list.module.css:169`). No messenger animates unread counts; it is the attention hook PSYCHOLOGY.md forbids. Animate once on change.
- **[minor] Time column.** "3:47" with no AM/PM (`lib/format.ts:9`) and no column reserved for rows without a message, so rows are ragged; the presence dot on an offline user is navy, the same as text. Fix: one formatter (today clock, weekday for 7 days, then date); dot only when online.
- **[minor] "Rescue me" and "Anonymous inbox" live in the ··· overflow** (`phone-chats-more.png`) next to Settings and Invite, while the anonymous composer is on `/new`. The emergency control is two hidden taps from Feed/Profile. Fix: one labelled SOS pill on every tab root; anonymous threads in the main list with a mask badge.
- **[nit] The people picker (`shots3/new-chat.png`) shows "offline"** under every contact; the useful secondary line is `@username`. "New anonymous thread" has no sub-label explaining it is a reveal game (Telegram sub-labels each New-X row).

#### Conversation (`phone-conversation.png`, `desk-conversation.png`, `shots3/chat-msg-longpress.png`, `phone-chat-plus-menu.png`, `desk-chat-plus-menu.png`)

- **[blocker] Incoming messages are not bubbles in light mode.** `--bubble-in #e8ecf5` on `--chat-bg #eef0f6` = 1.04:1: "always. what are you thinking" floats as loose text while outgoing messages are near-black slabs (`phone-conversation.png`). Dark is 1.18:1, also too close. WhatsApp light: white on `#efeae2`; Telegram: white with a 1px shadow; iMessage `#e9e9eb` on white. Fix: light `--bubble-in #ffffff` + `0 1px 1px rgba(23,32,47,.06)`; dark `#2a3042`; soften outgoing to a mid-navy so both sides share a weight band.
- **[blocker] No day dividers.** Five bubbles, five "03:47 PM" stamps at 10.5px (`chat.module.css:222`), no "Today/Yesterday", no sticky day while scrolling. Every reference messenger has a day chip. Fix: compute day changes in the existing `messages.map` loop, render a centred pill (reuse `.system`), make it sticky.
- **[blocker] "Your turn · you are ✕" is invisible** (`shots3/chat-msg-longpress.png`): `.tttStatusMine { color: var(--accent) }` (`expression.module.css:940`) paints light-mode ink `#12141c` on the `#1c2130` outgoing bubble, 1.15:1. The game is unplayable in light mode. Same token-on-wrong-surface bug on "0 votes" (`--muted` on the own bubble, 2.94:1). Fix: an `--on-bubble-out` / `--on-bubble-out-muted` pair used for every string inside `.own .bubble`, plus a non-colour cue ("Your move" pill); audit every `var(--accent)` inside own bubbles.
- **[major] No message grouping.** Every bubble has a full 16px radius, its own tail gap and timestamp; sender names in groups repeat per bubble in one colour, no avatar beside incoming group bubbles. iMessage/Telegram join corners (4px) and show one stamp per run. Fix: group same-sender runs within ~5 minutes; stamp and tail on the last bubble only; 28px avatar on the first; per-user hue for sender names; meta ≥11.5px.
- **[major] Delivered vs read is nearly indistinguishable** in outgoing bubbles (white ticks at 55% vs 100% opacity) while the list paints the same read state green. WhatsApp blue, Telegram grey→blue, Signal circle/check/filled. Fix: one read colour (`--ok`) in both places, or Signal's shape model.
- **[major] No jump-to-bottom and no unread divider.** `ChatView` only auto-sticks within 80px; scroll up and new arrivals land unseen; open a chat with 40 unread and there is no marker, although `last_read_seq` is on the client and `DESIGN-HANDOFF.md` claims a jump pill. Fix: floating ↓ pill with count when `stickRef` is false; "N unread messages" divider on open.
- **[major] The "+" grid has no Poll** (`phone-chat-plus-menu.png`: Photo, Doodle, Vibe check, Game, Puzzle, Location, Schedule). `POST /api/conversations/:id/polls` exists, the bubble renders ("pizza?"), the tour and the feature directory advertise it, `Composer.tsx:520–527` has no poll tile. WhatsApp/Telegram: Poll in the attach menu. Also no Camera, File or Contact tiles; Schedule duplicates hold-to-send. Fix: Poll and Watch-together tiles, Camera (`capture`), File; drop Schedule; 4-column grid.
- **[major] Message actions are hard to reach and incomplete.** Long-press is bound to `onTouchStart` only (no pointer events), so trackpads/pens open nothing and the Playwright long-press in `chat-msg-longpress.png` produced no menu; desktop has no `onContextMenu` and relies on a hover smiley; on touch `.reactBtn` is hidden so the 420ms long-press is the only path. The menu lacks Copy, Select, Star. Fix: `onContextMenu` + pointer-event long-press, a visible "⌄" on tap, Copy/Select/Save items, a 10ms vibrate on commit.
- **[major] Native confirm on delete, no "Delete for me".** `ChatView.tsx:1334 window.confirm('Delete this message for everyone?')`; pin-full uses `window.alert` (`:435`). WhatsApp/Telegram/Signal: a sheet with for-me / for-everyone. Fix: `ConfirmSheet`; inline "10/10 pinned" in the pinned bar.
- **[major] The review nudge paints over the stream** (`chat-msg-longpress.png`): a ~250px white card wedged above the composer slices the "asked about:" system line and a bubble in half. Fix: render it as a dismissible system card inside the scroll, not a fixed block that changes the viewport height.
- **[major] The chat "+" on desktop opens a full-width bottom sheet across 1280px** (`desk-chat-plus-menu.png`) and there is no paste or drop upload. WhatsApp Web/Telegram Web: anchored popover + paste/drop. Fix: 2-column popover above the button; `onPaste`/`onDrop` with a "Drop to send" overlay.
- **[major] Bubble typography.** "03:47 PM" with a forced leading zero (`formatClock` uses `hour: '2-digit'`) at ~13px inside bubbles vs "3:47" in the list; meta 10.5px. Fix: `hour: 'numeric'` in one formatter; 11px/65% stamps; cap body at 16px.
- **[minor] Typing indicator is its own row** between scroll and composer (`chat.module.css:785 .typingRow`), so layout shifts on every start/stop. WhatsApp/Telegram put it in the header subline. **[minor] Placeholder "Message… try @"** in a 1:1 DM, forever (WhatsApp: "Message"). **[minor] Enter sends on phones** (`Composer.tsx:858`), so a two-line message is impossible; every mobile messenger makes Enter a newline. **[minor] Poll renders as two components** (feed `.postPollQ` serif with a hard-coded `#3a4052` border; chat `.pollQ` sans in white rows); one `<Poll>` consuming tokens.
- **[nit] `navigator.vibrate` fires on page load** — 27 blocked calls per conversation page in `pw/shots2/phone-errors.txt` — while the gestures that deserve haptics fire none. Gate the ringer path behind the ring state.

#### Feed (`phone-feed.png`, `phone-feed-list.png`, `phone-feed-discover.png`, `phone-feed-compose.png`, `desk-feed.png`, `desk-feed-compose.png`, `shots3/feed-post-menu.png`, `shots3/feed-audience.png`)

- **[blocker] Other people's posts have no overflow menu.** The "⋯" (`FeedView.tsx:981`) renders only on own posts and its single item is "Delete post" (`:991`); `ReportSheet` is not imported in `FeedView.tsx`. The Discover lane shows strangers' public posts with no Report, Hide or Block — a trust-and-safety baseline on Instagram, Threads, X and Facebook, not a feature. Fix: ⋯ on every card — own: Edit · Copy link · Delete; others: Report (`ReportSheet`) · Hide · Block.
- **[major] Every list card carries a full comment composer** (image button + "Say something back…" pill + Reply) plus a lone reaction icon (`phone-feed-list.png`). The reply row is the tallest element in each card, taller than Maya's two-line post; ten posts are ten identical forms. This is Facebook-2012. Instagram/Threads/X: one action row, comments in a detail view or sheet. Fix: a 40px action row (React · Reply · Send) and a "View replies" line; the existing grid-tile sheet becomes the thread view.
- **[major] Grid is the default for the Friends lane** (`phone-feed.png`, `desk-feed.png`). Tiles strip identity: the text tile shows a 10–11px author line, the poll tile no author at all — in a *friends* feed, against the product's own "faces, not counters" rule. Instagram grids only a single author's profile and Explore. Fix: Friends defaults to list; grid for Discover; if grid stays, every tile gets an avatar+name pill.
- **[major] Post images open the raw proxy URL in a new tab** (`FeedView.tsx:1013` and `:1674` `<a href={a.url} target="_blank">`); on an installed PWA this throws the user into Safari/Chrome. `MediaLightbox` is used by chat, board and profile but not the feed. Fix: wire the lightbox, pass all attachments so it swipes.
- **[major] No post detail / permalink.** No `/feed/[id]` route; tapping body or timestamp does nothing; comment and mention notifications have no target. X/Threads/Instagram/Facebook: every post has a URL. Fix: `/feed/[postId]` or `?post=` opening the existing sheet.
- **[major] No "Send to a chat".** In a chat-first app the obvious share is to a DM; the card offers copy-public-link only, and friends-only posts cannot be shared at all although `ForwardSheet` exists. Fix: Send → `ForwardSheet`, server-enforced to conversations whose members can already see the post.
- **[major] The Audience row ("Audience … Connections ›") silently flips** friends↔public on tap (`shots3/feed-audience.png` is exactly the harness trying to open a picker). A chevron promises a sheet on every platform. Fix: a two-radio sheet, or drop the chevron and use a segmented control.
- **[major] The poll question renders twice** ("song for the ride?" in sans, then again in serif, `phone-feed-list.png`) because the composer has both a body field and a question field. X/Threads: the text is the question. Fix: hide the body for `kind=poll`; dedupe on render.
- **[major] Reacting — the primary interaction — is an 18px ghost smiley** at the left edge; posts get a 6-emoji quick row while comments get the full avatar-stamp picker. Instagram: 24px heart/comment/share row. Fix: proper action row, 44px targets, same `ReactionPicker` as comments and chat.
- **[major] The end card has no action** and, for a new account with zero connections, says "nothing new from your people — go make a plan" which is false (`FeedView.tsx:340` keys copy on lane only). Instagram's caught-up card offers "View older posts"; its empty home is a suggestions module. Fix: CTAs ("I'm bored — light a beacon", "See Discover" / "Find people"); branch on connection count.
- **[major] Feed header chrome.** Wordmark instead of a title, lanes as two full-width 1.5px-bordered buttons plus a boxed refresh (`phone-feed-discover.png`) — it looks like a form, not tabs — then a "+" (thin) that does not match the Chats "+" (heavy). Threads/Instagram: underlined text tabs. Fix: title, underlined lane tabs, refresh as a quiet "New posts" pill that appears only when the server reports newer items.
- **[major] The empty composer is taller than the phone** (`phone-feed-compose.png`): kind chips above the textarea ("Join my..." truncates with two ellipses at 390px), a permanent dashed 160×160 photo tile, "Add a place", the audience row, a policy sentence that repeats on every open, and a solid mid-grey disabled "Post" pill — the darkest element on the card while inert (`.btn:disabled { opacity: .55 }` on the ink gradient, `globals.css:420`). Instagram's Share stays brand-blue at reduced opacity. Fix: textarea first; icon toolbar (photo · place · audience); thumb strip only after a pick; policy note once per device; disabled = outline + `--muted` at 40% with a helper line, or keep enabled and validate on tap.
- **[minor]** Timestamps are a bare "3:47" with no am/pm or relative time (Threads/X/Instagram: "2h", "3d"). Author name and handle are plain text; only the 38px avatar links. Poll options show "0 0 0" before the viewer votes with the meta "be the tiebreaker before there's a tie". Comments under your own post show a bare "×" delete on every friend's reply. The own-post menu is a single red item with no Edit. Stories live only on the Chats tab; the Feed has no stories entry. The desktop header aligns to a 680px column while the feed is 560px, so the wordmark sits ~64px left of the lane bar and "+" ~64px right of refresh (`desk-feed.png`). With two posts the mosaic leaves an empty third cell and the end card floats mid-screen. Emoji as UI inside cards ("Join 🙋", "needs 2 more 🔥", 🎮 22px in the post menu).

#### Local (`phone-local.png`, `desk-local.png`, `phone-beacon-create.png`, `phone-beacon-detail.png`, `phone-listing-create.png`, `desk-beacon-create.png`)

- **[major] Visual hierarchy is inverted on the product's main screen.** The section headings "Bored together" and "The board" render visibly larger than the page title "Local", and the hero verb "+ I'm bored" is a dashed outline with muted text — the weakest element on the screen (`phone-local.png`). Dashed borders are the app's "empty slot" affordance (add-story ring, media tile, "No more requests"), so the primary action reads as a placeholder. Hinge: the primary action is always the highest-contrast element. Fix: "I'm bored" = `.btn-primary` full width, first; section h2 ≤ 22px; dashed only for drop targets and empty states.
- **[major] SOS is a 13px 🆘 emoji inside a ghost icon button** (~29px square, top-right of `phone-local.png`), with no label; it renders as a tiny red block on Linux/Android and a monochrome glyph on Windows. It is also duplicated in the Chats ··· overflow. Safety controls in Uber/Lyft/Bumble are persistent, labelled pills. Fix: a labelled 40–44px "Rescue" pill with a vector life-ring in `--danger`, on every tab root; remove from the overflow.
- **[major] Four products stacked vertically** with no sub-navigation: beacon card + "I'm bored", Bored together, the board with seven filter chips (the seventh clipped "Pla…" with no edge fade), area chip + search. At 844px the board — the actual community-board use case — starts below the fold and "Mine" is the last chip, two swipes away. WhatsApp Communities / Telegram folders: sticky segmented control. Fix: sticky segmented control (Plans · Bored now · Board), search above the chips, edge fade on chip rows, "Mine" second.
- **[major] "Make me discoverable" is the most prominent CTA on the screen with no first-time explainer** — what strangers see, that it lapses in an hour, how to report. Bumble/Hinge show a safety sheet before the first match. Fix: one-time consent sheet, remembered per account.
- **[major] Helper copy in `--faint`.** "Expires on its own. No one is notified when it fizzles." (12.5px, `beacons.module.css`), the detail footer "only Arjun Rao can edit or end it" — the sentences that explain the privacy promise are 2.13:1 on white. WhatsApp's encryption explainer is 13px at ~4.6:1. Fix: `--faint` for hairlines only; readable copy in `--muted` ≥13px.
- **[minor] A board listing has no container** ("GIG / Need a photographer for Saturday / ₹2000 / expires in 30d" sits on bare background under the search field, `phone-local.png`). Facebook Marketplace: card with an image slot even without a photo. Fix: listing card with a 64px placeholder tile by kind, title, price chip, hairline.
- **[minor]** "Light it" in the beacon sheet is visible on 390×844 only because the sheet is exactly full height and is clipped at 1280×800 (`desk-beacon-create.png`); the listing sheet opens as a 330px stub with "Post" in the header and the form below the fold. See Sheets.

#### Profile, own and public (`phone-settings-profile.png`, `desk-settings-profile.png`, `phone-profile-arjun.png`, `desk-profile-arjun.png`, `phone-profile-options.png`, `phone-connections.png`, `phone-people.png`, `phone-gallery.png`)

- **[major] Own profile and public `/u/` are two different components with different content.** The tab has POSTS/GALLERY/INTERESTS segments, "ritual lines", a My gallery card and no bio; the public page has a cover band, bio, Pics, Work/Skills and no posts grid. "View your public profile" therefore shows the owner something they cannot see while editing. Instagram renders both with the same screen. Fix: one `ProfileView`; `isMe` swaps only the action row.
- **[major] The same two activities appear twice on one screen** (`phone-settings-profile.png`): as two centred bullet lines under the handle (one centred, one ellipsised and left-flush, two bullet colours with no legend) and again under RECENTLY 400px lower — while the bio the user wrote never renders (code shows it only when there is no activity). Instagram: bio under the name, unconditionally; nothing appears twice. Fix: always render the bio; drop the ritual lines.
- **[major] Public profile header** (`phone-profile-arjun.png`): a 128px decorative gradient band (`profilepage.module.css:22`) with a beige blotch top-right that reads as a rendering artifact in light mode, a hard edge mid-avatar, back and kebab as mid-grey orbs that look disabled, a double ring on the avatar that reads as a stuck focus ring with no live story, and a 👻 emoji orb beside two text pills. On desktop it is a 680px band floating in 1280px. Instagram: no cover, 86px avatar, ring only with a live story. Fix: drop the band or make it full-bleed from the user's own avatar with a bottom fade; translucent 36px orbs with ink glyphs; ring only when a story exists; ghost action into the kebab.
- **[major] People management has four homes** with three words: Chats ··· → "Friend requests" sheet; Profile → "Connections" page (requests + YOUR PEOPLE + "+ FIND"); Chats magnifier → "Find people"; Feed audience "Connections ›". Fix: one People page (Requests · Your people · Suggestions), one word, reachable from Profile and the Chats overflow.
- **[major] Header icons and labels mismatch.** The Profile tab's two unlabelled icons are a chain-link (actually "View your public profile", reads as copy link) and sliders (Settings); "Edit profile" lands on a page titled "Account". Instagram: labelled "Share profile", a gear/hamburger. Fix: gear with label on desktop; "Share profile" button; rename the destination.
- **[minor]** In the Friends state nothing is primary: "✓ Friends" and "Message" are equal outline pills and tapping "✓ Friends" opens `window.confirm("Remove … as a friend?")` (`u/[username]/page.tsx:232`) — a destructive action behind a status-looking button; no call shortcuts. "last seen 43m ago" is minute-precise presence, the seen-at mechanism the never-build list rejects. Gallery has three entry points on one screen; "Things you can do" and Rescue are profile body content. Empty-state links on `phone-people.png` / `phone-connections.png` are slightly darker body text with no underline; "No more requests" implies requests existed; `phone-gallery.png` says the same sentence twice with nothing to tap. Own profile has no "Add photo" affordance on the initial-letter avatar and no completion nudge.

#### Settings pages (`phone-settings.png`, `shots3/settings-account.png`, `settings-privacy.png`, `settings-notifications.png`, `settings-chats.png`, `settings-calls.png`, `settings-security.png`, `settings-help.png`, `desk-settings-privacy.png`)

- **[blocker] No password change, email change, delete account or data export anywhere.** The only route to a new password is burning a recovery code, which signs out every device. WhatsApp/Telegram/Instagram all have Delete account; Apple requires in-app deletion. (Owned by the Account section of this audit; listed here because it is a missing settings surface.) Fix: Account group with Change password, Change email, Download my data, Delete account (danger red, re-auth, grace period).
- **[major] Boolean settings are word pills** (`settings-privacy.png`: a dark pill "On", a light pill "Off"; `privacy/page.tsx:295 {value ? 'On' : 'Off'}`), Notifications uses Enable/Disable buttons plus green "ON" words, Chat settings uses Default/On/Off segments — three vocabularies. The "Off" pill is pixel-identical to action buttons like "Open" and "Regenerate", on the screen where mistakes matter most. The markup is correct (`role=switch`); the visual is wrong. Fix: one `<Switch>` (track + thumb) for every boolean.
- **[major] Multi-choice settings are native `<select>`s** styled like text inputs (eight `SelectRow`s on Privacy, including four identical ones for Activity), showing the browser chevron and grey gradient on desktop (`desk-settings-privacy.png`). WhatsApp: "Last seen — Everyone ›" → radio list. Fix: value rows opening radio sheets; cut each paragraph hint to one sentence (Privacy is one ~2,100px scroll of seven card sections).
- **[major] Five sub-pages, four layouts.** Privacy = white cards + serif uppercase eyebrows (`.settings-section h2` inherits `h1,h2 { font-family: var(--font-display) }` then `text-transform: uppercase`, `globals.css:140–142, 524–527` — Marcellus has no small caps and tracks badly in caps); Notifications = flat rows, 12px/700 sans eyebrows, red/green status words; Chats = chevron rows; Calls = one card plus a loose sentence; Help = chip picker and an "OPEN" ticket badge in danger red. Three eyebrow systems coexist, sometimes on one page (`settings-account.png`: serif "GIF STRIP" and sans "MOOD"). Telegram/iOS: one grouped-list style everywhere. Fix: `SettingsGroup` + `SettingsRow`; one `.eyebrow` token (Instrument Sans 12px/700/0.08em/`--muted`); serif only on non-uppercase h1/h2; Open in `--info`.
- **[major] Two of seven categories are hollow.** Chats is two rows that say "this lives elsewhere" (`settings-chats.png`); Calls is a self-test and "Call history is coming soon." (`settings-calls.png`). WhatsApp's Chats page is one of its densest. Fix: fill Chats (theme toggle, text size, Enter-to-send, media auto-download, archived) or hide the row; delete "coming soon"; move ringtone/vibrate to Calls.
- **[major] Settings are filed by where they were built.** Mood effects (a viewer preference) and default beacon audience under Privacy; animated avatars and name style under Account between Bio and Save; Mood (ephemeral status) a card inside Account. Fix: Account = identity + credentials; new Appearance = theme/text size/motion/name style; Privacy = who-can-see only.
- **[major] Account has three save models on one page**: a mid-page Save, a bottom "Save interests & entries", and auto-saving GIF strip/mood; an added entry is silently lost on navigation; no dirty guard. Instagram: one Done with "Discard changes?". Fix: one header Done, or per-field autosave with inline ticks.
- **[major] Notifications is a single on/off plus three sound rows** — no categories, quiet hours, preview toggle or digest, although PSYCHOLOGY §6 says "settings offer digest, never all off". Fix: category rows with Instant/Digest, quiet hours.
- **[major] Help & about contains no about** (`settings-help.png`): no version, operator identity ("Send to the operator" — who?), legal links, retention statement or licences; the only trust line is the footer. Telegram: FAQ, Privacy Policy, version. Fix: About card fed from admin settings.
- **[major] Devices & security leaks dev-speak** (`settings-security.png`): "Passkeys are bound to this server's domain (localhost)", "generated 3:58" with no date, a disabled Continue with no hint it needs the 6-char code, "Authenticator codes OFF" + "Turn on", a passkey name input visible with zero passkeys. From the code, the sessions page prints the raw User-Agent truncated to 80 chars plus the raw IP and has no "Sign out all other devices" (not captured). Fix: parse UA, hide the domain line, dates as "today 15:58", single 2FA row, "Add a passkey" → name after success.
- **[minor]** Root list has no Appearance, Storage or Language rows, no grouping, and Sign out is bare red text with no confirm. Username is a disabled input that looks editable with no reason given; email an unlabelled grey line. Two privacy labels carry emoji ("🎭 Who can send you anonymous messages", "👀 Wave nudges"); "Blocked people" is a card with an "Open" button rather than a chevron row. No character counters on name/bio. Blocked and Sessions hard-code their Back targets. The spotlight tour fires on top of the Devices & security page (`shots/phone-settings-security.png`) with a tooltip about the Chats tab, and its step 2 targets `tab-plans`, which no longer exists.

#### Sheets and menus (`shots3/chat-info.png`, `phone-beacon-create.png`, `phone-story-add.png`, `phone-listing-create.png`, `phone-chats-more.png`, `phone-profile-options.png`, `phone-chat-plus-menu.png`)

- **[major] "Chat settings" for a DM is a personalisation panel, not chat info** (`chat-info.png`): nine unlabelled colour tiles and seven wallpaper tiles fill about two-thirds of the first viewport, "ONLY YOU SEE THIS" repeats four times, the selected tile is shown by a 2px border only, and the sheet never shows who the chat is with. No identity row, no Mute, no block/report, no shared media, no search; disappearing messages is below the wallpapers. WhatsApp: hero, media, mute, disappearing, wallpaper, block/report. Fix: identity hero → Mute · Search · Media · Disappearing · Pinned → "Appearance" collapsed into one row (named swatches with a check badge) → Clear · Block · Report · Leave; "Only you see this" once.
- **[major] Native dialogs inside a custom sheet system.** 15 `window.confirm`/`alert` sites in 12 files (listed in the verdict) — browser-grey boxes for leaving a chat, blocking, deleting a post, closing a listing, taking down a beacon, removing a friend, revealing an anonymous identity. Instagram/TikTok/WhatsApp/Telegram/Signal all use themed sheets. Fix: one `ConfirmSheet` (title, body, destructive red primary, cancel) replacing all 15; "Delete for me / for everyone" for messages; confirm on Sign out.
- **[major] Tall sheets have no sticky footer and no detents.** "Light a beacon" is full height with a 90px sliver of page behind it, "Chat settings" starts at 50px, "Add to your story" and "List on the board" at different heights; the primary CTA sits at the bottom of an internal scroll ("Light it" clipped at 1280×800 in `desk-beacon-create.png`; "Post" in the listing sheet's header with the form below the fold). iOS/Telegram: grabber, medium/large detents, long forms become pages with a pinned CTA. Fix: `Sheet` with grabber and detents; `position: sticky; bottom: 0` footer with safe-area padding; forms taller than ~70% of the viewport become pages.
- **[major] Disabled primaries look enabled.** "Light it", "Post story", "Send to the operator", "Continue" are the ink gradient at 55% opacity (`globals.css:420`) — a solid mid-grey pill with white text, the most button-like thing on the card. Fix: see Feed composer.
- **[major] No focus trap, no return-focus, no history integration.** 51 `aria-modal` sheets, 0 `focusTrap`/`inert`; Tab escapes behind every backdrop; Android Back navigates away instead of dismissing. Fix: a `useFocusTrap` in `Portal`-mounted sheets; `pushState` per modal, close on `popstate`.
- **[minor]** Destructive items in "···" menus are not visually distinguished (no red tier in `topbar.module.css`); the own-post menu is a single red item; `phone-profile-options.png` and `phone-chats-more.png` mix navigation, safety and utility in one list with no separators.

#### Desktop two-pane (`desk-chats.png`, `desk-conversation.png`, `desk-feed.png`, `desk-local.png`, `desk-people.png`, `desk-settings-profile.png`, `desk-settings-privacy.png`, `desk-profile-arjun.png`, `desk-things.png`, `desk-beacon-create.png`, `desk-feed-compose.png`)

- **[major] Desktop space is used only by Chats.** Feed, Local, People and Profile are a 680px column centred in a 1280px window (`desk-feed.png`: content from x≈336 to 1016, ~45% of the viewport empty) with no secondary pane, and the 72px rail disappears entirely on `/settings/*`, `/u/[username]` and `/things` — the user loses navigation and must use the back chevron. Rail labels are the same 10.5px `.tab`. Instagram web keeps the left nav on every route and widens content to ~935px; WhatsApp Web never drops the column; Threads fills the gutters with nav + composer. Fix: rail in the authed layout on every route; at ≥1100px a second pane for Feed (opened post) and Local (beacon/listing detail); `.page` max-width 760–840px; rail labels 11–12px or icon-only with tooltips.
- **[major] The rail has no Settings entry.** Settings is reachable only via the Chats ··· menu or the sliders icon on the Profile tab. Telegram Desktop pins it; Slack/Discord put avatar + gear at the bottom of the rail. Fix: avatar + gear at the bottom of the ≥900px rail.
- **[major] Settings sub-pages are full-page navigations** with a back chevron inside a 640px column (`desk-settings-privacy.png`); the own profile is a phone layout centred on 1280px (`desk-settings-profile.png`). WhatsApp Web: list left, detail right. Fix: two-pane Settings at ≥900px; horizontal profile header on desktop.
- **[major] Zero keyboard shortcuts, no right-click, no paste/drop, and the "+" opens a full-width bottom sheet** (see Conversation). The two-pane shell exists but cannot be driven like a desktop app.
- **[minor]** The idle pane and the empty chat list share the heading "It's quiet in here… too quiet.", so a new desktop user reads it twice on one screen; the idle pane's "drag someone new into the boredom" reads as a drag-and-drop instruction. Sheets become centred 480px modals at ≥600px (correct) but the beacon modal clips its CTA at 800px tall.

### If I had one week

1. **Theme and token pass (2 days).** `apps/web/app/globals.css`: design the light palette (bubble-in `#ffffff` + 1px shadow, panel-2 ≥ `#e6eaf3`, border `#cfd6e4`, light shadow token; delete the hard-coded `#3a4052` in `social.module.css:1484` and `.warn-text #ffd166`); add `--on-bubble-out` / `--on-bubble-out-muted` and use them for every string inside `.own .bubble` (`expression.module.css:940 .tttStatusMine`, `.pollMeta`); promote every readable `--faint`/`--inactive` use to `--muted` and raise `.tab` to 11–12px (`social.module.css:29–30`); disabled primaries keep the gradient at opacity .4 (`globals.css:420`); `html { font-size: 100% }` and a rem ramp with a 12px floor; `data-theme` on `<html>` with a Dark/Light/System row under a new Settings → Appearance (`app/settings/page.tsx`, new `app/settings/appearance/page.tsx`, a `theme` column or localStorage); `themeColor` in `app/layout.tsx:17–20` and `public/manifest.json` to `#12141c`. Re-shoot every screen in light.
2. **Chat table stakes (2 days).** `components/ChatView.tsx`: day-pill dividers and same-sender clustering in the `messages.map` loop (`:780`), an "N unread messages" divider from `last_read_seq`, a floating ↓ pill when `stickRef` is false, `onContextMenu` + pointer-event long-press with Copy added to `MessageActionsOverlay` (`:1707–1792`), the review nudge moved into the scroll (`:815`). `components/Composer.tsx`: Enter = newline on coarse pointers (`:858`), a Poll tile in the "+" grid (`:520–527`) posting to the existing `/polls` route, `onPaste`/`onDrop`, draft text per conversation in localStorage with a "Draft:" label in `ConversationListView.tsx`. `lib/format.ts`: one `formatTime` (`hour: 'numeric'`, relative coarse labels in lists).
3. **Chat-list management (1 day).** Migration `0013` adding `pinned_at`/`archived_at` to `participants` (`mutedUntil` exists); `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/participant` in the conversations module and `docs/API.md`; `ConversationListView.tsx`: inline search field that filters loaded rows (replace the `/people` Link at `:83`), long-press / right-click row sheet (Mute 8h · 1w · always, Pin, Archive, Mark unread), muted bell, pinned section; `ChatInfoSheet.tsx`: Mute row first, identity hero, appearance collapsed.
4. **ConfirmSheet, routes, boot gate, legal (1 day).** New `components/ConfirmSheet.tsx` replacing all 15 `window.confirm`/`alert` sites (plus Sign out in `app/settings/page.tsx:197`), with "Delete for me / for everyone" in `ChatView.tsx:1334`; `app/error.tsx`, `app/not-found.tsx`, `app/loading.tsx` with a shimmer-row primitive reused by Chats, Feed and Board; `Wordmark` on the `AppShell.tsx:32` boot gate; `app/terms/page.tsx` and `app/privacy/page.tsx` fed by operator-editable markdown in Admin → Settings, a consent line and 16+ attestation in `app/register/page.tsx`.
5. **Navigation persistence and hit areas (1 day).** Mount `Tabs` in the authed layout (`components/AppShell.tsx` / `GlobalOverlays.tsx`) so the bar stays on `/u/[username]`, `/settings/*`, `/new`, `/things`, `/puzzle`, `/anon` and the rail on every desktop route, with avatar + gear at the bottom of the rail; `router.back()` with a logical-parent fallback for the 24 hard Back links; `::before { inset: -8px }` on `.iconBtn` (`topbar.module.css:8`), `.orbBtn` (`profilepage.module.css:50`), `.cardKebab` (`beacons.module.css:276`), `.layoutBtn` (`social.module.css:1937`), and 36–40px pills for "+ LIST SOMETHING" / "+ FIND" / "Copy"; rewrite `TOUR_STEPS` in `packages/shared/src/constants.ts:1012–1030` to the four real tabs and start the tour only on the Chats root; one labelled "Rescue" pill on every tab root, removed from the Chats overflow; `aria-hidden` on decorative emoji in `<h1>`s and a shared `<Icon>` module as the first step of retiring emoji chrome.


---

## Platform, growth and operations — the plumbing, the loops, the operator's chair

**Verdict.** The headline is the account lifecycle that is not there: no delete-account, no change-password while signed in, no change-email, no forgot-password that works without a recovery code, no privacy or terms page, no theme toggle, no chat mute (the column and the copy exist, the control does not), and registration open to the whole internet with no off switch. Against that, the sync core is real engineering — gapless `seq`, an IndexedDB outbox with idempotent flush, exactly-once resync on every reconnect, QR device linking where the token never transits the phone, an OS share-target that stashes a share for a logged-out user — and the ops story (Caddy single origin, `./bored export/import`, a keyless update checker, zero analytics) is clean for a self-hosted app. Counted from the tables below: **15 of 37 basic-tier rows are fully there (41%)** and **25 of 103 standard-tier rows (24%)**; half-built rows get no credit, hygiene rows are not counted, and the three banned items carry no tier. The "operator cannot read private chats" line is a route-level policy (ADR-013: no admin route returns message bodies), not cryptography — there is no E2EE, bodies sit in Postgres, and the operator has `psql`. Several docs claim things that do not exist (muting, admin "warn", an "instance privacy note"), release notes stopped at v0.9, and every deploy leaves users on a stale shell until two full reloads — TROUBLESHOOTING.md:26 says so. Moderation is a one-disposition queue with free-text reasons: no taxonomy, no appeals, no keyword filters, and the operator can neither measure the product's own north-star metric nor reach `/healthz` from outside. Growth is throttled by plumbing: content leaves the app by clipboard only (one `navigator.share` call in the whole client), a shared post lands a new user on the generic Feed tab, and there is no contacts import — a real P1 growth gap that hashed matching against `users.email` would close without a third party. Fix the nine P0s first — most are small (theme, mute route, `navigator.share`, SW update toast, legal pages, change-password) — then contacts, profile QR and object URLs.

### What it has

| Feature | Tier | Also in | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Installable web app manifest (name, standalone, icons, theme colour) | basic | WhatsApp (Web), X (PWA), Instagram (limited), Facebook (Windows PWA), Luma, Meetup | `apps/web/public/manifest.json`. Polish gap: the same `icon-512.png` is declared for `any` and `maskable` (adaptive-icon crop risk) and `public/icons/` holds only the 192 and 512 PNGs — no apple-touch-icon. |
| Service worker with offline app shell | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, X, Discord | `sw.js:13` `SHELL_CACHE = 'socialchat-shell-v1'`; `/api/*` and `/ws` bypass the cache (line 42). Solid shell; the hard-coded v1 name is why a stale shell survives deploys (see Half-built). |
| iOS standalone meta + safe-area viewport | basic | Instagram, X, Facebook | `layout.tsx` appleWebApp, `viewportFit: cover`, safe-area paddings in globals.css. Done. |
| Desktop / tablet two-pane layout | standard | WhatsApp Web/Desktop, Telegram, Signal Desktop, Discord, Snapchat Web, Tinder Web, Bumble Web | `TwoPane.tsx`. Responsive PWA covers it. |
| Web push (VAPID subscribe/unsubscribe, delivery guards) | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, X (PWA), Discord, Facebook | `modules/push`, `lib/pushGuard.ts`, sw.js handlers. Call-ring actions, read-elsewhere suppression, per-browser unblock steps (`unblockSteps()`: iPhone/iPad, Firefox, Edge, Safari, Chrome). Needs VAPID keys (documented). |
| Offline outbox: queue sends, idempotent flush on reconnect | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, BeReal | `connection.ts` addOutbox / `flushOutbox()` (line 561), `db.ts` outbox store. Stronger than Discord, X, Reddit, Nextdoor, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge (no send queue at all). |
| Read cached chat history offline | basic | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Discord, Tinder, Hinge | `ChatView.tsx:220` `db.getMessagesTail`. Only previously opened chats; media still needs network. |
| Server-side multi-device sync (messages, reads, deletes, reactions, settings) | basic | all 17 | `GET /api/sync` + gapless seq (API.md §1.3, §2.8). Exactly-once across any outage; the strongest part of the platform. |
| Concurrent sign-in on several devices | basic | WhatsApp (4 companions), Telegram, Signal (5), Instagram, Facebook, Discord, Bumble, X | `auth/routes.ts:133-151`, sessions table as device registry. Unlimited; avoids Snapchat's one-device lock. |
| Linked-devices list with per-row revoke | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Discord, X | `GET/DELETE /api/auth/sessions` (auth/routes.ts:133, 151), `settings/sessions`. Works, but renders `user_agent.slice(0, 80)` and the raw IP (sessions/page.tsx:78-82) although `describeUserAgent()` (lib/auth.ts:174) exists and is used only on the QR-approval sheet; `SessionInfoSchema` (api.ts:835) has no name field; no revoke-all (see Missing). |
| QR cross-device login / link a desktop from the phone | standard | WhatsApp Web, Telegram, Signal linked devices, Discord | `auth/links.ts`, `login/page.tsx:481`, `link/[code]`. The token never transits the phone. |
| Restore on a new phone (server is the system of record) | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, BeReal, Hinge, Instagram, and every cloud-native benchmark | Postgres + MinIO hold everything; `/api/sync` on first login restores it. No client-side backup needed. |
| Username / display-name search | basic | all 17 | `GET /api/users?q=`, `UserSearch.tsx`. Prefix ILIKE on username and display name (users/routes.ts:127); no email/phone lookup — the privacy-positive choice. |
| People-you-may-know with permanent silent dismiss | standard | Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, Nextdoor | `modules/suggestions`, `SuggestionCards.tsx`, `suggestion_dismissals` (schema.ts:2077). Mutuals, area, shared groups, public interests; blocked pairs and ghost members excluded. |
| Post-signup "find your people" step | standard | Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, Threads, BeReal | `app/welcome`, register dest `/welcome` (register/page.tsx:107). Thin: one optional area field plus `SuggestionCards` without `showEmpty`/`inviteCard`, so a fresh account with no mutuals or area is shown nobody and no invite link (SuggestionCards.tsx:74). Never re-shown. |
| Share invite link via OS share sheet + clipboard | basic | all 17 | `InviteSheet.tsx:100`. The only `navigator.share` call in the client (confirmed by grep). |
| Invite landing page with OG unfurl | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord, Partiful, Luma, Instagram | `join/[token]` `generateMetadata` (page.tsx:55); 1200×630 banner composed server-side (invites/routes.ts:110). `PUBLIC_ORIGIN` tunnel caveat documented. |
| Contextual invite nudges in empty states | standard | Nextdoor, Partiful, Instagram, Snapchat, Threads | ConversationListView, TwoPane, connections page, SuggestionCards inviteCard. Done. |
| Admin kill-switch for invites | standard | Discord (pause invites), Facebook groups | `admin/settings` toggleInvites, `invitesEnabled()` (admin/service.ts:154; API §1.41). Does not gate registration (see Missing). |
| Deep-link return after login/register (`?next=`, same-site) | standard | all 17 | `safeNext()` (lib/api.ts:69), login, register, AppShell. Safe; weakened by the missing object URLs. |
| OS share-target (receive text/links/media into a chat) | basic | all 17 | manifest `share_target`, `app/share/route.ts`, `modules/share` (API §1.8), including the logged-out stash + claim (`SHARE_STASH_TTL_S` = 30 min, constants.ts:66; `/api/share-stash`, `/api/share-imports/claim`). |
| Save media to device / download | basic | WhatsApp, Snapchat (export), Telegram, Instagram, Facebook | `AttachmentView.tsx:107` `download` attr; long-press for images. Adequate for a PWA. |
| Public share pages with OG/Twitter meta for posts and stories | standard | Instagram, X, Threads, Reddit, TikTok, BeReal, Facebook | `/p/[token]`, `/s/[token]`, `modules/sharepages` (API §1.67). Token-gated and rate-limited; videos already play for non-users. FEATURES #110 (v1.0a block) is partly shipped here — the OS-share-sheet and profile-link halves are not (see Half-built). |
| Per-chat disappearing messages | standard | Signal, Snapchat, Telegram, WhatsApp | `DISAPPEAR_TTL_OPTIONS_S` (1h/1d/1w/30d, constants.ts:316), `disappear_changed` (v0.4 §1.25). Per-chat exists; no global device-side retention cap (fine). |
| Operator database backup command | standard | n/a (self-hosted equivalent) | `bored cmd_backup` (pg_dump gz, `bored:215`). **Media is NOT included** — line 216 prints "media lives in the minio_data docker volume" and stops. Operators who run it and think they are covered are not. |
| Full-instance export / import archive (DB + MinIO + .env) | standard | n/a | `bored cmd_export / cmd_import` (`bored:154-210`). Quiesces writers, refuses to clobber without `--force`, warns that the archive holds secrets. Table stakes for a self-hosted product; it is done properly. |
| Admin overview dashboard + first-party metrics | standard | Discord Server Insights, Reddit mod insights, Meta Business Suite, Luma, Meetup | `admin/page.tsx`, `GET /api/admin/metrics`: three time-series (registrations, DAU proxy from sliding sessions, messages/day) plus five totals (users, open beacons, listings, open reports, storage) — `AdminMetricsSchema` api.ts:2784-2793. Plain bars; nothing about funnels or the north-star. |
| Admin user management (search, last-seen, IP 90d, suspend/ban with notes, revoke) | standard | Discord, Reddit, Meta, Nextdoor, TikTok | `admin/users` (suspend/ban confirms at page.tsx:66-68), `gc.ts runIpRetention` (`IP_RETENTION_DAYS` = 90). Ban = public invisibility, sockets closed immediately. |
| User reports queue with review, close, audit note | basic | all 17 | `admin/reports`, `GET /api/admin/reports` (admin/routes.ts:161), `POST /:id/close` (206), reporter-consented context viewer (`/:id/context`, content.ts:202-203; §1.69 #81c). The consent-gated viewer is thoughtful. |
| Public-content takedown with required reason + neutral author notice | standard | Meta, TikTok, Reddit, Discord, Nextdoor | `admin/content.ts:57-202` (§1.69 #81a); notice via `sendSupportMessage()` (content.ts:183). Private chats are out of the admin console's reach by owner decision — a route-level policy (ADR-013), not encryption; the operator still has the database. |
| Admin audit / action log viewer | advanced | Discord audit log, Reddit mod log | `admin/logs`, `lib/logring.ts`, `LOG_RING_MAX` = 1000 (constants.ts:529). Redis-capped, not durable — fine for an instance, not an audit trail. |
| In-app support tickets with admin reply | basic | all 17 | `settings/help`, `modules/support`, `admin/support` (§1.69 #81b); `SUPPORT_CATEGORIES` bug/question/idea/safety (constants.ts:1245). Clever reuse of the conversation machinery. |
| Server-side rate limiting on every public/auth surface | hygiene (not counted) | all 17 | `lib/ratelimit.ts` `fixedWindow`; register 3/h/IP (auth/routes.ts:28), login 10/min/IP (line 99), share, stash. Engineering hygiene, not a user-facing feature — listed, not scored. |
| User-facing "slow down" / rate-limited feedback | standard | Telegram (flood wait), X, Instagram ("Action blocked") | Server emits 429 `{retry_after}`; `status === 429` gets human copy in 23 client files (grep `429`: login, register, security, help, link, InviteSheet, FeedView, listings, beacons, stories, mood, review, report, anon…). Caveat: no central interceptor — `Composer.tsx:351` falls back to `'Send failed.'`, though `POST …/messages` is not rate-limited server-side anyway (no `fixedWindow` in messages/routes.ts). |
| Operator update checker + one-command / auto update | standard | Discord, Signal Desktop, WhatsApp (forced updates) | `jobs/update.ts` (git smart-protocol `info/refs`, every 6 h — `UPDATE_CHECK_INTERVAL_S` constants.ts:1269), admin UpdateBanner, `bored update` / autoupdate cron. No SaaS, no key — consistent with rule 7. |
| Single-origin self-hosted deploy, auto-HTTPS, one-command ops CLI | standard | n/a | Caddyfile, docker-compose, `bored` (status, update, tunnel, backup, export, import). `./bored tunnel` shells out to cloudflared (`bored:117-125`), a third-party binary on the host — worth an explicit note against rule 7. |
| Data-minimisation retention sweeps (IPs 90d, activity 30d, tokens, stash) | hygiene (not counted) | Signal, Telegram; Reddit/Meta (policy-driven) | `jobs/gc.ts`; `IP_RETENTION_DAYS` 90, `ACTIVITY_RETENTION_DAYS` 30 (constants.ts:526, 987). Good practice, undocumented to users (no privacy note exists) — listed, not scored. |
| Passkey / password-less sign-in | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram, X, Discord, Instagram | `auth/passkeys.ts`, `lib/webauthn.ts`, `settings/security`; `PASSKEY_LOGIN_RATE_PER_MIN` 10. Tunnel-origin caveat documented. |
| TOTP two-factor authentication (second step on password and passkey login) with recovery-code bypass | standard | Telegram, Discord, Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit | `auth/totp.ts` routes at 65/84/119/154 (RFC 6238, AES-256-GCM secret), setup QR at `security/page.tsx:182`, login mode `totp` (login/page.tsx:46). FEATURES #85. Neither inventory named it; shipped and reachable. |
| Invite link expiry, usage caps, open-link cap | standard | WhatsApp, Telegram, Discord | `INVITE_TTL_S` 7d, `INVITE_MAX_USES` 50, `INVITE_OPEN_MAX` 20, `INVITE_CREATE_RATE_PER_HOUR` 30 (constants.ts:78-91); the join page names expired/revoked states (API §1.11). InviteSheet reuses the newest open link; only the list/revoke UI is missing (see Half-built). |
| Self-serve recovery codes (generate, regenerate, recover + reset) | standard | Discord, GitHub-style, Telegram (cloud password) | `auth/recovery.ts` 95/114/135/173, `RecoveryCodes.tsx`, login modes `recover`/`reset`; `RECOVERY_LOGIN_RATE_PER_HOUR` 5. **This is the only functioning forgot-password path** — email is inert. |
| Operator-initiated per-user JSON export | standard | every GDPR-subject platform does this operator-side | `GET /api/admin/users/:id/export` (admin/routes.ts:358), admin Users `exportUser()` (users/page.tsx:91). User row minus secrets plus related rows, never message bodies. Obvious base for the user-facing takeout that is only "planned". |
| First-run spotlight tour / coach marks | basic | Snapchat, TikTok, BeReal use coach marks; Instagram/Telegram dropped them | `SpotlightTour.tsx` mounted in `GlobalOverlays` (line 43), `TOUR_STEPS`/`TOUR_SEEN_KEY`, skippable, reduced-motion fallback. Shipped, but one of its four anchors — `tab-plans` (constants.ts:1018) — no longer exists in `Tabs.tsx` (the comment at Tabs.tsx:14 admits it) and the Feed tab has no step at all. |

### Half-built

| Feature | Tier | What exists | What is missing | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| "New version available, reload" prompt after deploy | basic | `sw.js` shell cache; operator-only UpdateBanner on `/admin` | Versioned cache name, `updatefound`/`controllerchange` listener, a "Reload to update" toast. TROUBLESHOOTING.md:26 tells users to "close fully and reopen twice". Every user hits this on day one of any upgrade. | **P0** |
| Canonical in-app URL for every object | basic | `/c/[id]`, `/u/[username]`, `/p/[token]`, `/s/[token]`, `/join/[token]` | `/feed/<id>`, `/local/<id>`, `/plans/<id>`. `SharePageView.deepLink()` (line 123) returns `/feed`, `/local` or `/`. API.md:2622 promises "the client completes the deep link back to the object in-app"; the client cannot — a new user from a shared post lands on the generic Feed tab. | **P0** |
| Share OUT via OS share sheet (posts, beacons, listings, groups, stories) | basic | `lib/share.ts copyShareLink()` clipboard-only (line 30), 5 callers (FeedView:969, BeaconDetailSheet:283, ListingDetailSheet:476, GroupSettingsSection:208, StoryViewer:366) | `navigator.share` at those five sites — two lines next to the existing copy call. SPECS #110 (share audit) commits to "OS share sheet" on every surface; the client has not caught up. "Copy link" is a dead end for most phone users; this is how content leaves the app and brings people in. | **P0** |
| Forgot-password by email / email verification / change email | basic | Recovery-code reset works (login modes `recover`→`reset`, `POST /api/auth/recover`, `/reset-password`, revokes all sessions). SMTP form saves encrypted; `AuthSettingsSchema.features` persists; design in SPECS 175-180 | No mailer ships (`mailer_available` is always false — api.ts:2871-2883). No change-email (`UpdateMeRequestSchema` api.ts:855-865 accepts only display_name/bio/avatar_motion/area/name_style). No change-password while signed in. Registration collects an email it never verifies or uses (`users.email_verified_at` exists, nothing sets it); a user who loses the codes is locked out until the operator runs `cli reset-password`. | **P0** |
| Mute a chat | basic | `participants.muted_until` column, `pushGuard.ts` honours it, notifications copy says "Muted chats stay silent", FEATURES #9 claims "muting" shipped | No route in `modules/conversations` (grep `mute`: nothing), no UI anywhere. Users will hunt for it in the first noisy group. | **P0** |
| Offline / reconnecting banner on every surface | basic | `.offline-banner` in ConversationListView only (grep) | Feed, Local, Plans render empty or erroring lists offline with no explanation. | P1 |
| Offline attachment send | standard | Outbox carries `attachment_ids` only; streaming multipart upload | Upload queue + retry. A photo picked offline fails and is never retried while the text beside it is queued. | P1 |
| Failed-send state ("not sent, tap to retry") | standard | "Queued (offline)" label (ChatView:807) | `flushOutbox()` removes any non-network rejection with a `// rejected for good` comment (connection.ts:579) and no UI state; the message simply vanishes. | P1 |
| Live cross-device sync of privacy/notification settings | standard | Durable on reload; `prefs.updated` WS event exists (shared/ws.ts:277) | `modules/settings/routes.ts` publishes nothing (grep `publish`: 0); a second device keeps old toggles until Privacy is reopened. | P1 |
| Discoverability controls (who can find me / message me) | standard | `invites_paused`, `group_add_policy` (api.ts:1245-1247; privacy page) | No `findable`, no `dm_policy` (grep: nothing); `POST /api/conversations {dm}` checks blocks only. Anyone findable can DM anyone — matters more once Bored Together puts strangers in front of each other. | P1 |
| Prefilled SMS / email / WhatsApp invite buttons | basic | Share…/Copy in InviteSheet | `sms:`, `mailto:`, `wa.me` links. Phone share sheet covers SMS/WhatsApp; desktop users get Copy only. | P1 |
| Manage my invite links (list, status, revoke) | standard | `GET /api/invites`, `DELETE /api/invites/:id` (API §1.11, lines 707-708) | Client only reuses-newest, mints, public, accepts. No list page, no revoke call. A link pasted into the wrong group cannot be killed; only TTL / 50 uses / 20 opens ends it. | P1 |
| Profile link that unfurls as the person (OG on `/u/<username>`) | standard | Join, `/p`, `/s` all have OG | `/u/[username]/page.tsx` is `'use client'` with no `generateMetadata`. Pasting a profile into WhatsApp shows the generic site title. SPECS #110 plans "a standalone share link" for profiles. | P1 |
| Share-target into a feed post or story | standard | `compose/page.tsx` conversation picker; manifest accepts image/video | No story/feed target (grep `story|feed|post` in compose: nothing). A camera-roll photo cannot become a post or story from the OS sheet. | P1 |
| Per-chat "Media, links, docs" browser | standard | `/gallery` (own uploads only) | ChatInfoSheet has no per-chat media tab. A chat's shared photos and links are not browsable once they scroll away. | P1 |
| Report dispositions + one-tap escalation | standard | `POST /api/admin/reports/:id/close` with note | Resolve-as, suspend/ban the author from the report. Every escalation is a manual detour through the users table. | P1 |
| Help center / FAQ with working "try it" links | basic | Help & feedback page; "Things you can do" directory (`/things`) | 15 of 25 `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` hrefs point at `/` (12) or `/settings` (3) — constants.ts:1037-1065; no FAQ, no privacy/retention explainer; directory not linked from Help & about. | P1 |
| Bug report with auto-attached diagnostics | standard | Tickets accept a screenshot | No version/UA/log attached; operator cannot tell which build or browser a ticket came from. | P1 |
| Anti-bot registration defence | standard | register 3/h/IP (auth/routes.ts:28); `users.email_verified_at` column | Verification inert; no captcha/Turnstile. Behind a tunnel or shared NAT 3/h/IP is too strict for real users and trivial for a proxy bot farm; invite-only mode is the real defence and it does not exist either. | P1 |
| Externally reachable health endpoint | standard | `GET /healthz` (app.ts:110), compose-network only | Caddyfile routes only `/api/*` and `/ws` (confirmed: no healthz in Caddyfile). UptimeRobot-style monitors cannot reach it — a one-line change. | P1 |
| In-app "what's new" cards | standard | `WhatsNewCard.tsx`; release_notes seeded in migrations 0009-0011 (v0.8a/b, v0.9) | 0012 adds none (grep `release_notes`: 0); v1.0a, #103 sounds and v1.1 Royal shipped with no note. Mechanism works, content stopped three releases ago; FEATURE_DIRECTORY entries tagged since v1.0 can never earn a "new" badge. | P1 |
| App version visible in About + attached to tickets | basic | `GIT_COMMIT` baked into the server image (config.ts:43, Dockerfile:27); `running_version` on `/api/admin/update` | Help footer says "Bored Chat · self-hosted" with a source comment "no invented version number" (help/page.tsx:220-222). Show the short hash. | P1 |
| Per-category notification toggles, quiet hours / DND, digest | standard | `wave_nudges` opt-in with worker batching per opt-in hour (FEATURES #78a); per-chat text-sound override (device-local); deliberately no "all off" (PSYCHOLOGY §6) | Quiet hours, per-category toggles for messages/calls/plans/stories, a general digest. The app's own psychology doc demands digest batching; everything else pushes in real time. | P1 |
| Logout wipes local data | standard | `logout()` → `db.clearOutbox()` + `store.resetOnLogout()` (connection.ts:125-126) | `db.ts` has no clear-all/`deleteDatabase` (its outbox helpers are add/list/remove/clear only). Cached message bodies survive Sign out in IndexedDB; the next person on a shared PC can read them offline. | P1 |
| Shareable call links | standard | `?call=` deep link on `/c/:id` via ring push only (jobs/push.ts:296) | No mint-a-call-link route. | P2 |
| Reduce-motion / animation toggle in-app | standard | `prefers-reduced-motion` rules; SpotlightTour and GlobalOverlays fallbacks | No in-app override. Fine for most. | P2 |
| Admin UI for instance settings beyond invites | standard | `PATCH /api/admin/settings {release_notes}` (admin/routes.ts:309-312) | No UI; the admin settings page shows only "Invite links" and "Call relay (TURN)" (settings/page.tsx:66, 86). Release notes can only be appended by migration or raw PATCH. | P2 |
| Admin roles / who are the admins / grant from UI | standard | `is_admin` flag shown per row (users/page.tsx:174) | Grant/revoke CLI-only (`cli.ts:73` usage: reset-password, grant-admin, revoke-admin). Fine for one operator; opaque for a team. | P2 |
| Wellbeing / time-management tools | standard | Finite feeds with a hard end (structural) | Quiet hours (see notifications). | P2 |
| Device / phone bans, ban-evasion detection | standard | Account ban; registration/last IP visible to admins | No IP soft-block, no fingerprint. A banned user re-registers in seconds. | P2 |

| Account security notices channel | standard | `sendSupportMessage()` posts into the user's Support conversation; used for recovery-code use (recovery.ts:155) and takedown notices (content.ts:183) | Not used for new-device login (login route notifies nothing), passkey/TOTP changes, session revokes. Plumbing exists; only triggers are missing. | P2 |

| Password strength policy / meter / breached-password check | basic | `PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH = 8` (constants.ts:74); Argon2 | No meter. HIBP needs a third-party API (rule 7 forbids), so a local estimator is the ceiling. | P2 |

Owner transfer is "future scope" (owner cannot leave). | P3 |

Not strict; the YouTube player phones home when used. Caddyfile comment acknowledges it as the one exception. | P3 |
