### Missing

| Feature | Tier | Priority | Which apps have it | Why it matters here |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Delete my account (user-initiated, irreversible, with cooldown) | basic | **P0** | all 17 | No `DELETE /api/users/me` (only `/me/avatar` — users/routes.ts:97), no row on the Account page. Only an admin ban exists and it deletes nothing. A hard legal and trust basic; the first thing a privacy-minded user checks. |
| Privacy policy / Terms / instance privacy note | basic | **P0** | all 17 | No `/privacy` or `/terms` route (app dir listing); `admin/users/page.tsx:108-109` and FEATURES #54 reference an "instance privacy note" that does not exist; register has no terms line. The 90-day IP retention and the admin-console-never-returns-messages rule (ADR-013) are documented nowhere a user can read. |
| Manual theme (light / dark / system) and text size | basic | **P0** | all 17 | Light is driven by `prefers-color-scheme` only (globals.css:83); no `data-theme` hook, no appearance page under `settings/` (dir listing). The Royal redesign made dark good; users cannot pick it or enlarge text. |
| Change password while signed in (with current-password re-proof) | basic | **P0** | all 17 | Only `/reset-password` behind a one-shot recovery token; `UpdateMeRequestSchema` has no password field; Security page asks for the password only to regenerate codes. Rotating a leaked password means burning a recovery code and signing out every device, or asking the operator (`cli.ts reset-password`). |
| Address-book / contacts import with hashed matching | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, BeReal, Nextdoor, Partiful | A real growth gap: a new user with zero friends gets username search and a suggestions pool. Dating apps and Reddit skip it; a messenger+community app should not. It is implementable self-hosted — `users.email` exists, so Contact Picker API → sha256(email) matching fits rule 7 — and must ship with a "delete uploaded contacts" control. P1, not P0: the lifecycle and share-out gaps above cost more users per day. |
| Sign out of all other devices (one-tap revoke all) | basic | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Instagram, Discord, X | `revokeAllSessions()` exists in `auth/service.ts:106`, called only from recovery (recovery.ts:152), admin suspend (admin/routes.ts:135) and the CLI. No route, no button. The first thing anyone does after losing a phone. |
| Registration gate: open / invite-only / closed from the admin console | basic | P1 | Matrix/Synapse, Rocket.Chat, Mastodon (self-hosted peers); Discord servers | `InstanceSettingsSchema` (api.ts:2893-2897) holds only `invites_enabled`, `release_notes`, `auth`; `POST /api/auth/register` checks only 3/h/IP and uniqueness. Anyone who finds the public origin can register. The invite kill-switch does not gate registration. The single biggest ops gap for an instance exposed via auto-HTTPS. |
| Operator-side account deletion / purge | basic | P1 | every GDPR-subject platform | `cli.ts` has only reset-password / grant-admin / revoke-admin; no `app.delete` in any admin module, no MinIO purge. Combined with no self-delete, nobody can erase an account from this instance. An EU operator cannot honour an erasure request; spam accounts can only be banned. |
| In-app "Install the app" prompt / Add-to-Home-Screen guidance | standard | P1 | X (PWA), Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Reddit | No `beforeinstallprompt` (grep: 0); the only A2HS mention is the iPhone branch of `unblockSteps()`, which users see only after a denial. iOS users cannot receive push until installed and the app never says so where they land (see How it looks). A day's work; the single highest-leverage retention fix for a PWA. |
| Cached feed / board / stories / profiles readable offline | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, X, Reddit, TikTok, Threads, Nextdoor, Snapchat, Discord | `sw.js:42` never caches `/api/*`; feed/listings/stories fetch live on mount. Only chat has an offline story; the second-most-used tab is blank offline. |
| Local draft persistence per chat / composer | standard | P1 | X, Reddit, TikTok, Threads, Telegram, WhatsApp, Bumble | `Composer.tsx` has only `ScheduleDraft` (line 24), no storage. Navigating away from a half-typed message loses it; every benchmark keeps drafts. |
| New-device sign-in alert to existing devices | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Telegram, Discord, X, Snapchat | A hijacked session is invisible until the victim opens Settings → Sessions. The support-conversation channel already exists; this is one trigger. |
| Profile QR code / scan-to-connect | standard | P1 | Instagram, Snapchat, Threads, Telegram, WhatsApp, Discord, Partiful | `QrCode.tsx` is used only on login and TOTP setup (grep). Not on any roadmap item (SPECS/FEATURES mention QR only for login, #83). A QR of `/u/<username>` on the profile sheet is an afternoon's work and is THE in-person growth loop for a "local community" app. |
| Data saver / "use less data" toggle | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord | Nothing honours `navigator.connection.saveData` (grep: 0). A mobile-data community app with zero data control is odd. |
| Per-media-type auto-download rules by network | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Messenger | Thumbs load eagerly, video `preload=metadata`, no setting. Damage bounded by 512px WebP q80 thumbs + blurhash (jobs/media.ts:2, 173), but stories, chat backgrounds and full media load with no user say. |
| Video autoplay control (Wi-Fi only / off) | standard | P1 | Instagram, Facebook, Nextdoor, Reddit, Discord, X, Tinder | `StoryViewer autoPlay={!reducedMotion}` (line 217), `ChatBackground autoPlay` (line 108). Only the OS reduced-motion setting suppresses it. |
| Storage usage view with clear cache / bulk delete | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Snapchat, TikTok, X, Discord, BeReal, Facebook | No `navigator.storage.estimate()` (grep: 0); `storage_bytes` is admin-only (api.ts:2648). Users cannot see or purge what the PWA caches, nor their server-side footprint. |
| Warn user / strikes / account standing | standard | P1 | Instagram, Discord, TikTok, Facebook, Reddit, Threads | FEATURES #54 claims "warn/suspend/ban"; no `warn` in any admin route (grep); API §1.41 lists suspend/ban only. Documented as shipped but absent; the only gradient between nothing and suspend is a support reply. |
| Appeals flow for suspensions / bans | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Discord, Bumble, Snapchat, Reddit, Telegram | No appeal anywhere; a suspended user cannot log in to file a ticket. Even a reason + contact line on the 403 login error would do. |
| Report reason taxonomy | standard | P1 | all 17 | `ReportSheet` single free-text reason; `CreateReportRequestSchema` `reason: z.string().max(500).default('')` (api.ts:1305). The queue cannot be triaged — a self-harm report looks identical to spam. `SUPPORT_CATEGORIES` exist for tickets but not reports. |
| Keyword filters / Hidden Words / AutoMod | standard | P1 | Instagram, TikTok, Hinge, Discord, Reddit, Facebook | Zero exists, user-side or operator-side. Standard self-defence for anyone receiving strangers' messages. |
| Age gate / 18+ or 13+ attestation | standard | P1 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Instagram/Threads, Snapchat | Register collects username, display name, email, password (register/page.tsx:182-236) — no age line. A stranger-meeting pool (Bored Together) with no age attestation is a liability for any operator. |
| Feature-adoption / signup-funnel / retention analytics (first-party) | standard | P1 | Instagram Insights, TikTok, Discord, Luma, Meetup, X analytics | No event table; admin metrics are DB-derived series only. The operator cannot see where signups drop or which features are dead except via SQL. |
| North-star outcome metric (plans completed, conversations started) | standard | P1 | n/a — unique to this product's PSYCHOLOGY.md | PSYCHOLOGY rule 1 names it; no counter anywhere. The product's own definition of success is unmeasured. |
| Public status page / incident history | standard | P1 | Signal, Discord, Reddit, Snapchat, Meta, Luma, Meetup | When the instance is down users have nowhere to look; `./bored status` is host-only. A static page on another host would do. |
| App lock (PIN / biometric re-auth) | standard | P1 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram (regional), Discord | WebAuthn is already wired for login; re-auth on resume is plumbing, and it matters for a messenger with disappearing messages. |
| Custom 404 / error boundary pages | basic | P2 | all 17 | No `not-found.tsx`, `error.tsx`, `global-error.tsx`, `loading.tsx` anywhere under `app/` (find). Broken deep links fall to Next.js defaults; an error boundary is also the natural place to report crashes to the operator. |
| robots.txt / noindex policy | basic | P2 | all 17 | No `robots.txt` or `robots` metadata. Share pages deliberately emit full OG, so once a token URL is posted anywhere crawlable it is indexable with nothing telling crawlers otherwise. One file. |
| Android App Links / iOS Universal Links | standard | P2 | all 17 native apps | No `.well-known/`. Moot until a native wrapper exists; ship together. |
| Offline message search over cached history | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | No message search at all yet (planned #108), so offline search is moot for now. |
| Drafts synced across devices | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Threads, Instagram | No draft store at all. Ship local drafts first. |
| Saved Messages / note-to-self | standard | P2 | Telegram, WhatsApp ("You"), Signal | A self-DM with a pinned label; the habitual way people move links phone↔laptop. |
| "Contact joined" re-engagement notification | standard | P2 | Telegram, BeReal, Snapchat, WhatsApp | Depends on contacts import; must obey PSYCHOLOGY §6 digest rules. |
| Guest RSVP / join a plan without an account | standard | P2 | Partiful, Luma | Partiful's biggest conversion lever, but it collides with the blocks/privacy model and the no-mailer reality. Decide deliberately rather than drift into it. |
| Upload quality choice (HD / compressed) | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, Telegram | Server-fixed webp q80 (jobs/media.ts:173) / jpeg q85 (uploads/pipeline.ts:215). Sensible default, no choice. |
| NSFW / sensitive-media blur and flag | standard | P2 | Reddit, X, Instagram, Discord, Telegram | No sensitive flag on uploads. A stranger-meeting pool makes this more than polish. |
| Export a single chat | standard | P2 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal | Nice-to-have once takeout exists. |
| Deactivate / pause account | standard | P2 | Instagram, Facebook, Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | `invites_paused` pauses connection requests only. Snooze is the gentler cousin of delete and fits the calm-defaults ethos. |
| Client-side error / crash reporting into the operator log | standard | P2 | Signal, Instagram, Facebook, Threads, Snapchat, TikTok | Admin logs show pino server logs only. A rate-limited `POST /api/client-log` closes the loop with zero third-party code. |
| Calendar export / ICS for plans and beacons | standard | P2 | Luma, Meetup, Facebook events, Partiful | A plan that cannot land in the phone calendar is easier to forget, and the north-star is "plans completed". |
| Change username / handle | standard | P2 | Telegram, Instagram | `UpdateMeRequestSchema` has no username; the Account page renders it as a disabled input (account/page.tsx:224); the handle is the public `/u/<username>` URL, permanent from signup. |
| Installed-app icon badge (`navigator.setAppBadge`) | standard | P2 | native messengers; installed PWAs on Android/desktop/iOS 16.4+ | No `setAppBadge` anywhere (grep: 0); unread counts exist only inside the chat list UI. The data is already there. |
| Background Sync for the offline outbox | standard | P2 | native apps; PWA Background Sync | `sw.js` has no `sync` listener (grep); `flushOutbox` runs only in a live client. Messages composed offline only send if the app is reopened while online. |
| Operator announcement / broadcast to all members | standard | P2 | Discord, Facebook/Nextdoor groups, Mastodon | The only operator→everyone channel is `release_notes` (no UI, release-scoped). Per PSYCHOLOGY this should stay a pull-based card, but it needs a UI. |
| Automated abuse detection (image hashing, classifiers, spam ML) | advanced | P2 | WhatsApp, Instagram, Snapchat, TikTok, Bumble, Hinge, Tinder, BeReal, Discord, Nextdoor | No classifier; rate limits only. Rule 7 makes this hard; perceptual-hash dedupe of reported/taken-down images is the realistic first step. |
| Lite / low-end-device build | standard | P3 | Instagram Lite, Facebook Lite, TikTok Lite | Mega-app problem; the PWA is already small. Not worth building. |
| Beta / canary release channel | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Facebook, Snapchat, Discord, Reddit | Operators can pin any commit; low value. |
| Multiple accounts / switching | standard | P3 | X, Instagram, Reddit, Discord | Single `sid` cookie per origin. Low value for a community app. |
| Contacts used only for blocking | standard | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge | Dating-app pattern; irrelevant until contacts import exists. |
| Manage / delete uploaded contacts | standard | P3 | Facebook, X, WhatsApp, Telegram | Must ship in the same PR as contacts import if that is built. |
| Short links / vanity URLs | standard | P3 | Reddit, TikTok, X, Facebook, Telegram | A self-hosted origin is short enough. |
| Custom URL scheme / open-in-app banners | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram, Signal, Instagram, X, Discord, Tinder, Bumble | Moot until native apps. |
| Direct-share targets inside the OS sheet | standard | P3 | WhatsApp, Signal, Telegram, Instagram | Requires a native share extension. Moot. |
| Creator / host insights | standard | P3 | Instagram, TikTok, X, Luma, Meetup, Nextdoor, Snapchat | In tension with the no-numbers ethos; beacons already show joiners as named faces, which is the right answer. |
| A/B testing framework | standard | P3 | Tinder, Bumble, Hinge, Instagram, BeReal | At odds with the calm-product ethos; skip. |
| Desktop keyboard shortcuts | standard | P3 | WhatsApp Web, Telegram Desktop, Slack, Discord | Only `DoodleSheet` and the puzzle page handle modifier keys (grep `ctrlKey|metaKey`); no global handler despite the two-pane layout. |
| Maintenance / read-only mode with user-facing notice | standard | P3 | every hosted platform | Backups/imports stop api+worker while users see raw connection errors; `./bored update` restarts with no notice. |
| Wearable / TV / console clients | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, TikTok, Discord | Irrelevant. |
| Push delivery independent of Google/Apple | advanced | P3 | Signal | Inherent to Web Push; worth a line in the privacy note, nothing to build. |
| Offline tickets / wallet passes | advanced | P3 | Luma | Plans are free and local; no value. |
| Mailed postcard invitations | advanced | P3 | Nextdoor | Not applicable. |
| Encrypted cloud backup with user-owned key | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Signal, Messenger | The operator's backup is the backup; revisit with E2EE. |
| Transfer / import from other platforms | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Luma | Not applicable. |
| Archive / recently-deleted recovery window | advanced | P3 | Instagram, Facebook | Tombstones only. Low value. |
| Volunteer / community moderator roles | advanced | P3 | Reddit, Discord, Nextdoor, X | Single CLI-granted flag. Fine at this scale. |
| Transparency report | advanced | P3 | Meta, TikTok, Snapchat, Reddit, Discord, X | Not applicable at instance scale. |
| Proxy / censorship circumvention | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Telegram | Self-hosting on your own domain is the circumvention. |
| Developer platform (API tokens, bots, webhooks, oEmbed) | advanced | P3 | Telegram, Discord, Facebook, Instagram, X, Threads, Reddit, TikTok, Luma, Nextdoor | Cookie-only auth. Backlog-worthy once federation is scheduled. |
| AI assistant / summaries | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp, Facebook, X, Nextdoor, Bumble | Rule 7 forbids third-party APIs; not a gap worth filling. |
| Community Notes | advanced | P3 | X, Facebook, Threads | Not applicable. |
| OAuth "Login with…" for third parties | advanced | P3 | X, Snapchat, TikTok, Nextdoor | Not applicable. |
| Business / organisation profiles, verification badges | advanced | P3 | WhatsApp Business, Instagram, Facebook Pages, Nextdoor, BeReal, X | The listings board covers local gigs without a business tier. |
| Embed widgets / cross-posting | advanced | P3 | Luma, Meetup, Meta cross-post | Rule 7 territory. |

**Coverage, from these tables.** Basic tier: 15 has ÷ (15 has + 11 half-built + 10 missing + 1 planned) = **15/37 = 41%**. Standard tier: 25 has ÷ (25 + 26 + 46 + 6 planned) = **25/103 = 24%**. Advanced: 1/20. Half-built earns nothing; the five rows tagged "hygiene" (rate limiting, retention sweeps, CI gates, security headers, container healthchecks) are listed for completeness and excluded; the three banned items below carry no tier. Duplicate rows the verifier filed twice (TOTP, container healthchecks, change-password, registration mode, sign-out-others, operator announcement, app badge) have been collapsed.

### Planned already

- **Native iOS and Android apps** (basic, P1) — FEATURES #56 "Capacitor wrappers", backlog. No capacitor/react-native/tauri in any package.json. The PWA is the chosen strategy; the day-one pain is fixable by the install prompt + stale-shell fix, which are not planned.
- **End-to-end encryption with device key registry** (advanced, P2) — FEATURES #57; SPECS keeps it backlog. Until this ships, "admin never reads private message content" (ADR-013:53) is a statement about which routes exist, not about what the operator can read from the Postgres volume.
- **User self-service data export / takeout** (standard, P1) — API.md:1642 "future user-facing scope". The operator-side export already exists; this is a route and a button.
- **Scheduled automatic backups with retention + restore from admin UI** (standard, P1) — FEATURES #58 "One-click backup/restore"; README cron recipe only; container never gets docker.sock (ADR-013/020). An unscheduled backup is the most likely way a community loses everything.
- **Language override / i18n** (standard, P1) — FEATURES #58; `<html lang="en">` hard-coded, all copy inline. Retrofit cost grows with every component shipped.
- **Server-side search over my own messages** (standard, P1) — FEATURES #108 (v1.0 block); design at SPECS.md:266-269 (per-user trigram/FTS). No endpoint yet.
- **Per-instance branding ("make-it-yours kit")** (standard, P2) — FEATURES #55; name hard-coded in manifest and layout.
- **Email notifications / digest channel** (standard, P2) — SMTP persists encrypted, no mailer ships; SPECS 175-180 covers auth emails only, not digests.
- **Federation / ActivityPub** (advanced, P3) — FEATURES #58 "someday".

### Deliberately not built

- **Referral rewards / invite-to-earn** — PSYCHOLOGY rule 4 (no XP/points; external rewards crowd out intrinsic fun). **The ban is right.** But it is being used as cover: the reward is banned, the loop is not. Contacts import (P1), profile QR (P1) and a real share sheet (P0) are not incentives, and none of them exist.
- **Paid storage / premium tiers** — CLAUDE.md rule 7, SPECS standing decision "no payments". **Right for this product.** The operator pays hosting; a quota tier would make the operator a billing company.
- **Ads / sponsored content / subscriptions / creator payouts** — rule 7, PSYCHOLOGY, SPECS. **Right, and the strongest trust claim the product has.** It is also unused: nothing on login, register or Help says it.

### How it looks

Screens that decide whether a stranger signs up, stays, and can get out again — restricted to screens in the captured set. The recovery-codes wall, `/welcome`, the spotlight tour, the Devices page and the admin console were not captured; their code-level findings live in the tables above, not here.

- **Register (`phone-register.png`)** — zero trust or legal signals: no "By continuing you agree…", no terms/privacy page anywhere, no age attestation, no "hosted by". Heading is "Join the fun" (register/page.tsx:175) over a form that collects an email for an app with a stranger-meeting pool and a drunk mode. Hinge/Bumble: DOB + 18+ + terms before anything; Instagram: terms line under the button. Fix: consent sentence linking `/terms` and `/privacy` (operator-edited markdown in Admin → Settings, sane default), an "I'm 16 or older" checkbox, "Hosted by <operator>".
- **Register — email field** — required (`reg-email`), never verified, never used, no helper text. Instagram verifies with a 6-digit code before the account exists. Fix: make optional with "Only for account recovery once this server has email set up. Never shown to anyone", or verify when SMTP is on.
- **Register — validation** — username rules and the 8-char minimum (`PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH`) appear only as post-submit errors; availability only after a round-trip failure (no availability endpoint exists — grep `username-available`: nothing); no strength bar. Telegram shows live "available/taken". Fix: helper text, debounced `GET /api/auth/username-available`, 3-segment strength bar.
- **Login (`phone-login.png`)** — the only recovery door is a small footer button "Lost everything?" (login/page.tsx:698-706); nothing under the password field says "Forgot password?"; the recover form takes only a code and never mentions asking the operator. The wordmark cycles Bored/Happy/Sad/Drunk every 2.6 s (`WORDMARK_FLIP_INTERVAL_MS = 2600`, constants.ts:774) beside a password field. "Log in with phone · QR" (line 685) renders at every width — no media query in `authpack.module.css` hides it — on the one device that cannot use it. Fix: static wordmark on auth pages; inline "Forgot password?"; recover screen explains code vs operator contact (and email once SMTP is on); hide the QR door below the desktop breakpoint.
- **Login/register — value proposition** — nothing says what the app is or why to trust it; the self-hosted/no-ads/feeds-end story is the strongest lever and is unused. Hinge: "Designed to be deleted" carousel. Fix: three short lines under the card + "What is this?" link.
- **Help & about (`settings-help.png`)** — no about: no version, no operator identity ("Send to the operator" — who?), no terms/privacy, no retention statement, and the "open" ticket chip is styled with `--danger` (community.module.css:1218-1221). Telegram: FAQ, Privacy Policy, version string. Fix: About block with version + commit, operator name/contact from admin settings, "What we keep", links, Things you can do.
- **Settings root (`phone-settings.png`)** — no Appearance, no Storage, no Language (settings dir: account, blocked, calls, chats, help, notifications, privacy, profile, security, sessions); Sign out is bare red text that logs out on tap with no confirmation (settings/page.tsx:194). Telegram has all three. Fix: add Appearance (System/Dark/Light, text size) and Storage; confirm sheet on Sign out.
- **Account / Security (`settings-account.png`, `settings-security.png`)** — no change password, no change email, no delete account, no download-my-data; username is a disabled input with no reason (account/page.tsx:224); email is an unlabelled grey line (line 226). WhatsApp Settings → Account has every one of these. Fix: Account group with Change password, Change email, Download my data, Delete account (red, bottom, re-auth, 7–30 day grace).
- **Chat settings sheet (`chat-info.png`)** — no Mute anywhere; the sheet leads with theme swatches. WhatsApp: mute from the row and from contact info. Fix: "Mute notifications" row (8h / 1w / always) at the top of the sheet patching `muted_until`; muted bell on list rows.
- **Feed card (`phone-feed-list.png`)** — the only share is a copy-public-link ghost icon; no "Send to a chat", no OS share, no post permalink; images open the raw proxy URL in a new tab (`FeedView.tsx:1013` `target="_blank"`) and throw an installed PWA out to the browser, although `MediaLightbox.tsx` already exists. Instagram: paper-plane to DM + share sheet + a detail URL per post. Fix: action row React · Reply · Send (`ForwardSheet.tsx` exists) + `navigator.share`; `/feed/[id]` route; MediaLightbox for images.
- **Feed end card (`phone-feed.png`)** — "You're all caught up" (FeedView.tsx:337) for an account with zero people. Instagram's empty home is a "Find people" module. Fix: branch on connections count; zero → "Your feed is posts from your people. You don't have any yet." + Find people / Share invite link.
- **Connections (`phone-connections.png`)** — "No more requests — share your link to meet people." (connections/page.tsx:205) implies requests existed; bare "Copy" with no explanation of what the link does; "+ Find" is a 12px link (`community.module.css:1455`). Telegram: full-width "Invite friends" into the share sheet. Fix: "No requests yet"; "Share invite link" with caption "7 days, lands them in a chat with you"; promote Find to a button when fewer than 3 people.
- **Notifications (`settings-notifications.png`)** — the blocked state is handled properly: `unblockSteps()` (notifications/page.tsx:39-54) branches for iPhone/iPad, Firefox, Edge, Safari and Chrome, and the iPhone branch tells people to add the app to the home screen first. The hole is the state iOS users actually land in: Safari in a tab has no Notification API, so they get "Not supported in this browser." (line 146), a disabled toggle, and no install guidance — the only Add-to-Home-Screen instruction in the app sits behind a denial they can never trigger. Fix: an iOS-aware `unsupported` branch with the A2HS steps; a one-time "Install Bored Chat" card after the first conversation (`beforeinstallprompt` on Chromium — zero hits in the client today).
- **Local (`phone-local.png`)** — "Make me discoverable" (DiscoverySection.tsx:132) is the most prominent CTA on the screen with no first-time explainer of what strangers see or how to report; report/block sit on each card but nothing says so before the first opt-in. Tinder/Bumble: guidelines/safety interstitial before the first swipe. Fix: one-time consent sheet, remembered per account.

### If I had one week

1. **Day 1 — the small P0 batch, all shippable in one PR.** Appearance page with a `data-theme` override + text-size (the CSS tokens already exist; light just needs a selector beside globals.css:83); `PATCH /api/conversations/:id/mute` + a Mute row in ChatInfoSheet + muted bell in the list; `navigator.share` with clipboard fallback at the five `copyShareLink` callers; versioned SW cache name from `GIT_COMMIT` + a "Reload to update" toast on `controllerchange`; Caddy `handle /api/healthz` → api; Referrer-Policy / Permissions-Policy / nosniff in the same Caddy block; short commit hash in Help & about and on every support ticket.
2. **Day 2 — account basics and the legal floor.** `POST /api/auth/password` (current + new, optional revoke-others), `POST /api/auth/sessions/revoke-others` + button, `DELETE /api/users/me` (re-auth, 7-day grace, cancel-on-login, tombstone semantics from ADR-013), `registration_mode: open | invite_only | closed` in `InstanceSettingsSchema` enforced in `POST /api/auth/register` and surfaced on the admin settings page, and `/terms` + `/privacy` rendered from operator-edited markdown with a default that states the 90-day IP window and that the admin console never returns message bodies — and that this is policy, not encryption. Consent line + age checkbox on register. Move the recovery-codes step out of the signup path into Settings → Security.
3. **Day 3 — object URLs.** `/feed/[postId]`, `/local/[listingId]`, `/local/b/[beaconId]` rendering the existing cards; fix `SharePageView.deepLink()` and `safeNext()` targets so a shared post lands on the post after signup; `generateMetadata` on `/u/[username]`; fix the 15 dead `FEATURE_DIRECTORY` hrefs; `not-found.tsx` + `error.tsx` that posts to a rate-limited `POST /api/client-log`; `robots.txt`.
4. **Day 4 — the two growth loops that are banned-adjacent but not banned.** Profile QR on the profile sheet and a "Scan" entry (QrCode.tsx exists; `/u/<username>` is the payload); contacts import as the P1 it is — Contact Picker API → `POST /api/contacts/match` with sha256(email) against `users.email`, results shown as "On Bored Chat" rows with a connect button and a "Delete uploaded contacts" control shipped in the same PR; `sms:` and `mailto:` buttons in InviteSheet; always render the invite card on `/welcome` (`inviteCard`, `showEmpty`); branch the feed end card on connections count.
5. **Day 5 — ops honesty.** Seed release notes for v1.0a, #103 and v1.1 Royal and add a release-notes textarea to the admin settings page; api/web/worker healthchecks in compose backed by `/healthz`; make `./bored backup` either include MinIO or refuse to call itself a backup (print "DATABASE ONLY — media is not included" in red); a `plans_completed` counter (beacon reached end with ≥2 joiners) and `dm_started` counter in the admin metrics so the north-star is at least a number on a screen; new-device login → `sendSupportMessage()` trigger, since the channel already exists.
